"workspace"

	( setup_animals() has a source maximum of 100 life forms.  Classification
	  is retained separately, then active mammal and bird records are packed
	  without truncating that population. )
	VHGNDfaunatypes = 100;
	VHGNDfaunascales = 100;
	( x, z, heading*100, phase, movement type, speed, scale,
	  target heading*100, target speed, original LFS index. )
	VHGNDanidata = 1000;
	( Previous authoritative positions used only by the 60-Hz presenter. )
	VHGNDanimalprevx = 100; VHGNDanimalprevz = 100; VHGNDanimalprevdir = 100;
	( x, z, heading*100, phase, altitude, target altitude, capture count,
	  scale, speed, target speed, original LFS index, target heading*100. )
	VHGNDbirddata = 1200;
	VHGNDbirdprevx = 100; VHGNDbirdprevz = 100; VHGNDbirdprevquote = 100; VHGNDbirdprevdir = 100;
	( Twenty-five source wave records: x, y, z, radius, height, width, delta. )
	VHGNDwavedata = 175;
	VHGNDwaveprevradius = 25; VHGNDwaveprevheight = 25;
	( ruinschart: one source marker per 200x200 height-map point. )
	VHGNDruins = 40000;
	VHGNDskycache = 64000;
	( Per-frame exact landed-grid transform cache.  Each surface point keeps
	  the binary64 carriers produced by polymap's narrowed rotation, its
	  near-plane flag, and the two nearest-even projected integers. )
	VHGNDvcstamp = 40000; VHGNDvcrx0 = 40000; VHGNDvcrx1 = 40000;
	VHGNDvcry0 = 40000; VHGNDvcry1 = 40000;
	VHGNDvcrz0 = 40000; VHGNDvcrz1 = 40000;
	VHGNDvcvisible = 40000; VHGNDvcpx = 40000; VHGNDvcpy = 40000;
	( Two static triangle normals per cell, cached as their exact binary32 bits. )
	VHGNDnormstamp = 80000; VHGNDnormx = 80000;
	VHGNDnormy = 80000; VHGNDnormz = 80000;
	( Three possible source objects per 200x200 quadrant.  Positions and the
	  post-coordinate fast RNG state are immutable for a generated surface. )
	VHGNDobjcachex = 120000; VHGNDobjcachey = 120000;
	VHGNDobjcachez = 120000; VHGNDobjcacheseed = 120000;
	VHGNDsqc = 32;
	VHGNDepoctext = 96;
	( Depth-first build_fractal_tree stack.  Source giant trees need levels 0..4. )
	VHGNDtsx = 6; VHGNDtsy = 6; VHGNDtsz = 6; VHGNDtsscale = 6;
	VHGNDtslayers = 6; VHGNDtsroot = 6; VHGNDtsocc = 6;
	VHGNDtscount = 6; VHGNDtsbranch = 6; VHGNDtslseed = 6; VHGNDtsrange = 6;
	VHTsintab = 361; VHTcostab = 361;
	VHGNDenvtext = 128;

"programme"

"VHGND generate"
	( Generate the two landable bodies in the opening system through their
	  real build_surface switch arms: type 8 milky/quartz and type 4 rocky.
	  Body 2 is type 6 and is rejected by VHG land. )
	[VHGNDtick] = 0; [VHGNDptr] = 0; [VHGNDplayerstep] = 0;
	[VHGNDvccacheok] = 0;
	[VHGNDnormgen]+; A = [VHGNDnormgen]; ? A != 0 -> VHGND normal generation ready;
	[VHGNDptr] = 0;
    "VHGND normal cache clear"
	A = VHGNDnormstamp; A + [VHGNDptr]; [A] = 0;
	[VHGNDptr]+; A = [VHGNDptr]; ? A < 80000 -> VHGND normal cache clear;
	[VHGNDnormgen] = 1;
    "VHGND normal generation ready"
	[VHGNDptr] = 0;
	[VHGNDwaswet] = 0; [VHGNDwavescalm] = 0;
	[VHGNDstormflashes] = 0; [VHGNDflashactive] = 0; [VHGNDflashpending] = 0;
	[VHGNDflashlast] = 0;
	[VHGNDflashgain] = 63;
	A = [VHGNDlat]; A '* [VHGNDlon]; [VHGNDlocseed] = A;
	[GRgseed] = [VHGNDseed]; [GRsctype] = 0; [GRalbedo] = 32;
	=> VHGND landing identity;
    "VHGND wipe surface"
	A = RPSM; A + [VHGNDptr]; [MBptr] = A; [MBval] = 0; => MEM put byte;
	[VHGNDptr]+; A = [VHGNDptr]; ? A '< 40000 -> VHGND wipe surface;
	[VHGNDptr] = 0;
    "VHGND wipe objects"
	A = ROBJ; A + [VHGNDptr]; [MBptr] = A; [MBval] = 0; => MEM put byte;
	[VHGNDptr]+; A = [VHGNDptr]; ? A '< 40000 -> VHGND wipe objects;
	[VHGNDptr] = 0;
    "VHGND wipe ruins"
	A = VHGNDruins; A + [VHGNDptr]; [A] = 0;
	[VHGNDptr]+; A = [VHGNDptr]; ? A '< 40000 -> VHGND wipe ruins;
	( _fmemset(txtr,16,65535): RPBG[0..65534]=16, slot 65535 remains 0. )
	[VHGNDptr] = 0;
    "VHGND wipe texture"
	A = [VHGNDtexbase]; A + [VHGNDptr]; [MBptr] = A; [MBval] = 16; => MEM put byte;
	[VHGNDptr]+; A = [VHGNDptr]; ? A '< 65535 -> VHGND wipe texture;
	A = [VHGNDtexbase]; A + 65535; [MBptr] = A; [MBval] = 0; => MEM put byte;

	=> SU ledger reset; => GR float init;
	[VHGNDrockscale] = 500; [VHGNDrockpeak] = 250; [VHGNDrockdensity] = 15;
	A = [VHGNDlat]; A - 60; ? A >= 0 -> VHGND latitude absolute; A +-;
    "VHGND latitude absolute"
	A '* 3; A '/ 2; [GRlat] = A;
	[VHGNDsctype] = 0; A = [GRiptype]; ? A != 3 -> VHGND scenario ready;
	( NIV+ planetary_main: most sites derive their biome from orbital
	  orientation; a five-percent branch is locally random.  Low sampled
	  albedo forces ocean and high latitude forces ice. )
	A = [VHGNDlocseed]; A & 0FFFFh; => SU srand;
	C = 100; => SU rnd; A = C; ? A '<= 5 -> VHGND scenario local;
	A = [VHGNDplanet]; A < 1; E = nspororient; E + A;
	[FB0] = [E plus 0]; [FB1] = [E plus 1]; A = 3; => GeoSetK;
	=> GeoKMulChopLive; A = [FI]; A % 4; A + 1; [VHGNDsctype] = A;
	-> VHGND scenario albedo;
    "VHGND scenario local"
	C = 4; => SU rnd; C + 1; [VHGNDsctype] = C;
    "VHGND scenario albedo"
	A = [GRalbedo]; ? A '>= 25 -> VHGND scenario polar; [VHGNDsctype] = 1;
    "VHGND scenario polar"
	A = [GRlat]; ? A '<= 75 -> VHGND scenario near polar; [VHGNDsctype] = 4;
    "VHGND scenario near polar"
	A = [GRlat]; ? A '<= 60 -> VHGND scenario rain; C = 3; => SU rnd;
	? C = 0 -> VHGND scenario rain; [VHGNDsctype] = 4;
    "VHGND scenario rain"
	( Dry and icy biomes reduce the sampled cloud load using the same BRTL
	  stream that selected the scenario. )
	A = [VHGNDsctype]; ? A = 3 -> VHGND scenario desert rain;
	? A = 4 -> VHGND scenario icy rain; -> VHGND scenario ready;
    "VHGND scenario desert rain"
	C = 4; => SU rnd; C + 1; -> VHGND scenario scale rain;
    "VHGND scenario icy rain"
	C = 3; => SU rnd; C + 2;
    "VHGND scenario scale rain"
	[FS0] = [VHGNDcloudrain]; => FLoadF32; [VHGNDtmp] = C; [FI] = C; => IntToF;
	[FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1]; [FS0] = [VHGNDcloudrain]; => FLoadF32;
	=> FQuo; => F32Narrow; => FStoreF32; [VHGNDcloudrain] = [FS0];
    "VHGND scenario ready"
	[GRsctype] = [VHGNDsctype];
	=> VHGND build surface;
	-> VHGND generate world setup;

"VHGND build surface"
	( Clean build_surface boundary shared by production and the generation
	  regression driver.  Callers provide GRgseed/GRiptype/GRsctype/GRalbedo,
	  GRlat, VHGNDlocseed and VHGNDtexbase, with RPSM/ROBJ/texture initialized. )
	=> GR prologue;
	( build_surface resets both random streams from landing_pt_lat*landing_pt_lon
	  immediately before the type-specific terrain switch. )
	A = [VHGNDlocseed]; => SU fast srand;
	A = [VHGNDlocseed]; A & 0FFFFh; => SU srand;
	[VHGNDtscale] = 32; [VHGNDquartz] = 0;
	A = [GRiptype]; ? A = 8 -> VHGND generate type8;
	? A = 1 -> VHGND generate type1;
	? A = 2 -> VHGND generate type2;
	? A = 3 -> VHGND generate type3;
	? A = 4 -> VHGND generate type4;
	? A = 5 -> VHGND generate type5;
	? A = 7 -> VHGND generate type7;

	( The source switch has no default arm.  Non-landable types retain the
	  prologue state and proceed directly to the common post-switch work. )
	-> VHGND build surface done;

    "VHGND generate type1"
	( Airless lunar rock: rounded ground, raised rim craters, mottled
	  texture and either many sharp stones or a completely bare field. )
	C = 5; => SU rnd; [VHGNDtmp] = C; A = C;
	? A '<= 2 -> VHGND type1 smooth; ? A = 3 -> VHGND type1 rough;
	[GRrough] = 10; [GRround] = 2; C = 5; => SU rnd; A = 0; A - C; [GRlevel] = A;
	=> GR rockyground; -> VHGND type1 craters;
    "VHGND type1 smooth"
	[GRrough] = 25; C = 4; => SU rnd; C + 4; [GRround] = C; [GRlevel] = 0;
	=> GR rockyground; -> VHGND type1 craters;
    "VHGND type1 rough"
	C = 5; => SU rnd; C + 5; [GRrough] = C; [GRround] = 1; [GRlevel] = 1;
	=> GR rockyground;
    "VHGND type1 craters"
	C = 48; => SU rnd; A = C; A + 32; A - [GRalbedo];
	? A >= 0 -> VHGND type1 crater nonnegative; A = 0;
    "VHGND type1 crater nonnegative"
	? A '<= 30 -> VHGND type1 crater count ready; A = 30;
    "VHGND type1 crater count ready"
	[VHGNDgenn] = A;
    "VHGND type1 crater loop"
	A = [VHGNDgenn]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND type1 texture craters;
	C = 32; => SU rnd; [FI] = C; => IntToF;
	[FB0] = 7AE147AEh; [FB1] = 3F847AE1h; => FMul; => F32Narrow; => FStoreF32; [GRfht] = [FS0];
	C = 20; => SU rnd; C + 5; [FI] = C; => IntToF;
	[FB0] = 33333333h; [FB1] = 3FB33333h; => FMul; => F32Narrow; => FStoreF32; [GRfhmt] = [FS0];
	C = 50; => SU rnd; C + 5; [SUia] = C;
	C = 200; => SU rnd; [SUcyi] = C; C = 200; => SU rnd; [SUcxi] = C;
	[GRfscl] = 127; [GRfsca] = 200; [GRscmap] = RPSM; => GR std crater;
	[VHGNDgenn]-; -> VHGND type1 crater loop;
    "VHGND type1 texture craters"
	C = 48; => SU rnd; A = C; A + 64; A - [GRalbedo];
	? A >= 0 -> VHGND type1 texture nonnegative; A = 0;
    "VHGND type1 texture nonnegative"
	[VHGNDgenn] = A;
    "VHGND type1 texture crater loop"
	A = [VHGNDgenn]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND type1 texture lines;
	C = 200; => SU rnd; [SUcxi] = C; C = 200; => SU rnd; [SUcyi] = C;
	C = 32; => SU rnd; C + 10; [VHGNDgeni] = C; [SUia] = C;
	[GRfht] = 3EB33333h; [GRfhmt] = 3F800000h;
	[GRfscl] = 31; [GRfsca] = 256; [GRscmap] = [VHGNDtexbase]; => GR std crater;
	A = [VHGNDgeni]; A & 1; ? A = 0 -> VHGND type1 texture crater next;
	A = [VHGNDgeni]; A / 3; C = [SUcxi]; C + A; [SUcxi] = C;
	C = [SUcyi]; C + A; [SUcyi] = C; A = 0; A - [VHGNDgeni]; [SUia] = A;
	=> GR std crater;
    "VHGND type1 texture crater next"
	[VHGNDgenn]-; -> VHGND type1 texture crater loop;
    "VHGND type1 texture lines"
	C = 100; => SU rnd;
	[VHGNDgenn] = C;
    "VHGND type1 line loop"
	A = [VHGNDgenn]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND type1 rocks;
	C = 1000; => SU rnd; [VHGNDlinelen] = C;
	[VHGNDlinetrendx] = 0FFFFFFFFh; [VHGNDlinetrendz] = 0FFFFFFFFh; => VHGND texture darkline;
	[VHGNDgenn]-; -> VHGND type1 line loop;
    "VHGND type1 rocks"
	C = 2; => SU rnd; [VHGNDtmp] = C; C = 2; => SU rnd; C '* 16; C + 15;
	A = C; A '* [VHGNDtmp]; [VHGNDrockdensity] = A;
	C = 500; => SU rnd; C + 150; [VHGNDrockscale] = C;
	C = 300; => SU rnd; C + 100; [VHGNDrockpeak] = C;
	-> VHGND build surface done;

    "VHGND generate type8"
	( NOCTIS-1.CPP:2532-2580.  Albedo 32 skips the dark-zone dome arm.
	  Borland evaluates the four random call arguments right-to-left. )
	A = 100; A - [GRalbedo]; A '* 2; [VHGNDgenn] = A;
    "VHGND type8 hill loop"
	C = 25; => SU rnd; C + 1; [VHGNDhr] = C;       ( h )
	C = 25; => SU rnd; C + 1; [VHGNDgeni] = C;     ( radius )
	C = 200; => SU rnd; [SUcyi] = C;
	C = 200; => SU rnd; [SUcxi] = C;
	[FI] = [VHGNDhr]; => IntToF; => F32Narrow; => FStoreF32;
	[GRfht] = [FS0]; [GRfhmt] = 0; [GRfcanyon] = 1;
	[SUia] = [VHGNDgeni]; => GR round hill;
	[VHGNDgenn]-; A = [VHGNDgenn]; ? A != 0 -> VHGND type8 hill loop;
	[VHGNDquartz] = 1;
	( rockscaling, rockpeaking, rockdensity draws )
	C = 300; => SU rnd; C + 50; [VHGNDrockscale] = C;
	C = 300; => SU rnd; C + 50; [VHGNDrockpeak] = C;
	C = 2; => SU rnd; C '* 8; C + 7; [VHGNDrockdensity] = C;
	( snowy/frosty branch before goto similar )
	C = 2; => SU rnd; [VHGNDfrosty] = 0; ? C != 0 -> VHGND type8 texture;
	[VHGNDfrosty] = 1;
    "VHGND type8 texture"
	=> VHGND similar texture;
	( `goto similar` lands inside the type-3 scenario switch.  Its `break`
	  then reaches the shared `if (!random(5)) quartz=1` before the outer
	  planet-type switch exits.  Quartz is already set here; the draw still
	  controls every subsequent post-surface crevasse. )
	C = 5; => SU rnd;
	-> VHGND build surface done;

    "VHGND generate type2"
	( NOCTIS-1.CPP:2100-2140, the thick-atmosphere/Venusian arm used
	  by both moons of the opening gas giant. )
	[GRrough] = 10; [GRround] = 1; [GRlevel] = 0; => GR rockyground;
	C = 100; => SU rnd; C + [GRalbedo]; [VHGNDgenn] = C;
    "VHGND type2 hill loop"
	A = [VHGNDgenn]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND type2 hills done;
	( round_hill(random(200),random(200),random(100)+50,
	  random(50)+10,0,1): Borland evaluates arguments right-to-left. )
	C = 50; => SU rnd; C + 10; [VHGNDhr] = C;
	C = 100; => SU rnd; C + 50; [VHGNDgeni] = C;
	C = 200; => SU rnd; [SUcyi] = C; C = 200; => SU rnd; [SUcxi] = C;
	[FI] = [VHGNDhr]; => IntToF; => F32Narrow; => FStoreF32;
	[GRfht] = [FS0]; [GRfhmt] = 0; [GRfcanyon] = 1;
	[SUia] = [VHGNDgeni]; => GR round hill;
	[VHGNDgenn]-; -> VHGND type2 hill loop;
    "VHGND type2 hills done"
	C = 2; => SU rnd; ? C != 0 -> VHGND type2 lines;
	( Nebulous texture: mixed positive/negative shallow craters. )
	C = 200; => SU rnd; [VHGNDtmp] = C;
	C = 100; => SU rnd; A = [GRalbedo]; A + [VHGNDtmp]; A - C; [VHGNDgenn] = A;
	C = 10; => SU rnd; [FI] = C; => IntToF;
	[FB0] = 47AE147Bh; [FB1] = 3F947AE1h; => FMul;
	=> F32Narrow; => FStoreF32; [GRfht] = [FS0];
	A = [VHGNDgenn]; ? A < 0 -> VHGND type2 crater none;
    "VHGND type2 crater loop"
	A = [VHGNDgenn]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND type2 crater none;
	C = 256; => SU rnd; [SUcxi] = C; C = 256; => SU rnd; [SUcyi] = C;
	C = 8; => SU rnd; C + 8; [VHGNDgeni] = C;
	C = 2; => SU rnd; A = [VHGNDgeni]; ? C = 0 -> VHGND type2 crater sign;
	C = 0; C - A; A = C;
    "VHGND type2 crater sign"
	[SUia] = A; [GRfhmt] = 3F800000h; [GRfscl] = 31; [GRfsca] = 256; [GRscmap] = [VHGNDtexbase];
	=> GR std crater; [VHGNDgenn]-; -> VHGND type2 crater loop;
    "VHGND type2 crater none"
	-> VHGND type2 rocks;
    "VHGND type2 lines"
	C = 500; => SU rnd; C + [GRalbedo]; [VHGNDgenn] = C;
	C = 2000; => SU rnd; [VHGNDtmp] = C;
    "VHGND type2 line loop"
	A = [VHGNDgenn]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND type2 rocks;
	C = [VHGNDtmp]; => SU rnd; [VHGNDlinelen] = C;
	[VHGNDlinetrendx] = 0FFFFFFFFh; [VHGNDlinetrendz] = 0FFFFFFFFh;
	=> VHGND texture darkline; [VHGNDgenn]-; -> VHGND type2 line loop;
    "VHGND type2 rocks"
	C = 500; => SU rnd; C + 500; [VHGNDrockscale] = C;
	C = 2; => SU rnd; C '* 8; C + 7; [VHGNDrockdensity] = C;
	C = 150; => SU rnd; C + 50; [VHGNDrockpeak] = C;
	-> VHGND build surface done;

    "VHGND generate type3"
	( NOCTIS-1.CPP:2151-2294.  GR prologue and the switch preamble have
	  already seeded both streams from landing_pt_lat*landing_pt_lon. )
	A = [VHGNDsctype]; ? A = 1 -> VHGND generate ocean;
	? A = 3 -> VHGND generate desert; ? A = 4 -> VHGND generate icy;
	-> VHGND type3 plains;

    "VHGND type3 plains"
	( `revert:`: source PLAINS chooses either 5..54 low hills or 10..34
	  mountains.  Borland evaluates each round_hill call right-to-left. )
	C = 2; => SU rnd; ? C = 0 -> VHGND type3 mountains;
	C = 50; => SU rnd; C + 5; [VHGNDgenn] = C;
    "VHGND type3 low hill loop"
	C = 30; => SU rnd; C + 1; [VHGNDhr] = C;
	C = 200; => SU rnd; C + 1; [VHGNDgeni] = C;
	C = 200; => SU rnd; [SUcyi] = C; C = 200; => SU rnd; [SUcxi] = C;
	[FI] = [VHGNDhr]; => IntToF; => F32Narrow; => FStoreF32;
	[GRfht] = [FS0]; [GRfhmt] = 0; [GRfcanyon] = 1;
	[SUia] = [VHGNDgeni]; => GR round hill;
	[VHGNDgenn]-; A = [VHGNDgenn]; ? A != 0 -> VHGND type3 low hill loop;
	-> VHGND type3 addtrees;
    "VHGND type3 mountains"
	C = 25; => SU rnd; C + 10; [VHGNDgenn] = C;
    "VHGND type3 mountain loop"
	C = 100; => SU rnd; C + 1; [VHGNDhr] = C;
	C = 200; => SU rnd; C + 1; [VHGNDgeni] = C;
	C = 200; => SU rnd; [SUcyi] = C; C = 200; => SU rnd; [SUcxi] = C;
	[FI] = [VHGNDhr]; => IntToF; => F32Narrow; => FStoreF32;
	[GRfht] = [FS0]; [GRfhmt] = 0; [GRfcanyon] = 1;
	[SUia] = [VHGNDgeni]; => GR round hill;
	[VHGNDgenn]-; A = [VHGNDgenn]; ? A != 0 -> VHGND type3 mountain loop;
	-> VHGND type3 addtrees;
    "VHGND generate ocean"
	( A bright OCEAN site is the source's shoreline case: retain water,
	  suppress incoming swell, and generate the same PLAINS hill field. )
	[VHGNDwaswet] = 1;
	A = [GRalbedo]; ? A '<= 20 -> VHGND type3 open ocean;
	[VHGNDwavescalm] = 1; -> VHGND type3 plains;
    "VHGND type3 open ocean"
	[VHGNDwaswet] = 0;
	( Scattered rocks at albedo 17..20 make a calm sea. )
	A = [GRalbedo]; ? A '<= 16 -> VHGND type3 island test;
	C = 2; => SU rnd; ? C = 0 -> VHGND type3 island test;
	C = 2; => SU rnd; [GRround] = C; [GRrough] = 10; [GRlevel] = 0FFFFFFFBh;
	=> GR rockyground; [VHGNDwavescalm] = 1;
    "VHGND type3 island test"
	C = 3; => SU rnd; ? C != 0 -> VHGND type3 full ocean;
	C = 100; => SU rnd; C + 50; [VHGNDislandx] = C;
	C = 100; => SU rnd; C + 50; [VHGNDislandz] = C;
	( round_hill(cx+random(15),cz+random(15),random(100)+25,
	  random(10)+1,0,1), evaluated right-to-left. )
	C = 10; => SU rnd; C + 1; [VHGNDhr] = C;
	C = 100; => SU rnd; C + 25; [VHGNDgeni] = C;
	C = 15; => SU rnd; C + [VHGNDislandz]; [SUcyi] = C;
	C = 15; => SU rnd; C + [VHGNDislandx]; [SUcxi] = C;
	[FI] = [VHGNDhr]; => IntToF; => F32Narrow; => FStoreF32;
	[GRfht] = [FS0]; [GRfhmt] = 0; [GRfcanyon] = 1;
	[SUia] = [VHGNDgeni]; => GR round hill;
	( round_hill(cx,cz,random(100)+25,random(100)+1,0,1). )
	C = 100; => SU rnd; C + 1; [VHGNDhr] = C;
	C = 100; => SU rnd; C + 25; [VHGNDgeni] = C;
	[SUcxi] = [VHGNDislandx]; [SUcyi] = [VHGNDislandz];
	[FI] = [VHGNDhr]; => IntToF; => F32Narrow; => FStoreF32;
	[GRfht] = [FS0]; [GRfhmt] = 0; [GRfcanyon] = 1;
	[SUia] = [VHGNDgeni]; => GR round hill;
	[VHGNDwaswet] = 1; -> VHGND type3 addtrees;
    "VHGND type3 full ocean"
	[VHGNDtscale] = 128; C = 30; => SU rnd; C + 2; [VHGNDtexn] = C;
	=> VHGND texture random;
	C = 75; => SU rnd; C + 50; [VHGNDrockscale] = C;
	C = 25; => SU rnd; C + 30; [VHGNDrockpeak] = C;
	C = 3; => SU rnd; [VHGNDrockdensity] = 0; ? C = 0 -> VHGND type3 done;
	[VHGNDrockdensity] = 31; -> VHGND type3 done;

    "VHGND type3 addtrees"
	( `addtrees:`: preserve the otherwise-unused random(6), assign the
	  three packed vegetation classes, then paint source grass. )
	C = 6; => SU rnd;
	=> VHGND type3 object classes;
	[VHGNDtscale] = 128; C = 15; => SU rnd; C + 2; [VHGNDtexn] = C;
	=> VHGND texture random;
	C = 500; => SU rnd; C + 100; [VHGNDgenn] = C;
    "VHGND type3 asterism loop"
	C = 15; => SU rnd; C + 6; [VHGNDastarsize] = C;
	C = 25; => SU rnd; C + 6; [VHGNDastardensity] = C;
	C = 16; => SU rnd; [VHGNDastarvariation] = C;
	C = 16; => SU rnd; [VHGNDastarbase] = C;
	C = 256; => SU rnd; [VHGNDastary] = C;
	C = 256; => SU rnd; [VHGNDastarx] = C;
	=> VHGND asterism;
	[VHGNDgenn]-; A = [VHGNDgenn]; ? A != 0 -> VHGND type3 asterism loop;
	( Non-wet plains always receive fast noise; a shoreline does one time
	  in five.  This is an ADD, matching the observed original binary. )
	A = [VHGNDwaswet]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND type3 add noise;
	C = 5; => SU rnd; ? C != 0 -> VHGND type3 rocks;
    "VHGND type3 add noise"
	=> GR plains noise;
    "VHGND type3 rocks"
	C = 200; => SU rnd; C + 100; [VHGNDrockscale] = C;
	C = 200; => SU rnd; C + 100; [VHGNDrockpeak] = C;
	C = 2; => SU rnd; C '* 4; C + 3; [VHGNDrockdensity] = C;
	-> VHGND type3 done;

    "VHGND generate desert"
	( DESERT: source dune rule, with greater random relief receiving more
	  smoothing; coarse 128x sand and no scattered rocks. )
	C = 100; => SU rnd; [VHGNDtmp] = C;
	A = C; A + 50; [GRrough] = A;
	A = [VHGNDtmp]; A > 4; A + 5; [GRround] = A; [GRlevel] = 0;
	=> GR rockyground;
	[VHGNDtscale] = 128; [VHGNDtexn] = 32; => VHGND texture random;
	[VHGNDrockscale] = 0; [VHGNDrockpeak] = 0; [VHGNDrockdensity] = 0;
	-> VHGND type3 done;

    "VHGND generate icy"
	( ICY: the four original orography classes: snowfield, bare ice,
	  rounded snow hills, or broken iceberg relief. )
	[VHGNDfrosty] = 0; C = 4; => SU rnd; A = C;
	? A = 0 -> VHGND icy snowfield; ? A = 1 -> VHGND icy bare;
	? A = 2 -> VHGND icy hills; -> VHGND icy bergs;
    "VHGND icy snowfield"
	[GRrough] = 15; [GRround] = 5; [GRlevel] = 0; => GR rockyground;
	-> VHGND icy terrain done;
    "VHGND icy bare"
	C = 2; => SU rnd; C + 1; [GRround] = C;
	C = 10; => SU rnd; C + 10; [GRrough] = C; [GRlevel] = 0;
	[VHGNDfrosty] = 1; => GR rockyground; -> VHGND icy terrain done;
    "VHGND icy hills"
	C = 50; => SU rnd; C + 50; [VHGNDgenn] = C;
    "VHGND icy hill loop"
	C = 75; => SU rnd; C + 1; [VHGNDhr] = C;
	C = 200; => SU rnd; C + 1; [VHGNDgeni] = C;
	C = 200; => SU rnd; [SUcyi] = C; C = 200; => SU rnd; [SUcxi] = C;
	[FI] = [VHGNDhr]; => IntToF; => F32Narrow; => FStoreF32;
	[GRfht] = [FS0]; [GRfhmt] = 0; [GRfcanyon] = 1; [SUia] = [VHGNDgeni];
	=> GR round hill; [VHGNDgenn]-; A = [VHGNDgenn]; ? A != 0 -> VHGND icy hill loop;
	-> VHGND icy terrain done;
    "VHGND icy bergs"
	C = 40; => SU rnd; C + 20; A = 0; A - C; [GRlevel] = A;
	C = 3; => SU rnd; C + 3; [GRround] = C;
	C = 50; => SU rnd; C + 50; [GRrough] = C;
	[VHGNDfrosty] = 1; => GR rockyground;
    "VHGND icy terrain done"
	C = 500; => SU rnd; C + 200; [VHGNDrockscale] = C;
	C = 250; => SU rnd; C + 150; [VHGNDrockpeak] = C;
	C = 2; => SU rnd; C '* 2; [VHGNDrockdensity] = C;
	=> VHGND similar texture;
	-> VHGND type3 done;

    "VHGND type3 done"
	( NIV+ keeps quartz as surface state and consumes this final PLAINS
	  random(5) for every type-3 biome. )
	C = 5; => SU rnd; ? C != 0 -> VHGND build surface done;
	[VHGNDquartz] = 1; -> VHGND build surface done;

    "VHGND generate type4"
	( NOCTIS-1.CPP:2389-2443.  Preserve the right-to-left random order
	  of rockyground's arguments and the binary32 hr/ht stores. )
	C = 5; => SU rnd; A = 0; A - C; [GRlevel] = A;
	C = 3; => SU rnd; C + 3; [GRround] = C; [GRrough] = 15;
	=> GR rockyground;
	C = 15; => SU rnd; [VHGNDgenn] = C;
    "VHGND type4 hill loop"
	A = [VHGNDgenn]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND type4 hills done;
	C = 15; => SU rnd; C + 7; [VHGNDgeni] = C;     ( hf / radius )
	=> VHGND flandom;
	[FB0] = 0; [FB1] = 400C0000h; => FMul;
	[FB0] = 0; [FB1] = 400C0000h; => FAdd;
	[FT0] = [FA0]; [FT1] = [FA1];
	[FI] = [VHGNDgeni]; => IntToF;
	[FB0] = [FT0]; [FB1] = [FT1]; => FMul;
	=> F32Narrow; => FStoreF32; [GRfht] = [FS0];
	=> VHGND flandom;
	[FB0] = 9999999Ah; [FB1] = 3FC99999h; => FMul;
	[FB0] = 33333333h; [FB1] = 3FD33333h; => FAdd;
	[FT0] = [FA0]; [FT1] = [FA1];
	[FS0] = [GRfht]; => FLoadF32;
	[FB0] = [FT0]; [FB1] = [FT1]; => FMul;
	=> F32Narrow; => FStoreF32; [GRfhmt] = [FS0];
	C = 200; => SU rnd; [SUcyi] = C;
	C = 200; => SU rnd; [SUcxi] = C;
	[SUia] = [VHGNDgeni]; [GRfcanyon] = 0; => GR round hill;
	[VHGNDgenn]-; -> VHGND type4 hill loop;
    "VHGND type4 hills done"
	C = 2; => SU rnd; C + 1; => GR smoothterrain;
	A = 64; A - [GRalbedo]; [VHGNDgenn] = A;
    "VHGND type4 crater loop"
	C = 150; => SU rnd; C + 25; [SUcxi] = C;
	C = 150; => SU rnd; C + 25; [SUcyi] = C;
	C = 10; => SU rnd; C + 15; A = 0; A - C; [SUia] = A;
	[GRfht] = 3E800000h; [GRfhmt] = 3F800000h;
	[GRfscl] = 31; [GRfsca] = 256; [GRscmap] = [VHGNDtexbase];
	=> GR std crater;
	[VHGNDgenn]-; A = [VHGNDgenn]; ? A != 0 -> VHGND type4 crater loop;
	( remaining rock-parameter draws )
	C = 200; => SU rnd; C + 100; [VHGNDrockscale] = C;
	C = 2; => SU rnd; C '* 4; C + 3; [VHGNDrockdensity] = C;
	C = 200; => SU rnd; C + 100; [VHGNDrockpeak] = C;
	-> VHGND build surface done;

    "VHGND generate type5"
	( Thin-atmosphere worlds: Martian plains or broken permafrost, eroded
	  profile craters, dense texture fissures, and sparse-or-dense stones. )
	C = 2; => SU rnd; ? C = 0 -> VHGND type5 broken;
	C = 10; => SU rnd; C + 5; [GRrough] = C; [GRlevel] = 0;
	-> VHGND type5 rough ready;
    "VHGND type5 broken"
	( Source computes n before evaluating rockyground's -random(24)
	  argument, so the roughness draw precedes the level draw. )
	C = 32; => SU rnd; C + 15; [GRrough] = C;
	C = 24; => SU rnd; A = 0; A - C; [GRlevel] = A;
    "VHGND type5 rough ready"
	A = [GRalbedo]; ? A '<= 48 -> VHGND type5 rough adjusted;
	A = [GRrough]; A / 2; [GRrough] = A;
    "VHGND type5 rough adjusted"
	[GRround] = 1; => GR rockyground;
    "VHGND type5 craters"
	C = 68; => SU rnd; A = C; A - [GRalbedo]; ? A >= 1 -> VHGND type5 crater low; A = 1;
    "VHGND type5 crater low"
	? A '<= 10 -> VHGND type5 crater count ready; A = 10;
    "VHGND type5 crater count ready"
	[VHGNDgenn] = A;
    "VHGND type5 crater loop"
	C = 5; => SU rnd; [FI] = C; => IntToF;
	[FB0] = 0EB851EB8h; [FB1] = 3F8EB851h; => FMul; => F32Narrow; => FStoreF32; [GRfht] = [FS0];
	C = 10; => SU rnd; C + 10; [FI] = C; => IntToF;
	[FB0] = 147AE148h; [FB1] = 3FD147AEh; => FMul; => F32Narrow; => FStoreF32; [GRfhmt] = [FS0];
	C = 35; => SU rnd; C + 5; [SUia] = C;
	C = 200; => SU rnd; [SUcyi] = C; C = 200; => SU rnd; [SUcxi] = C;
	[GRfscl] = 127; [GRfsca] = 200; [GRscmap] = RPSM; => GR std crater;
	[VHGNDgenn]-; A = [VHGNDgenn]; ? A != 0 -> VHGND type5 crater loop;
	C = 400; => SU rnd; C + 50; [VHGNDrockscale] = C;
	C = 250; => SU rnd; C + 50; [VHGNDrockpeak] = C;
	C = 2; => SU rnd; C '* 30; C + 1; [VHGNDrockdensity] = C;
	C = 1500; => SU rnd; C + 500; [VHGNDtmp] = C;
	A = [GRalbedo]; A '* 5; [VHGNDgenn] = A;
    "VHGND type5 line loop"
	A = [VHGNDgenn]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND build surface done;
	C = [VHGNDtmp]; => SU rnd; [VHGNDlinelen] = C;
	[VHGNDlinetrendx] = 0FFFFFFFFh; [VHGNDlinetrendz] = 0FFFFFFFFh; => VHGND texture darkline;
	[VHGNDgenn]-; -> VHGND type5 line loop;

    "VHGND generate type7"
	( Frozen striated world: high base shelf, long surface crevasses,
	  dark texture pits and short wandering cracks. )
	C = 100; => SU rnd; C + 20; [GRlevel] = C;
	A = [GRalbedo]; A / 8; C = 10; C - A; [GRrough] = C; [GRround] = 0;
	=> GR rockyground;
	C = [GRalbedo]; => SU rnd; A = [GRalbedo]; A - C; A + 10; [VHGNDgenn] = A;
    "VHGND type7 surface line loop"
	A = [VHGNDgenn]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND type7 texture craters;
	C = 500; => SU rnd; [GRn] = C; [SUia] = 200;
	[GRfrlo] = 0FFFFFFFFh; [GRfrhi] = 0FFFFFFFFh; => GR srf darkline;
	[VHGNDgenn]-; -> VHGND type7 surface line loop;
    "VHGND type7 texture craters"
	( These random calls are separate expression operands in the source, not
	  function arguments: Borland emits random(200), then random(100). )
	C = 200; => SU rnd; [VHGNDtmp] = C; C = 100; => SU rnd;
	A = [GRalbedo]; A + [VHGNDtmp]; A - C; ? A >= 0 -> VHGND type7 texture nonnegative; A = 0;
    "VHGND type7 texture nonnegative"
	[VHGNDgenn] = A;
    "VHGND type7 texture crater loop"
	A = [VHGNDgenn]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND type7 small lines;
	C = 192; => SU rnd; C + 32; [SUcxi] = C; C = 192; => SU rnd; C + 32; [SUcyi] = C;
	C = 16; => SU rnd; C + 16; A = 0; A - C; [SUia] = A;
	[GRfht] = 3E19999Ah; [GRfhmt] = 3F800000h;
	[GRfscl] = 31; [GRfsca] = 256; [GRscmap] = [VHGNDtexbase]; => GR std crater;
	[VHGNDgenn]-; -> VHGND type7 texture crater loop;
    "VHGND type7 small lines"
	C = 100; => SU rnd; [VHGNDtmp] = C; C = 50; => SU rnd;
	A = [GRalbedo]; A + [VHGNDtmp]; A - C; ? A >= 0 -> VHGND type7 small nonnegative; A = 0;
    "VHGND type7 small nonnegative"
	A / 2;
	[VHGNDgenn] = A;
    "VHGND type7 small line loop"
	A = [VHGNDgenn]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND type7 rocks;
	( Function arguments are evaluated right-to-left: z trend, x trend,
	  then the random line length. )
	C = 2; => SU rnd; A = 0; A - C; [VHGNDlinetrendz] = A;
	C = 2; => SU rnd; A = 0; A - C; [VHGNDlinetrendx] = A;
	C = 100; => SU rnd; [VHGNDlinelen] = C; => VHGND texture darkline;
	[VHGNDgenn]-; -> VHGND type7 small line loop;
    "VHGND type7 rocks"
	C = 400; => SU rnd; C + 50; [VHGNDrockscale] = C;
	C = 200; => SU rnd; C + 50; [VHGNDrockpeak] = C;
	C = 2; => SU rnd; C '* 4; C + 3; [VHGNDrockdensity] = C;
    "VHGND build surface done"
	=> VHGND post surface;
	( NOCTIS-1.CPP:2620-2625 liquid_water is part of build_surface,
	  before the clean return boundary. )
	A = [GRiptype]; ? A != 3 -> VHGND build surface water done;
	A = [VHGNDsctype]; ? A != 1 -> VHGND build surface water done;
	=> VHGND water clear objects;
    "VHGND build surface water done"
	end;

    "VHGND generate world setup"
	=> VHGND historical ruins;
	=> VHGND cache objects;
	( Choose deterministic local high points for the two steep type-2 maps.
	  Their complete 11x11 walking neighborhoods sit at or below the centre,
	  so the eye near plane cannot begin inside a neighboring hill. )
	[VHGNDdropx] = 1646592; [VHGNDdropz] = 1646592;
	A = [VHGNDplanet]; ? A = 3 -> VHGND drop moon3;
	? A = 4 -> VHGND drop moon4; -> VHGND drop site ready;
    "VHGND drop moon3"
	[VHGNDdropx] = 909312; [VHGNDdropz] = 1597440; -> VHGND drop site ready;
    "VHGND drop moon4"
	[VHGNDdropx] = 1155072; [VHGNDdropz] = 188416;
    "VHGND drop site ready"
	( Cache the selected site's exact HPOINT for descent and the persistent
	  capsule mesh. )
	[VHGNDcamx] = [VHGNDdropx]; [VHGNDcamz] = [VHGNDdropz];
	=> VHGND eye height; A = [VHGNDheight]; A + 600; [VHGNDdropy] = A;
	=> VHGND animals setup;
	=> VHGND birds setup;
	=> VHGND waves init;

	end;

"VHGND water clear objects"
	( liquid_water: zero the packed object count wherever height is sea level. )
	[VHGNDptr] = 0;
    "VHGND water object loop"
	A = RPSM; A + [VHGNDptr]; [MBptr] = A; => MEM get byte;
	A = [MBval]; ? A != 0 -> VHGND water object next;
	A = ROBJ; A + [VHGNDptr]; [MBptr] = A; => MEM get byte;
	A = [MBval]; A & 0FCh; [MBval] = A;
	A = ROBJ; A + [VHGNDptr]; [MBptr] = A; => MEM put byte;
    "VHGND water object next"
	[VHGNDptr]+; A = [VHGNDptr]; ? A < 40000 -> VHGND water object loop;
	end;

"VHGND cache objects"
	( fragment() derives every object's position solely from its final terrain
	  quadrant, packed count and deterministic fast RNG seed.  Calculate those
	  immutable coordinates once after generation while retaining the exact
	  post-coordinate RNG state used by nearby bushes and grass. )
	[VHGNDptr] = 0;
    "VHGND cache object cell"
	A = [VHGNDptr]; A / VHGNDMAP; [VHGNDz] = A;
	A '* VHGNDMAP; C = [VHGNDptr]; C - A; [VHGNDx] = C;
	A = [VHGNDx]; ? A '>= 199 -> VHGND cache object next cell;
	A = [VHGNDz]; ? A '>= 199 -> VHGND cache object next cell;
	A = ROBJ; A + [VHGNDptr]; [MBptr] = A; => MEM get byte;
	A = [MBval]; A & 3; [VHGNDocount] = A;
	? A = 0 -> VHGND cache object next cell;
	A = RPSM; A + [VHGNDptr]; [MBptr] = A; => MEM get byte; [VHGNDs1] = [MBval];
	A = RPSM; A + [VHGNDptr]; A + 1; [MBptr] = A; => MEM get byte; [VHGNDs2] = [MBval];
	A = RPSM; A + [VHGNDptr]; A + 201; [MBptr] = A; => MEM get byte; [VHGNDs3] = [MBval];
	A = RPSM; A + [VHGNDptr]; A + 200; [MBptr] = A; => MEM get byte; [VHGNDs4] = [MBval];
	[VHGNDobjid] = 0;
    "VHGND cache object record"
	A = [VHGNDx]; A '* [VHGNDz]; A '* [VHGNDocount]; => SU fast srand;
	[SUfmask] = 16383; => SU frnd; [VHGNDicx] = C;
	[SUfmask] = 16383; => SU frnd; [VHGNDicz] = C;
	A = [VHGNDx]; A < 14; A + [VHGNDicx]; [VHGNDoox] = A;
	A = [VHGNDz]; A < 14; A + [VHGNDicz]; [VHGNDooz] = A;
	A = [VHGNDicx]; A + [VHGNDicz]; ? A '< VHGNDTS -> VHGND cache object height upper;
	A = [VHGNDs3]; A < 14; C = 0; C - A; [VHGNDtmp] = C;
	A = [VHGNDs4]; A - [VHGNDs3]; C = VHGNDTS; C - [VHGNDicx]; A '* C;
	C = [VHGNDtmp]; C - A; [VHGNDtmp] = C;
	A = [VHGNDs2]; A - [VHGNDs3]; C = VHGNDTS; C - [VHGNDicz]; A '* C;
	C = [VHGNDtmp]; C - A; C / 8; [VHGNDooy] = C; -> VHGND cache object height ready;
    "VHGND cache object height upper"
	A = [VHGNDs1]; A < 14; C = 0; C - A; [VHGNDtmp] = C;
	A = [VHGNDs2]; A - [VHGNDs1]; A '* [VHGNDicx]; C = [VHGNDtmp]; C - A; [VHGNDtmp] = C;
	A = [VHGNDs4]; A - [VHGNDs1]; A '* [VHGNDicz]; C = [VHGNDtmp]; C - A; C / 8; [VHGNDooy] = C;
    "VHGND cache object height ready"
	A = [VHGNDptr]; A '* 3; A + [VHGNDobjid]; [VHGNDobjcachep] = A;
	C = VHGNDobjcachex; C + A; [C] = [VHGNDoox];
	C = VHGNDobjcachey; C + A; [C] = [VHGNDooy];
	C = VHGNDobjcachez; C + A; [C] = [VHGNDooz];
	C = VHGNDobjcacheseed; C + A; [C] = [SUfseed];
	[VHGNDobjid]+; [VHGNDocount]-;
	A = [VHGNDocount]; ? A > 0 -> VHGND cache object record;
    "VHGND cache object next cell"
	[VHGNDptr]+; A = [VHGNDptr]; ? A < 40000 -> VHGND cache object cell;
	end;

"VHGND animals setup"
	( NOCTIS-1.CPP:3143-3262 setup_animals().  Preserve the source LFS=100
	  biome ranges and its repeated random(3)/random(18) class selection.
	  Reptiles remain classified even though NIV+ has no reptile draw branch. )
	[VHGNDpopulation] = 0; [VHGNDanimals] = 0; [VHGNDbirds] = 0;
	[VHGNDcaptures] = 0; [VHGNDgroundbirds] = 0;
	A = [GRiptype]; ? A != 3 -> VHGND animals setup done;
	[VHGNDfaunaseedsave] = [brtlseed]; [VHGNDfaunafastsave] = [SUfseed];
	A = [VHGNDseed]; C = [VHGNDsctype]; A + C; => BrtlSrand;
	A = [VHGNDsctype]; ? A = 1 -> VHGND fauna count ocean;
	? A = 3 -> VHGND fauna count plains; ? A = 2 -> VHGND fauna count desert;
	[VHGNDfaunabase] = 10; [VHGNDfaunarange] = 50; -> VHGND fauna count draw;
    "VHGND fauna count ocean"
	[VHGNDfaunabase] = 20; [VHGNDfaunarange] = 80; -> VHGND fauna count draw;
    "VHGND fauna count plains"
	[VHGNDfaunabase] = 50; [VHGNDfaunarange] = 50; -> VHGND fauna count draw;
    "VHGND fauna count desert"
	[VHGNDfaunabase] = 10; [VHGNDfaunarange] = 20;
    "VHGND fauna count draw"
	A = [VHGNDfaunarange]; => BrtlRandom; A + [VHGNDfaunabase]; [VHGNDpopulation] = A;
	( setup_animals() first consumes one full-range fast draw for ani_seed and
	  one fast_flandom for scale for every LFS member, before classification. )
	A = [VHGNDseed]; => SU fast srand; [VHGNDfaunascalei] = 0;
    "VHGND fauna scale one"
	A = [VHGNDfaunascalei]; ? A '>= [VHGNDpopulation] -> VHGND fauna scale done;
	[SUfmask] = 0FFFFFFFFh; => SU frnd;
	[SUfmask] = 32767; => SU frnd; A = C; A '* 10; A '/ 32767; A + 5;
	C = VHGNDfaunascales; C + [VHGNDfaunascalei]; [C] = A;
	[VHGNDfaunascalei]+; -> VHGND fauna scale one;
    "VHGND fauna scale done"

	( The original reseeds rand(global_surface_seed) before drawing the three
	  class probabilities and classifying the complete population. )
	A = [VHGNDseed]; => BrtlSrand;
	A = [VHGNDsctype]; ? A = 1 -> VHGND fauna probability ocean;
	? A = 3 -> VHGND fauna probability plains; ? A = 2 -> VHGND fauna probability desert;
	A = 2; => BrtlRandom; A + 2; [VHGNDfaunabirdp] = A;
	A = 2; => BrtlRandom; [VHGNDfaunareptp] = A;
	A = 2; => BrtlRandom; [VHGNDfaunamammp] = A; -> VHGND fauna classify begin;
    "VHGND fauna probability ocean"
	A = 9; => BrtlRandom; A + 1; [VHGNDfaunabirdp] = A;
	[VHGNDfaunareptp] = 0; [VHGNDfaunamammp] = 0; -> VHGND fauna classify begin;
    "VHGND fauna probability plains"
	A = 9; => BrtlRandom; A + 5; [VHGNDfaunabirdp] = A;
	A = 3; => BrtlRandom; A + 3; [VHGNDfaunareptp] = A;
	A = 4; => BrtlRandom; A + 5; [VHGNDfaunamammp] = A; -> VHGND fauna classify begin;
    "VHGND fauna probability desert"
	A = 2; => BrtlRandom; A + 1; [VHGNDfaunabirdp] = A;
	A = 9; => BrtlRandom; A + 9; [VHGNDfaunareptp] = A;
	A = 2; => BrtlRandom; A + 1; [VHGNDfaunamammp] = A;
    "VHGND fauna classify begin"
	[VHGNDanii] = 0;
    "VHGND fauna classify one"
	A = [VHGNDanii]; ? A '>= [VHGNDpopulation] -> VHGND fauna records begin;
    "VHGND fauna classify retry"
	A = 3; => BrtlRandom; [VHGNDfaunatype] = A;
	A = 18; => BrtlRandom; [VHGNDfaunachance] = A;
	A = [VHGNDfaunatype]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND fauna classify bird;
	? A = 1 -> VHGND fauna classify reptile;
	A = [VHGNDfaunachance]; ? A '> [VHGNDfaunamammp] -> VHGND fauna classify retry;
	A = 5; -> VHGND fauna classify store;
    "VHGND fauna classify bird"
	A = [VHGNDfaunachance]; ? A '> [VHGNDfaunabirdp] -> VHGND fauna classify retry;
	A = 1; -> VHGND fauna classify store;
    "VHGND fauna classify reptile"
	A = [VHGNDfaunachance]; ? A '> [VHGNDfaunareptp] -> VHGND fauna classify retry;
	A = 4;
    "VHGND fauna classify store"
	C = VHGNDfaunatypes; C + [VHGNDanii]; [C] = A;
	[VHGNDanii]+; -> VHGND fauna classify one;

    "VHGND fauna records begin"
	A = [VHGNDseed]; A + 12345; => SU fast srand;
	A = [VHGNDseed]; A + 12345; => BrtlSrand;
	[VHGNDanii] = 0;
    "VHGND fauna record one"
	A = [VHGNDanii]; ? A '>= [VHGNDpopulation] -> VHGND fauna records done;
	C = VHGNDfaunatypes; C + A; A = [C]; [VHGNDfaunatype] = A;
	=> VHGND fauna record values;
	A = [VHGNDfaunatype];
	? A = 1 -> VHGND fauna record bird; ? A = 5 -> VHGND fauna record mammal;
	-> VHGND fauna record next;
    "VHGND fauna record bird"
	A = [VHGNDbirds]; A '* 12; A + VHGNDbirddata; [VHGNDanip] = A; C = A;
	[C plus 0] = [VHGNDanix]; [C plus 1] = [VHGNDaniz];
	[C plus 2] = [VHGNDaniheading]; [C plus 3] = [VHGNDaniphase];
	[C plus 4] = [VHGNDbirdquote]; [C plus 5] = [VHGNDbirdquote];
	[C plus 6] = 0; [C plus 7] = [VHGNDaniscale];
	[C plus 8] = [VHGNDanispeed]; [C plus 9] = [VHGNDanispeed];
	[C plus 10] = [VHGNDanii]; [C plus 11] = [VHGNDaniheading];
	[VHGNDbirds]+; -> VHGND fauna record next;
    "VHGND fauna record mammal"
	A = [VHGNDanimals]; A '* 10; A + VHGNDanidata; [VHGNDanip] = A; C = A;
	[C plus 0] = [VHGNDanix]; [C plus 1] = [VHGNDaniz];
	[C plus 2] = [VHGNDaniheading]; [C plus 3] = [VHGNDaniphase];
	[C plus 4] = [VHGNDanimtype]; [C plus 5] = [VHGNDanispeed];
	[C plus 6] = [VHGNDaniscale]; [C plus 7] = A; [C plus 8] = [VHGNDanispeed];
	[C plus 9] = [VHGNDanii];
	[VHGNDanimals]+;
    "VHGND fauna record next"
	[VHGNDanii]+; -> VHGND fauna record one;
    "VHGND fauna records done"
	[brtlseed] = [VHGNDfaunaseedsave]; [SUfseed] = [VHGNDfaunafastsave];
    "VHGND animals setup done"
	end;

"VHGND fauna record values"
	( Source runtime order: x, z, optional bird altitude, pitch, speed, then
	  Borland-random mammal morphology.  x/z use the exact identity
	  floor(draw*3276800/32767) = draw*100 + floor(draw*100/32767). )
	[SUfmask] = 32767; => SU frnd; A = C; [VHGNDtmp] = A; A '* 100; [VHGNDanix] = A;
	A = [VHGNDtmp]; A '* 100; A '/ 32767; A + [VHGNDanix]; [VHGNDanix] = A;
	[SUfmask] = 32767; => SU frnd; A = C; [VHGNDtmp] = A; A '* 100; [VHGNDaniz] = A;
	A = [VHGNDtmp]; A '* 100; A '/ 32767; A + [VHGNDaniz]; [VHGNDaniz] = A;
	[VHGNDbirdquote] = 0; A = [VHGNDfaunatype]; ? A != 1 -> VHGND fauna quote done;
	[SUfmask] = 32767; => SU frnd; A = C; A '* 25000; A '/ 32767; [VHGNDbirdquote] = A;
    "VHGND fauna quote done"
	[SUfmask] = 32767; => SU frnd; A = C; A '* 36000; A '/ 32767; [VHGNDaniheading] = A;
	A / 100; [VHGNDaniphase] = A; A / 90; A & 3; [VHGNDanidir] = A;
	[SUfmask] = 32767; => SU frnd; A = C; A '* 100; A '/ 32767; [VHGNDanispeed] = A;
	A = 3; => BrtlRandom; [VHGNDanimtype] = A;
	C = VHGNDfaunascales; C + [VHGNDanii]; [VHGNDaniscale] = [C];
	end;

"VHGND birds setup"
	( Classification and records are built together above so no LFS entry is
	  lost between separate mammal and bird arrays. )
	end;

"VHGND restore captures"
	( Checkpoints store the source-shaped capture count, not transient animal
	  records.  Reapply that progress after deterministic fauna generation so
	  loading cannot resurrect and recapture the same birds. )
	A = [VHGNDcaptures]; ? A '<= [VHGNDbirds] -> VHGND restore count ready;
	[VHGNDcaptures] = [VHGNDbirds];
    "VHGND restore count ready"
	A = [VHGNDcaptures]; ? A '<= 0 -> VHGND restore captures done;
	[VHGNDanii] = 0;
    "VHGND restore capture one"
	A = [VHGNDanii]; A '* 12; A + VHGNDbirddata; [VHGNDanip] = A;
	A = 0; A - 1; C = [VHGNDanip]; [C plus 6] = A;
	[VHGNDanii]+; A = [VHGNDanii]; ? A '< [VHGNDcaptures] -> VHGND restore capture one;
    "VHGND restore captures done"
	end;

"VHGND post surface"
	( NOCTIS-1.CPP:2583-2615.  These shared post-switch crevasses and
	  inclination-weighted object counts apply to every planet type. )
	C = 5; => SU rnd; [VHGNDpostn] = C;
	A = C; ? A = 0 -> VHGND post objects;
    "VHGND post crack loop"
	C = 500; => SU rnd; [VHGNDlinelen] = C;
	=> VHGND felisian line;
	[VHGNDpostn]-; A = [VHGNDpostn]; ? A != 0 -> VHGND post crack loop;

	( One in-place five-neighbour smoothing sweep, exactly ptr 200..38799. )
	[VHGNDptr] = 200;
    "VHGND post smooth loop"
	A = RPSM; A + [VHGNDptr]; [MBptr] = A; => MEM get byte; [VHGNDtmp] = [MBval];
	A = RPSM; A + [VHGNDptr]; A - 1; [MBptr] = A; => MEM get byte;
	A = [VHGNDtmp]; A + [MBval]; [VHGNDtmp] = A;
	A = RPSM; A + [VHGNDptr]; A + 1; [MBptr] = A; => MEM get byte;
	A = [VHGNDtmp]; A + [MBval]; [VHGNDtmp] = A;
	A = RPSM; A + [VHGNDptr]; A - 200; [MBptr] = A; => MEM get byte;
	A = [VHGNDtmp]; A + [MBval]; [VHGNDtmp] = A;
	A = RPSM; A + [VHGNDptr]; A + 200; [MBptr] = A; => MEM get byte;
	A = [VHGNDtmp]; A + [MBval]; A '/ 5; [MBval] = A;
	A = RPSM; A + [VHGNDptr]; [MBptr] = A; => MEM put byte;
	[VHGNDptr]+; A = [VHGNDptr]; ? A < 38800 -> VHGND post smooth loop;

    "VHGND post objects"
	[VHGNDptr] = 0;
    "VHGND post object loop"
	A = RPSM; A + [VHGNDptr]; [MBptr] = A; => MEM get byte; [VHGNDs1] = [MBval];
	A = RPSM; A + [VHGNDptr]; A + 1; [MBptr] = A; => MEM get byte; [VHGNDs2] = [MBval];
	A = [VHGNDs1]; A - [VHGNDs2]; ? A >= 0 -> VHGND post abs1; C = 0; C - A; A = C;
    "VHGND post abs1"
	[VHGNDincl] = A;
	A = RPSM; A + [VHGNDptr]; A + 200; [MBptr] = A; => MEM get byte; [VHGNDs2] = [MBval];
	A = [VHGNDs1]; A - [VHGNDs2]; ? A >= 0 -> VHGND post abs2; C = 0; C - A; A = C;
    "VHGND post abs2"
	A + [VHGNDincl]; [VHGNDincl] = A; [VHGNDoval] = 0;
	A = [VHGNDincl]; ? A '>= 20 -> VHGND post object store;
	C = 2; => SU rnd; [VHGNDoval] = C;
	A = [VHGNDincl]; ? A '>= 15 -> VHGND post object store;
	C = 3; => SU rnd; [VHGNDoval] = C;
	A = [VHGNDincl]; ? A '>= 10 -> VHGND post object store;
	C = 4; => SU rnd; [VHGNDoval] = C;
    "VHGND post object store"
	A = ROBJ; A + [VHGNDptr]; [MBptr] = A; => MEM get byte;
	A = [MBval]; A & 0FCh; A | [VHGNDoval]; [MBval] = A;
	A = ROBJ; A + [VHGNDptr]; [MBptr] = A; => MEM put byte;
	[VHGNDptr]+; A = [VHGNDptr]; ? A < 40000 -> VHGND post object loop;
	end;

"VHGND historical ruins"
	( NIV+ recognises these three named systems by quantised nearstar_identity.
	  Preserve those gates and their body/style tables; ordinary procedural
	  systems remain untouched. )
	[VHGNDruinanchor] = 0; [VHGNDruinmarks] = 0;
	[FI] = 1000000; => IntToF; [FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHTid0]; [FA1] = [VHTid1]; => FMul; => FToIntChop;
	A = [FI]; ? A = 0FFFF6C3Ch -> VHGND ruins Balas;
	[FI] = 100000; => IntToF; [FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHTid0]; [FA1] = [VHTid1]; => FMul; => FToIntChop;
	A = [FI]; ? A = 5F5739F0h -> VHGND ruins Fenia;
	[FI] = 100000000; => IntToF; [FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHTid0]; [FA1] = [VHTid1]; => FMul; => FToIntChop;
	A = [FI]; ? A = 0FF4FDBAEh -> VHGND ruins Ylas;
	end;
    "VHGND ruins Balas"
	[VHGNDruinanchor] = 1;
	A = [VHGNDplanet]; ? A != 3 -> VHGND ruins Balas moons;
	[VHGNDruinstyle0] = 0; [VHGNDruinstyle1] = 1; [VHGNDruinstyle2] = 1;
	[VHGNDruinstyle3] = 2; [VHGNDruinstyle4] = 2; [VHGNDruindensity] = 3;
	=> VHGND make ruins; end;
    "VHGND ruins Balas moons"
	A = [GRiptype]; ? A != 3 -> VHGND historical ruins done;
	[VHGNDruinstyle0] = 3; [VHGNDruinstyle1] = 3; [VHGNDruinstyle2] = 3;
	[VHGNDruinstyle3] = 1; [VHGNDruinstyle4] = 4; [VHGNDruindensity] = 1;
	=> VHGND make ruins; end;
    "VHGND ruins Fenia"
	[VHGNDruinanchor] = 2;
	A = [VHGNDplanet]; ? A = 2 -> VHGND ruins Fenia Peach;
	? A = 3 -> VHGND ruins Fenia Pleasance; ? A = 6 -> VHGND ruins Fenia Wetwick;
	end;
    "VHGND ruins Fenia Peach"
	[VHGNDruinstyle0] = 0; [VHGNDruinstyle1] = 0; [VHGNDruinstyle2] = 0;
	[VHGNDruinstyle3] = 1; [VHGNDruinstyle4] = 1; [VHGNDruindensity] = 2;
	=> VHGND make ruins; end;
    "VHGND ruins Fenia Pleasance"
	[VHGNDruinstyle0] = 2; [VHGNDruinstyle1] = 2; [VHGNDruinstyle2] = 3;
	[VHGNDruinstyle3] = 3; [VHGNDruinstyle4] = 4; [VHGNDruindensity] = 2;
	=> VHGND make ruins; end;
    "VHGND ruins Fenia Wetwick"
	[VHGNDruinstyle0] = 3; [VHGNDruinstyle1] = 4; [VHGNDruinstyle2] = 4;
	[VHGNDruinstyle3] = 4; [VHGNDruinstyle4] = 4; [VHGNDruindensity] = 1;
	=> VHGND make ruins; end;
    "VHGND ruins Ylas"
	[VHGNDruinanchor] = 3;
	A = [VHGNDplanet]; ? A != 3 -> VHGND historical ruins done;
	[VHGNDruinstyle0] = 2; [VHGNDruinstyle1] = 4; [VHGNDruinstyle2] = 5;
	[VHGNDruinstyle3] = 5; [VHGNDruinstyle4] = 5; [VHGNDruindensity] = 2;
	=> VHGND make ruins;
	( NOCTIS-1.CPP:2690-2709 restores the old photographed Suricrasian
	  Cube only at LQ 018:060.  It is a 25x25 plateau at maximum terrain
	  height, with the same two northern rows and four east/west columns
	  marked as ruin faces so the ordinary close-range ruin pass exposes
	  its monumental vertical walls. )
	A = [VHGNDlon]; ? A != 18 -> VHGND historical ruins done;
	A = [VHGNDlat]; ? A != 60 -> VHGND historical ruins done;
	[VHGNDruinx] = 112;
    "VHGND Suricrasian cube x"
	[VHGNDruinz] = 103;
    "VHGND Suricrasian cube z"
	[VHGNDruinvalue] = 127; [VHGNDruinmark] = 0;
	A = [VHGNDruinz]; ? A = 104 -> VHGND Suricrasian cube marked;
	? A = 105 -> VHGND Suricrasian cube marked;
	A = [VHGNDruinx]; ? A = 114 -> VHGND Suricrasian cube marked;
	? A = 115 -> VHGND Suricrasian cube marked;
	? A = 131 -> VHGND Suricrasian cube marked;
	? A = 132 -> VHGND Suricrasian cube marked;
	-> VHGND Suricrasian cube store;
    "VHGND Suricrasian cube marked"
	[VHGNDruinmark] = 1;
    "VHGND Suricrasian cube store"
	=> VHGND ruin set;
	[VHGNDruinz]+; A = [VHGNDruinz]; ? A < 128 -> VHGND Suricrasian cube z;
	[VHGNDruinx]+; A = [VHGNDruinx]; ? A < 137 -> VHGND Suricrasian cube x;
    "VHGND historical ruins done"
	end;

"VHGND make ruins"
	( make_ruins(): scenario-weighted building count after the source resets
	  both random streams from landing_pt_lat*landing_pt_lon. )
	A = [VHGNDlocseed]; => SU fast srand;
	A = [VHGNDlocseed]; A & 0FFFFh; => SU srand;
	[VHGNDruinbuildings] = 0; A = [VHGNDsctype];
	? A = 1 -> VHGND ruins count ocean; ? A = 2 -> VHGND ruins count plains;
	? A = 3 -> VHGND ruins count desert; ? A = 4 -> VHGND ruins count icy;
	end;
    "VHGND ruins count ocean"
	C = 4; => SU rnd; [VHGNDruinbuildings] = C;
	C = 10; => SU rnd; ? C != 0 -> VHGND ruins count ready;
	C = 2; => SU rnd; C + 2; A = [VHGNDruinbuildings]; A '* C; [VHGNDruinbuildings] = A;
	-> VHGND ruins count ready;
    "VHGND ruins count plains"
	C = 8; => SU rnd; [VHGNDruinbuildings] = C;
	C = 5; => SU rnd; ? C != 0 -> VHGND ruins count ready;
	C = 2; => SU rnd; C + 3; A = [VHGNDruinbuildings]; A '* C; [VHGNDruinbuildings] = A;
	-> VHGND ruins count ready;
    "VHGND ruins count desert"
	C = 3; => SU rnd; [VHGNDruinbuildings] = C; -> VHGND ruins count ready;
    "VHGND ruins count icy"
	C = 2; => SU rnd; [VHGNDruinbuildings] = C;
    "VHGND ruins count ready"
	A = [VHGNDruinbuildings]; A '* [VHGNDruindensity]; [VHGNDruinbuildings] = A;
    "VHGND ruins building loop"
	A = [VHGNDruinbuildings]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND make ruins done;
	C = 5; => SU rnd; A = C; ? A = 0 -> VHGND ruins style zero;
	? A = 1 -> VHGND ruins style one; ? A = 2 -> VHGND ruins style two;
	? A = 3 -> VHGND ruins style three; [VHGNDruinstyle] = [VHGNDruinstyle4];
	-> VHGND ruins style ready;
    "VHGND ruins style zero"
	[VHGNDruinstyle] = [VHGNDruinstyle0]; -> VHGND ruins style ready;
    "VHGND ruins style one"
	[VHGNDruinstyle] = [VHGNDruinstyle1]; -> VHGND ruins style ready;
    "VHGND ruins style two"
	[VHGNDruinstyle] = [VHGNDruinstyle2]; -> VHGND ruins style ready;
    "VHGND ruins style three"
	[VHGNDruinstyle] = [VHGNDruinstyle3];
    "VHGND ruins style ready"
	=> VHGND build ruin;
	[VHGNDruinbuildings]-; -> VHGND ruins building loop;
    "VHGND make ruins done"
	end;

"VHGND build ruin"
	A = [VHGNDruinstyle]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND ruin tower;
	? A = 1 -> VHGND ruin walls; ? A = 2 -> VHGND ruin plaza;
	? A = 3 -> VHGND ruin palace; ? A = 4 -> VHGND ruin cross;
	=> VHGND ruin dome; end;
    "VHGND ruin tower"
	C = 25; => SU rnd; C + 10; [VHGNDruinheight] = C;
	C = 160; => SU rnd; C + 20; [VHGNDruincx] = C;
	C = 160; => SU rnd; C + 20; [VHGNDruincz] = C;
	C = 3; => SU rnd; C + 1; [VHGNDruinrad] = C;
	=> VHGND ruin solid; end;
    "VHGND ruin palace"
	C = 10; => SU rnd; C + 10; [VHGNDruinheight] = C;
	C = 160; => SU rnd; C + 20; [VHGNDruincx] = C;
	C = 160; => SU rnd; C + 20; [VHGNDruincz] = C;
	C = 2; => SU rnd; C + 1; [VHGNDruinrad] = C;
	=> VHGND ruin solid; end;

"VHGND ruin solid"
	=> VHGND ruin centre height;
	A = [VHGNDruincx]; A - [VHGNDruinrad]; [VHGNDruinxlo] = A; [VHGNDruinx] = A;
	A = [VHGNDruincx]; A + [VHGNDruinrad]; [VHGNDruinxhi] = A;
	A = [VHGNDruincz]; A - [VHGNDruinrad]; [VHGNDruinzlo] = A; [VHGNDruinz] = A;
	A = [VHGNDruincz]; A + [VHGNDruinrad]; [VHGNDruinzhi] = A;
    "VHGND ruin solid z"
	[VHGNDruinx] = [VHGNDruinxlo];
    "VHGND ruin solid x"
	A = [VHGNDruinbase]; A + [VHGNDruinheight]; [VHGNDruinvalue] = A;
	C = 5; => SU rnd; ? C != 0 -> VHGND ruin solid value; [VHGNDruinvalue]-;
    "VHGND ruin solid value"
	[VHGNDruinmark] = 1; => VHGND ruin set;
	[VHGNDruinx]+; A = [VHGNDruinx]; ? A '< [VHGNDruinxhi] -> VHGND ruin solid x;
	[VHGNDruinz]+; A = [VHGNDruinz]; ? A '< [VHGNDruinzhi] -> VHGND ruin solid z;
	end;

"VHGND ruin walls"
	C = 8; => SU rnd; [VHGNDruinheight] = C;
	C = 160; => SU rnd; C + 20; [VHGNDruincx] = C;
	C = 160; => SU rnd; C + 20; [VHGNDruincz] = C;
	C = 5; => SU rnd; C + 3; [VHGNDruinrad] = C;
	A = [VHGNDruincx]; A - [VHGNDruinrad]; [VHGNDruinxlo] = A; [VHGNDruinx] = A;
	A = [VHGNDruincx]; A + [VHGNDruinrad]; [VHGNDruinxhi] = A;
	A = [VHGNDruincz]; A - [VHGNDruinrad]; [VHGNDruinzlo] = A;
	A = [VHGNDruincz]; A + [VHGNDruinrad]; [VHGNDruinzhi] = A;
    "VHGND ruin wall x"
	[VHGNDruinz] = [VHGNDruinzlo]; C = 2; => SU rnd; C + [VHGNDruinheight]; [VHGNDtmp] = C; => VHGND ruin add marked;
	[VHGNDruinz] = [VHGNDruinzhi]; C = 3; => SU rnd; C + [VHGNDruinheight]; [VHGNDtmp] = C; => VHGND ruin add marked;
	[VHGNDruinx]+; A = [VHGNDruinx]; ? A '< [VHGNDruinxhi] -> VHGND ruin wall x;
	[VHGNDruinz] = [VHGNDruinzlo];
    "VHGND ruin wall z"
	[VHGNDruinx] = [VHGNDruinxlo]; C = 2; => SU rnd; C + [VHGNDruinheight]; [VHGNDtmp] = C; => VHGND ruin add marked;
	[VHGNDruinx] = [VHGNDruinxhi]; C = 3; => SU rnd; C + [VHGNDruinheight]; [VHGNDtmp] = C; => VHGND ruin add marked;
	[VHGNDruinz]+; A = [VHGNDruinz]; ? A '< [VHGNDruinzhi] -> VHGND ruin wall z;
	end;

"VHGND ruin plaza"
	C = 10; => SU rnd; C + 5; [VHGNDruinheight] = C;
	C = 140; => SU rnd; C + 30; [VHGNDruincx] = C;
	C = 140; => SU rnd; C + 30; [VHGNDruincz] = C;
	C = 15; => SU rnd; C + 15; [VHGNDruinrad] = C;
	=> VHGND ruin centre height;
	A = [VHGNDruincx]; A - [VHGNDruinrad]; [VHGNDruinxlo] = A; [VHGNDruinx] = A;
	A = [VHGNDruincx]; A + [VHGNDruinrad]; [VHGNDruinxhi] = A;
	A = [VHGNDruincz]; A - [VHGNDruinrad]; [VHGNDruinzlo] = A; [VHGNDruinz] = A;
	A = [VHGNDruincz]; A + [VHGNDruinrad]; [VHGNDruinzhi] = A;
    "VHGND ruin plaza z"
	[VHGNDruinx] = [VHGNDruinxlo];
    "VHGND ruin plaza x"
	[VHGNDruinvalue] = [VHGNDruinbase]; [VHGNDruinmark] = 0; => VHGND ruin set;
	[VHGNDruinx]+; A = [VHGNDruinx]; ? A '< [VHGNDruinxhi] -> VHGND ruin plaza x;
	[VHGNDruinz]+; A = [VHGNDruinz]; ? A '< [VHGNDruinzhi] -> VHGND ruin plaza z;
	A = [VHGNDruinrad]; A / 2; [VHGNDruinrad] = A;
	C = 3; => SU rnd; ? C != 0 -> VHGND ruin plaza z columns;
	C = 3; => SU rnd; C + 2; [VHGNDruinspacing] = C;
	A = [VHGNDruincx]; A - [VHGNDruinrad]; [VHGNDruinx] = A;
	A = [VHGNDruincx]; A + [VHGNDruinrad]; [VHGNDruinxhi] = A;
    "VHGND ruin plaza x columns"
	A = [VHGNDruincz]; A - [VHGNDruinrad]; [VHGNDruinz] = A; C = 2; => SU rnd; C + [VHGNDruinheight]; C + [VHGNDruinbase]; [VHGNDruinvalue] = C; [VHGNDruinmark] = 1; => VHGND ruin set;
	A = [VHGNDruincz]; A + [VHGNDruinrad]; [VHGNDruinz] = A; C = 3; => SU rnd; C + [VHGNDruinheight]; C + [VHGNDruinbase]; [VHGNDruinvalue] = C; [VHGNDruinmark] = 1; => VHGND ruin set;
	A = [VHGNDruinx]; A + [VHGNDruinspacing]; [VHGNDruinx] = A; ? A '< [VHGNDruinxhi] -> VHGND ruin plaza x columns;
    "VHGND ruin plaza z columns"
	C = 3; => SU rnd; ? C != 0 -> VHGND ruin plaza done;
	C = 3; => SU rnd; C + 2; [VHGNDruinspacing] = C;
	A = [VHGNDruincz]; A - [VHGNDruinrad]; [VHGNDruinz] = A;
	A = [VHGNDruincz]; A + [VHGNDruinrad]; [VHGNDruinzhi] = A;
    "VHGND ruin plaza z column loop"
	A = [VHGNDruincx]; A - [VHGNDruinrad]; [VHGNDruinx] = A; C = 2; => SU rnd; C + [VHGNDruinheight]; C + [VHGNDruinbase]; [VHGNDruinvalue] = C; [VHGNDruinmark] = 1; => VHGND ruin set;
	A = [VHGNDruincx]; A + [VHGNDruinrad]; [VHGNDruinx] = A; C = 3; => SU rnd; C + [VHGNDruinheight]; C + [VHGNDruinbase]; [VHGNDruinvalue] = C; [VHGNDruinmark] = 1; => VHGND ruin set;
	A = [VHGNDruinz]; A + [VHGNDruinspacing]; [VHGNDruinz] = A; ? A '< [VHGNDruinzhi] -> VHGND ruin plaza z column loop;
    "VHGND ruin plaza done"
	end;

"VHGND ruin cross"
	C = 3; => SU rnd; C + 1; [VHGNDruinheight] = C;
	C = 150; => SU rnd; C + 25; [VHGNDruincx] = C;
	C = 150; => SU rnd; C + 25; [VHGNDruincz] = C;
	C = 5; => SU rnd; C + 5; [VHGNDruinrad] = C;
	C = 5; => SU rnd; ? C != 0 -> VHGND ruin cross size ready;
	A = [VHGNDruinrad]; A '* 2; [VHGNDruinrad] = A;
    "VHGND ruin cross size ready"
	A = [VHGNDruincx]; A - [VHGNDruinrad]; [VHGNDruinx] = A;
	A = [VHGNDruincx]; A + [VHGNDruinrad]; [VHGNDruinxhi] = A;
    "VHGND ruin cross x"
	[VHGNDruinz] = [VHGNDruincz]; A = [VHGNDruinx]; A - [VHGNDruincx]; ? A >= 0 -> VHGND ruin cross absx; C = 0; C - A; A = C;
    "VHGND ruin cross absx"
	C = [VHGNDruinrad]; C - A; C + 1; C '* [VHGNDruinheight]; [VHGNDtmp] = C; => VHGND ruin add marked;
	[VHGNDruinx]+; A = [VHGNDruinx]; ? A '< [VHGNDruinxhi] -> VHGND ruin cross x;
	A = [VHGNDruincz]; A - [VHGNDruinrad]; [VHGNDruinz] = A;
	A = [VHGNDruincz]; A + [VHGNDruinrad]; [VHGNDruinzhi] = A;
    "VHGND ruin cross z"
	[VHGNDruinx] = [VHGNDruincx]; A = [VHGNDruinz]; A - [VHGNDruincz]; ? A >= 0 -> VHGND ruin cross absz; C = 0; C - A; A = C;
    "VHGND ruin cross absz"
	C = [VHGNDruinrad]; C - A; C + 1; C '* [VHGNDruinheight]; [VHGNDtmp] = C; => VHGND ruin add marked;
	[VHGNDruinz]+; A = [VHGNDruinz]; ? A '< [VHGNDruinzhi] -> VHGND ruin cross z;
	end;

"VHGND ruin dome"
	C = 10; => SU rnd; C + 10; [VHGNDruinheight] = C;
	C = 160; => SU rnd; C + 20; [VHGNDruincx] = C;
	C = 160; => SU rnd; C + 20; [VHGNDruincz] = C;
	C = 8; => SU rnd; C + 12; [VHGNDruinrad] = C;
	=> VHGND ruin centre height;
	A = [VHGNDruincx]; A - [VHGNDruinrad]; [VHGNDruinxlo] = A; [VHGNDruinx] = A;
	A = [VHGNDruincx]; A + [VHGNDruinrad]; [VHGNDruinxhi] = A;
	A = [VHGNDruincz]; A - [VHGNDruinrad]; [VHGNDruinzlo] = A; [VHGNDruinz] = A;
	A = [VHGNDruincz]; A + [VHGNDruinrad]; [VHGNDruinzhi] = A;
    "VHGND ruin dome block z"
	[VHGNDruinx] = [VHGNDruinxlo];
    "VHGND ruin dome block x"
	A = [VHGNDruinbase]; A + [VHGNDruinheight]; [VHGNDruinvalue] = A; [VHGNDruinmark] = 1; => VHGND ruin set;
	[VHGNDruinx]+; A = [VHGNDruinx]; ? A '< [VHGNDruinxhi] -> VHGND ruin dome block x;
	[VHGNDruinz]+; A = [VHGNDruinz]; ? A '< [VHGNDruinzhi] -> VHGND ruin dome block z;
	A = [VHGNDruinrad]; A '* 2; A / 3; [VHGNDruinrad] = A;
	C = 2; => SU rnd; [VHGNDruinmark] = C;
	A = [VHGNDruincx]; A - [VHGNDruinrad]; [VHGNDruinxlo] = A;
	A = [VHGNDruincx]; A + [VHGNDruinrad]; [VHGNDruinxhi] = A;
	A = [VHGNDruincz]; A - [VHGNDruinrad]; [VHGNDruinzlo] = A; [VHGNDruinz] = A;
	A = [VHGNDruincz]; A + [VHGNDruinrad]; [VHGNDruinzhi] = A;
    "VHGND ruin dome z"
	[VHGNDruinx] = [VHGNDruinxlo];
    "VHGND ruin dome x"
	A = [VHGNDruinx]; A - [VHGNDruincx]; ? A >= 0 -> VHGND ruin dome absx; C = 0; C - A; A = C;
    "VHGND ruin dome absx"
	[VHGNDruindx] = A; A = [VHGNDruinz]; A - [VHGNDruincz]; ? A >= 0 -> VHGND ruin dome absz; C = 0; C - A; A = C;
    "VHGND ruin dome absz"
	C = [VHGNDruindx]; ? A '<= C -> VHGND ruin dome dist ready; A = C;
    "VHGND ruin dome dist ready"
	[VHGNDruindist] = A; ? A '>= [VHGNDruinrad] -> VHGND ruin dome next;
	C = [VHGNDruinrad]; C - A; C '* 3; C '* [VHGNDruinheight]; C / [VHGNDruinrad];
	C + [VHGNDruinbase]; C + 1; [VHGNDruinvalue] = C; => VHGND ruin set;
    "VHGND ruin dome next"
	[VHGNDruinx]+; A = [VHGNDruinx]; ? A '< [VHGNDruinxhi] -> VHGND ruin dome x;
	[VHGNDruinz]+; A = [VHGNDruinz]; ? A '< [VHGNDruinzhi] -> VHGND ruin dome z;
	end;

"VHGND ruin centre height"
	[VHGNDruinx] = [VHGNDruincx]; [VHGNDruinz] = [VHGNDruincz]; => VHGND ruin get;
	end;

"VHGND ruin get"
	A = [VHGNDruinz]; A '* VHGNDMAP; A + [VHGNDruinx]; [VHGNDptr] = A;
	A + RPSM; [MBptr] = A; => MEM get byte; [VHGNDruinbase] = [MBval];
	end;

"VHGND ruin add marked"
	A = [VHGNDtmp]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND ruin add done;
	=> VHGND ruin get; A = [VHGNDruinbase]; A + [VHGNDtmp]; [VHGNDruinvalue] = A;
	[VHGNDruinmark] = 1; => VHGND ruin set;
    "VHGND ruin add done"
	end;

"VHGND ruin set"
	A = [VHGNDruinvalue]; ? A >= 0 -> VHGND ruin set low; A = 0;
    "VHGND ruin set low"
	? A '<= 127 -> VHGND ruin set value; A = 127;
    "VHGND ruin set value"
	[MBval] = A; A = [VHGNDruinz]; A '* VHGNDMAP; A + [VHGNDruinx]; [VHGNDptr] = A;
	A + RPSM; [MBptr] = A; => MEM put byte;
	A = VHGNDruins; A + [VHGNDptr]; [A] = [VHGNDruinmark];
	A = [VHGNDruinmark]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND ruin set done; [VHGNDruinmarks]+;
    "VHGND ruin set done"
	end;

"VHGND type3 object classes"
	( NOCTIS-1.CPP:2238-2252, packed quadrant class bits. )
	[VHGNDptr] = 0;
    "VHGND type3 class loop"
	A = RPSM; A + [VHGNDptr]; [MBptr] = A; => MEM get byte;
	A = [MBval]; A / 25; ? A = 0 -> VHGND type3 class low;
	? A = 1 -> VHGND type3 class mid; ? A = 2 -> VHGND type3 class high;
	A = 168; -> VHGND type3 class store;
    "VHGND type3 class low"
	A = 80; -> VHGND type3 class store;
    "VHGND type3 class mid"
	A = 144; -> VHGND type3 class store;
    "VHGND type3 class high"
	A = 160;
    "VHGND type3 class store"
	[MBval] = A; A = ROBJ; A + [VHGNDptr]; [MBptr] = A; => MEM put byte;
	[VHGNDptr]+; A = [VHGNDptr]; ? A < 40000 -> VHGND type3 class loop;
	end;

"VHGND asterism"
	( NOCTIS-1.CPP:1635-1670.  The five locals are binary32 in the
	  original.  Narrow every assignment so the occasional density+1 ray
	  and all following RNG consumption remain faithful. )
	[FI] = [VHGNDastardensity]; => IntToF;
	[FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = 54442D18h; [FA1] = 401921FBh; => FQuo;
	=> F32Narrow; => FStoreF32; [VHGNDastarad] = [FS0];
	[VHGNDastarang] = 0;
    "VHGND asterism ray"
	[FS0] = [VHGNDastarang]; => FLoadF32;
	[FB0] = 54442D18h; [FB1] = 401921FBh; => FCmp;
	A = [FI]; ? A >= 0 -> VHGND asterism done;
	( shift_d = (float)random(1000)/1000; shift_d *= size; )
	C = 1000; => SU rnd; [FI] = C; => IntToF;
	[FB0] = 0; [FB1] = 408F4000h; => FQuo;
	=> F32Narrow; => FStoreF32; [VHGNDastard] = [FS0];
	[FT0] = [FA0]; [FT1] = [FA1];
	[FI] = [VHGNDastarsize]; => IntToF;
	[FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1]; [FA0] = [FT0]; [FA1] = [FT1];
	=> FMul; => F32Narrow; => FStoreF32; [VHGNDastard] = [FS0];
	A = [VHGNDastard]; ? A '< 3F800000h -> VHGND asterism next ray;
	( var=(float)variation/shift_d; color=(float)base. )
	[FI] = [VHGNDastarvariation]; => IntToF; [FT0] = [FA0]; [FT1] = [FA1];
	[FS0] = [VHGNDastard]; => FLoadF32; [FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [FT0]; [FA1] = [FT1]; => FQuo;
	=> F32Narrow; => FStoreF32; [VHGNDastarvar] = [FS0];
	[FI] = [VHGNDastarbase]; => IntToF;
	=> F32Narrow; => FStoreF32; [VHGNDastarcolor] = [FS0];
    "VHGND asterism pixel"
	A = [VHGNDastard]; A & 7FFFFFFFh; ? A = 0 -> VHGND asterism next ray;
	A = [VHGNDastard]; A & 80000000h; ? A != 0 -> VHGND asterism next ray;
	( shift_x = chop(cos(ang)*shift_d+x). )
	[FS0] = [VHGNDastard]; => FLoadF32; [FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1];
	[FS0] = [VHGNDastarang]; => FLoadF32; => FCos; => FMul;
	[FT0] = [FA0]; [FT1] = [FA1]; [FI] = [VHGNDastarx]; => IntToF;
	[FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1]; [FA0] = [FT0]; [FA1] = [FT1];
	=> FAdd; => FToIntChop; [VHGNDastarpx] = [FI];
	( shift_y = chop(sin(ang)*shift_d+y). )
	[FS0] = [VHGNDastard]; => FLoadF32; [FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1];
	[FS0] = [VHGNDastarang]; => FLoadF32; => FSin; => FMul;
	[FT0] = [FA0]; [FT1] = [FA1]; [FI] = [VHGNDastary]; => IntToF;
	[FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1]; [FA0] = [FT0]; [FA1] = [FT1];
	=> FAdd; => FToIntChop; [VHGNDastarpy] = [FI];
	A = [VHGNDastarpx]; ? A <= 0 -> VHGND asterism advance;
	? A >= 256 -> VHGND asterism advance;
	A = [VHGNDastarpy]; ? A <= 0 -> VHGND asterism advance;
	? A >= 256 -> VHGND asterism advance;
	[FS0] = [VHGNDastarcolor]; => FLoadF32; => FToIntChop;
	[MBval] = [FI]; A = [VHGNDastarpy]; A '* 256; A + [VHGNDastarpx];
	A + [VHGNDtexbase]; [MBptr] = A; => MEM put byte;
    "VHGND asterism advance"
	( color += var; shift_d -= 1; both stores narrow to float. )
	[FS0] = [VHGNDastarvar]; => FLoadF32; [FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1];
	[FS0] = [VHGNDastarcolor]; => FLoadF32; => FAdd;
	=> F32Narrow; => FStoreF32; [VHGNDastarcolor] = [FS0];
	[FS0] = [VHGNDastard]; => FLoadF32;
	[FB0] = 0; [FB1] = 3FF00000h; => FSub;
	=> F32Narrow; => FStoreF32; [VHGNDastard] = [FS0];
	-> VHGND asterism pixel;
    "VHGND asterism next ray"
	( ang += ad, stored as binary32. )
	[FS0] = [VHGNDastarad]; => FLoadF32; [FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1];
	[FS0] = [VHGNDastarang]; => FLoadF32; => FAdd;
	=> F32Narrow; => FStoreF32; [VHGNDastarang] = [FS0];
	-> VHGND asterism ray;
    "VHGND asterism done"
	end;

"VHGND felisian line"
	( felisian_srf_darkline over the 200x200 height map. )
	C = 200; => SU rnd; [VHGNDlinex] = C;
	C = 200; => SU rnd; [VHGNDlinez] = C;
	C = 25; => SU rnd; C - 50; [VHGNDdeviation] = C;
	C = 10; => SU rnd; C + 2; [VHGNDvariability] = C;
    "VHGND felisian pixel"
	A = [VHGNDlinelen]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND felisian done;
	C = 3; => SU rnd; A = [VHGNDlinex]; A + C; A - 1; [VHGNDlinex] = A;
	C = 3; => SU rnd; A = [VHGNDlinez]; A + C; A - 1; [VHGNDlinez] = A;
	C = [VHGNDvariability]; => SU rnd;
	A = [VHGNDvariability]; A > 1; C - A; A = [VHGNDdeviation]; A + C; [VHGNDdeviation] = A;
	A = [VHGNDlinez]; A '* 200; A + [VHGNDlinex]; A & 0FFFFh; [VHGNDptr] = A;
	? A '<= 0 -> VHGND felisian next; ? A '>= 40000 -> VHGND felisian next;
	A = RPSM; A + [VHGNDptr]; [MBptr] = A; => MEM get byte;
	A = [MBval]; A + [VHGNDdeviation]; ? A >= 0 -> VHGND felisian low; A = 0;
    "VHGND felisian low"
	? A '<= 127 -> VHGND felisian peak; A = 127;
    "VHGND felisian peak"
	[VHGNDtmp] = A; [MBval] = A;
	A = RPSM; A + [VHGNDptr]; [MBptr] = A; => MEM put byte;
	A = [VHGNDptr]; A + 1; ? A '>= 40000 -> VHGND felisian left; A + RPSM; [MBptr] = A; [MBval] = [VHGNDtmp]; => MEM put byte;
    "VHGND felisian left"
	A = [VHGNDptr]; A - 1; ? A '<= 0 -> VHGND felisian down; A + RPSM; [MBptr] = A; [MBval] = [VHGNDtmp]; => MEM put byte;
    "VHGND felisian down"
	A = [VHGNDptr]; A + 200; ? A '>= 40000 -> VHGND felisian up; A + RPSM; [MBptr] = A; [MBval] = [VHGNDtmp]; => MEM put byte;
    "VHGND felisian up"
	A = [VHGNDptr]; A - 200; ? A '<= 0 -> VHGND felisian next; A + RPSM; [MBptr] = A; [MBval] = [VHGNDtmp]; => MEM put byte;
    "VHGND felisian next"
	[VHGNDlinelen]-; -> VHGND felisian pixel;
    "VHGND felisian done"
	end;

"VHGND flandom"
	( Borland helper: random(32767) * 0.000030518, kept wide until
	  the caller's float store. )
	C = 32767; => SU rnd; [FI] = C; => IntToF;
	[FB0] = 7EDA0BE1h; [FB1] = 3F00000Eh; => FMul;
	end;

"VHGND similar texture"
	( Shared `similar:` texture body at NOCTIS-1.CPP:2353-2379. )
	[VHGNDtscale] = 32;
	C = 16; => SU rnd; C + 16; [VHGNDtexn] = C;
	[VHGNDptr] = 65535;
    "VHGND texture fill"
	C = [VHGNDtexn]; => SU rnd;
	A = [VHGNDtexbase]; A + [VHGNDptr]; [MBptr] = A; [MBval] = C; => MEM put byte;
	[VHGNDptr]-; A = [VHGNDptr]; ? A != 0 -> VHGND texture fill;
	C = 3; => SU rnd; C + 1; [VHGNDtexpasses] = C;
    "VHGND texture smooth pass"
	[VHGNDptr] = 65278;
    "VHGND texture smooth pixel"
	A = [VHGNDtexbase]; A + [VHGNDptr]; [MBptr] = A; => MEM get byte; [VHGNDtmp] = [MBval];
	A = [VHGNDtexbase]; A + [VHGNDptr]; A + 1; [MBptr] = A; => MEM get byte;
	A = [VHGNDtmp]; A + [MBval]; [VHGNDtmp] = A;
	A = [VHGNDtexbase]; A + [VHGNDptr]; A + 256; [MBptr] = A; => MEM get byte;
	A = [VHGNDtmp]; A + [MBval]; [VHGNDtmp] = A;
	A = [VHGNDtexbase]; A + [VHGNDptr]; A + 257; [MBptr] = A; => MEM get byte;
	A = [VHGNDtmp]; A + [MBval]; A > 2; [MBval] = A;
	A = [VHGNDtexbase]; A + [VHGNDptr]; [MBptr] = A; => MEM put byte;
	[VHGNDptr]-; A = [VHGNDptr]; ? A != 0 -> VHGND texture smooth pixel;
	[VHGNDtexpasses]-; A = [VHGNDtexpasses]; ? A != 0 -> VHGND texture smooth pass;
	A = [VHGNDfrosty]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND similar done;
	C = 48; => SU rnd; C + 16; [VHGNDtscale] = C;
	C = 250; => SU rnd; [VHGNDgenn] = C;
    "VHGND frost line loop"
	A = [VHGNDgenn]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND similar done;
	C = 2; => SU rnd; A = 0; A - C; [VHGNDlinetrendx] = A;
	C = 200; => SU rnd; C + 100; [VHGNDlinelen] = C;
	[VHGNDlinetrendz] = 0; => VHGND texture darkline;
	[VHGNDgenn]-; -> VHGND frost line loop;
    "VHGND similar done"
	end;

"VHGND texture random"
	( Source texture fill: slot zero retains its cleared value. )
	[VHGNDptr] = 65535;
    "VHGND texture random pixel"
	C = [VHGNDtexn]; => SU rnd;
	A = [VHGNDtexbase]; A + [VHGNDptr]; [MBptr] = A; [MBval] = C; => MEM put byte;
	[VHGNDptr]-; A = [VHGNDptr]; ? A != 0 -> VHGND texture random pixel;
	end;

"VHGND texture darkline"
	( srf_darkline over the real 256x256 p_background texture. )
	C = 256; => SU rnd; [VHGNDlinex] = C;
	C = 256; => SU rnd; [VHGNDlinez] = C;
    "VHGND darkline pixel"
	A = [VHGNDlinelen]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND darkline done;
	C = 3; => SU rnd; A = [VHGNDlinex]; A + C; A + [VHGNDlinetrendx]; [VHGNDlinex] = A;
	C = 3; => SU rnd; A = [VHGNDlinez]; A + C; A + [VHGNDlinetrendz]; [VHGNDlinez] = A;
	A = [VHGNDlinez]; A '* 256; A + [VHGNDlinex]; A & 0FFFFh; [VHGNDptr] = A;
	? A '<= 0 -> VHGND darkline next;
	( The 16-bit mask already proves 1..65535.  Inline the bounded byte
	  read/write while retaining the source's skip-zero and halving rules. )
	A + [VHGNDtexbase]; A + nw; C = [A]; C & 255; C > 1; [A] = C;
    "VHGND darkline next"
	[VHGNDlinelen]-; -> VHGND darkline pixel;
    "VHGND darkline done"
	end;

"VHGND palette"
	( Stable, deliberately bounded landed-view palette.  The full source
	  create_sky palette stays out of the live handoff until its long-running
	  painter can be scheduled without freezing the GUI. )
	=> PAL zero; => PAL range;
	[PVsrc] = range8088; [PVself] = 0; [PVfirst] = 0; [PVn] = 64;
	[PVfr] = 38; [PVfg] = 42; [PVfb] = 48; => PAL tavola;
	[PVsrc] = range8088; [PVself] = 0; [PVfirst] = 64; [PVn] = 64;
	[PVfr] = 22; [PVfg] = 34; [PVfb] = 54; => PAL tavola;
	[PVsrc] = range8088; [PVself] = 0; [PVfirst] = 128; [PVn] = 128;
	[PVfr] = 52; [PVfg] = 47; [PVfb] = 39; => PAL tavola;
	end;

( ==================================================================== )
( selected globe pixel -> live landing identity                         )
( ==================================================================== )

"VHGND landing identity"
	( The opening 000:060 site remains pinned to its measured reference
	  values.  Every other site runs the already-verified surface() port,
	  samples exactly p_background[360*lat+lon] and its paired atmosphere
	  byte, then releases those buffers to the terrain generator. )
	[VHGNDsurfaceok] = 0; [VHGNDcloudrain] = 0; [GRalbedo] = 32;
	A = [VHTtx]; ? A != 3979984 -> VHGND identity general;
	A = [VHTty]; ? A != 0FFFF5671h -> VHGND identity general;
	A = [VHTtz]; ? A != 0FFFF5430h -> VHGND identity general;
	A = [VHGNDlon]; ? A != 0 -> VHGND identity general;
	A = [VHGNDlat]; ? A != 60 -> VHGND identity general;
	A = [VHGNDplanet]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND identity done;
	[GRalbedo] = 20; ? A = 1 -> VHGND identity done;
	? A != 3 -> VHGND identity opening outer; [VHGNDcloudrain] = 40A00000h;
	-> VHGND identity done;
    "VHGND identity opening outer"
	? A != 4 -> VHGND identity done; [GRalbedo] = 4;
	-> VHGND identity done;

    "VHGND identity general"
	=> VHGND globe surface;

	A = [VHGNDlat]; A '* 360; A + [VHGNDlon]; [VHGNDptr] = A;
	A = [SUpbase]; A + [VHGNDptr]; [MBptr] = A; => MEM get byte;
	[VHGNDsample] = [MBval];
	A = [VHGNDptr]; A > 1; A + ROBJ; [MBptr] = A; => MEM get byte;
	[VHGNDcloud] = [MBval];

	( The ground filters are the selected merged globe colour, followed by
	  the independent source tint stream below. )
	A = [VHGNDsample]; A + [VHGNDcbase]; A '* 3; A + sutmppal;
	[VHGNDgrf] = [A plus 0]; [VHGNDggf] = [A plus 1]; [VHGNDgbf] = [A plus 2];

	( Recover underlying landing albedo.  The night branch first reverses
	  surface()'s two-bit terminator shift; atmospheric types then subtract
	  their cloud overlay before quantising to multiples of four. )
	A = [VHGNDsample]; C = [GRSKnightzone]; ? C = 0 -> VHGND identity albedo atmosphere;
	A '* 4;
    "VHGND identity albedo atmosphere"
	C = [GRiptype]; ? C = 3 -> VHGND identity albedo subtract;
	? C = 5 -> VHGND identity albedo subtract; -> VHGND identity albedo quantise;
    "VHGND identity albedo subtract"
	A - [VHGNDcloud];
    "VHGND identity albedo quantise"
	A '/ 4; A '* 4; C = [GRiptype]; ? C = 3 -> VHGND identity albedo double;
	? C = 5 -> VHGND identity albedo double; -> VHGND identity albedo ready;
    "VHGND identity albedo double"
	A '* 2;
    "VHGND identity albedo ready"
	[GRalbedo] = A;

	( rainy = min(atmosphere/4, 5), retained as binary32 for create_sky and
	  the foreground storm renderer. )
	A = [VHGNDcloud]; ? A '<= 20 -> VHGND identity cloud capped; A = 20;
    "VHGND identity cloud capped"
	[FI] = A; => IntToF; [FB0] = 0; [FB1] = 3FD00000h; => FMul;
	=> F32Narrow; => FStoreF32; [VHGNDcloudrain] = [FS0];

	( fast_srand(p_orb_seed * p_orb_ecc * 12345), followed by the exact
	  fast_random masks used for sky and ground tinting. )
	A = [VHGNDplanet]; A < 1; E = nsporbseed; E + A;
	[FB0] = [E plus 0]; [FB1] = [E plus 1]; E = nsporbecc; E + A;
	[FC0] = [E plus 0]; [FC1] = [E plus 1]; A = 4; => GeoSetK;
	=> GeoTwoMulKChopLive; A = [FI]; => SU fast srand;
	[SUfmask] = 31; => SU frnd; C + 32; [VHGNDsrf] = C;
	[SUfmask] = 15; => SU frnd; C + 48; [VHGNDsgf] = C;
	[SUfmask] = 15; => SU frnd; C + 48; [VHGNDsbf] = C;
	[SUfmask] = 15; => SU frnd; C + [VHGNDgrf]; [VHGNDgrf] = C;
	[SUfmask] = 15; => SU frnd; C + [VHGNDggf]; [VHGNDggf] = C;
	[SUfmask] = 15; => SU frnd; C + [VHGNDgbf]; [VHGNDgbf] = C;
	[VHGNDsurfaceok] = 1;
    "VHGND identity done"
	end;

"VHGND globe surface"
	( The close-body renderer needs surface() itself, not build_surface().
	  Keep the original 360x180 albedo in p_background/s_background and copy
	  only its generated 64-colour band into the live presenter palette. )
	=> VHGND general phase setup;
	=> VHG fpu clean; => SU fp init; => SU pal init;
	[SUid] = [VHGNDplanet]; [SUtype] = [GRiptype]; [SUplwp] = [VHGNDplwp];
	[SUowner] = [VHGNDowner]; [SUnsr] = [VHTred]; [SUnsg] = [VHTgreen]; [SUnsb] = [VHTblue];
	[SUsv0] = [VHGNDseedval0]; [SUsv1] = [VHGNDseedval1]; [SUledon] = 0;
	A = [VHGNDowner]; ? A >= 0 -> VHGND globe moon;
	[SUcbase] = 192; [VHGNDcbase] = 192; => SU select planet; -> VHGND globe selected;
    "VHGND globe moon"
	[SUcbase] = 128; [VHGNDcbase] = 128; => SU select moon;
    "VHGND globe selected"
	=> SU select ovl; => SU wipe; => SU wipe ovl; => SU surface;
	A = [SUcbase]; A '* 3; C = A;
	A = sutmppal; A + C; D = pal6; D + C; [VHGNDpalcount] = 192;
    "VHGND globe palette copy"
	C = [A]; [D] = C; A+; D+; [VHGNDpalcount]-;
	C = [VHGNDpalcount]; ? C != 0 -> VHGND globe palette copy;
	[PUn] = 256; => PAL upload;
	end;

"VHGND general phase setup"
	[VHGNDphaseindex] = [VHGNDplanet]; [VHGNDmoonmass] = 0;
	E = nspowner; E + [VHGNDplanet]; A = [E]; [VHGNDowner] = A;
	? A < 0 -> VHGND phase owner ready;
	E = nsptype; E + A; A = [E]; ? A = 10 -> VHGND phase companion moon;
	[VHGNDphaseindex] = [VHGNDowner]; -> VHGND phase owner ready;
    "VHGND phase companion moon"
	[VHGNDmoonmass] = 1;
    "VHGND phase owner ready"
	=> VHGND UTC seconds; => VHGND orbital phase; => VHGND terminator;
	end;

"VHGND sky"
	( The default landing point is longitude 0, latitude 60.  Its opening-system
	  colours were derived through
	  the delivered topology/surface references for the actual class-0 star
	  at (3979984,-43407,-43984), at source time zero/plwp zero.  Planet 0 is
	  type 8; planet 1 is type 4.  The exact create_sky path is cheap for both
	  types and produces the source surface palette as well as atmosphere. )
	[GRSKptype] = [GRiptype]; [GRSKsctype] = [VHGNDsctype];
	[GRSKatmosphere] = [VHGNDatmosphere]; [GRSKnightzone] = 0; [VHGNDsunxf] = 1;
	[GRSKtarget] = [VHGNDplanet]; [GRSKowner] = 0FFFFFFFFh;
	[GRSKstarclass] = 0; [GRSKlat] = [VHGNDlat]; [GRSKquadwords] = 16000;
	[GRSKexposure] = 41DB20C5h; ( 27.391f, source 35-degree day exposure )

	( Preserve the measured opening landing exactly.  Every other selected
	  star takes the generated UTC/orbit/terminator path below. )
	A = [VHTtx]; ? A != 3979984 -> VHGND general sky;
	A = [VHTty]; ? A != 0FFFF5671h -> VHGND general sky;
	A = [VHTtz]; ? A != 0FFFF5430h -> VHGND general sky;
	A = [VHGNDlon]; ? A != 0 -> VHGND general sky;
	A = [VHGNDlat]; ? A != 60 -> VHGND general sky;

	( planet 0, type 8 milky/quartz )
	[GRSKseed] = 149130; [GRSKalbedo] = 32; [GRSKrainy] = 0;
	[GRSKbrightness] = 48;
	[GRSKsrf] = 53; [GRSKsgf] = 58; [GRSKsbf] = 53;
	[GRSKgrf] = 75; [GRSKggf] = 63; [GRSKgbf] = 52;
	[GRSKdsd1] = 42066738h; ( 33.6007996f )
	A = [VHGNDplanet]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND sky inputs ready;

	( planet 1, type 4 rocky/airless )
	[GRSKseed] = 293154; [GRSKalbedo] = 20; [GRSKrainy] = 0;
	[GRSKbrightness] = 0;
	[GRSKsrf] = 46; [GRSKsgf] = 57; [GRSKsbf] = 52;
	[GRSKgrf] = 24; [GRSKggf] = 44; [GRSKgbf] = 29;
	[GRSKdsd1] = 428BC1D5h; ( 69.8785782f )
	A = [VHGNDplanet]; ? A = 1 -> VHGND sky inputs ready;

	( body 3, type-2 inner moon of the gas giant )
	[GRSKowner] = 2; [GRSKseed] = 19668; [GRSKalbedo] = 20;
	[GRSKrainy] = 40A00000h; [GRSKbrightness] = 63;
	[GRSKsrf] = 49; [GRSKsgf] = 55; [GRSKsbf] = 48;
	[GRSKgrf] = 31; [GRSKggf] = 37; [GRSKgbf] = 32;
	[GRSKdsd1] = 43045180h; ( owner orbit 132.31836f )
	A = [VHGNDplanet]; ? A = 3 -> VHGND sky inputs ready;

	( body 4, type-2 outer moon )
	[GRSKseed] = 31169; [GRSKalbedo] = 4; [GRSKrainy] = 0;
	[GRSKbrightness] = 63;
	[GRSKsrf] = 57; [GRSKsgf] = 55; [GRSKsbf] = 56;
	[GRSKgrf] = 10; [GRSKggf] = 17; [GRSKgbf] = 16;
    "VHGND sky opening ready"
	-> VHGND sky inputs ready;

"VHGND general sky"
	( Source planets() derives the live landing light from UTC seconds, the
	  body's rotation period and its orbital viewpoint.  nstopo retains the
	  complete phase-A geometry used below, including eccentricity and tilt;
	  ordinary moons compose their parent planet's solar vector and moons of a
	  companion star use that owner as the viewpoint. )
	[GRSKtarget] = [VHGNDplanet]; [GRSKstarclass] = [nsclass];
	[GRSKseed] = [VHGNDseed]; [GRSKalbedo] = [GRalbedo]; [GRSKowner] = [VHGNDowner];
	A = [VHGNDsurfaceok]; ? A != 0 -> VHGND general phase ready;
	=> VHGND general phase setup; [GRSKowner] = [VHGNDowner];
    "VHGND general phase ready"

	( dsd1 is the active sun distance: the parent planet's star orbit for
	  ordinary moons, or the body's own orbit otherwise. )
	A = [VHGNDphaseindex]; A < 1; E = nsporbray; E + A;
	[FA0] = [E plus 0]; [FA1] = [E plus 1]; => F32Narrow; => FStoreF32;
	[GRSKdsd1] = [FS0];

	( The live landing generator retains the selected surface palette colour,
	  cloud load and exact tint stream.  Keep the old deterministic fallback
	  only for standalone callers that invoke sky without first generating a
	  surface. )
	A = [VHGNDsurfaceok]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND general filters fallback;
	[GRSKsrf] = [VHGNDsrf]; [GRSKsgf] = [VHGNDsgf]; [GRSKsbf] = [VHGNDsbf];
	[GRSKgrf] = [VHGNDgrf]; [GRSKggf] = [VHGNDggf]; [GRSKgbf] = [VHGNDgbf];
	[GRSKrainy] = [VHGNDcloudrain]; -> VHGND general filters ready;
    "VHGND general filters fallback"
	A = [VHGNDseed]; A '* 12345; => SU fast srand;
	C = 31; => SU rfr; C + 32; [GRSKsrf] = C;
	C = 15; => SU rfr; C + 48; [GRSKsgf] = C;
	C = 15; => SU rfr; C + 48; [GRSKsbf] = C;
	A = [GRalbedo]; A '/ 2; A + 32; [VHGNDtmp] = A;
	C = 15; => SU rfr; C + [VHGNDtmp]; [GRSKgrf] = C;
	A = [GRalbedo]; A '/ 3; A + 32; [VHGNDtmp] = A;
	C = 15; => SU rfr; C + [VHGNDtmp]; [GRSKggf] = C;
	A = [GRalbedo]; A '/ 4; A + 32; [VHGNDtmp] = A;
	C = 15; => SU rfr; C + [VHGNDtmp]; [GRSKgbf] = C;

	( Retained surface identity also supplies a stable 0..3.75 cloud load,
	  in the source quarter-step domain. )
	A = [VHGNDseed]; A & 15; [FI] = A; => IntToF;
	[FB0] = 0; [FB1] = 3FD00000h; => FMul; => F32Narrow; => FStoreF32;
	[GRSKrainy] = [FS0];
    "VHGND general filters ready"

	A = [VHGNDatmosphere]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND general airless;
	A = [GRSKnightzone]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND general daylight;
	A = [GRSKsgf]; A '/ 2; [GRSKsgf] = A;
	A = [GRSKsbf]; A '/ 2; [GRSKsbf] = A;
	[GRSKbrightness] = 32; A = [VHGNDcrep]; ? A <= 5 -> VHGND sky inputs ready;
	A = [GRSKsrf]; A '/ 2; [GRSKsrf] = A; [GRSKbrightness] = 8;
	-> VHGND sky inputs ready;
    "VHGND general daylight"
	[GRSKbrightness] = 48; A = [VHGNDcrep]; ? A > 5 -> VHGND sky inputs ready;
	A = [GRSKsgf]; A '/ 2; [GRSKsgf] = A;
	A = [GRSKsbf]; A '/ 3; [GRSKsbf] = A; [GRSKbrightness] = 40;
	-> VHGND sky inputs ready;
    "VHGND general airless"
	[GRSKbrightness] = 0; [GRSKrainy] = 0;
    "VHGND sky general ready"
 	-> VHGND sky inputs ready;

"VHGND UTC seconds"
	( NIV+ clock basis: elapsed UTC seconds since 1984-01-01, including its
	  original every-four-years leap rule.  The 32-bit value remains in range
	  through the dates supported by this port. )
	A = [VHGNDclockoverride]; ? A != 0 -> VHGND pinned seconds;
	[Timer Command] = READ UTC TIME; isocall;
	( The win32 runtime does not preserve x87 state across isocalls. )
	=> VHG fpu clean; => SU fp init;
	A = [Year]; A - 1984; [VHGNDdays] = A; A '* 365; [VHGNDsecs] = A;
	A = [VHGNDdays]; A '/ 4; A + [VHGNDsecs]; [VHGNDdays] = A;
	[VHGNDmonthbase] = 0; A = [Month]; ? A <= 1 -> VHGND month ready;
	[VHGNDmonthbase] = 31; ? A = 2 -> VHGND month ready;
	[VHGNDmonthbase] = 59; ? A = 3 -> VHGND month ready;
	[VHGNDmonthbase] = 90; ? A = 4 -> VHGND month ready;
	[VHGNDmonthbase] = 120; ? A = 5 -> VHGND month ready;
	[VHGNDmonthbase] = 151; ? A = 6 -> VHGND month ready;
	[VHGNDmonthbase] = 181; ? A = 7 -> VHGND month ready;
	[VHGNDmonthbase] = 212; ? A = 8 -> VHGND month ready;
	[VHGNDmonthbase] = 243; ? A = 9 -> VHGND month ready;
	[VHGNDmonthbase] = 273; ? A = 10 -> VHGND month ready;
	[VHGNDmonthbase] = 304; ? A = 11 -> VHGND month ready;
	[VHGNDmonthbase] = 334;
    "VHGND month ready"
	A = [VHGNDdays]; A + [VHGNDmonthbase]; [VHGNDdays] = A;
	A = [Month]; ? A <= 2 -> VHGND leap ready;
	A = [Year]; A '% 4; ? A != 0 -> VHGND leap ready; [VHGNDdays]+;
    "VHGND leap ready"
	A = [Day]; A - 1; A + [VHGNDdays]; A '* 86400; [VHGNDsecs] = A;
	A = [Hour]; A '* 3600; A + [VHGNDsecs]; [VHGNDsecs] = A;
	A = [Minute]; A '* 60; A + [VHGNDsecs]; A + [Second]; [VHGNDsecs] = A;
	[FI] = A; => IntToF; [SUsec0] = [FA0]; [SUsec1] = [FA1];
	-> VHGND seconds ready;
    "VHGND pinned seconds"
	A = [VHGNDclocksecs]; [VHGNDsecs] = A;
	=> VHG fpu clean; => SU fp init;
	[FI] = A; => IntToF; [SUsec0] = [FA0]; [SUsec1] = [FA1];
    "VHGND seconds ready"
	end;

"VHGND orbital phase"
	( p_riv = sqrt(star_mass / orbit_radius^2); orbital phase in source
	  degrees is secs*p_riv.  Companion-owned moons use the owner's body
	  mass constant, matching planet_xyz's alternate branch. )
	=> VHG fpu clean;
	A = [VHGNDmoonmass]; ? A != 0 -> VHGND orbital moon mass;
	[FS0] = [nsstarray]; => FLoadF32; -> VHGND orbital mass loaded;
    "VHGND orbital moon mass"
	A = [VHGNDowner]; A < 1; E = nspray; E + A;
	[FA0] = [E plus 0]; [FA1] = [E plus 1];
    "VHGND orbital mass loaded"
	[VHGNDmass0] = [FA0]; [VHGNDmass1] = [FA1];
	[FB0] = [VHGNDmass0]; [FB1] = [VHGNDmass1]; => FMul;
	[FB0] = [VHGNDmass0]; [FB1] = [VHGNDmass1]; => FMul;
	A = [VHGNDmoonmass]; ? A != 0 -> VHGND orbital moon constant;
	[FB0] = 0A01627EEh; [FB1] = 3E31FD9Fh; -> VHGND orbital mass scale;
    "VHGND orbital moon constant"
	[FB0] = 1735C01Dh; [FB1] = 3F28284Fh;
    "VHGND orbital mass scale"
	=> FMul; [VHGNDmass0] = [FA0]; [VHGNDmass1] = [FA1];
	A = [VHGNDphaseindex]; A < 1; E = nsporbray; E + A;
	[FA0] = [E plus 0]; [FA1] = [E plus 1];
	[VHGNDorbit0] = [FA0]; [VHGNDorbit1] = [FA1];
	[FB0] = [VHGNDorbit0]; [FB1] = [VHGNDorbit1]; => FMul;
	[FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1]; [FA0] = [VHGNDmass0]; [FA1] = [VHGNDmass1];
	=> FQuo; => FSqrt;
	[FB0] = [SUsec0]; [FB1] = [SUsec1]; => FMul; => FToIntNear;
	A = [FI]; A % 360; ? A >= 0 -> VHGND orbital phase ready; A + 360;
    "VHGND orbital phase ready"
	[VHGNDphase] = A;

	( surface() seeds the rotation-period stream from its original live
	  body expression, independent of global_surface_seed. )
	=> VHGND rotation seed;
	[FB0] = [VHGNDseedval0]; [FB1] = [VHGNDseedval1];
	=> GeoAdd4112ChopLive; A = [FI]; => SU fast srand;
	C = 50; => SU rfr; A = C; A + 1; A '* 10; [VHGNDperiod] = A;
	C = 25; => SU rfr; A = C; A '* 10; A + [VHGNDperiod]; [VHGNDperiod] = A;
	C = 250; => SU rfr; A = C; A + [VHGNDperiod]; A + 41; [VHGNDperiod] = A;
	[SUia] = A; => SU secs rot; A = [SUti]; A % 360;
	? A >= 0 -> VHGND rotation ready; A + 360;
    "VHGND rotation ready"
	[VHGNDrotation] = A;
	end;

"VHGND rotation seed"
	( Reset all factors to 1.0, then spell the source's separate planet and
	  moon seedval expressions in left-to-right x87 order. )
	A = 0; [VHGNDsf00] = A; [VHGNDsf10] = A; [VHGNDsf20] = A;
	[VHGNDsf30] = A; [VHGNDsf40] = A; [VHGNDsf50] = A;
	A = 3FF00000h; [VHGNDsf01] = A; [VHGNDsf11] = A; [VHGNDsf21] = A;
	[VHGNDsf31] = A; [VHGNDsf41] = A; [VHGNDsf51] = A;
	A = [VHGNDplanet]; A < 1; E = nspororient; E + A;
