	[VHGNDsf50] = [E plus 0]; [VHGNDsf51] = [E plus 1];
	E = nspowner; E + [VHGNDplanet]; A = [E]; ? A >= 0 -> VHGND rotation moon seed;
	( planet: 1e6*type*seed*tilt*ecc*orient, with the type-zero n variant )
	[VHGNDsf00] = 0; [VHGNDsf01] = 412E8480h;
	E = nsptype; E + [VHGNDplanet]; A = [E];
	? A != 0 -> VHGND rotation planet typed;
	[VHGNDsf01] = 413E8480h; [FI] = [VHGNDplanet]; => IntToF;
	[VHGNDsf10] = [FA0]; [VHGNDsf11] = [FA1]; -> VHGND rotation planet fields;
    "VHGND rotation planet typed"
	[FI] = A; => IntToF; [VHGNDsf10] = [FA0]; [VHGNDsf11] = [FA1];
    "VHGND rotation planet fields"
	A = [VHGNDplanet]; A < 1; E = nsporbseed; E + A;
	[VHGNDsf20] = [E plus 0]; [VHGNDsf21] = [E plus 1];
	E = nsporbtlt; E + A; [VHGNDsf30] = [E plus 0]; [VHGNDsf31] = [E plus 1];
	E = nsporbecc; E + A; [VHGNDsf40] = [E plus 0]; [VHGNDsf41] = [E plus 1];
	-> VHGND rotation seed calculate;
    "VHGND rotation moon seed"
	( moon: 1e6*nearstar_ray*type*orient, with the type-zero n variant )
	[VHGNDsf00] = 0; [VHGNDsf01] = 412E8480h;
	[FS0] = [nsstarray]; => FLoadF32;
	[VHGNDsf10] = [FA0]; [VHGNDsf11] = [FA1];
	E = nsptype; E + [VHGNDplanet]; A = [E]; ? A != 0 -> VHGND rotation moon typed;
	[VHGNDsf01] = 413E8480h; [FI] = [VHGNDplanet]; => IntToF;
	[VHGNDsf20] = [FA0]; [VHGNDsf21] = [FA1]; -> VHGND rotation seed calculate;
    "VHGND rotation moon typed"
	[FI] = A; => IntToF; [VHGNDsf20] = [FA0]; [VHGNDsf21] = [FA1];
"VHGND rotation seed calculate"
	{
	    DD 87 <dVHGNDsf00 mtp bytesperunit>	(fld qword [edi+VHGNDsf00*4])
	    DC 8F <dVHGNDsf10 mtp bytesperunit>	(fmul qword [edi+VHGNDsf10*4])
	    DC 8F <dVHGNDsf20 mtp bytesperunit>	(fmul qword [edi+VHGNDsf20*4])
	    DC 8F <dVHGNDsf30 mtp bytesperunit>	(fmul qword [edi+VHGNDsf30*4])
	    DC 8F <dVHGNDsf40 mtp bytesperunit>	(fmul qword [edi+VHGNDsf40*4])
	    DC 8F <dVHGNDsf50 mtp bytesperunit>	(fmul qword [edi+VHGNDsf50*4])
	    DD 9F <dVHGNDseedval0 mtp bytesperunit>	(fstp qword [edi+VHGNDseedval0*4])
	}
	end;

"VHGND orbit angle"
	( Input VHGNDvecindex; output VHGNDangle in radians.  This is the
	  planet_xyz p_riv branch, including a moon's parent-body mass. )
	E = nspowner; E + [VHGNDvecindex]; A = [E]; [VHGNDvecowner] = A;
	? A >= 0 -> VHGND orbit body mass;
	[FS0] = [nsstarray]; => FLoadF32; -> VHGND orbit radius mass;
    "VHGND orbit body mass"
	A < 1; E = nspray; E + A; [FA0] = [E plus 0]; [FA1] = [E plus 1];
    "VHGND orbit radius mass"
	[VHGNDmass0] = [FA0]; [VHGNDmass1] = [FA1];
	[FB0] = [VHGNDmass0]; [FB1] = [VHGNDmass1]; => FMul;
	[FB0] = [VHGNDmass0]; [FB1] = [VHGNDmass1]; => FMul;
	A = [VHGNDvecowner]; ? A >= 0 -> VHGND orbit body constant;
	[FB0] = 0A01627EEh; [FB1] = 3E31FD9Fh; -> VHGND orbit mass ready;
    "VHGND orbit body constant"
	[FB0] = 1735C01Dh; [FB1] = 3F28284Fh;
    "VHGND orbit mass ready"
	=> FMul; [VHGNDmass0] = [FA0]; [VHGNDmass1] = [FA1];
	A = [VHGNDvecindex]; A < 1; E = nsporbray; E + A;
	[FA0] = [E plus 0]; [FA1] = [E plus 1];
	[VHGNDorbit0] = [FA0]; [VHGNDorbit1] = [FA1];
	[FB0] = [VHGNDorbit0]; [FB1] = [VHGNDorbit1]; => FMul;
	[FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHGNDmass0]; [FA1] = [VHGNDmass1]; => FQuo; => FSqrt;
	[FB0] = [SUsec0]; [FB1] = [SUsec1]; => FMul;
	[FB0] = 54442D18h; [FB1] = 400921FBh; => FMul;
	[FB0] = 0; [FB1] = 40668000h; => FQuo;
	[VHGNDangle0] = [FA0]; [VHGNDangle1] = [FA1];
	end;

"VHGND body vector"
	( Input VHGNDvecindex; output x/y/z relative to this body's primary.
	  This is planet_xyz() through its orientation rotation.  The source Y
	  component depends only on orbital radius and orbital tilt. )
	=> VHGND orbit angle;
	[FA0] = [VHGNDangle0]; [FA1] = [VHGNDangle1]; => FSin;
	[VHGNDsin0] = [FA0]; [VHGNDsin1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHGNDangle0]; [FA1] = [VHGNDangle1]; => FCos;
	[VHGNDcos0] = [FA0]; [VHGNDcos1] = [FA1];
	A = [VHGNDvecindex]; A < 1; E = nsporbtlt; E + A;
	[FA0] = [E plus 0]; [FA1] = [E plus 1];
	[FB0] = A2529D39h; [FB1] = 3F91DF46h; => FMul;
	[VHGNDct0] = [FA0]; [VHGNDct1] = [FA1]; => FSin;
	A = [VHGNDvecindex]; A < 1; E = nsporbray; E + A;
	[FB0] = [E plus 0]; [FB1] = [E plus 1]; => FMul;
	[VHGNDvecy0] = [FA0]; [VHGNDvecy1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHGNDct0]; [FA1] = [VHGNDct1]; => FCos;
	[VHGNDct0] = [FA0]; [VHGNDct1] = [FA1];
	E = nsporbray; E + A; [FA0] = [E plus 0]; [FA1] = [E plus 1];
	[FB0] = [VHGNDsin0]; [FB1] = [VHGNDsin1]; => FMul;
	[FB0] = [VHGNDct0]; [FB1] = [VHGNDct1]; => FMul; => FNeg;
	[VHGNDxx0] = [FA0]; [VHGNDxx1] = [FA1];
	E = nsporbray; E + A; [FA0] = [E plus 0]; [FA1] = [E plus 1];
	[FB0] = [VHGNDcos0]; [FB1] = [VHGNDcos1]; => FMul;
	[FB0] = [VHGNDct0]; [FB1] = [VHGNDct1]; => FMul;
	E = nsporbecc; E + A; [FB0] = [E plus 0]; [FB1] = [E plus 1]; => FMul;
	[VHGNDzz0] = [FA0]; [VHGNDzz1] = [FA1];
	E = nspororient; E + A; [FA0] = [E plus 0]; [FA1] = [E plus 1]; => FSin;
	[VHGNDso0] = [FA0]; [VHGNDso1] = [FA1];
	E = nspororient; E + A; [FA0] = [E plus 0]; [FA1] = [E plus 1]; => FCos;
	[VHGNDco0] = [FA0]; [VHGNDco1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHGNDxx0]; [FA1] = [VHGNDxx1];
	[FB0] = [VHGNDco0]; [FB1] = [VHGNDco1]; => FMul;
	[VHGNDvecx0] = [FA0]; [VHGNDvecx1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHGNDzz0]; [FA1] = [VHGNDzz1];
	[FB0] = [VHGNDso0]; [FB1] = [VHGNDso1]; => FMul;
	[FB0] = [VHGNDvecx0]; [FB1] = [VHGNDvecx1]; => FAdd;
	[VHGNDvecx0] = [FA0]; [VHGNDvecx1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHGNDzz0]; [FA1] = [VHGNDzz1];
	[FB0] = [VHGNDco0]; [FB1] = [VHGNDco1]; => FMul;
	[VHGNDvecz0] = [FA0]; [VHGNDvecz1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHGNDxx0]; [FA1] = [VHGNDxx1];
	[FB0] = [VHGNDso0]; [FB1] = [VHGNDso1]; => FMul;
	[FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHGNDvecz0]; [FA1] = [VHGNDvecz1]; => FSub;
	[VHGNDvecz0] = [FA0]; [VHGNDvecz1] = [FA1];
	end;

"VHGND absolute body vector"
	( planet_xyz() result relative to the primary star.  A moon first gets
	  its own vector and then its owner's moonorigin() vector. )
	=> VHGND body vector;
	[VHGNDownx0] = [VHGNDvecx0]; [VHGNDownx1] = [VHGNDvecx1];
	[VHGNDowny0] = [VHGNDvecy0]; [VHGNDowny1] = [VHGNDvecy1];
	[VHGNDownz0] = [VHGNDvecz0]; [VHGNDownz1] = [VHGNDvecz1];
	E = nspowner; E + [VHGNDvecindex]; A = [E]; ? A < 0 -> VHGND absolute body done;
	[VHGNDvecindex] = A; => VHGND body vector;
	[FA0] = [VHGNDownx0]; [FA1] = [VHGNDownx1];
	[FB0] = [VHGNDvecx0]; [FB1] = [VHGNDvecx1]; => FAdd;
	[VHGNDownx0] = [FA0]; [VHGNDownx1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHGNDowny0]; [FA1] = [VHGNDowny1];
	[FB0] = [VHGNDvecy0]; [FB1] = [VHGNDvecy1]; => FAdd;
	[VHGNDowny0] = [FA0]; [VHGNDowny1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHGNDownz0]; [FA1] = [VHGNDownz1];
	[FB0] = [VHGNDvecz0]; [FB1] = [VHGNDvecz1]; => FAdd;
	[VHGNDownz0] = [FA0]; [VHGNDownz1] = [FA1];
    "VHGND absolute body done"
	[VHGNDvecx0] = [VHGNDownx0]; [VHGNDvecx1] = [VHGNDownx1];
	[VHGNDvecy0] = [VHGNDowny0]; [VHGNDvecy1] = [VHGNDowny1];
	[VHGNDvecz0] = [VHGNDownz0]; [VHGNDvecz1] = [VHGNDownz1];
	end;

"VHGND exact viewpoint"
	( Analytic equivalent of planet_viewpoint's 360-point nearest search. )
	[VHGNDvecindex] = [VHGNDplanet]; => VHGND body vector;
	[VHGNDownx0] = [VHGNDvecx0]; [VHGNDownx1] = [VHGNDvecx1];
	[VHGNDownz0] = [VHGNDvecz0]; [VHGNDownz1] = [VHGNDvecz1];
	E = nspowner; E + [VHGNDplanet]; A = [E]; ? A < 0 -> VHGND viewpoint vector ready;
	E = nsptype; E + A; C = [E]; ? C = 10 -> VHGND viewpoint vector ready;
	[VHGNDvecindex] = A; => VHGND body vector;
	[FA0] = [VHGNDownx0]; [FA1] = [VHGNDownx1];
	[FB0] = [VHGNDvecx0]; [FB1] = [VHGNDvecx1]; => FAdd;
	[VHGNDownx0] = [FA0]; [VHGNDownx1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHGNDownz0]; [FA1] = [VHGNDownz1];
	[FB0] = [VHGNDvecz0]; [FB1] = [VHGNDvecz1]; => FAdd;
	[VHGNDownz0] = [FA0]; [VHGNDownz1] = [FA1];
    "VHGND viewpoint vector ready"
	[FA0] = [VHGNDownz0]; [FA1] = [VHGNDownz1]; => FNeg;
	[FB0] = [VHGNDownx0]; [FB1] = [VHGNDownx1];
	A = [FB0]; [VHGNDxx0] = A; A = [FB1]; [VHGNDxx1] = A;
	[FA0] = [VHGNDxx0]; [FA1] = [VHGNDxx1]; => FNeg;
	[FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHGNDownz0]; [FA1] = [VHGNDownz1]; => FNeg; => FAtan2;
	[FB0] = 0; [FB1] = 40668000h; => FMul;
	[FB0] = 54442D18h; [FB1] = 400921FBh; => FQuo; => FToIntNear;
	A = [FI]; A % 360; ? A >= 0 -> VHGND viewpoint normalized; A + 360;
    "VHGND viewpoint normalized"
	[VHGNDviewpoint] = A;
	[FI] = A;
	end;

"VHGND terminator"
	=> VHG fpu clean;
	=> VHGND exact viewpoint;
	A = 89; A - [FI]; A + [VHGNDrotation]; A % 360;
	? A >= 0 -> VHGND plwp ready; A + 360;
    "VHGND plwp ready"
	[VHGNDplwp] = A; A + 35; ? A < 360 -> VHGND term start ready; A - 360;
    "VHGND term start ready"
	[VHGNDtermstart] = A; A + 130; ? A < 360 -> VHGND term end ready; A - 360;
    "VHGND term end ready"
	[VHGNDtermend] = A; [GRSKnightzone] = 0;
	A = [VHGNDtermstart]; ? A <= [VHGNDtermend] -> VHGND night straight;
	A = [VHGNDlon]; ? A >= [VHGNDtermstart] -> VHGND night set;
	? A < [VHGNDtermend] -> VHGND night set; -> VHGND night ready;
    "VHGND night straight"
	A = [VHGNDlon]; ? A < [VHGNDtermstart] -> VHGND night ready;
	? A >= [VHGNDtermend] -> VHGND night ready;
    "VHGND night set"
	[GRSKnightzone] = 1;
    "VHGND night ready"
	A = [VHGNDtermstart]; A - [VHGNDlon]; A % 360; ? A >= 0 -> VHGND start normalized; A + 360;
    "VHGND start normalized"
	? A <= 180 -> VHGND start distance ready; C = 360; C - A; A = C;
    "VHGND start distance ready"
	[VHGNDcrep] = A;
	A = [VHGNDtermend]; A - [VHGNDlon]; A % 360; ? A >= 0 -> VHGND end normalized; A + 360;
    "VHGND end normalized"
	? A <= 180 -> VHGND end distance ready; C = 360; C - A; A = C;
    "VHGND end distance ready"
	? A >= [VHGNDcrep] -> VHGND exposure ready;
	[VHGNDcrep] = A; A = 0; A - 1; [VHGNDsunxf] = A;
    "VHGND exposure ready"
	[FI] = [VHGNDcrep]; => IntToF;
	[FB0] = 27BB2FECh; [FB1] = 3FE90B0Fh; => FMul; => F32Narrow; => FStoreF32;
	[GRSKexposure] = [FS0];
	end;
    "VHGND sky inputs ready"
	=> VHGND secondary sun setup;

	A = nw; A + RSBG; [VHGNDskyfill] = A; [VHGNDskycount] = 64864;
	C = [GRSKbrightness]; C & 255;
    "VHGND fill sky loop"
	A = [VHGNDskyfill]; [A] = C; [VHGNDskyfill]+;
	[VHGNDskycount]-; A = [VHGNDskycount]; ? A != 0 -> VHGND fill sky loop;
	=> SU ledger reset; => GRSK create; => GRSK horizon;

	( planetary_main fades to the complete surface_palette.  The GUI uses
	  the same uploaded palette through its indexed-to-RGB presenter. )
	A = srfpal6; D = pal6; [VHGNDpalcount] = 768;
    "VHGND sky palette copy"
	C = [A]; [D] = C; A+; D+; [VHGNDpalcount]-;
	C = [VHGNDpalcount]; ? C != 0 -> VHGND sky palette copy;
	[PUn] = 256; => PAL upload;
	[VHGNDbgcacheok] = 0;
	end;

"VHGND guard band"
	( TDPOLYGS.H clips the rendered world to x=5..311 and y=10..190.
	  The DOS page outside that rectangle starts cleared and remains the
	  dark frame seen around the original planetary view.  Normalize it
	  after the wrapping panorama mapper so its edge cannot become ragged. )
	[VHGNDptr] = 0;
    "VHGND guard top"
	A = nw; A + RADPT; A + [VHGNDptr]; [A] = 0;
	[VHGNDptr]+; A = [VHGNDptr]; ? A < 3200 -> VHGND guard top;
	[VHGNDptr] = 61120;
    "VHGND guard bottom"
	A = nw; A + RADPT; A + [VHGNDptr]; [A] = 0;
	[VHGNDptr]+; A = [VHGNDptr]; ? A < NPIX -> VHGND guard bottom;
	[VHGNDtmp] = 10;
    "VHGND guard sides"
	A = [VHGNDtmp]; A '* 320; A + RADPT; A + nw; D = A;
	[D plus 0] = 0; [D plus 1] = 0; [D plus 2] = 0; [D plus 3] = 0; [D plus 4] = 0;
	[D plus 312] = 0; [D plus 313] = 0; [D plus 314] = 0; [D plus 315] = 0;
	[D plus 316] = 0; [D plus 317] = 0; [D plus 318] = 0; [D plus 319] = 0;
	[VHGNDtmp]+; A = [VHGNDtmp]; ? A <= 190 -> VHGND guard sides;
	end;

"VHGND background"
	( NOCTIS-1.CPP:3939-3942, with this port's yaw in beta and pitch in
	  alpha.  The original offset-map panorama is regenerated when the view
	  changes, then cached as a complete 320x200 indexed sky.  Steady walking
	  restores that cache at the same cost as the former flat page clear. )
	( Immediately before background(), NIV+ assigns alfa=user_alfa (pitch)
	  and beta=user_beta (yaw).  Preserve its full 360*pitch panorama stride
	  before the 16-bit source-cursor wrap. )
	( Canonicalize equivalent negative headings before forming both panorama
	  cursors.  The original 16-bit mapper can otherwise walk through the
	  736-byte allocation tail at the -180/180 representation seam, painting
	  its zero-filled host padding as a moving black five-pixel column. )
	A = [VHGNDbeta]; ? A >= 0 -> VHGND background angle ready; A + 360;
    "VHGND background angle ready"
	[VHGNDbgangle] = A;
	A = [VHGNDalpha]; A + 51; A '* 360;
	C = [VHGNDbgangle]; C % 360; A - C; [VHGNDbgstart] = A;
	( The two integer-multiple remainder terms in the source are zero.  Its
	  remaining shift is -4 - 639 - trunc(yaw/72)*320. )
	A = [VHGNDbgangle]; A '/ 72; A '* 320; C = A;
	A = 0; A - 643; A - C;
	[VHGNDbgshift] = A;
	[BGstart] = [VHGNDbgstart]; [BGshift] = [VHGNDbgshift];
	[BGbytes] = OMBYTES; [BGtabreg] = RGOFF; [BGsrcreg] = RGSBG;
	[BGdstreg] = RGADP;
	A = [VHGNDflashactive]; ? A != 0 -> VHGND background lightning;
	A = [VHGNDbgcacheok]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND background regenerate;
	A = [VHGNDalpha]; ? A != [VHGNDbgcachealpha] -> VHGND background regenerate;
	A = [VHGNDbeta]; ? A != [VHGNDbgcachebeta] -> VHGND background regenerate;
	=> VHGND background cache restore;
	end;
    "VHGND background regenerate"
	( The source enters planetary_main with adapted cleared to index zero.
	  background() paints the 306x180 view but deliberately leaves its guard
	  band alone.  Filling the whole page with sky_brightness turned that
	  guard band white on bright surface palettes. )
	[SPpreg] = RGADP; [SPpn] = NPIX; [SPval] = 0; => SP fill page;
	=> VHGND background direct;
	=> VHGND guard band;
	=> VHGND background cache save;
	[VHGNDbgcachealpha] = [VHGNDalpha]; [VHGNDbgcachebeta] = [VHGNDbeta];
	[VHGNDbgcacheok] = 1;
	end;
    "VHGND background lightning"
	( NOCTIS-1.CPP temporarily maps each source sky entry 40000..1 to
	  63-value, draws the panorama, then applies the same involution again. )
	[SPpreg] = RGADP; [SPpn] = NPIX; [SPval] = 0; => SP fill page;
	=> VHGND background lightning invert;
	=> VHGND background direct;
	=> VHGND background lightning invert;
	=> VHGND guard band;
	end;

"VHGND background lightning invert"
	[VHGNDflashskyp] = 40000;
    "VHGND background lightning invert one"
	A = nw; A + RSBG; A + [VHGNDflashskyp]; D = A;
	A = [D]; C = 63; C - A; [D] = C;
	[VHGNDflashskyp]-; A = [VHGNDflashskyp];
	? A != 0 -> VHGND background lightning invert one;
	end;

"VHGND background cache save"
	[VHGNDbgcachep] = VHGNDskycache; A = nw; A + RADPT; [VHGNDbgcacheq] = A;
	[VHGNDbgcachecount] = NPIX;
	[VHGNDbgcachefrom] = [VHGNDbgcacheq]; [VHGNDbgcacheto] = [VHGNDbgcachep];
	=> VHGND background cache copy native;
	end;

"VHGND background cache restore"
	[VHGNDbgcachep] = VHGNDskycache; A = nw; A + RADPT; [VHGNDbgcacheq] = A;
	[VHGNDbgcachecount] = NPIX;
	[VHGNDbgcachefrom] = [VHGNDbgcachep]; [VHGNDbgcacheto] = [VHGNDbgcacheq];
	=> VHGND background cache copy native;
	end;

"VHGND background cache copy native"
	( Fixed, non-overlapping Lino-unit ranges.  Keep EDI on the workspace and
	  walk the resolved source/destination through ESI/EDX.  The previous native
	  attempt borrowed EDI and then used it to fetch the count, so that read came
	  from the destination page and damaged the local sun flare. )
	{
	    56
	    8B B7 <dVHGNDbgcachefrom mtp bytesperunit>
	    8D 34 B7
	    8B 97 <dVHGNDbgcacheto mtp bytesperunit>
	    8D 14 97
	    8B 8F <dVHGNDbgcachecount mtp bytesperunit>
	    85 C9
	    74 0D
	    8B 06
	    89 02
	    83 C6 04
	    83 C2 04
	    49
	    75 F3
	    5E
	}
	A = [VHGNDbgcachep]; A + NPIX; [VHGNDbgcachep] = A;
	A = [VHGNDbgcacheq]; A + NPIX; [VHGNDbgcacheq] = A;
	[VHGNDbgcachecount] = 0;
	end;

"VHGND background direct"
	( Direct specialization of SP background for the fixed Noctis buffers.
	  Region offsets cancel against their -4 segment origins exactly as in
	  spbg; every destination row and source cursor still wraps at 16 bits. )
	A = [BGshift]; A + 4; A & 65535; [BGdx] = A;
	A = [BGstart]; A + 4; A & 65535; [BGbp] = A;
	[BGsi] = 0; A = OMBYTES; A > 1; [BGcxr] = A;
    "VHGND bg record"
	A = nw; A + RNOFF; A + [BGsi];
	C = [A]; C & 255; D = [A plus 1]; D & 255; D < 8; C + D; [BGw] = C;
	A = [BGw]; ? A '>= 64000 -> VHGND bg skip;
	A + [BGdx]; A & 65535; [BGdi] = A;
	A = [BGbp]; A & 65535; A + SSBG; A + nw; C = [A]; C & 255; [BGpx] = C;
	[BGi] = 0;
    "VHGND bg row"
	A = [BGi]; A '* 320; A + [BGdi]; A & 65535; [VHGNDbgrowbase] = A;
	? A '<= 65531 -> VHGND bg row contiguous;
	[VHGNDbgcol] = 0;
    "VHGND bg row wrapped"
	A = [VHGNDbgrowbase]; A + [VHGNDbgcol]; A & 65535; A + SADPT; A + nw;
	C = [BGpx]; [A] = C; [VHGNDbgcol]+;
	A = [VHGNDbgcol]; ? A < 5 -> VHGND bg row wrapped;
	-> VHGND bg row done;
    "VHGND bg row contiguous"
	D = nw; D + SADPT; D + [VHGNDbgrowbase]; C = [BGpx];
	[D plus 0] = C; [D plus 1] = C; [D plus 2] = C; [D plus 3] = C; [D plus 4] = C;
    "VHGND bg row done"
	[BGi]+; A = [BGi]; ? A < 5 -> VHGND bg row;
    "VHGND bg consume"
	A = [BGbp]; A + 1; A & 65535; [BGbp] = A;
	-> VHGND bg next;
    "VHGND bg skip"
	A = [BGw]; A - 64000; A + [BGbp]; A & 65535; [BGbp] = A;
    "VHGND bg next"
	A = [BGsi]; A + 2; [BGsi] = A;
	[BGcxr]-; A = [BGcxr]; ? A != 0 -> VHGND bg record;
	end;

"VHGND eye height"
	( Exact HPOINT float path.  Interpolating byte heights as integers can
	  lose almost two full height units (4096 world units), enough to put the
	  600-unit eye below the triangles.  Preserve both source float stores.
	  Bound the sampled tile before addressing the 200x200 height map.  Motion
	  is normally clamped by the caller, but height is sampled before that
	  clamp while stepping and jumping. )
	A = [VHGNDcamx]; A > 14; [VHGNDtilex] = A;
	A = [VHGNDcamz]; A > 14; [VHGNDtilez] = A;
	A = [VHGNDtilex]; ? A >= 0 -> VHGND height x low; [VHGNDtilex] = 0;
    "VHGND height x low"
	A = [VHGNDtilex]; ? A <= 198 -> VHGND height x ready; [VHGNDtilex] = 198;
    "VHGND height x ready"
	A = [VHGNDtilez]; ? A >= 0 -> VHGND height z low; [VHGNDtilez] = 0;
    "VHGND height z low"
	A = [VHGNDtilez]; ? A <= 198 -> VHGND height z ready; [VHGNDtilez] = 198;
    "VHGND height z ready"
	A = [VHGNDcamx]; A & 16383; [VHGNDfx] = A;
	A = [VHGNDcamz]; A & 16383; [VHGNDfz] = A;
	A = [VHGNDtilez]; A '* VHGNDMAP; A + [VHGNDtilex]; [VHGNDh1] = A;
	( Keep the live HPOINT reads self-contained instead of routing collision
	  sampling through MEM get byte's shared scratch pointer. )
	A = nw; A + RPSM; A + [VHGNDh1]; A = [A]; A & 255; [VHGNDs1] = A;
	A = nw; A + RPSM; A + [VHGNDh1]; A + 1; A = [A]; A & 255; [VHGNDs2] = A;
	A = nw; A + RPSM; A + [VHGNDh1]; A + 201; A = [A]; A & 255; [VHGNDs3] = A;
	A = nw; A + RPSM; A + [VHGNDh1]; A + 200; A = [A]; A & 255; [VHGNDs4] = A;
	A = [VHGNDs1]; A < VHGNDHS; C = A; A = 0; A - C; [VHGNDh1i] = A;
	A = [VHGNDs2]; A < VHGNDHS; C = A; A = 0; A - C; [VHGNDh2i] = A;
	A = [VHGNDs3]; A < VHGNDHS; C = A; A = 0; A - C; [VHGNDh3i] = A;
	A = [VHGNDs4]; A < VHGNDHS; C = A; A = 0; A - C; [VHGNDh4i] = A;
	A = [VHGNDfx]; A + [VHGNDfz]; ? A '< VHGNDTS -> VHGND height upper;
	( lower triangle: h3+(h4-h3)*(1-fx/TS), then +(h2-h3)*(1-fz/TS) )
	A = VHGNDTS; A - [VHGNDfx]; [FI] = A; => IntToF;
	[FB0] = VHGNDQIDLO; [FB1] = VHGNDQIDHI; => FMul; [FT0] = [FA0]; [FT1] = [FA1];
	[FI] = [VHGNDh3i]; => IntToF; => VHGND fb fa;
	[FI] = [VHGNDh4i]; => IntToF; => FSub; [FB0] = [FT0]; [FB1] = [FT1]; => FMul;
	[FT0] = [FA0]; [FT1] = [FA1]; [FI] = [VHGNDh3i]; => IntToF;
	[FB0] = [FT0]; [FB1] = [FT1]; => FAdd; => F32Narrow; => FStoreF32; [VHGNDpy] = [FS0];
	A = VHGNDTS; A - [VHGNDfz]; [FI] = A; => IntToF;
	[FB0] = VHGNDQIDLO; [FB1] = VHGNDQIDHI; => FMul; [FT0] = [FA0]; [FT1] = [FA1];
	[FI] = [VHGNDh3i]; => IntToF; => VHGND fb fa;
	[FI] = [VHGNDh2i]; => IntToF; => FSub; [FB0] = [FT0]; [FB1] = [FT1]; => FMul;
	[FT0] = [FA0]; [FT1] = [FA1]; [FS0] = [VHGNDpy]; => FLoadF32;
	[FB0] = [FT0]; [FB1] = [FT1]; => FAdd; => F32Narrow; => FStoreF32; [VHGNDpy] = [FS0];
	-> VHGND height finish;
    "VHGND height upper"
	( upper triangle: h1+(h2-h1)*fx/TS, then +(h4-h1)*fz/TS )
	[FI] = [VHGNDfx]; => IntToF; [FB0] = VHGNDQIDLO; [FB1] = VHGNDQIDHI;
	=> FMul; [FT0] = [FA0]; [FT1] = [FA1];
	[FI] = [VHGNDh1i]; => IntToF; => VHGND fb fa;
	[FI] = [VHGNDh2i]; => IntToF; => FSub; [FB0] = [FT0]; [FB1] = [FT1]; => FMul;
	[FT0] = [FA0]; [FT1] = [FA1]; [FI] = [VHGNDh1i]; => IntToF;
	[FB0] = [FT0]; [FB1] = [FT1]; => FAdd; => F32Narrow; => FStoreF32; [VHGNDpy] = [FS0];
	[FI] = [VHGNDfz]; => IntToF; [FB0] = VHGNDQIDLO; [FB1] = VHGNDQIDHI;
	=> FMul; [FT0] = [FA0]; [FT1] = [FA1];
	[FI] = [VHGNDh1i]; => IntToF; => VHGND fb fa;
	[FI] = [VHGNDh4i]; => IntToF; => FSub; [FB0] = [FT0]; [FB1] = [FT1]; => FMul;
	[FT0] = [FA0]; [FT1] = [FA1]; [FS0] = [VHGNDpy]; => FLoadF32;
	[FB0] = [FT0]; [FB1] = [FT1]; => FAdd; => F32Narrow; => FStoreF32; [VHGNDpy] = [FS0];
    "VHGND height finish"
	[FS0] = [VHGNDpy]; => FLoadF32; => FToIntChop;
	A = [FI]; A - 600; [VHGNDheight] = A;
	end;

"VHGND fb fa"
	[FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1];
	end;

"VHGND render"
	( Sky first, then NIV+'s fully textured unit fragments through depth 64,
	  traversed in the source quadrant painter order.  The source Manhattan
	  and facing gates reject only fragments and triangles NIV+ would not draw. )
	A = [VHGNDdosim]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND render tick ready; [VHGNDtick]+;
    "VHGND render tick ready"
	=> VHGND wave impact tick;
	=> VHGND weather lightning begin;
	[VHGNDruindrawn] = 0; => VHGND background;
	=> PGF constants;
	[VHGNDviewx] = [VHGNDcamx]; [VHGNDviewz] = [VHGNDcamz];
	A = [VHGNDcamx]; A > 14; [VHGNDcamtx] = A;
	A = [VHGNDcamz]; A > 14; [VHGNDcamtz] = A;
	[PGFi] = FSUNEG; [PGFt] = 1120403456; => PGF setf32; ( 100.0f )
	[VHVcamxi] = [VHGNDcamx]; [VHVcamyi] = [VHGNDcamy]; [VHVcamzi] = [VHGNDcamz];
	[VHValpha] = [VHGNDalpha]; [VHVbeta] = [VHGNDbeta]; => VH set view;
	=> VHGND tree wind;
	( NIV+ installs the player's angles but a zero camera before whitesun:
	  the local solar vector is already relative to the observer. )
	[VHVcamxi] = 0; [VHVcamyi] = 0; [VHVcamzi] = 0;
	[VHValpha] = [VHGNDalpha]; [VHVbeta] = [VHGNDbeta]; => VH set view;
	=> VHGND local sun;
	( Terrain and all world objects use the walking camera again. )
	[VHVcamxi] = [VHGNDcamx]; [VHVcamyi] = [VHGNDcamy]; [VHVcamzi] = [VHGNDcamz];
	[VHValpha] = [VHGNDalpha]; [VHVbeta] = [VHGNDbeta]; => VH set view;
	( Source fragment() maps p_background across every ground triangle. )
	[FI] = [VHGNDtscale]; => IntToF;
	[FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1]; [FI] = 256; => IntToF; => FMul;
	[PGFi] = FSTX; => PGF sa; [PGFi] = FSTY; => PGF sa;
	[PGtexf] = 5; [SPsrc] = 1; [SPflar] = 0; [SPcull] = 0; [SPhalf] = 0;
	A = [GRiptype]; ? A != 3 -> VHGND water render done;
	A = [VHGNDsctype]; ? A = 1 -> VHGND water render;
	? A != 4 -> VHGND water render done;
    "VHGND water render"
	=> VHGND water;
    "VHGND water render done"
	=> VHGND terrain cache frame;
	=> VHGND object view setup;
	[VHGNDanimorphs] = 0; [VHGNDgroundbirds] = 0;
	=> VHGND traverse faithful;
    "VHGND render terrain done"
	( The faithful unit mesh already visits every ruinschart tile in source
	  painter order.  The old second ruins pass existed only to patch holes
	  left by the removed coarse mesh and would now redraw terrain late. )
	=> VHGND sun flares;
	A = [GRiptype]; ? A != 2 -> VHGND render weather ready;
	=> VHGND dense atmosphere; -> VHGND render finish;
    "VHGND render weather ready"
	=> VHGND render weather;
    "VHGND render finish"
	end;

"VHGND terrain cache frame"
	( Reuse the exact projected ground grid whenever every camera input is
	  bit-identical.  Simulation changes waves, fauna and weather, but none of
	  those mutate p_surfacemap; water reflections already take the generic
	  mirror path and never consume this ordinary-terrain cache. )
	A = [VHGNDvccacheok]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND terrain cache invalidate;
	A = [VHGNDcamx]; ? A != [VHGNDvccamx] -> VHGND terrain cache invalidate;
	A = [VHGNDcamy]; ? A != [VHGNDvccamy] -> VHGND terrain cache invalidate;
	A = [VHGNDcamz]; ? A != [VHGNDvccamz] -> VHGND terrain cache invalidate;
	A = [VHGNDalpha]; ? A != [VHGNDvcalpha] -> VHGND terrain cache invalidate;
	A = [VHGNDbeta]; ? A != [VHGNDvcbeta] -> VHGND terrain cache invalidate;
	end;
    "VHGND terrain cache invalidate"
	[VHGNDvccamx] = [VHGNDcamx]; [VHGNDvccamy] = [VHGNDcamy];
	[VHGNDvccamz] = [VHGNDcamz]; [VHGNDvcalpha] = [VHGNDalpha];
	[VHGNDvcbeta] = [VHGNDbeta]; [VHGNDvccacheok] = 1;
	( A generation stamp makes invalidation constant-time. The wrap clear is
	  reached only after 2^32 actual cache invalidations. )
	[VHGNDvcgen]+; A = [VHGNDvcgen]; ? A != 0 -> VHGND terrain cache frame ready;
	[VHGNDptr] = 0;
    "VHGND terrain cache clear"
	A = VHGNDvcstamp; A + [VHGNDptr]; [A] = 0;
	[VHGNDptr]+; A = [VHGNDptr]; ? A < 40000 -> VHGND terrain cache clear;
	[VHGNDvcgen] = 1;
    "VHGND terrain cache frame ready"
	end;

"VHGND terrain vertex index"
	( The faithful unit tile's two triangles address [0,1,200] and
	  [1,201,200] relative to h1. )
	A = [VHGNDvi]; ? A = 2 -> VHGND terrain vertex index bottom;
	C = [VHGNDvctri]; ? C = 0 -> VHGND terrain vertex index first;
	A = [VHGNDvi]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND terrain vertex index second top;
	A = [VHGNDh1]; A + 201; [VHGNDvcindex] = A; end;
    "VHGND terrain vertex index second top"
	A = [VHGNDh1]; A + 1; [VHGNDvcindex] = A; end;
    "VHGND terrain vertex index first"
	A = [VHGNDh1]; A + [VHGNDvi]; [VHGNDvcindex] = A; end;
    "VHGND terrain vertex index bottom"
	A = [VHGNDh1]; A + 200; [VHGNDvcindex] = A; end;

"VHGND terrain vertex ensure"
	=> VHGND terrain vertex index;
	A = VHGNDvcstamp; A + [VHGNDvcindex]; C = [A];
	A = [VHGNDvcgen]; ? A = C -> VHGND terrain vertex ensure done;

	( Rotate only the selected input slot.  PJ rotate selected executes the
	  same narrowed x87 sequence as polymap, so cached carriers are bit copies
	  of the ordinary path rather than a second approximation. )
	[PJmode] = 1; [PJvr] = [VHGNDvi];
	A = [VHGNDvi]; A + 1; [PJnrv] = A;
	=> PJ rotate selected;

	A = [VHGNDvi]; A + FSRXF; A + A; A + fw; D = A;
	A = VHGNDvcrx0; A + [VHGNDvcindex]; C = [D]; [A] = C;
	A = VHGNDvcrx1; A + [VHGNDvcindex]; C = [D plus 1]; [A] = C;
	A = [VHGNDvi]; A + FSRYF; A + A; A + fw; D = A;
	A = VHGNDvcry0; A + [VHGNDvcindex]; C = [D]; [A] = C;
	A = VHGNDvcry1; A + [VHGNDvcindex]; C = [D plus 1]; [A] = C;
	A = [VHGNDvi]; A + FSRZF; A + A; A + fw; D = A;
	A = VHGNDvcrz0; A + [VHGNDvcindex]; C = [D]; [A] = C;
	A = VHGNDvcrz1; A + [VHGNDvcindex]; C = [D plus 1]; [A] = C;

	A = rwf; A + [VHGNDvi]; C = [A];
	A = VHGNDvcvisible; A + [VHGNDvcindex]; [A] = C;
	? C = 0 -> VHGND terrain vertex stamped;

	( Project the one visible rotated point with the mapper's exact routine.
	  Slot zero is scratch here; all triangle slots are restored from cache
	  after the three lookups finish. )
	A = [VHGNDvi]; A + FSRXF; A + A; A + fw; D = A;
	A = FSUX; A + A; A + fw; C = [D]; [A] = C; C = [D plus 1]; [A plus 1] = C;
	A = [VHGNDvi]; A + FSRYF; A + A; A + fw; D = A;
	A = FSUY; A + A; A + fw; C = [D]; [A] = C; C = [D plus 1]; [A plus 1] = C;
	A = [VHGNDvi]; A + FSRZF; A + A; A + fw; D = A;
	A = FSUZ; A + A; A + fw; C = [D]; [A] = C; C = [D plus 1]; [A plus 1] = C;
	[PJvr2] = 1; => PJ projectmap;
	A = mp; C = [A]; A = VHGNDvcpx; A + [VHGNDvcindex]; [A] = C;
	A = mp; C = [A plus 1]; A = VHGNDvcpy; A + [VHGNDvcindex]; [A] = C;

    "VHGND terrain vertex stamped"
	A = VHGNDvcstamp; A + [VHGNDvcindex]; C = [VHGNDvcgen]; [A] = C;
    "VHGND terrain vertex ensure done"
	end;

"VHGND terrain vertex load"
	=> VHGND terrain vertex index;
	A = [VHGNDvi]; A + FSRXF; A + A; A + fw; D = A;
	A = VHGNDvcrx0; A + [VHGNDvcindex]; C = [A]; [D] = C;
	A = VHGNDvcrx1; A + [VHGNDvcindex]; C = [A]; [D plus 1] = C;
	A = [VHGNDvi]; A + FSRYF; A + A; A + fw; D = A;
	A = VHGNDvcry0; A + [VHGNDvcindex]; C = [A]; [D] = C;
	A = VHGNDvcry1; A + [VHGNDvcindex]; C = [A]; [D plus 1] = C;
	A = [VHGNDvi]; A + FSRZF; A + A; A + fw; D = A;
	A = VHGNDvcrz0; A + [VHGNDvcindex]; C = [A]; [D] = C;
	A = VHGNDvcrz1; A + [VHGNDvcindex]; C = [A]; [D plus 1] = C;
	A = [VHGNDvi]; A + A; A + mp; D = A;
	A = VHGNDvcpx; A + [VHGNDvcindex]; C = [A]; [D] = C;
	A = VHGNDvcpy; A + [VHGNDvcindex]; C = [A]; [D plus 1] = C;
	A = rwf; A + [VHGNDvi]; [A] = 1;
	end;

"VHGND terrain cached bounds"
	( This is projectmap's four-entry min/max walk, including its seed values
	  and comparison order.  Slot three is the closing duplicate below. )
	A = mp; [VHGNDmpbase] = A;
	{
	    56
	    8B B7 <dVHGNDmpbase mtp bytesperunit>
	    8D 34 B7
	    C7 87 <dPJminx mtp bytesperunit> 37 01 00 00
	    C7 87 <dPJmaxx mtp bytesperunit> 05 00 00 00
	    C7 87 <dBXminy mtp bytesperunit> BE 00 00 00
	    C7 87 <dBXmaxy mtp bytesperunit> 0A 00 00 00
	    B9 04 00 00 00
	    8B 06
	    3B 87 <dPJminx mtp bytesperunit>
	    7D 06
	    89 87 <dPJminx mtp bytesperunit>
	    3B 87 <dPJmaxx mtp bytesperunit>
	    7E 06
	    89 87 <dPJmaxx mtp bytesperunit>
	    8B 46 04
	    3B 87 <dBXminy mtp bytesperunit>
	    7D 06
	    89 87 <dBXminy mtp bytesperunit>
	    3B 87 <dBXmaxy mtp bytesperunit>
	    7E 06
	    89 87 <dBXmaxy mtp bytesperunit>
	    83 C6 08
	    49
	    75 BD
	    5E
	}
	[PJvr] = 4;
	end;

"VHGND terrain mapped"
	( Mirrored heights are not the cached surface.  Near-plane triangles keep
	  polymap's generic four-edge clipping and projection path unchanged. )
	[SPterrain] = 1;
	A = [VHGNDmirror]; ? A != 0 -> VHGND terrain mapped generic;
	( Shared unit-grid vertices normally arrived through the preceding
	  triangle.  Check the three exact cache slots directly before entering
	  the general ensure/front loops; this removes six tiny Lino calls from
	  the overwhelmingly common cache-hit path without changing a transform. )
	A = [VHGNDvctri]; ? A != 0 -> VHGND terrain mapped cache second;
	A = VHGNDvcstamp; A + [VHGNDh1]; C = [A];
	A = [VHGNDvcgen]; ? A != C -> VHGND terrain mapped ensure begin;
	A = VHGNDvcvisible; A + [VHGNDh1]; A = [A]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND terrain mapped generic;
	A = [VHGNDh1]; A + 1; D = A; -> VHGND terrain mapped cache middle;
    "VHGND terrain mapped cache second"
	A = [VHGNDh1]; A + 1; D = A;
	A = VHGNDvcstamp; A + D; C = [A];
	A = [VHGNDvcgen]; ? A != C -> VHGND terrain mapped ensure begin;
	A = VHGNDvcvisible; A + D; A = [A]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND terrain mapped generic;
	A = [VHGNDh1]; A + 201; D = A;
    "VHGND terrain mapped cache middle"
	A = VHGNDvcstamp; A + D; C = [A];
	A = [VHGNDvcgen]; ? A != C -> VHGND terrain mapped ensure begin;
	A = VHGNDvcvisible; A + D; A = [A]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND terrain mapped generic;
	A = [VHGNDh1]; A + 200; D = A;
	A = VHGNDvcstamp; A + D; C = [A];
	A = [VHGNDvcgen]; ? A != C -> VHGND terrain mapped ensure begin;
	A = VHGNDvcvisible; A + D; A = [A]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND terrain mapped generic;
	-> VHGND terrain mapped load begin;
    "VHGND terrain mapped ensure begin"
	[VHGNDvi] = 0;
    "VHGND terrain mapped ensure"
	=> VHGND terrain vertex ensure;
	[VHGNDvi]+; A = [VHGNDvi]; ? A < 3 -> VHGND terrain mapped ensure;

	[VHGNDvi] = 0;
    "VHGND terrain mapped front"
	=> VHGND terrain vertex index;
	A = VHGNDvcvisible; A + [VHGNDvcindex]; A = [A];
	? A = 0 -> VHGND terrain mapped generic;
	[VHGNDvi]+; A = [VHGNDvi]; ? A < 3 -> VHGND terrain mapped front;

	"VHGND terrain mapped load begin"
	[VHGNDvi] = 0;
    "VHGND terrain mapped load"
	=> VHGND terrain vertex load;
	[VHGNDvi]+; A = [VHGNDvi]; ? A < 3 -> VHGND terrain mapped load;

	( Preserve polymap's four-entry closing edge in every rotated, projected,
	  visibility and ultima workspace consumed downstream. )
	A = FSRXF; A + 2; A + A; A + fw; C = [A]; [A plus 2] = C; C = [A plus 1]; [A plus 3] = C;
	A = FSRYF; A + 2; A + A; A + fw; C = [A]; [A plus 2] = C; C = [A plus 1]; [A plus 3] = C;
	A = FSRZF; A + 2; A + A; A + fw; C = [A]; [A plus 2] = C; C = [A plus 1]; [A plus 3] = C;
	A = mp; C = [A plus 4]; [A plus 6] = C; C = [A plus 5]; [A plus 7] = C;
	A = rwf; [A plus 3] = 1; [PJdoflag] = 4;
	=> VHGND terrain cached bounds;
	[PJpreproject] = 1; [PJnrv] = 3; => PG polymap;
	[SPterrain] = 0; end;

"VHGND terrain facing"
	( Unit terrain geometry is immutable until the next surface generation.
	  Cache the original routine's narrowed cross product, then retain its exact
	  camera-relative dot schedule each frame.  Reflections use generic facing. )
	A = [VHGNDmirror]; ? A != 0 -> VHGND terrain facing generic;
	A = [VHGNDh1]; A + A; A + [VHGNDvctri]; [VHGNDnormindex] = A;
	A = VHGNDnormstamp; A + [VHGNDnormindex]; C = [A];
	A = [VHGNDnormgen]; ? A != C -> VHGND terrain facing build;
	A = VHGNDnormx; A + [VHGNDnormindex]; [PGFt] = [A]; [PGFi] = FSRX; => PGF setf32;
	A = VHGNDnormy; A + [VHGNDnormindex]; [PGFt] = [A]; [PGFi] = FSRY; => PGF setf32;
	A = VHGNDnormz; A + [VHGNDnormindex]; [PGFt] = [A]; [PGFi] = FSRZ; => PGF setf32;
	=> PG terrain facing dot native; end;
    "VHGND terrain facing build"
	=> PG facing;
	[PGFi] = FSRX; => PGF a; => PGF stf32;
	A = VHGNDnormx; A + [VHGNDnormindex]; C = [PGFt]; [A] = C;
	[PGFi] = FSRY; => PGF a; => PGF stf32;
	A = VHGNDnormy; A + [VHGNDnormindex]; C = [PGFt]; [A] = C;
	[PGFi] = FSRZ; => PGF a; => PGF stf32;
	A = VHGNDnormz; A + [VHGNDnormindex]; C = [PGFt]; [A] = C;
	A = VHGNDnormstamp; A + [VHGNDnormindex]; C = [VHGNDnormgen]; [A] = C;
	end;
    "VHGND terrain facing generic"
	=> PG facing; end;

    "VHGND terrain mapped generic"
	[PJpreproject] = 0; [PJnrv] = 3; => PG polymap;
	[SPterrain] = 0; end;

"VHGND secondary sun setup"
	( NOCTIS-1.CPP chooses the class-10 companion nearest the landed world.
	  When the world belongs to a companion, that owner remains the active
	  daylight sun and the primary star becomes this secondary observer. )
	[VHGNDsecactive] = 0; [VHGNDseci] = 0;
	[VHGNDvecindex] = [VHGNDplanet]; => VHGND absolute body vector;
	[VHGNDsectx0] = [VHGNDvecx0]; [VHGNDsectx1] = [VHGNDvecx1];
	[VHGNDsecty0] = [VHGNDvecy0]; [VHGNDsecty1] = [VHGNDvecy1];
	[VHGNDsectz0] = [VHGNDvecz0]; [VHGNDsectz1] = [VHGNDvecz1];
    "VHGND secondary scan"
	A = [VHGNDseci]; ? A >= [nsnob] -> VHGND secondary scan done;
	E = nsptype; E + A; C = [E]; ? C != 10 -> VHGND secondary scan next;
	[VHGNDvecindex] = A; => VHGND absolute body vector;
	[FA0] = [VHGNDsectx0]; [FA1] = [VHGNDsectx1];
	[FB0] = [VHGNDvecx0]; [FB1] = [VHGNDvecx1]; => FSub;
	[VHGNDxx0] = [FA0]; [VHGNDxx1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHGNDsecty0]; [FA1] = [VHGNDsecty1];
	[FB0] = [VHGNDvecy0]; [FB1] = [VHGNDvecy1]; => FSub;
	[VHGNDowny0] = [FA0]; [VHGNDowny1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHGNDsectz0]; [FA1] = [VHGNDsectz1];
	[FB0] = [VHGNDvecz0]; [FB1] = [VHGNDvecz1]; => FSub;
	[VHGNDzz0] = [FA0]; [VHGNDzz1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHGNDxx0]; [FA1] = [VHGNDxx1];
	[FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1]; => FMul;
	[VHGNDmass0] = [FA0]; [VHGNDmass1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHGNDowny0]; [FA1] = [VHGNDowny1];
	[FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1]; => FMul;
	[FB0] = [VHGNDmass0]; [FB1] = [VHGNDmass1]; => FAdd;
	[VHGNDmass0] = [FA0]; [VHGNDmass1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHGNDzz0]; [FA1] = [VHGNDzz1];
	[FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1]; => FMul;
	[FB0] = [VHGNDmass0]; [FB1] = [VHGNDmass1]; => FAdd; => FSqrt;
	=> F32Narrow; [VHGNDsuncoord] = [FS0]; [FS0] = [VHGNDsuncoord]; => FLoadF32;
	A = [VHGNDsecactive]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND secondary take;
	[FS0] = [VHGNDsecbest]; => FLoadF32; [FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1];
	[FS0] = [VHGNDsuncoord]; => FLoadF32; => FCmp;
	A = [FI]; ? A >= 0 -> VHGND secondary scan next;
    "VHGND secondary take"
	[VHGNDsecactive] = 1; [VHGNDsecbest] = [VHGNDsuncoord];
	[VHGNDsecindex] = [VHGNDseci];
	[VHGNDsecox0] = [VHGNDvecx0]; [VHGNDsecox1] = [VHGNDvecx1];
	[VHGNDsecoy0] = [VHGNDvecy0]; [VHGNDsecoy1] = [VHGNDvecy1];
	[VHGNDsecoz0] = [VHGNDvecz0]; [VHGNDsecoz1] = [VHGNDvecz1];
	A = [VHGNDseci]; A < 1; E = nspray; E + A;
	[FA0] = [E plus 0]; [FA1] = [E plus 1]; => F32Narrow;
	[VHGNDsecray] = [FS0];
    "VHGND secondary scan next"
	[VHGNDseci]+; -> VHGND secondary scan;
    "VHGND secondary scan done"
	A = [VHGNDsecactive]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND secondary setup done;

	E = nspowner; E + [VHGNDplanet]; A = [E];
	? A < 0 -> VHGND secondary regular;
	E = nsptype; E + A; C = [E]; ? C != 10 -> VHGND secondary regular;
	( Companion-owned world: calculate the owner's latitude from the primary,
	  then make the primary at the system origin the secondary observer. )
	[VHGNDvecindex] = A; => VHGND absolute body vector;
	[VHGNDsecox0] = [VHGNDvecx0]; [VHGNDsecox1] = [VHGNDvecx1];
	[VHGNDsecoy0] = [VHGNDvecy0]; [VHGNDsecoy1] = [VHGNDvecy1];
	[VHGNDsecoz0] = [VHGNDvecz0]; [VHGNDsecoz1] = [VHGNDvecz1];
	=> VHGND secondary companion distance;
	[VHGNDsecx0] = [VHGNDsecoy0]; [VHGNDsecx1] = [VHGNDsecoy1];
	=> VHGND secondary latitude;
	[FA0] = [VHGNDsectx0]; [FA1] = [VHGNDsectx1];
	[FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1]; => FMul;
	[VHGNDmass0] = [FA0]; [VHGNDmass1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHGNDsecty0]; [FA1] = [VHGNDsecty1];
	[FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1]; => FMul;
	[FB0] = [VHGNDmass0]; [FB1] = [VHGNDmass1]; => FAdd;
	[VHGNDmass0] = [FA0]; [VHGNDmass1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHGNDsectz0]; [FA1] = [VHGNDsectz1];
	[FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1]; => FMul;
	[FB0] = [VHGNDmass0]; [FB1] = [VHGNDmass1]; => FAdd; => FSqrt;
	=> F32Narrow; [VHGNDsecdist] = [FS0]; [VHGNDsecray] = [nsstarray];
	A = 0; [VHGNDsecox0] = A; [VHGNDsecox1] = A;
	[VHGNDsecoy0] = A; [VHGNDsecoy1] = A; [VHGNDsecoz0] = A; [VHGNDsecoz1] = A;
	-> VHGND secondary viewpoint;

    "VHGND secondary regular"
	( Primary-owned world: the closest companion is the secondary observer. )
	[VHGNDsecdist] = [VHGNDsecbest];
	=> VHGND secondary companion distance;
	[FA0] = [VHGNDsecoy0]; [FA1] = [VHGNDsecoy1]; => FNeg;
	[VHGNDsecx0] = [FA0]; [VHGNDsecx1] = [FA1];
	=> VHGND secondary latitude;

    "VHGND secondary viewpoint"
	( Analytic planet_viewpoint(target, secondary observer). )
	[FA0] = [VHGNDsectx0]; [FA1] = [VHGNDsectx1];
	[FB0] = [VHGNDsecox0]; [FB1] = [VHGNDsecox1]; => FSub; => FNeg;
	[VHGNDxx0] = [FA0]; [VHGNDxx1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHGNDsectz0]; [FA1] = [VHGNDsectz1];
	[FB0] = [VHGNDsecoz0]; [FB1] = [VHGNDsecoz1]; => FSub; => FNeg;
	[FB0] = [VHGNDxx0]; [FB1] = [VHGNDxx1]; => FAtan2;
	[FB0] = 0; [FB1] = 40668000h; => FMul;
	[FB0] = 54442D18h; [FB1] = 400921FBh; => FQuo; => FToIntNear;
	A = [FI]; A % 360; ? A >= 0 -> VHGND secondary view normalized; A + 360;
    "VHGND secondary view normalized"
	[VHGNDsecview] = A; C = 89; C - A; C + [VHGNDrotation]; A = C; A % 360;
	? A >= 0 -> VHGND secondary phase ready; A + 360;
    "VHGND secondary phase ready"
	A + 35; ? A < 360 -> VHGND secondary term start ready; A - 360;
    "VHGND secondary term start ready"
	[VHGNDsectstart] = A; A + 130; ? A < 360 -> VHGND secondary term end ready; A - 360;
    "VHGND secondary term end ready"
	[VHGNDsectend] = A; [VHGNDsecnight] = 0; [VHGNDsecxf] = 1;
	A = [VHGNDsectstart]; ? A <= [VHGNDsectend] -> VHGND secondary night straight;
	A = [VHGNDlon]; ? A >= [VHGNDsectstart] -> VHGND secondary night set;
	? A < [VHGNDsectend] -> VHGND secondary night set; -> VHGND secondary night ready;
    "VHGND secondary night straight"
	A = [VHGNDlon]; ? A < [VHGNDsectstart] -> VHGND secondary night ready;
	? A >= [VHGNDsectend] -> VHGND secondary night ready;
    "VHGND secondary night set"
	[VHGNDsecnight] = 1;
    "VHGND secondary night ready"
	A = [VHGNDsectstart]; A - [VHGNDlon]; A % 360;
	? A >= 0 -> VHGND secondary start normalized; A + 360;
    "VHGND secondary start normalized"
	? A <= 180 -> VHGND secondary start distance ready; C = 360; C - A; A = C;
    "VHGND secondary start distance ready"
	[VHGNDseccrep] = A;
	A = [VHGNDsectend]; A - [VHGNDlon]; A % 360;
	? A >= 0 -> VHGND secondary end normalized; A + 360;
    "VHGND secondary end normalized"
	? A <= 180 -> VHGND secondary end distance ready; C = 360; C - A; A = C;
    "VHGND secondary end distance ready"
	? A >= [VHGNDseccrep] -> VHGND secondary exposure ready;
	[VHGNDseccrep] = A; A = 0; A - 1; [VHGNDsecxf] = A;
    "VHGND secondary exposure ready"
	[FI] = [VHGNDseccrep]; => IntToF;
	[FB0] = 27BB2FECh; [FB1] = 3FE90B0Fh; => FMul; => F32Narrow;
	[VHGNDsecexp] = [FS0];

	( alfa = deg*(90-latitude+pri_latitude), beta = deg*pri_exposure. )
	A = [VHGNDlat]; A - 60; ? A >= 0 -> VHGND secondary surface latitude;
	C = 0; C - A; A = C;
    "VHGND secondary surface latitude"
	[FI] = A; => IntToF; [FB0] = 0; [FB1] = 3FF80000h; => FMul;
	[FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1]; [FI] = 90; => IntToF; => FSub;
	[FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1]; [FS0] = [VHGNDseclat]; => FLoadF32; => FAdd;
	[FB0] = A2529D39h; [FB1] = 3F91DF46h; => FMul;
	=> F32Narrow; [VHGNDsuncoord] = [FS0]; [FS0] = [VHGNDsuncoord]; => FLoadF32;
	[VHGNDsuna0] = [FA0]; [VHGNDsuna1] = [FA1];
	[FS0] = [VHGNDsecexp]; => FLoadF32;
	[FB0] = A2529D39h; [FB1] = 3F91DF46h; => FMul;
	=> F32Narrow; [VHGNDsuncoord] = [FS0]; [FS0] = [VHGNDsuncoord]; => FLoadF32;
	[VHGNDsunb0] = [FA0]; [VHGNDsunb1] = [FA1];
	=> FSin; [VHGNDsbs0] = [FA0]; [VHGNDsbs1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHGNDsuna0]; [FA1] = [VHGNDsuna1]; => FSin;
	[VHGNDsas0] = [FA0]; [VHGNDsas1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHGNDsuna0]; [FA1] = [VHGNDsuna1]; => FCos;
	[VHGNDsac0] = [FA0]; [VHGNDsac1] = [FA1];
	[FS0] = [VHGNDsecdist]; => FLoadF32;
	[VHGNDsund0] = [FA0]; [VHGNDsund1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHGNDsunb0]; [FA1] = [VHGNDsunb1]; => FCos;
	[FB0] = [VHGNDsund0]; [FB1] = [VHGNDsund1]; => FMul; => FNeg;
	A = [VHGNDsecxf]; ? A >= 0 -> VHGND secondary x sign ready; => FNeg;
    "VHGND secondary x sign ready"
	=> F32Narrow; [VHGNDsuncoord] = [FS0]; [FS0] = [VHGNDsuncoord]; => FLoadF32;
	[VHGNDsecx0] = [FA0]; [VHGNDsecx1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHGNDsund0]; [FA1] = [VHGNDsund1];
	[FB0] = [VHGNDsbs0]; [FB1] = [VHGNDsbs1]; => FMul;
	[FB0] = [VHGNDsas0]; [FB1] = [VHGNDsas1]; => FMul; => FNeg;
	=> F32Narrow; [VHGNDsuncoord] = [FS0]; [FS0] = [VHGNDsuncoord]; => FLoadF32;
	[VHGNDsecy0] = [FA0]; [VHGNDsecy1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHGNDsund0]; [FA1] = [VHGNDsund1];
	[FB0] = [VHGNDsbs0]; [FB1] = [VHGNDsbs1]; => FMul;
	[FB0] = [VHGNDsac0]; [FB1] = [VHGNDsac1]; => FMul;
	=> F32Narrow; [VHGNDsuncoord] = [FS0]; [FS0] = [VHGNDsuncoord]; => FLoadF32;
	[VHGNDsecz0] = [FA0]; [VHGNDsecz1] = [FA1];
    "VHGND secondary setup done"
	end;

"VHGND secondary companion distance"
	[FA0] = [VHGNDsecox0]; [FA1] = [VHGNDsecox1];
	[FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1]; => FMul;
	[VHGNDmass0] = [FA0]; [VHGNDmass1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHGNDsecoy0]; [FA1] = [VHGNDsecoy1];
	[FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1]; => FMul;
	[FB0] = [VHGNDmass0]; [FB1] = [VHGNDmass1]; => FAdd;
	[VHGNDmass0] = [FA0]; [VHGNDmass1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHGNDsecoz0]; [FA1] = [VHGNDsecoz1];
	[FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1]; => FMul;
	[FB0] = [VHGNDmass0]; [FB1] = [VHGNDmass1]; => FAdd; => FSqrt;
	=> F32Narrow; [VHGNDseccdist] = [FS0];
	end;

"VHGND secondary latitude"
	( VHGNDsecx is the signed vertical star separation. )
	[FS0] = [VHGNDseccdist]; => FLoadF32; [FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHGNDsecx0]; [FA1] = [VHGNDsecx1]; => FAtan2;
	[FB0] = A2529D39h; [FB1] = 3F91DF46h; => FQuo;
	=> F32Narrow; [VHGNDseclat] = [FS0];
	end;

"VHGND local sun"
	( NOCTIS-1.CPP planetary_main draws the active local sun after the sky
	  and before terrain.  Its precomputed surface coordinates use the
	  landing latitude, terminator exposure, and dawn/dusk side exactly;
	  rain at 2.5 or above and the night zone suppress it. )
	A = [GRSKnightzone]; ? A != 0 -> VHGND local primary done;
	A = [GRSKrainy]; ? A '>= 40200000h -> VHGND local primary done;
	=> VHG fpu clean;
	[FS0] = [GRSKdsd1]; => FLoadF32;
	[VHGNDsund0] = [FA0]; [VHGNDsund1] = [FA1];

	( alfa = deg * (90 - abs(landing_pt_lat - 60) * 1.5) )
	A = [VHGNDlat]; A - 60; ? A >= 0 -> VHGND sun latitude absolute;
	C = 0; C - A; A = C;
    "VHGND sun latitude absolute"
	[FI] = A; => IntToF; [FB0] = 0; [FB1] = 3FF80000h; => FMul;
	[FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1]; [FI] = 90; => IntToF; => FSub;
	[FB0] = A2529D39h; [FB1] = 3F91DF46h; => FMul;
	=> F32Narrow; [VHGNDsuncoord] = [FS0]; [FS0] = [VHGNDsuncoord]; => FLoadF32;
	[VHGNDsuna0] = [FA0]; [VHGNDsuna1] = [FA1];

	( beta = deg * exposure )
	[FS0] = [GRSKexposure]; => FLoadF32;
	[FB0] = A2529D39h; [FB1] = 3F91DF46h; => FMul;
	=> F32Narrow; [VHGNDsuncoord] = [FS0]; [FS0] = [VHGNDsuncoord]; => FLoadF32;
	[VHGNDsunb0] = [FA0]; [VHGNDsunb1] = [FA1];
	=> FSin; [VHGNDsbs0] = [FA0]; [VHGNDsbs1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHGNDsuna0]; [FA1] = [VHGNDsuna1]; => FSin;
	[VHGNDsas0] = [FA0]; [VHGNDsas1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHGNDsuna0]; [FA1] = [VHGNDsuna1]; => FCos;
	[VHGNDsac0] = [FA0]; [VHGNDsac1] = [FA1];

	( x = -d*cos(beta)*side; y = -d*sin(beta)*sin(alfa);
	  z = d*sin(beta)*cos(alfa). )
	[FA0] = [VHGNDsunb0]; [FA1] = [VHGNDsunb1]; => FCos;
	[FB0] = [VHGNDsund0]; [FB1] = [VHGNDsund1]; => FMul; => FNeg;
	A = [VHGNDsunxf]; ? A >= 0 -> VHGND sun x ready; => FNeg;
    "VHGND sun x ready"
	=> F32Narrow; [VHGNDsuncoord] = [FS0]; [FS0] = [VHGNDsuncoord]; => FLoadF32;
	[VHGNDsunx0] = [FA0]; [VHGNDsunx1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHGNDsund0]; [FA1] = [VHGNDsund1];
	[FB0] = [VHGNDsbs0]; [FB1] = [VHGNDsbs1]; => FMul;
	[FB0] = [VHGNDsas0]; [FB1] = [VHGNDsas1]; => FMul; => FNeg;
	=> F32Narrow; [VHGNDsuncoord] = [FS0]; [FS0] = [VHGNDsuncoord]; => FLoadF32;
	[VHGNDsuny0] = [FA0]; [VHGNDsuny1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [VHGNDsund0]; [FA1] = [VHGNDsund1];
	[FB0] = [VHGNDsbs0]; [FB1] = [VHGNDsbs1]; => FMul;
	[FB0] = [VHGNDsac0]; [FB1] = [VHGNDsac1]; => FMul;
	=> F32Narrow; [VHGNDsuncoord] = [FS0]; [FS0] = [VHGNDsuncoord]; => FLoadF32;
	[VHGNDsunz0] = [FA0]; [VHGNDsunz1] = [FA1];

	( A companion-class owner is the active sun; otherwise use the system
	  star, matching nray1 in the source. )
	[VHGNDsunray] = [nsstarray];
	A = [VHGNDowner]; ? A < 0 -> VHGND sun radius ready;
	E = nsptype; E + A; C = [E]; ? C != 10 -> VHGND sun radius ready;
	A < 1; E = nspray; E + A; [FA0] = [E plus 0]; [FA1] = [E plus 1];
	=> F32Narrow; => FStoreF32; [VHGNDsunray] = [FS0];
    "VHGND sun radius ready"
	A = [GRSKatmosphere]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND sun airless;
	[FI] = 4; [WHfgm] = 0; -> VHGND sun magnitude;
    "VHGND sun airless"
	[FI] = 3; [WHfgm] = 3F000000h;
    "VHGND sun magnitude"
	=> IntToF; [FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1];
	[FS0] = [VHGNDsunray]; => FLoadF32; => FMul; => F32Narrow;
	[WHmag] = [FS0];
	[FA0] = [VHGNDsunx0]; [FA1] = [VHGNDsunx1]; [PGFi] = SFXX; => PGF sa;
	[FA0] = [VHGNDsuny0]; [FA1] = [VHGNDsuny1]; [PGFi] = SFYY; => PGF sa;
	[FA0] = [VHGNDsunz0]; [FA1] = [VHGNDsunz1]; [PGFi] = SFZZ; => PGF sa;
	[WHshape] = 1; [WHsun] = 1; [WHdstreg] = RGADP; => SP white;
    "VHGND local primary done"
	=> VHGND secondary sun;
    "VHGND local sun done"
	end;

"VHGND secondary sun"
	A = [VHGNDsecactive]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND secondary sun done;
	A = [VHGNDsecnight]; ? A != 0 -> VHGND secondary sun done;
	A = [GRSKrainy]; ? A '>= 40000000h -> VHGND secondary sun done;
	[FA0] = [VHGNDsecx0]; [FA1] = [VHGNDsecx1]; [PGFi] = SFXX; => PGF sa;
	[FA0] = [VHGNDsecy0]; [FA1] = [VHGNDsecy1]; [PGFi] = SFYY; => PGF sa;
	[FA0] = [VHGNDsecz0]; [FA1] = [VHGNDsecz1]; [PGFi] = SFZZ; => PGF sa;
	A = [GRSKatmosphere]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND secondary sun airless;
	[FI] = 4; [WHfgm] = 0; -> VHGND secondary sun magnitude;
    "VHGND secondary sun airless"
	[FI] = 3; [WHfgm] = 3F000000h;
    "VHGND secondary sun magnitude"
	=> IntToF; [FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1];
	[FS0] = [VHGNDsecray]; => FLoadF32; => FMul; => F32Narrow;
	[WHmag] = [FS0]; [WHshape] = 1; [WHsun] = 1; [WHdstreg] = RGADP;
	=> SP white;
    "VHGND secondary sun done"
	end;

"VHGND sun flares"
	( Restore the player's view matrix before the post-world flare pass.  The
	  flare projector consumes already-relative sun coordinates, so only the
	  angle table matters; keeping the real camera installed also leaves later
	  weather rendering in its normal state. )
	[VHVcamxi] = [VHGNDcamx]; [VHVcamyi] = [VHGNDcamy]; [VHVcamzi] = [VHGNDcamz];
	[VHValpha] = [VHGNDalpha]; [VHVbeta] = [VHGNDbeta]; => VH set view;

	( Local flare: rainy < 1.2, ordinary emitting classes only, and the
	  class-11 quarter-turn gate retained by the source. )
	A = [GRSKnightzone]; ? A != 0 -> VHGND primary flare done;
	A = [GRSKrainy]; ? A '>= 3F99999Ah -> VHGND primary flare done;
	A = [nsclass]; ? A = 5 -> VHGND primary flare done;
	? A = 6 -> VHGND primary flare done; ? A = 10 -> VHGND primary flare done;
	? A != 11 -> VHGND primary flare project;
	A = [VHTphase]; A % 360; ? A >= 90 -> VHGND primary flare done;
    "VHGND primary flare project"
	[FA0] = [VHGNDsunx0]; [FA1] = [VHGNDsunx1]; [PGFi] = SFXX; => PGF sa;
	[FA0] = [VHGNDsuny0]; [FA1] = [VHGNDsuny1]; [PGFi] = SFYY; => PGF sa;
	[FA0] = [VHGNDsunz0]; [FA1] = [VHGNDsunz1]; [PGFi] = SFZZ; => PGF sa;
	=> VHGND flare project;
	A = [VHGNDflareok]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND primary flare done;
	[VHFcx] = [VHGNDflarecx]; [VHFcy] = [VHGNDflarecy];
	[VHFdist] = [GRSKdsd1]; [VHFray] = [VHGNDsunray]; => VH surface flare;
    "VHGND primary flare done"

	( Secondary flare has its own terminator and the source's 2.1 rain gate. )
	A = [VHGNDsecactive]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND sun flares done;
	A = [VHGNDsecnight]; ? A != 0 -> VHGND sun flares done;
	A = [GRSKrainy]; ? A '>= 40066666h -> VHGND sun flares done;
	[FA0] = [VHGNDsecx0]; [FA1] = [VHGNDsecx1]; [PGFi] = SFXX; => PGF sa;
	[FA0] = [VHGNDsecy0]; [FA1] = [VHGNDsecy1]; [PGFi] = SFYY; => PGF sa;
	[FA0] = [VHGNDsecz0]; [FA1] = [VHGNDsecz1]; [PGFi] = SFZZ; => PGF sa;
	=> VHGND flare project;
	A = [VHGNDflareok]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND sun flares done;
	[VHFcx] = [VHGNDflarecx]; [VHFcy] = [VHGNDflarecy];
	[VHFdist] = [VHGNDsecdist]; [VHFray] = [VHGNDsecray]; => VH surface flare;
    "VHGND sun flares done"
	end;

"VHGND flare project"
	( lens_flares_for's double rotation using the p/t camera tables. )
	[VHGNDflareok] = 0;
	[PGFi] = SFXX; => PGF a; [PGFi] = FSPCB; => PGF mul;
	[PGFi] = SFT0; => PGF sa;
	[PGFi] = SFZZ; => PGF a; [PGFi] = FSPSB; => PGF mul;
	[PGFi] = SFT0; => PGF add; [PGFi] = SFRX; => PGF sa;
	[PGFi] = SFZZ; => PGF a; [PGFi] = FSTCB; => PGF mul;
	[PGFi] = SFT0; => PGF sa;
	[PGFi] = SFXX; => PGF a; [PGFi] = FSTSB; => PGF mul;
	[PGFi] = SFT0; => PGF rsub; [PGFi] = SFZ2; => PGF sa;
	[PGFi] = SFZ2; => PGF a; [PGFi] = FSTCA; => PGF mul;
	[PGFi] = SFT0; => PGF sa;
	[PGFi] = SFYY; => PGF a; [PGFi] = FSTSA; => PGF mul;
	[PGFi] = SFT0; => PGF add; [PGFi] = SFRZ; => PGF sa;
	[PGFi] = SFYY; => PGF a; [PGFi] = FSPCA; => PGF mul;
	[PGFi] = SFT0; => PGF sa;
	[PGFi] = SFZ2; => PGF a; [PGFi] = FSPSA; => PGF mul;
	[PGFi] = SFT0; => PGF rsub; [PGFi] = SFRY; => PGF sa;
	[FI] = 1; => IntToF; [FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1];
	[PGFi] = SFRZ; => PGF a; => FCmp;
	A = [FI]; ? A <= 0 -> VHGND flare project done;
	[PGFi] = SFRX; => PGF a; [PGFi] = SFRZ; => PGF quo; => FToIntChop;
	A = [FI]; A + 1; C = 0; C - 150; ? A <= C -> VHGND flare project done;
	? A >= 160 -> VHGND flare project done; A + 160; [VHGNDflarecx] = A;
	[PGFi] = SFRY; => PGF a; [PGFi] = SFRZ; => PGF quo; => FToIntChop;
	A = [FI]; A + 1; C = 0; C - 90; ? A <= C -> VHGND flare project done;
	? A >= 90 -> VHGND flare project done; A + 100; [VHGNDflarecy] = A;
	[VHGNDflareok] = 1;
    "VHGND flare project done"
	end;

"VHGND surrounding frame"
	( NOCTIS-0.CPP surrounding(), shared by the Stardrifter and surface views.
	  Its moving four-row edge precedes the ten graded outer rows and columns,
	  exactly as the source draws it. )
	A = [VHGdrawhud]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND surrounding moving done;
	[VHGNDframei] = 0;
    "VHGND surrounding moving row"
	A = [VHGhudcount]; A + 9; A - [VHGNDframei]; [VHGNDframey] = A;
	A '* 320; A + 10; A + RADPT; A + nw; D = A;
	A = 54; A + [VHGNDsurlight]; C = [VHGNDframei]; C '* 3; A + C; C = A;
	[VHGNDframecount] = 300;
    "VHGND surrounding moving pixel"
	[D] = C; D+; [VHGNDframecount]-;
	A = [VHGNDframecount]; ? A != 0 -> VHGND surrounding moving pixel;
	[VHGNDframei]+; A = [VHGNDframei]; ? A < 4 -> VHGND surrounding moving row;
    "VHGND surrounding moving done"
	[VHGNDframei] = 0;
    "VHGND surrounding horizontal"
	A = 64; A + [VHGNDsurlight]; A - [VHGNDframei]; [VHGNDframecol] = A;
	A = 9; A - [VHGNDframei]; A '* 320; [VHGNDframeoff] = A;
	[VHGNDframecount] = 320;
    "VHGND surrounding top row"
	A = nw; A + RADPT; A + [VHGNDframeoff]; D = A; C = [VHGNDframecol]; [D] = C;
	[VHGNDframeoff]+; [VHGNDframecount]-;
	A = [VHGNDframecount]; ? A != 0 -> VHGND surrounding top row;
	A = 190; A + [VHGNDframei]; A '* 320; [VHGNDframeoff] = A;
	[VHGNDframecount] = 320;
    "VHGND surrounding bottom row"
	A = nw; A + RADPT; A + [VHGNDframeoff]; D = A; C = [VHGNDframecol]; [D] = C;
	[VHGNDframeoff]+; [VHGNDframecount]-;
	A = [VHGNDframecount]; ? A != 0 -> VHGND surrounding bottom row;
	[VHGNDframei]+; A = [VHGNDframei]; ? A < 10 -> VHGND surrounding horizontal;
	A = [VHGseamless]; ? A != 0 -> VHGND surrounding seamless;
	[VHGNDframey] = 10;
    "VHGND surrounding side row"
	[VHGNDframei] = 0;
    "VHGND surrounding side pixel"
	A = 64; A + [VHGNDsurlight]; A - [VHGNDframei]; [VHGNDframecol] = A;
	A = [VHGNDframey]; A '* 320; C = 9; C - [VHGNDframei]; A + C;
	A + RADPT; A + nw; D = A; C = [VHGNDframecol]; [D] = C;
	A = [VHGNDframey]; A '* 320; C = 310; C + [VHGNDframei]; A + C;
	A + RADPT; A + nw; D = A; C = [VHGNDframecol]; [D] = C;
	[VHGNDframei]+; A = [VHGNDframei]; ? A < 10 -> VHGND surrounding side pixel;
	[VHGNDframey]+; A = [VHGNDframey]; ? A < 190 -> VHGND surrounding side row;
	-> VHGND surrounding done;

    "VHGND surrounding seamless"
	( NIV+ seamless_border=1 tapers each side strip into the already graded
	  top and bottom rows: outer columns span the full page, inner columns
	  begin and end progressively nearer the 180-line world opening. )
	[VHGNDframei] = 0;
    "VHGND surrounding seamless column"
	A = 64; A + [VHGNDsurlight]; A - [VHGNDframei]; [VHGNDframecol] = A;
	A = 9; A - [VHGNDframei]; [VHGNDframey] = A;
	A + A; C = 200; C - A; [VHGNDframecount] = C;
    "VHGND surrounding seamless row"
	A = [VHGNDframey]; A '* 320; C = 9; C - [VHGNDframei]; A + C;
	A + RADPT; A + nw; D = A; C = [VHGNDframecol]; [D] = C;
	A = [VHGNDframey]; A '* 320; C = 310; C + [VHGNDframei]; A + C;
	A + RADPT; A + nw; D = A; C = [VHGNDframecol]; [D] = C;
	[VHGNDframey]+; [VHGNDframecount]-;
	A = [VHGNDframecount]; ? A != 0 -> VHGND surrounding seamless row;
	[VHGNDframei]+; A = [VHGNDframei]; ? A < 10 -> VHGND surrounding seamless column;
    "VHGND surrounding done"
	=> VHGND HUD lamps;
	=> VHGND epoch HUD;
	=> VHGND surface coordinate HUD;
	=> VHGND compass;
	=> VHGND environment HUD;
	end;

"VHGND HUD lamps"
	( surrounding()'s four visor lamps. The ordinary 4x4 lights use
	  min(127,64+3*surlight); active surface jetpack input raises the original
	  5x5 flash to palette index 127. )
	A = [VHGdrawhud]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND HUD lamps done;
	[VHGNDlampsize] = 4; A = 64; C = [VHGNDsurlight]; C '* 3; A + C;
	? A <= 127 -> VHGND HUD lamp colour ready; A = 127;
    "VHGND HUD lamp colour ready"
	[VHGNDframecol] = A; [VHGNDframeoff] = 308; [VHGNDframey] = 188;
	A = [VHGmode]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND HUD lamp positions ready;
	A = [VHGsurfjet]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND HUD lamp positions ready;
	[VHGNDlampsize] = 5; [VHGNDframecol] = 127;
	[VHGNDframeoff] = 307; [VHGNDframey] = 187;
    "VHGND HUD lamp positions ready"
	[VHGNDlampx] = 9; [VHGNDlampy] = 9; => VHGND HUD lamp;
	[VHGNDlampx] = [VHGNDframeoff]; [VHGNDlampy] = 9; => VHGND HUD lamp;
	[VHGNDlampx] = 9; [VHGNDlampy] = [VHGNDframey]; => VHGND HUD lamp;
	[VHGNDlampx] = [VHGNDframeoff]; [VHGNDlampy] = [VHGNDframey]; => VHGND HUD lamp;
    "VHGND HUD lamps done"
	end;

"VHGND HUD lamp"
	[VHGNDlamprow] = 0;
    "VHGND HUD lamp row"
	A = [VHGNDlampy]; A + [VHGNDlamprow]; A '* 320; A + [VHGNDlampx];
	A + RADPT; A + nw; D = A; [VHGNDframecount] = [VHGNDlampsize]; C = [VHGNDframecol];
    "VHGND HUD lamp pixel"
	[D] = C; D+; [VHGNDframecount]-;
	A = [VHGNDframecount]; ? A != 0 -> VHGND HUD lamp pixel;
	[VHGNDlamprow]+; A = [VHGNDlamprow]; ? A < [VHGNDlampsize] -> VHGND HUD lamp row;
	end;

"VHGND compass"
	( Surface surrounding(1): the original 28-character N...E...S...W strip,
	  scrolled by user_beta in nine-degree cells and rendered through the
	  retained 3x5 HUD glyph shapes. )
	A = [VHGdrawhud]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND compass done;
	A = [VHGmode]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND compass done;
	[VHGNDhudy] = 2;
	A = [VHGbeta]; A % 360; ? A >= 0 -> VHGND compass beta ready; A + 360;
    "VHGND compass beta ready"
	C = 360; C - A; A = C; ? A <= 359 -> VHGND compass heading ready; A - 360;
    "VHGND compass heading ready"
	[VHGNDcompassrem] = A; A / 9; [VHGNDcompasspos] = A;
	A = [VHGNDcompassrem]; A '* 4; A / 9; [VHGNDcompassrem] = A;
	A % 4; C = 200; C - A; [VHGNDcompassx] = C;
	( Fixed centre pointer from surrounding(). )
	[VHGNDlampx] = 254; [VHGNDlampy] = 1; [VHGNDlampsize] = 5;
	[VHGNDframecol] = 64; => VHGND compass marker;
	A = 8; A '* 320; A + 256; A + RADPT; A + nw; D = A; [D] = 127;
	[VHGNDcompassi] = 0;
    "VHGND compass character"
	A = [VHGNDcompasspos]; A + [VHGNDcompassi]; C = A; A % 10;
	? A = 0 -> VHGND compass direction; [VHGNDcompassdir] = 4; -> VHGND compass glyph;
    "VHGND compass direction"
	A = C; A / 10; A % 4; [VHGNDcompassdir] = A;
    "VHGND compass glyph"
	[VHGNDcompassrow] = 0;
    "VHGND compass glyph row"
	=> VHGND compass row mask;
	C = [VHGNDcompassmask]; C & 1; ? C = 0 -> VHGND compass pixel one;
	[VHGNDframeoff] = [VHGNDcompassx]; => VHGND compass invert;
    "VHGND compass pixel one"
	C = [VHGNDcompassmask]; C & 2; ? C = 0 -> VHGND compass pixel two;
	A = [VHGNDcompassx]; A + 1; [VHGNDframeoff] = A; => VHGND compass invert;
    "VHGND compass pixel two"
	C = [VHGNDcompassmask]; C & 4; ? C = 0 -> VHGND compass row done;
	A = [VHGNDcompassx]; A + 2; [VHGNDframeoff] = A; => VHGND compass invert;
    "VHGND compass row done"
	[VHGNDcompassrow]+; A = [VHGNDcompassrow]; ? A < 5 -> VHGND compass glyph row;
	A = [VHGNDcompassx]; A + 4; [VHGNDcompassx] = A;
	[VHGNDcompassi]+; A = [VHGNDcompassi]; ? A < 28 -> VHGND compass character;
    "VHGND compass done"
	end;

"VHGND compass marker"
	( 5x7 centre block; reuse the lamp row writer with two extra rows. )
	[VHGNDlamprow] = 0;
    "VHGND compass marker row"
	A = [VHGNDlampy]; A + [VHGNDlamprow]; A '* 320; A + [VHGNDlampx];
	A + RADPT; A + nw; D = A; [VHGNDframecount] = 5; C = [VHGNDframecol];
    "VHGND compass marker pixel"
	[D] = C; D+; [VHGNDframecount]-;
	A = [VHGNDframecount]; ? A != 0 -> VHGND compass marker pixel;
	[VHGNDlamprow]+; A = [VHGNDlamprow]; ? A < 7 -> VHGND compass marker row;
	end;

"VHGND compass row mask"
	[VHGNDcompassmask] = 0; A = [VHGNDcompassdir];
	? A = 0 -> VHGND compass row north; ? A = 1 -> VHGND compass row east;
	? A = 2 -> VHGND compass row south; ? A = 3 -> VHGND compass row west;
	A = [VHGNDcompassrow]; ? A != 4 -> VHGND compass row mask done;
	[VHGNDcompassmask] = 2; -> VHGND compass row mask done;
    "VHGND compass row north"
	[VHGNDcompassmask] = 5; A = [VHGNDcompassrow]; ? A = 1 -> VHGND compass row seven;
	? A = 2 -> VHGND compass row seven; -> VHGND compass row mask done;
    "VHGND compass row east"
	[VHGNDcompassmask] = 7; A = [VHGNDcompassrow]; ? A = 1 -> VHGND compass row one;
	? A = 2 -> VHGND compass row three; ? A = 3 -> VHGND compass row one;
	-> VHGND compass row mask done;
    "VHGND compass row south"
	[VHGNDcompassmask] = 7; A = [VHGNDcompassrow]; ? A = 1 -> VHGND compass row one;
	? A = 3 -> VHGND compass row four; -> VHGND compass row mask done;
    "VHGND compass row west"
	[VHGNDcompassmask] = 5; A = [VHGNDcompassrow]; ? A = 2 -> VHGND compass row seven;
	? A = 3 -> VHGND compass row seven; -> VHGND compass row mask done;
    "VHGND compass row seven"
	[VHGNDcompassmask] = 7; -> VHGND compass row mask done;
    "VHGND compass row one"
	[VHGNDcompassmask] = 1; -> VHGND compass row mask done;
    "VHGND compass row three"
	[VHGNDcompassmask] = 3; -> VHGND compass row mask done;
    "VHGND compass row four"
	[VHGNDcompassmask] = 4;
    "VHGND compass row mask done"
	end;

"VHGND compass invert"
	A = [VHGNDcompassrow]; A + [VHGNDhudy]; A '* 320; A + [VHGNDframeoff];
	A + RADPT; A + nw; D = A; C = [D]; A = 191; A - C; A & 255; [D] = A;
	end;

"VHGND surface coordinate HUD"
	( surrounding(1)'s surface-coordinate part: selected landing longitude and
	  latitude, followed by the live 16384-unit terrain-square coordinates
	  relative to the source's 100,100 map origin. alphavalue() emits these as
	  variable-width decimal integers, so no port-specific zero padding is used. )
	A = [VHGdrawhud]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND surface coordinate done;
	A = [VHGmode]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND surface coordinate done;
	[VHGNDhudy] = 2;
	[VHGNDhudptr] = VHGNDsqc;
	A = 38; => VHGND HUD append; A = 32; => VHGND HUD append;
	A = 83; => VHGND HUD append; A = 81; => VHGND HUD append;
	A = 67; => VHGND HUD append; A = 32; => VHGND HUD append;
	A = [VHGlandinglon]; => VHGND HUD append number;
	A = 46; => VHGND HUD append;
	A = [VHGlandinglat]; => VHGND HUD append number;
	A = 58; => VHGND HUD append;
	A = [VHGx]; A / VHGNDTS; A - 100; => VHGND HUD append number;
	A = 46; => VHGND HUD append;
	A = [VHGz]; A / VHGNDTS; A - 100; => VHGND HUD append number;
	A = 0; => VHGND HUD append;
	[VHGNDhudsource] = VHGNDsqc; [VHGNDhudx] = 94; => VHGND HUD draw string;
    "VHGND surface coordinate done"
	end;

"VHGND epoch HUD"
	( EPOC and the three zero-padded sub-billion second triads from
	  surrounding(). VHGutcsecs is refreshed on each original simulation tick. )
	A = [VHGdrawhud]; ? A != 0 -> VHGND epoch HUD visible;
	A = [VHGgraphics]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND epoch HUD done;
    "VHGND epoch HUD visible"
	[VHGNDhudy] = 2;
	[VHGNDhudptr] = VHGNDepoctext;
	A = 69; => VHGND HUD append; A = 80; => VHGND HUD append;
	A = 79; => VHGND HUD append; A = 67; => VHGND HUD append;
	A = 32; => VHGND HUD append;
	A = [VHGutcsecs]; A / 1000000000; A + 6011; => VHGND HUD append number;
	A = 32; => VHGND HUD append; A = 38; => VHGND HUD append;
	A = 32; => VHGND HUD append;
	A = [VHGutcsecs]; A / 1000000; A % 1000; => VHGND HUD append triad;
	A = 46; => VHGND HUD append;
	A = [VHGutcsecs]; A / 1000; A % 1000; => VHGND HUD append triad;
	A = 46; => VHGND HUD append;
	A = [VHGutcsecs]; A % 1000; => VHGND HUD append triad;
	A = [VHGmode]; ? A != 0 -> VHGND epoch HUD terminate;
	A = [VHGonroof]; ? A != 0 -> VHGND epoch HUD terminate;
	A = VHGNDshiphints; => VHGND HUD append text;
    "VHGND epoch HUD terminate"
	A = 0; => VHGND HUD append;
	[VHGNDhudsource] = VHGNDepoctext; [VHGNDhudx] = 2; => VHGND HUD draw string;
    "VHGND epoch HUD done"
	end;

"VHGND environment HUD"
	( surrounding()'s exact lower visor line, driven by the source-rate smoothed
	  gravity, temperature, pressure and pulse fields maintained on simulation ticks. )
	A = [VHGdrawhud]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND environment HUD done;
	A = [VHGmode]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND environment HUD done;
	[VHGNDhudptr] = VHGNDenvtext;
	A = VHGNDenvgravity; => VHGND HUD append text;
	A = [VHGsurfgravdisp]; => VHGND HUD append fixed three;
	A = VHGNDenvtemp; => VHGND HUD append text;
	A = [VHGsurftempdisp]; => VHGND HUD append signed fixed one;
	A = VHGNDenvpress; => VHGND HUD append text;
	A = [VHGsurfpressdisp]; => VHGND HUD append fixed three;
	A = VHGNDenvpulse; => VHGND HUD append text;
	A = [VHGsurfpulsedisp]; => VHGND HUD append width three;
	A = VHGNDenvpps; => VHGND HUD append text;
	A = 0; => VHGND HUD append;
	[VHGNDhudsource] = VHGNDenvtext; [VHGNDhudx] = 2; [VHGNDhudy] = 192;
	=> VHGND HUD draw string;
    "VHGND environment HUD done"
	end;

"VHGND HUD draw string"
	[VHGNDhudi] = 0;
    "VHGND HUD draw character"
	A = [VHGNDhudsource]; A + [VHGNDhudi]; [VHGNDhudchar] = [A];
	A = [VHGNDhudchar]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND HUD draw done;
	[VHGNDhudrow] = 0;
    "VHGND HUD draw glyph row"
	=> VHGND HUD row mask;
	C = [VHGNDcompassmask]; C & 1; ? C = 0 -> VHGND HUD draw pixel one;
	[VHGNDframeoff] = [VHGNDhudx]; [VHGNDcompassrow] = [VHGNDhudrow]; => VHGND compass invert;
    "VHGND HUD draw pixel one"
	C = [VHGNDcompassmask]; C & 2; ? C = 0 -> VHGND HUD draw pixel two;
	A = [VHGNDhudx]; A + 1; [VHGNDframeoff] = A;
	[VHGNDcompassrow] = [VHGNDhudrow]; => VHGND compass invert;
    "VHGND HUD draw pixel two"
	C = [VHGNDcompassmask]; C & 4; ? C = 0 -> VHGND HUD draw row done;
	A = [VHGNDhudx]; A + 2; [VHGNDframeoff] = A;
	[VHGNDcompassrow] = [VHGNDhudrow]; => VHGND compass invert;
    "VHGND HUD draw row done"
	[VHGNDhudrow]+; A = [VHGNDhudrow]; ? A < 5 -> VHGND HUD draw glyph row;
	A = [VHGNDhudx]; A + 4; [VHGNDhudx] = A;
	[VHGNDhudi]+; A = [VHGNDhudi]; ? A < 80 -> VHGND HUD draw character;
    "VHGND HUD draw done"
	end;

"VHGND HUD append"
	D = [VHGNDhudptr]; [D] = A; [VHGNDhudptr]+;
	end;

"VHGND HUD append text"
	[VHGNDhudsource] = A; [VHGNDhudi] = 0;
    "VHGND HUD append text character"
	A = [VHGNDhudsource]; A + [VHGNDhudi]; A = [A];
	? A = 0 -> VHGND HUD append text done;
	=> VHGND HUD append; [VHGNDhudi]+; -> VHGND HUD append text character;
    "VHGND HUD append text done"
	end;

"VHGND HUD append number"
	[VHGNDhudvalue] = A; ? A >= 0 -> VHGND HUD number magnitude;
	A = 45; => VHGND HUD append; A = 0; A - [VHGNDhudvalue]; [VHGNDhudvalue] = A;
    "VHGND HUD number magnitude"
	A = [VHGNDhudvalue]; ? A < 1000 -> VHGND HUD number hundreds;
	A / 1000; A + 48; => VHGND HUD append;
	A = [VHGNDhudvalue]; A / 100; A % 10; A + 48; => VHGND HUD append;
	A = [VHGNDhudvalue]; A / 10; A % 10; A + 48; => VHGND HUD append;
	-> VHGND HUD number ones;
    "VHGND HUD number hundreds"
	A = [VHGNDhudvalue]; ? A < 100 -> VHGND HUD number tens;
	A / 100; A + 48; => VHGND HUD append;
	A = [VHGNDhudvalue]; A / 10; A % 10; A + 48; => VHGND HUD append;
	-> VHGND HUD number ones;
    "VHGND HUD number tens"
	A = [VHGNDhudvalue]; ? A < 10 -> VHGND HUD number ones;
	A / 10; A + 48; => VHGND HUD append;
    "VHGND HUD number ones"
	A = [VHGNDhudvalue]; A % 10; A + 48; => VHGND HUD append;
	end;

"VHGND HUD append triad"
	[VHGNDhudvalue] = A; A / 100; A + 48; => VHGND HUD append;
	A = [VHGNDhudvalue]; A / 10; A % 10; A + 48; => VHGND HUD append;
	A = [VHGNDhudvalue]; A % 10; A + 48; => VHGND HUD append;
	end;

"VHGND HUD append fixed three"
	[VHGNDhudvalue] = A; ? A >= 0 -> VHGND HUD fixed three magnitude; [VHGNDhudvalue] = 0;
    "VHGND HUD fixed three magnitude"
	A = [VHGNDhudvalue]; A / 1000; => VHGND HUD append number;
	A = 46; => VHGND HUD append;
	A = [VHGNDhudvalue]; A % 1000; => VHGND HUD append triad;
	end;

"VHGND HUD append signed fixed one"
	[VHGNDhudvalue] = A; A = 43; C = [VHGNDhudvalue]; ? C >= 0 -> VHGND HUD signed fixed sign;
	A = 45; C = 0; C - [VHGNDhudvalue]; [VHGNDhudvalue] = C;
    "VHGND HUD signed fixed sign"
	=> VHGND HUD append;
	A = [VHGNDhudvalue]; A / 10; => VHGND HUD append number;
	A = 46; => VHGND HUD append;
	A = [VHGNDhudvalue]; A % 10; A + 48; => VHGND HUD append;
	end;

"VHGND HUD append width three"
	[VHGNDhudvalue] = A; ? A >= 100 -> VHGND HUD width three number;
	A = 32; => VHGND HUD append;
	A = [VHGNDhudvalue]; ? A >= 10 -> VHGND HUD width three number;
	A = 32; => VHGND HUD append;
    "VHGND HUD width three number"
	A = [VHGNDhudvalue]; => VHGND HUD append number;
	end;

"VHGND HUD row mask"
	[VHGNDhudpacked] = 0; A = [VHGNDhudchar];
	? A = 48 -> VHGND HUD glyph zero; ? A = 49 -> VHGND HUD glyph one;
	? A = 50 -> VHGND HUD glyph two; ? A = 51 -> VHGND HUD glyph three;
	? A = 52 -> VHGND HUD glyph four; ? A = 53 -> VHGND HUD glyph five;
	? A = 54 -> VHGND HUD glyph six; ? A = 55 -> VHGND HUD glyph seven;
	? A = 56 -> VHGND HUD glyph eight; ? A = 57 -> VHGND HUD glyph nine;
	? A = 40 -> VHGND HUD glyph open paren; ? A = 41 -> VHGND HUD glyph close paren;
	? A = 42 -> VHGND HUD glyph asterisk; ? A = 44 -> VHGND HUD glyph comma;
	? A = 47 -> VHGND HUD glyph slash; ? A = 63 -> VHGND HUD glyph question;
	? A = 83 -> VHGND HUD glyph five; ? A = 81 -> VHGND HUD glyph Q;
	? A = 67 -> VHGND HUD glyph letter C; ? A = 45 -> VHGND HUD glyph hyphen;
	? A = 46 -> VHGND HUD glyph dot; ? A = 58 -> VHGND HUD glyph colon;
	? A = 69 -> VHGND HUD glyph E; ? A = 80 -> VHGND HUD glyph P;
	? A = 79 -> VHGND HUD glyph zero; ? A = 38 -> VHGND HUD glyph ampersand;
	? A = 65 -> VHGND HUD glyph letter A; ? A = 66 -> VHGND HUD glyph letter B;
	? A = 70 -> VHGND HUD glyph letter F; ? A = 74 -> VHGND HUD glyph letter J;
	? A = 71 -> VHGND HUD glyph letter G; ? A = 73 -> VHGND HUD glyph letter I;
	? A = 75 -> VHGND HUD glyph letter K; ? A = 76 -> VHGND HUD glyph letter L;
	? A = 77 -> VHGND HUD glyph letter M;
	? A = 82 -> VHGND HUD glyph letter R; ? A = 84 -> VHGND HUD glyph letter T;
	? A = 85 -> VHGND HUD glyph letter U; ? A = 86 -> VHGND HUD glyph letter V;
	? A = 87 -> VHGND HUD glyph letter W; ? A = 88 -> VHGND HUD glyph letter X;
	? A = 89 -> VHGND HUD glyph letter Y; ? A = 90 -> VHGND HUD glyph letter Z;
	? A = 64 -> VHGND HUD glyph at;
	? A = 43 -> VHGND HUD glyph add sign;
	? A = 68 -> VHGND HUD glyph letter D; ? A = 72 -> VHGND HUD glyph letter H;
	? A = 78 -> VHGND HUD glyph letter N; ? A = 92 -> VHGND HUD glyph backslash;
	-> VHGND HUD glyph selected;
    "VHGND HUD glyph zero"
	[VHGNDhudpacked] = 31599; -> VHGND HUD glyph selected;
    "VHGND HUD glyph one"
	[VHGNDhudpacked] = 29843; -> VHGND HUD glyph selected;
    "VHGND HUD glyph two"
	[VHGNDhudpacked] = 29671; -> VHGND HUD glyph selected;
    "VHGND HUD glyph three"
	[VHGNDhudpacked] = 31143; -> VHGND HUD glyph selected;
    "VHGND HUD glyph four"
	[VHGNDhudpacked] = 20340; -> VHGND HUD glyph selected;
    "VHGND HUD glyph five"
	[VHGNDhudpacked] = 31183; -> VHGND HUD glyph selected;
    "VHGND HUD glyph six"
	[VHGNDhudpacked] = 31695; -> VHGND HUD glyph selected;
    "VHGND HUD glyph seven"
	[VHGNDhudpacked] = 18727; -> VHGND HUD glyph selected;
    "VHGND HUD glyph eight"
	[VHGNDhudpacked] = 31727; -> VHGND HUD glyph selected;
    "VHGND HUD glyph nine"
	[VHGNDhudpacked] = 18927; -> VHGND HUD glyph selected;
    "VHGND HUD glyph open paren"
	[VHGNDhudpacked] = 17556; -> VHGND HUD glyph selected;
    "VHGND HUD glyph close paren"
	[VHGNDhudpacked] = 5265; -> VHGND HUD glyph selected;
    "VHGND HUD glyph asterisk"
	[VHGNDhudpacked] = 9664; -> VHGND HUD glyph selected;
    "VHGND HUD glyph comma"
	[VHGNDhudpacked] = 5120; -> VHGND HUD glyph selected;
    "VHGND HUD glyph slash"
	[VHGNDhudpacked] = 672; -> VHGND HUD glyph selected;
    "VHGND HUD glyph question"
	[VHGNDhudpacked] = 8615; -> VHGND HUD glyph selected;
    "VHGND HUD glyph Q"
	[VHGNDhudpacked] = 21359; -> VHGND HUD glyph selected;
    "VHGND HUD glyph letter C"
	[VHGNDhudpacked] = 29263; -> VHGND HUD glyph selected;
    "VHGND HUD glyph hyphen"
	[VHGNDhudpacked] = 448; -> VHGND HUD glyph selected;
    "VHGND HUD glyph dot"
	[VHGNDhudpacked] = 8192; -> VHGND HUD glyph selected;
    "VHGND HUD glyph colon"
	[VHGNDhudpacked] = 1040;
	-> VHGND HUD glyph selected;
    "VHGND HUD glyph E"
	[VHGNDhudpacked] = 29391; -> VHGND HUD glyph selected;
    "VHGND HUD glyph P"
	[VHGNDhudpacked] = 5103; -> VHGND HUD glyph selected;
    "VHGND HUD glyph ampersand"
	[VHGNDhudpacked] = 128; -> VHGND HUD glyph selected;
    "VHGND HUD glyph letter A"
	[VHGNDhudpacked] = 23535; -> VHGND HUD glyph selected;
    "VHGND HUD glyph letter B"
	[VHGNDhudpacked] = 31471; -> VHGND HUD glyph selected;
    "VHGND HUD glyph letter F"
	[VHGNDhudpacked] = 4815; -> VHGND HUD glyph selected;
    "VHGND HUD glyph letter G"
	[VHGNDhudpacked] = 31567; -> VHGND HUD glyph selected;
    "VHGND HUD glyph letter I"
	[VHGNDhudpacked] = 9362; -> VHGND HUD glyph selected;
    "VHGND HUD glyph letter J"
	[VHGNDhudpacked] = 31524; -> VHGND HUD glyph selected;
    "VHGND HUD glyph letter K"
	[VHGNDhudpacked] = 23277; -> VHGND HUD glyph selected;
    "VHGND HUD glyph letter L"
	[VHGNDhudpacked] = 29257; -> VHGND HUD glyph selected;
    "VHGND HUD glyph letter M"
	[VHGNDhudpacked] = 23423; -> VHGND HUD glyph selected;
    "VHGND HUD glyph letter R"
	[VHGNDhudpacked] = 23279; -> VHGND HUD glyph selected;
    "VHGND HUD glyph letter T"
	[VHGNDhudpacked] = 9367; -> VHGND HUD glyph selected;
    "VHGND HUD glyph letter U"
	[VHGNDhudpacked] = 31597; -> VHGND HUD glyph selected;
    "VHGND HUD glyph letter V"
	[VHGNDhudpacked] = 11117; -> VHGND HUD glyph selected;
    "VHGND HUD glyph letter W"
	[VHGNDhudpacked] = 24557; -> VHGND HUD glyph selected;
    "VHGND HUD glyph letter X"
	[VHGNDhudpacked] = 23213; -> VHGND HUD glyph selected;
    "VHGND HUD glyph letter Y"
	[VHGNDhudpacked] = 9709; -> VHGND HUD glyph selected;
    "VHGND HUD glyph letter Z"
	[VHGNDhudpacked] = 29351; -> VHGND HUD glyph selected;
    "VHGND HUD glyph at"
	[VHGNDhudpacked] = 16; -> VHGND HUD glyph selected;
    "VHGND HUD glyph add sign"
	[VHGNDhudpacked] = 1488; -> VHGND HUD glyph selected;
    "VHGND HUD glyph letter D"
	[VHGNDhudpacked] = 15211; -> VHGND HUD glyph selected;
    "VHGND HUD glyph letter H"
	[VHGNDhudpacked] = 23533; -> VHGND HUD glyph selected;
    "VHGND HUD glyph letter N"
	[VHGNDhudpacked] = 24573; -> VHGND HUD glyph selected;
    "VHGND HUD glyph backslash"
	[VHGNDhudpacked] = 6105;
    "VHGND HUD glyph selected"
	A = [VHGNDhudpacked]; [VHGNDhudshift] = [VHGNDhudrow];
    "VHGND HUD glyph shift"
	C = [VHGNDhudshift]; ? C = 0 -> VHGND HUD glyph mask ready;
	A / 8; [VHGNDhudshift]-; -> VHGND HUD glyph shift;
    "VHGND HUD glyph mask ready"
	A & 7; [VHGNDcompassmask] = A;
	end;

"VHGND dense atmosphere"
	( NOCTIS-1.CPP:4449-4452.  Venusian/type-2 views discard palette-band
	  identity, then run psmooth_grays over adapted+2880.  The live steady
	  page uses 14,560 dwords, so the shifted mask spans 58,240 bytes and
	  the four-row smoothing kernel writes 56,960 safely bounded bytes. )
	D = nw; D + RADPT; D + 2880; B = 58240;
    "VHGND dense mask"
	C = [D]; C & 63; [D] = C; D+;
	B ^ VHGND dense mask;

	A = nw; A + RADPT; A + 2880; [SUsp] = A; [VHGNDdensebase] = A;
	[SUsi] = 320; B = 56960;
    "VHGND dense smooth"
	=> VHGND dense sample native;
	[SUsi]+;
	B ^ VHGND dense smooth;
	end;

"VHGND dense sample native"
	( Exact in-place psmooth_grays sample.  Packing four adjacent pixel units
	  into each little-endian dword preserves the source's inter-byte carries;
	  EBX is saved because the outer Lino loop owns B as its counter. )
	{
	    53                                      (push ebx)
	    8B B7 <dVHGNDdensebase mtp bytesperunit>
	    8D 34 B7                               (esi = workspace byte address)
	    8B 8F <dSUsi mtp bytesperunit>         (ecx = destination unit index)

	    8B 84 8E 00 FB FF FF 25 FF 00 00 00
	    8B 94 8E 04 FB FF FF 81 E2 FF 00 00 00 C1 E2 08 09 D0
	    8B 94 8E 08 FB FF FF 81 E2 FF 00 00 00 C1 E2 10 09 D0
	    8B 94 8E 0C FB FF FF 81 E2 FF 00 00 00 C1 E2 18 09 D0
	    89 C3                                  (first packed row)

	    8B 04 8E 25 FF 00 00 00
	    8B 54 8E 04 81 E2 FF 00 00 00 C1 E2 08 09 D0
	    8B 54 8E 08 81 E2 FF 00 00 00 C1 E2 10 09 D0
	    8B 54 8E 0C 81 E2 FF 00 00 00 C1 E2 18 09 D0
	    01 C3                                  (second packed row)

	    8B 84 8E 00 05 00 00 25 FF 00 00 00
	    8B 94 8E 04 05 00 00 81 E2 FF 00 00 00 C1 E2 08 09 D0
	    8B 94 8E 08 05 00 00 81 E2 FF 00 00 00 C1 E2 10 09 D0
	    8B 94 8E 0C 05 00 00 81 E2 FF 00 00 00 C1 E2 18 09 D0
	    01 C3                                  (third packed row)

	    8B 84 8E 00 0A 00 00 25 FF 00 00 00
	    8B 94 8E 04 0A 00 00 81 E2 FF 00 00 00 C1 E2 08 09 D0
	    8B 94 8E 08 0A 00 00 81 E2 FF 00 00 00 C1 E2 10 09 D0
	    8B 94 8E 0C 0A 00 00 81 E2 FF 00 00 00 C1 E2 18 09 D0
	    01 C3                                  (fourth packed row)

	    81 E3 FC FC FC FC C1 EB 02            (mask lanes, divide by four)
	    89 D8 25 FF 00 00 00                  (eax = lane 0)
	    89 DA C1 EA 08 81 E2 FF 00 00 00 01 D0 25 FF 00 00 00
	    C1 EB 10 89 DA 81 E2 FF 00 00 00 01 D0 25 FF 00 00 00
	    C1 EB 08 81 E3 FF 00 00 00 01 D8 25 FF 00 00 00
	    C1 E8 02                               (horizontal average)
	    89 04 8E                               (write current unit in place)
	    5B                                      (pop ebx)
	}
	end;

"VHGND weather lightning begin"
	( flash is selected at the end of one source frame and consumed before
	  background() on the next. Presentation-only frames retain that state. )
	A = [VHGNDdosim]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND weather lightning begin done;
	[VHGNDflashactive] = 0;
	A = [VHGNDatmosphere]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND weather lightning clear;
	A = [GRiptype]; ? A != 3 -> VHGND weather lightning clear;
	A = [GRSKrainy]; ? A '>= 40000000h -> VHGND weather lightning consume;
	A = [VHGNDstormflashes]; ? A > 5 -> VHGND weather lightning consume;
    "VHGND weather lightning clear"
	[VHGNDflashpending] = 0; -> VHGND weather lightning palette;
    "VHGND weather lightning consume"
	[VHGNDflashactive] = [VHGNDflashpending]; [VHGNDflashpending] = 0;
	A = [VHGNDflashactive]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND weather lightning palette;
	A = [VHGNDseed]; A + [VHGNDtick]; A '* 1103515245; A + 12345;
	[VHGNDrainseed] = A; => VHGND weather random;
	A = [VHGNDrainrnd]; A & 7FFFFFFFh; A % 64; A + 64; [VHGNDflashgain] = A;
	[VHGNDstormflashes]+; A = [VHGNDstormflashes];
	? A '<= 30 -> VHGND weather lightning palette; [VHGNDstormflashes] = 30;
    "VHGND weather lightning palette"
	=> VHGND weather flash palette;
    "VHGND weather lightning begin done"
	end;

"VHGND render weather"
	( NOCTIS-1.CPP planetary_main weather gate.  Lightning begins at rainy
	  2.0 on habitable type-3 worlds; rain sticks begin above 3.0.  Cadence
	  state advances only on the source-rate simulation tick, while the drop
	  field uses the presentation frame so 60 Hz mode remains visually fluid. )
	[VHGNDrainstreaks] = 0;
	A = [VHGNDatmosphere]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND weather dry;
	A = [GRiptype]; ? A != 3 -> VHGND weather dry;
	A = [GRSKrainy]; ? A '< 40000000h -> VHGND weather dry;

	A = [VHGNDdosim]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND weather rain gate;
	( rainy is a non-negative binary32.  Its integer bit order is monotonic,
	  so these source thresholds need no floating compare. )
	( random(150/rainy): retain the source's continuous raininess rather than
	  replacing it with three coarse probability buckets. )
	[FS0] = [GRSKrainy]; => FLoadF32; [FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1];
	[FI] = 150; => IntToF; => FQuo; => FToIntChop; [VHGNDflashden] = [FI];
	A = [VHGNDflashden]; ? A > 0 -> VHGND weather denominator ready;
	[VHGNDflashden] = 1;
    "VHGND weather denominator ready"
	A = [VHGNDseed]; A + [VHGNDtick]; A '* 1664525; A + 1013904223; [VHGNDrainseed] = A;
	=> VHGND weather random; A = [VHGNDrainrnd]; A & 7FFFFFFFh; A % 3; A + 1;
	[VHGNDflashtries] = A;
    "VHGND weather flash roll"
	=> VHGND weather random; A = [VHGNDrainrnd]; A & 7FFFFFFFh; A % [VHGNDflashden];
	[VHGNDflashpending] = 0; ? A != 0 -> VHGND weather flash roll next;
	[VHGNDflashpending] = 1;
    "VHGND weather flash roll next"
	[VHGNDflashtries]-; A = [VHGNDflashtries]; ? A != 0 -> VHGND weather flash roll;
    "VHGND weather rain gate"
	A = [GRSKrainy]; ? A '<= 40400000h -> VHGND weather done;

	( Source density is random(25*flashes)+50.  Flashes itself is already
	  source-capped at 30, so mature storms retain the full 50..799 field. )
	A = [VHGNDstormflashes];
	A '* 25; [VHGNDflashtries] = A;
	A = [VHGNDseed]; A + [VHGframe]; A '* 7919; A + [VHGNDtick]; [VHGNDrainseed] = A;
	[VHGNDraincount] = 50; A = [VHGNDflashtries]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND weather count ready;
	=> VHGND weather random; A = [VHGNDrainrnd]; A & 7FFFFFFFh; A % [VHGNDflashtries];
	A + 50; [VHGNDraincount] = A;
    "VHGND weather count ready"

	( wdircos/wp and wdirsin/wp, narrowed to integer world units once for
	  the whole field.  The pod owns the live source wind state. )
	A = [VHGCwindangle]; A + 8; A / 16; [VHVangle] = A; => VHV sincos;
	[FI] = [VHGCwindpower]; => IntToF; [PGFi] = FSW1; => PGF sa;
	[PGFi] = FSW0; [PGFt] = [VHVcos]; => PGF setf32;
	[PGFi] = FSW0; => PGF a; [PGFi] = FSW1; => PGF mul; => PGF int;
	A = [FI]; A / 10; [VHGNDrainwindx] = A;
	[PGFi] = FSW0; [PGFt] = [VHVsin]; => PGF setf32;
	[PGFi] = FSW0; => PGF a; [PGFi] = FSW1; => PGF mul; => PGF int;
	A = [FI]; A / 10; [VHGNDrainwindz] = A;
	( The source subtracts one third of this tick's player displacement from
	  the rain vector, so walking changes the apparent streak direction. )
	A = [VHGNDcamx]; A - [VHGNDplayerrefx]; A '* 333; A / 1000;
	C = [VHGNDrainwindx]; C - A; [VHGNDrainwindx] = C;
	A = [VHGNDcamz]; A - [VHGNDplayerrefz]; A '* 333; A / 1000;
	C = [VHGNDrainwindz]; C - A; [VHGNDrainwindz] = C;
	A = [VHGframe]; A % 18; A '* 100; [VHGNDrainphase] = A;
	( setfx(1); Forward(-1000): move only the rain camera backward along the
	  current view and use the source's luminous stick mode. )
