	[VHVangle] = [VHGNDbeta]; => VHV sincos;
	[VHGNDrainviewsinb] = [VHVsin]; [VHGNDrainviewcosb] = [VHVcos];
	[VHVangle] = [VHGNDalpha]; => VHV sincos;
	[VHGNDrainviewsina] = [VHVsin]; [VHGNDrainviewcosa] = [VHVcos];
	[FS0] = [VHGNDrainviewsinb]; => FLoadF32; [FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1];
	[FS0] = [VHGNDrainviewcosa]; => FLoadF32; => FMul; [FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1];
	[FI] = 1000; => IntToF; => FMul; => FToIntNear;
	A = [VHGNDcamx]; A + [FI]; [VHVcamxi] = A;
	[FS0] = [VHGNDrainviewcosb]; => FLoadF32; [FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1];
	[FS0] = [VHGNDrainviewcosa]; => FLoadF32; => FMul; [FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1];
	[FI] = 1000; => IntToF; => FMul; => FToIntNear;
	A = [VHGNDcamz]; A - [FI]; [VHVcamzi] = A;
	[FS0] = [VHGNDrainviewsina]; => FLoadF32; [FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1];
	[FI] = 1000; => IntToF; => FMul; => FToIntNear;
	A = [VHGNDcamy]; A - [FI]; [VHVcamyi] = A;
	=> VH set view;
	[VHGNDrainindex] = 0; [VHScolor] = 63;
	[VHSflare] = 1;
    "VHGND weather rain one"
	=> VHGND weather random; A = [VHGNDrainrnd]; A & 1023; C = [VHGNDcamx]; C + A; [VHGNDrainx] = C;
	=> VHGND weather random; A = [VHGNDrainrnd]; A & 1023; C = [VHGNDrainx]; C - A; [VHGNDrainx] = C;
	=> VHGND weather random; A = [VHGNDrainrnd]; A & 1023; C = [VHGNDcamz]; C + A; [VHGNDrainz] = C;
	=> VHGND weather random; A = [VHGNDrainrnd]; A & 1023; C = [VHGNDrainz]; C - A; [VHGNDrainz] = C;
	=> VHGND weather random; A = [VHGNDrainrnd]; A & 2047; C = [VHGNDcamy]; C - A;
	C + [VHGNDrainphase]; [VHGNDrainy] = C;
	A = [VHGNDrainx]; A + [VHGNDrainwindx]; [VHGNDrainx2] = A;
	A = [VHGNDrainy]; A + 100; [VHGNDrainy2] = A;
	A = [VHGNDrainz]; A + [VHGNDrainwindz]; [VHGNDrainz2] = A;

	[FI] = [VHGNDrainx]; => IntToF; => F32Narrow; => FStoreF32; [VHSx0] = [FS0];
	[FI] = [VHGNDrainy]; => IntToF; => F32Narrow; => FStoreF32; [VHSy0] = [FS0];
	[FI] = [VHGNDrainz]; => IntToF; => F32Narrow; => FStoreF32; [VHSz0] = [FS0];
	[FI] = [VHGNDrainx2]; => IntToF; => F32Narrow; => FStoreF32; [VHSx1] = [FS0];
	[FI] = [VHGNDrainy2]; => IntToF; => F32Narrow; => FStoreF32; [VHSy1] = [FS0];
	[FI] = [VHGNDrainz2]; => IntToF; => F32Narrow; => FStoreF32; [VHSz1] = [FS0];
	=> VH stick3d; [VHGNDrainstreaks]+;
	[VHGNDrainindex]+; A = [VHGNDrainindex]; ? A '< [VHGNDraincount] -> VHGND weather rain one;
	[VHSflare] = 0;
	[VHVcamxi] = [VHGNDcamx]; [VHVcamyi] = [VHGNDcamy]; [VHVcamzi] = [VHGNDcamz];
	=> VH set view;
	-> VHGND weather done;

    "VHGND weather dry"
    "VHGND weather done"
	end;

"VHGND weather random"
	( Weather-local LCG: clock-seeded source randomness without disturbing
	  either terrain's fast_random stream or the live Borland wind stream. )
	A = [VHGNDrainseed]; A '* 1664525; A + 1013904223; [VHGNDrainseed] = A;
	[VHGNDrainrnd] = A;
	end;

"VHGND weather flash palette"
	A = [VHGNDflashactive]; ? A != [VHGNDflashlast] -> VHGND weather palette changed;
	end;
    "VHGND weather palette changed"
	[VHGNDflashlast] = A;
	[VHGNDflashpalp] = srfpal6; [VHGNDflashpalq] = pal6; [VHGNDflashpaln] = 768;
	? A = 0 -> VHGND weather palette restore;
    "VHGND weather palette brighten"
	C = [VHGNDflashpalp]; C = [C]; C & 255; C '* [VHGNDflashgain]; C '/ 63;
	? C '<= 63 -> VHGND weather palette bright component; C = 63;
    "VHGND weather palette bright component"
	D = [VHGNDflashpalq]; [D] = C;
	[VHGNDflashpalp]+; [VHGNDflashpalq]+; [VHGNDflashpaln]-;
	A = [VHGNDflashpaln]; ? A != 0 -> VHGND weather palette brighten;
	-> VHGND weather palette upload;
    "VHGND weather palette restore"
	C = [VHGNDflashpalp]; C = [C]; D = [VHGNDflashpalq]; [D] = C;
	[VHGNDflashpalp]+; [VHGNDflashpalq]+; [VHGNDflashpaln]-;
	A = [VHGNDflashpaln]; ? A != 0 -> VHGND weather palette restore;
    "VHGND weather palette upload"
	[PUn] = 256; => PAL upload;
	end;

"VHGND water"
	( NIV+'s horizon quad surrounds the temporary camera.  Feeding its two
	  behind-eye corners to this port's non-clipping 32-bit mapper produced
	  the observed rainbow wedges. Rasterize the analytically clipped result
	  directly into the background: terrain and objects still overpaint it in
	  the original order, while the sea keeps the source 128/144 palette bands. )
	=> VHGND water backdrop;
	( The source reflects landscape only when the sea has no incoming wind
	  waves, and always on the frozen ICY scenario. )
	=> VHGND reflection;
	A = [VHGNDsctype]; ? A != 1 -> VHGND water waves done; => VHGND waves;
    "VHGND water waves done"

	( Restore fragment()'s ground texture scale and normal polygon mode. )
	[FI] = [VHGNDtscale]; => IntToF;
	[FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1]; [FI] = 256; => IntToF; => FMul;
	[PGFi] = FSTX; => PGF sa; [PGFi] = FSTY; => PGF sa;
	[PGtexf] = 5; [SPflar] = 0; [DBflar] = 0; [SPhalf] = 0; [SPcull] = 0;
	end;

"VHGND water backdrop"
	( The logical horizon moves about five rows per pitch degree in the source
	  320x200 projection. Keep it inside TDPOLYGS.H's y=10..190 guard. )
	A = [VHGNDalpha]; A '* 5; C = 100; C - A; [VHGNDwaterhorizon] = C;
	? C >= 10 -> VHGND water horizon low ready; [VHGNDwaterhorizon] = 10;
    "VHGND water horizon low ready"
	A = [VHGNDwaterhorizon]; ? A '<= 190 -> VHGND water horizon ready;
	[VHGNDwaterhorizon] = 190;
    "VHGND water horizon ready"
	C = 191; C - [VHGNDwaterhorizon]; [VHGNDwaterden] = C;
	[VHGNDwatery] = [VHGNDwaterhorizon];
    "VHGND water backdrop row"
	A = [GRSKnightzone]; [VHGNDwaterbase] = 128; ? A != 0 -> VHGND water backdrop colour;
	A = [VHGNDwatery]; A - [VHGNDwaterhorizon]; A '* 16; A '/ [VHGNDwaterden];
	? A '<= 15 -> VHGND water backdrop band ready; A = 15;
    "VHGND water backdrop band ready"
	A + 144; [VHGNDwaterbase] = A;
    "VHGND water backdrop colour"
	A = [VHGNDwatery]; A '* 320; A + 5; A + RADPT; A + nw; [VHGNDwaterptr] = A;
	[VHGNDwatercount] = 307;
    "VHGND water backdrop pixel"
	A = [VHGNDwaterptr]; C = [VHGNDwaterbase]; [A] = C; [VHGNDwaterptr]+;
	[VHGNDwatercount]-; A = [VHGNDwatercount]; ? A != 0 -> VHGND water backdrop pixel;
	[VHGNDwatery]+; A = [VHGNDwatery]; ? A '< 191 -> VHGND water backdrop row;
	end;

"VHGND reflection"
	[VHGNDreflected] = 0;
	A = [VHGlanded]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND reflection done;
	A = [VHGNDsctype]; ? A = 4 -> VHGND reflection active;
	A = [VHGNDwavesin]; ? A != 0 -> VHGND reflection done;
    "VHGND reflection active"
	[VHGNDmirror] = 1; [SPhalf] = 1; [SPflar] = 0; [DBflar] = 0;
	[FI] = [VHGNDtscale]; => IntToF;
	[FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1]; [FI] = 256; => IntToF; => FMul;
	[PGFi] = FSTX; => PGF sa; [PGFi] = FSTY; => PGF sa;
	[VHGNDlodstep] = 16; [VHGNDlodradius] = VHGNDFAR;
	[VHGNDmindepth] = VHGNDMID; [VHGNDmaxdepth] = VHGNDFAR; => VHGND traverse;
	[VHGNDlodstep] = 8; [VHGNDlodradius] = VHGNDMID;
	[VHGNDmindepth] = VHGNDHALF; [VHGNDmaxdepth] = VHGNDMID; => VHGND traverse;
	[VHGNDlodstep] = 1; [VHGNDlodradius] = VHGNDHALF;
	A = 0; A - 1; [VHGNDmindepth] = A; [VHGNDmaxdepth] = VHGNDHALF; => VHGND traverse;
	=> VHGND render ruins;
	[VHGNDmirror] = 0; [SPhalf] = 0;
    "VHGND reflection done"
	end;

"VHGND waves init"
	( planetary_main seeds ten incoming wind waves around (1E6,1E6), then
	  reserves records 10..24 for ripples made by the swimmer.  Keep those
	  source counts, ranges and record roles; the fast RNG leaves the terrain
	  generator's Borland stream untouched. )
	[VHGNDwavei] = 0; [VHGNDwavelw] = 10; [VHGNDwavesin] = 0;
	[VHGNDwaveblur] = 0; [VHGNDwavehits] = 0;
	[VHGNDimpactx] = 0; [VHGNDimpactz] = 0; [VHGNDimpacttilt] = 0;
	[VHGNDwavelastx] = [VHGNDdropx]; [VHGNDwavelastz] = [VHGNDdropz];
	A = [GRiptype]; ? A != 3 -> VHGND waves wipe all;
	A = [VHGNDsctype]; ? A != 1 -> VHGND waves wipe all;
	A = [VHGNDwavescalm]; ? A != 0 -> VHGND waves wipe all;
	( Open sea carries incoming swell.  At albedo 20 the source's random(2)
	  sometimes makes a calm rocky ocean; use a stable seed bit for that
	  surface-local choice. )
	[VHGNDwavesin] = 1; A = [GRalbedo]; ? A '<= 16 -> VHGND waves seed incoming;
	A = [VHGNDseed]; A > 7; A & 1; ? A = 0 -> VHGND waves seed incoming;
	[VHGNDwavesin] = 0; -> VHGND waves wipe all;
    "VHGND waves seed incoming"
	A = [VHGNDseed]; A + 5741; => SU fast srand;
    "VHGND wave init incoming"
	A = [VHGNDwavei]; A '* 7; A + VHGNDwavedata; [VHGNDwavep] = A;
	[SUfmask] = 32767; => SU frnd; A = C; A '% 30000; A + 1000000; [VHGNDtmp] = A;
	[SUfmask] = 32767; => SU frnd; A = C; A '% 30000; C = [VHGNDtmp]; C - A; [VHGNDtmp] = C;
	A = [VHGNDwavep]; [A plus 0] = [VHGNDtmp]; [A plus 1] = 50;
	[SUfmask] = 32767; => SU frnd; A = C; A '% 30000; A + 1000000; [VHGNDtmp] = A;
	[SUfmask] = 32767; => SU frnd; A = C; A '% 30000; C = [VHGNDtmp]; C - A; [VHGNDtmp] = C;
	A = [VHGNDwavep]; [A plus 2] = [VHGNDtmp];
	[SUfmask] = 32767; => SU frnd; C '% 2500; C '* 1000; A = 2500000; A - C; C = [VHGNDwavep]; [C plus 3] = A;
	[SUfmask] = 2047; => SU frnd; A = C; A '% 1500; ? A != 0 -> VHGND wave init height; A = 100;
    "VHGND wave init height"
	C = [VHGNDwavep]; [C plus 4] = A;
	[SUfmask] = 8191; => SU frnd; A = C; A '% 5000; A + 1000; C = [VHGNDwavep]; [C plus 5] = A;
	[SUfmask] = 511; => SU frnd; A = C; A '% 500; A + 500; C = 0; C - A; A = [VHGNDwavep]; [A plus 6] = C;
	[VHGNDwavei]+; A = [VHGNDwavei]; ? A < 10 -> VHGND wave init incoming;
	-> VHGND waves wipe outgoing;
    "VHGND waves wipe all"
	[VHGNDwavei] = 0; -> VHGND wave init outgoing;
    "VHGND waves wipe outgoing"
	( Non-ocean calls also clear every outgoing slot, preventing old ripples
	  from surviving a checkpoint load or a later landing. )
	[VHGNDwavei] = 10;
    "VHGND wave init outgoing"
	A = [VHGNDwavei]; A '* 7; A + VHGNDwavedata; [A plus 3] = 0;
	[VHGNDwavei]+; A = [VHGNDwavei]; ? A < 25 -> VHGND wave init outgoing;
	end;

"VHGND waves"
	( NOCTIS-1.CPP incoming/outgoing wave rings.  Ten wind rings contract
	  toward their source centre; fifteen wake slots expand from the player
	  and flatten until they disappear. )
	A = [VHGlanded]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND waves done;
	=> VHGND wave spawn;
	[FI] = 8192; => IntToF; [PGFi] = FSTX; => PGF sa; [PGFi] = FSTY; => PGF sa;
	[PGtexf] = 5; [SPsrc] = 1; [SPtinta] = 128; [SPescr] = 0;
	[SPflar] = 1; [DBflar] = 1; [SPcull] = 1; [SPhalf] = 0;
	[VHGNDwavei] = 0;
    "VHGND wave one"
	A = [VHGNDwavei]; A '* 7; A + VHGNDwavedata; [VHGNDwavep] = A;
	A = [VHGNDwavesin]; ? A != 0 -> VHGND wave record ready;
	A = [VHGNDwavei]; ? A < 10 -> VHGND wave next;
    "VHGND wave record ready"
	C = [VHGNDwavep]; [VHGNDwavecx] = [C plus 0]; [VHGNDwavecy] = [C plus 1];
	[VHGNDwavecz] = [C plus 2]; [VHGNDwaveradius] = [C plus 3];
	[VHGNDwaveheight] = [C plus 4]; [VHGNDwavewidth] = [C plus 5];
	[VHGNDwavedelta] = [C plus 6];
	A = [VHGNDwaveradius]; ? A <= 0 -> VHGND wave next;
	[VHGNDwaveout] = 0; A = [VHGNDwavei]; ? A < 10 -> VHGND wave incoming;
	[VHGNDwaveout] = 1; => VHGND wave snapshot; -> VHGND wave range;
    "VHGND wave incoming"
	=> VHGND wave snapshot;
	A = [VHGNDdosim]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND wave incoming live;
	A = [VHGNDwaveradius]; A + [VHGNDwavedelta]; [VHGNDwaveradius] = A;
	? A > 0 -> VHGND wave incoming live;
	( Source reset after an incoming crest reaches the centre. )
	[VHGNDwaveradius] = 1500000;
	A = [VHGNDtick]; C = [VHGNDwavei]; C '* 137; A + C; A & 1023; A + 500; [VHGNDwaveheight] = A;
	A = [VHGNDtick]; A & 255; A + 500; C = 0; C - A; [VHGNDwavedelta] = C;
	=> VHGND wave snapshot;
    "VHGND wave incoming live"
	C = [VHGNDwavep]; [C plus 3] = [VHGNDwaveradius]; [C plus 4] = [VHGNDwaveheight]; [C plus 6] = [VHGNDwavedelta];
    "VHGND wave range"
	[VHGNDwaveauthradius] = [VHGNDwaveradius]; [VHGNDwaveauthheight] = [VHGNDwaveheight];
	A = [VHGNDwavecx]; A - [VHGNDcamx]; ? A >= 0 -> VHGND wave absx; C = 0; C - A; A = C;
    "VHGND wave absx"
	[VHGNDwavedx] = A;
	A = [VHGNDwavecz]; A - [VHGNDcamz]; ? A >= 0 -> VHGND wave absz; C = 0; C - A; A = C;
    "VHGND wave absz"
	[VHGNDwavedz] = A;
	C = [VHGNDwavedx]; ? A '<= C -> VHGND wave dist zmax;
	A = C; C = [VHGNDwavedz]; C / 2; A + C; [VHGNDwavedist] = A; -> VHGND wave dist ready;
    "VHGND wave dist zmax"
	C = [VHGNDwavedx]; C / 2; A + C; [VHGNDwavedist] = A;
    "VHGND wave dist ready"
	=> VHGND wave impact check;
	A = [VHGNDwaveout]; ? A != 0 -> VHGND wave outgoing range;
	A = [VHGNDwavedist]; A - [VHGNDwaveradius]; ? A >= 0 -> VHGND wave radial abs; C = 0; C - A; A = C;
    "VHGND wave radial abs"
	C = [VHGNDwaveradius]; C / 2; C + 131072; ? A > C -> VHGND wave stored;
	-> VHGND wave draw;
    "VHGND wave outgoing range"
	A = [VHGNDwaveradius]; A + 262144; ? [VHGNDwavedist] > A -> VHGND wave stored;
    "VHGND wave draw"
	=> VHGND wave interpolate;
	A = [VHGNDwaveradius]; C = [VHGNDwavewidth];
	? [VHGNDwaveout] != 0 -> VHGND wave outer width; A - C; -> VHGND wave inner ready;
    "VHGND wave outer width"
	A + C;
    "VHGND wave inner ready"
	[VHGNDwaveinner] = A; [VHGNDwaveseg] = 0;
    "VHGND wave segment"
	A = [VHGNDwaveseg]; A '* 18; [VHGNDwaveangle] = A;
	A = [VHGNDwaveout]; ? A != 0 -> VHGND wave segment outgoing;
	[VHGNDwavepr] = [VHGNDwaveinner]; => VHGND wave point; [VHGNDwavep0x] = [VHGNDwavepx]; [VHGNDwavep0z] = [VHGNDwavepz];
	[VHGNDwavepr] = [VHGNDwaveradius]; => VHGND wave point; [VHGNDwavep1x] = [VHGNDwavepx]; [VHGNDwavep1z] = [VHGNDwavepz];
	A = [VHGNDwaveangle]; A + 18; [VHGNDwaveangle] = A;
	[VHGNDwavepr] = [VHGNDwaveradius]; => VHGND wave point; [VHGNDwavep2x] = [VHGNDwavepx]; [VHGNDwavep2z] = [VHGNDwavepz];
	[VHGNDwavepr] = [VHGNDwaveinner]; => VHGND wave point; [VHGNDwavep3x] = [VHGNDwavepx]; [VHGNDwavep3z] = [VHGNDwavepz];
	-> VHGND wave segment submit;
    "VHGND wave segment outgoing"
	[VHGNDwavepr] = [VHGNDwaveinner]; => VHGND wave point; [VHGNDwavep0x] = [VHGNDwavepx]; [VHGNDwavep0z] = [VHGNDwavepz];
	[VHGNDwavepr] = [VHGNDwaveradius]; => VHGND wave point; [VHGNDwavep1x] = [VHGNDwavepx]; [VHGNDwavep1z] = [VHGNDwavepz];
	A = [VHGNDwaveangle]; A + 18; [VHGNDwaveangle] = A;
	[VHGNDwavepr] = [VHGNDwaveradius]; => VHGND wave point; [VHGNDwavep2x] = [VHGNDwavepx]; [VHGNDwavep2z] = [VHGNDwavepz];
	[VHGNDwavepr] = [VHGNDwaveinner]; => VHGND wave point; [VHGNDwavep3x] = [VHGNDwavepx]; [VHGNDwavep3z] = [VHGNDwavepz];
	=> VHGND wave water point; A = [VHGNDwavewet]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND wave segment next;
    "VHGND wave segment submit"
	[VHGNDvi] = 0; [VHGNDvv] = [VHGNDwavep0x]; [VHGNDvslot] = FSINX; => VHGND vload;
	A = [VHGNDwavecy]; A - [VHGNDwaveheight]; [VHGNDvv] = A; [VHGNDvslot] = FSINY; => VHGND vload;
	[VHGNDvv] = [VHGNDwavep0z]; [VHGNDvslot] = FSINZ; => VHGND vload; [VHGNDvi]+;
	[VHGNDvv] = [VHGNDwavep1x]; [VHGNDvslot] = FSINX; => VHGND vload; [VHGNDvv] = [VHGNDwavecy]; [VHGNDvslot] = FSINY; => VHGND vload;
	[VHGNDvv] = [VHGNDwavep1z]; [VHGNDvslot] = FSINZ; => VHGND vload; [VHGNDvi]+;
	[VHGNDvv] = [VHGNDwavep2x]; [VHGNDvslot] = FSINX; => VHGND vload; [VHGNDvv] = [VHGNDwavecy]; [VHGNDvslot] = FSINY; => VHGND vload;
	[VHGNDvv] = [VHGNDwavep2z]; [VHGNDvslot] = FSINZ; => VHGND vload; [VHGNDvi]+;
	[VHGNDvv] = [VHGNDwavep3x]; [VHGNDvslot] = FSINX; => VHGND vload;
	A = [VHGNDwavecy]; A - [VHGNDwaveheight]; [VHGNDvv] = A; [VHGNDvslot] = FSINY; => VHGND vload;
	[VHGNDvv] = [VHGNDwavep3z]; [VHGNDvslot] = FSINZ; => VHGND vload;
	[PJnrv] = 4; => PG polymap;
    "VHGND wave segment next"
	[VHGNDwaveseg]+; A = [VHGNDwaveseg]; ? A < 20 -> VHGND wave segment;
    "VHGND wave stored"
	[VHGNDwaveradius] = [VHGNDwaveauthradius]; [VHGNDwaveheight] = [VHGNDwaveauthheight];
	A = [VHGNDwaveout]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND wave next;
	A = [VHGNDdosim]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND wave next;
	A = [VHGNDwaveradius]; A + [VHGNDwavedelta]; [VHGNDwaveradius] = A;
	A = [VHGNDwaveheight]; A '* 40; A / 41; [VHGNDwaveheight] = A;
	? A < 8 -> VHGND wave expire; C '* 100; ? [VHGNDwaveradius] > C -> VHGND wave expire;
	C = [VHGNDwavep]; [C plus 3] = [VHGNDwaveradius]; [C plus 4] = [VHGNDwaveheight]; -> VHGND wave next;
    "VHGND wave expire"
	C = [VHGNDwavep]; [C plus 3] = 0;
    "VHGND wave next"
	[VHGNDwavei]+; A = [VHGNDwavei]; ? A < 25 -> VHGND wave one;
    "VHGND waves done"
	end;

"VHGND wave snapshot"
	A = [VHGNDdosim]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND wave snapshot done;
	A = [VHGNDwavei]; C = VHGNDwaveprevradius; C + A; [C] = [VHGNDwaveradius];
	C = VHGNDwaveprevheight; C + A; [C] = [VHGNDwaveheight];
    "VHGND wave snapshot done"
	end;

"VHGND wave interpolate"
	( Render-only radius and crest-height interpolation. Impact checks and the
	  stored incoming/outgoing records continue to use authoritative source-tick
	  values. )
	A = [VHGNDfast]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND wave interpolate done;
	A = [VHGNDinterpok]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND wave interpolate done;
	A = [VHGNDwavei]; C = VHGNDwaveprevradius; C + A; [VHGNDwaveprevr] = [C];
	C = VHGNDwaveprevheight; C + A; [VHGNDwaveprevh] = [C];
	A = [VHGNDwaveauthradius]; A - [VHGNDwaveprevr]; A '* [VHGNDinterpacc]; A / 60000;
	A + [VHGNDwaveprevr]; [VHGNDwaveradius] = A;
	A = [VHGNDwaveauthheight]; A - [VHGNDwaveprevh]; A '* [VHGNDinterpacc]; A / 60000;
	A + [VHGNDwaveprevh]; [VHGNDwaveheight] = A;
    "VHGND wave interpolate done"
	end;

"VHGND wave impact tick"
	( While the wet-lens state lives, the ring pushes radially toward its
	  centre.  Updating only on simulation ticks keeps F5 presentation from
	  multiplying either the shove or the view disturbance. )
	[VHGNDimpactx] = 0; [VHGNDimpactz] = 0; [VHGNDimpacttilt] = 0;
	A = [VHGNDwaveblur]; ? A <= 0 -> VHGND wave impact tick done;
	A = [VHGNDdosim]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND wave impact tick done;
	A = [VHGNDcamx]; A - [VHGNDwavehitcx]; A / 200;
	A '* [VHGNDwavehitdelta]; A / 10000; [VHGNDimpactx] = A;
	A = [VHGNDcamz]; A - [VHGNDwavehitcz]; A / 200;
	A '* [VHGNDwavehitdelta]; A / 10000; [VHGNDimpactz] = A;
	A = [VHGNDtick]; A + [VHGNDwavehitdelta]; A & 3; A - 1; [VHGNDimpacttilt] = A;
    "VHGND wave impact tick done"
	end;

"VHGND wave impact check"
	( A crest crossing the player starts the source's one-to-three tick
	  wet-lens blur and retains this ring for its radial shove. )
	A = [VHGNDwaveout]; ? A != 0 -> VHGND wave impact checked;
	A = [VHGNDdosim]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND wave impact checked;
	A = [VHGNDcamy]; C = 0; C - 1250; ? A > C -> VHGND wave impact height;
	A = [VHGNDwaveblur]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND wave impact checked;
    "VHGND wave impact height"
	A = [VHGNDwavedist]; A - [VHGNDwaveradius]; ? A >= 0 -> VHGND wave impact radial; C = 0; C - A; A = C;
    "VHGND wave impact radial"
	C = [VHGNDwavewidth]; C '* 3; A - C; [VHGNDtmp] = A;
	A = [VHGNDwavedelta]; ? A >= 0 -> VHGND wave impact delta; C = 0; C - A; A = C;
    "VHGND wave impact delta"
	A + A; ? [VHGNDtmp] > A -> VHGND wave impact checked;
	A = [VHGNDtick]; A + [VHGNDwavei]; A '% 3; A + 1; [VHGNDwaveblur] = A;
	[VHGNDwavehitcx] = [VHGNDwavecx]; [VHGNDwavehitcz] = [VHGNDwavecz];
	[VHGNDwavehitdelta] = [VHGNDwavedelta]; [VHGNDwavehits]+;
    "VHGND wave impact checked"
	end;

"VHGND wave impact finish"
	( psmooth_64(adapted,160): exact central-page bounds and the original
	  two-by-two, low-six-bit colour average.  Direct page units keep this
	  rare one-to-three pass effect fast enough for interactive use. )
	A = [VHGNDwaveblur]; ? A <= 0 -> VHGND wave impact finish done;
	[VHGNDblurpasses] = A;
    "VHGND wave blur pass"
	[VHGNDblurp] = 2560;
    "VHGND wave blur pixel"
	D = nw; D + RADPT; D + [VHGNDblurp]; E = D; E + 320;
	A = [E]; [VHGNDblurval] = A; A & 63;
	C = E; C + 1; C = [C]; C & 63; A + C;
	C = E; C + 320; C = [C]; C & 63; A + C;
	C = E; C + 321; C = [C]; C & 63; A + C;
	A > 2; C = [VHGNDblurval]; C & 192; A | C; [D] = A;
	[VHGNDblurp]+; A = [VHGNDblurp]; ? A < 60480 -> VHGND wave blur pixel;
	[VHGNDblurpasses]-; A = [VHGNDblurpasses]; ? A > 0 -> VHGND wave blur pass;
	A = [VHGNDdosim]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND wave impact finish done; [VHGNDwaveblur]-;
    "VHGND wave impact finish done"
	end;

"VHGND wave spawn"
	( Source wakes are emitted while swimming.  A ripple is produced every
	  ten frames on sea-level terrain; movement makes it taller, wider and
	  faster, retaining the original step-dependent behavior. )
	A = [VHGNDdosim]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND wave spawn done;
	A = [VHGNDcamx]; A - [VHGNDwavelastx]; ? A >= 0 -> VHGND wave move x; C = 0; C - A; A = C;
    "VHGND wave move x"
	[VHGNDwavemove] = A;
	A = [VHGNDcamz]; A - [VHGNDwavelastz]; ? A >= 0 -> VHGND wave move z; C = 0; C - A; A = C;
    "VHGND wave move z"
	A + [VHGNDwavemove]; [VHGNDwavemove] = A;
	[VHGNDwavelastx] = [VHGNDcamx]; [VHGNDwavelastz] = [VHGNDcamz];
	A = [VHGNDtick]; A '% 10; ? A != 0 -> VHGND wave spawn done;
	[VHGNDwavep0x] = [VHGNDcamx]; [VHGNDwavep0z] = [VHGNDcamz]; => VHGND wave water point;
	A = [VHGNDwavewet]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND wave spawn done;
	A = [VHGNDwavelw]; A '* 7; A + VHGNDwavedata; [VHGNDwavep] = A;
	C = A; [C plus 0] = [VHGNDcamx]; [C plus 1] = 50; [C plus 2] = [VHGNDcamz]; [C plus 3] = 200;
	A = [VHGNDwavemove]; A '* 3; A + 100; ? A <= 2000 -> VHGND wave spawn height; A = 2000;
    "VHGND wave spawn height"
	[C plus 4] = A;
	A = [VHGNDwavemove]; A '* 2; A + 50; ? A <= 1500 -> VHGND wave spawn width; A = 1500;
    "VHGND wave spawn width"
	[C plus 5] = A;
	A = [VHGNDwavemove]; A '* 2; A + 12; ? A <= 1500 -> VHGND wave spawn delta; A = 1500;
    "VHGND wave spawn delta"
	[C plus 6] = A;
	[VHGNDwavelw]+; A = [VHGNDwavelw]; ? A < 25 -> VHGND wave spawn done; [VHGNDwavelw] = 10;
    "VHGND wave spawn done"
	end;

"VHGND wave point"
	[VHVangle] = [VHGNDwaveangle]; => VHV sincos;
	[FI] = [VHGNDwavepr]; => IntToF; [PGFi] = FSW1; => PGF sa;
	[PGFi] = FSW0; [PGFt] = [VHVcos]; => PGF setf32;
	[PGFi] = FSW0; => PGF a; [PGFi] = FSW1; => PGF mul; => PGF int;
	A = [FI]; A + [VHGNDwavecx]; [VHGNDwavepx] = A;
	[PGFi] = FSW0; [PGFt] = [VHVsin]; => PGF setf32;
	[PGFi] = FSW0; => PGF a; [PGFi] = FSW1; => PGF mul; => PGF int;
	A = [FI]; A + [VHGNDwavecz]; [VHGNDwavepz] = A;
	end;

"VHGND wave water point"
	[VHGNDwavewet] = 0; A = [VHGNDwavep0x]; ? A < 0 -> VHGND wave water done;
	? A '>= 3276800 -> VHGND wave water done; A > 14; [VHGNDx] = A;
	A = [VHGNDwavep0z]; ? A < 0 -> VHGND wave water done;
	? A '>= 3276800 -> VHGND wave water done; A > 14; A '* VHGNDMAP; A + [VHGNDx]; A + RPSM;
	[MBptr] = A; => MEM get byte; A = [MBval]; ? A != 0 -> VHGND wave water done;
	[VHGNDwavewet] = 1;
    "VHGND wave water done"
	end;

"VHGND mamm rear list"
	( Exact mamm_reartoto masks from NOCTIS-1.CPP. )
	A = pvlst;
	[A plus 0] = 0C007h; [A plus 1] = 0F008h; [A plus 2] = 0F009h;
	[A plus 3] = 0F00Eh; [A plus 4] = 6012h; [A plus 5] = 0F00Ch;
	[A plus 6] = 9013h; [A plus 7] = 0C015h; [A plus 8] = 0F00Ah;
	[A plus 9] = 0F00Fh; [A plus 10] = 0F00Dh; [A plus 11] = 0F00Bh;
	[A plus 12] = 0F02Eh; [A plus 13] = 0F02Fh; [A plus 14] = 0F032h;
	[A plus 15] = 0F033h; [A plus 16] = 7030h; [A plus 17] = 7031h;
	[A plus 18] = 0FFFh;
	end;

"VHGND mamm tail list"
	A = pvlst;
	[A plus 0] = 0F02Eh; [A plus 1] = 0F02Fh; [A plus 2] = 0F032h;
	[A plus 3] = 0F033h; [A plus 4] = 7030h; [A plus 5] = 7031h;
	[A plus 6] = 0FFFh;
	end;

"VHGND mamm legs list"
	( Exact mamm_legs masks from NOCTIS-1.CPP. )
	A = pvlst;
	[A plus 0] = 0F000h; [A plus 1] = 3002h; [A plus 2] = 1016h;
	[A plus 3] = 0F001h; [A plus 4] = 3003h; [A plus 5] = 2017h;
	[A plus 6] = 0C008h; [A plus 7] = 0F00Ah; [A plus 8] = 600Eh;
	[A plus 9] = 0F00Fh; [A plus 10] = 0C00Ch; [A plus 11] = 0F00Dh;
	[A plus 12] = 0C009h; [A plus 13] = 0F00Bh; [A plus 14] = 0FFFh;
	end;

"VHGND bird legs list"
	A = pvlst; [A plus 0] = 4012h; [A plus 1] = 2013h; [A plus 2] = 0FFFh;
	end;

"VHGND bird wing one list"
	A = pvlst; [A plus 0] = 7000h; [A plus 1] = 7001h; [A plus 2] = 0FFFh;
	end;

"VHGND bird wing two list"
	A = pvlst; [A plus 0] = 7002h; [A plus 1] = 7003h; [A plus 2] = 0FFFh;
	end;

"VHGND fauna relocate nearby"
	( live_animal() relocates an out-of-range record to player position plus
	  two independent 0..100000 fast_flandom draws on each horizontal axis.
	  Continue from live_animal's last seeded draw exactly; a custom relocation
	  reseed made each animal repeat one truncated offset forever. )
	=> VHGND fauna relocation draw; [VHGNDanioldx] = C;
	=> VHGND fauna relocation draw; A = [VHGNDcamx]; A + [VHGNDanioldx]; A - C; [VHGNDanix] = A;
	=> VHGND fauna relocation draw; [VHGNDanioldz] = C;
	=> VHGND fauna relocation draw; A = [VHGNDcamz]; A + [VHGNDanioldz]; A - C; [VHGNDaniz] = A;
	[VHGNDbirdquote] = 0; A = [VHGNDfaunatype]; ? A != 1 -> VHGND fauna relocate done;
	[SUfmask] = 32767; => SU frnd; A = C; A '* 25000; A '/ 32767; [VHGNDbirdquote] = A;
    "VHGND fauna relocate done"
	end;

"VHGND fauna relocation draw"
	( Exact floor(draw*100000/32767) without overflowing signed multiplication:
	  100000 = 3*32767 + 1699. )
	[SUfmask] = 32767; => SU frnd; A = C; A '* 3; [VHGNDtmp] = A;
	A = C; A '* 1699; A '/ 32767; A + [VHGNDtmp]; C = A;
	end;

"VHGND animal snapshot"
	A = [VHGNDanii]; C = VHGNDanimalprevx; C + A; [C] = [VHGNDanix];
	C = VHGNDanimalprevz; C + A; [C] = [VHGNDaniz];
	C = VHGNDanimalprevdir; C + A; [C] = [VHGNDanidir];
	end;

"VHGND animal interpolate"
	( live_animal renders ax/az captured before the authoritative movement.
	  Original cadence uses that old pose directly; F5 interpolates from it. )
	A = [VHGNDanii]; C = VHGNDanimalprevx; C + A; [VHGNDaniprevx] = [C];
	C = VHGNDanimalprevz; C + A; [VHGNDaniprevz] = [C];
	C = VHGNDanimalprevdir; C + A; [VHGNDtmp] = [C];
	A = [VHGNDfast]; ? A != 0 -> VHGND animal interpolate fast;
	[VHGNDanix] = [VHGNDaniprevx]; [VHGNDaniz] = [VHGNDaniprevz];
	[VHGNDanidir] = [VHGNDtmp]; -> VHGND animal interpolate done;
    "VHGND animal interpolate fast"
	A = [VHGNDinterpok]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND animal interpolate done;
	A = [VHGNDanix]; A - [VHGNDaniprevx]; A '* [VHGNDinterpacc]; A / 60000;
	A + [VHGNDaniprevx]; [VHGNDanix] = A;
	A = [VHGNDaniz]; A - [VHGNDaniprevz]; A '* [VHGNDinterpacc]; A / 60000;
	A + [VHGNDaniprevz]; [VHGNDaniz] = A;
	A = [VHGNDanidir]; A - [VHGNDtmp]; A '* [VHGNDinterpacc]; A / 60000;
	A + [VHGNDtmp]; [VHGNDanidir] = A;
    "VHGND animal interpolate done"
	end;

"VHGND bird snapshot"
	A = [VHGNDanii]; C = VHGNDbirdprevx; C + A; [C] = [VHGNDanix];
	C = VHGNDbirdprevz; C + A; [C] = [VHGNDaniz];
	C = VHGNDbirdprevquote; C + A; [C] = [VHGNDbirdquote];
	C = VHGNDbirdprevdir; C + A; [C] = [VHGNDanidir];
	end;

"VHGND bird interpolate"
	( Bird translation, altitude and heading share the same source-old pose. )
	A = [VHGNDanii]; C = VHGNDbirdprevx; C + A; [VHGNDaniprevx] = [C];
	C = VHGNDbirdprevz; C + A; [VHGNDaniprevz] = [C];
	C = VHGNDbirdprevquote; C + A; [VHGNDaniprevquote] = [C];
	C = VHGNDbirdprevdir; C + A; [VHGNDtmp] = [C];
	A = [VHGNDfast]; ? A != 0 -> VHGND bird interpolate fast;
	[VHGNDanix] = [VHGNDaniprevx]; [VHGNDaniz] = [VHGNDaniprevz];
	[VHGNDbirdquote] = [VHGNDaniprevquote]; [VHGNDanidir] = [VHGNDtmp];
	-> VHGND bird interpolate done;
    "VHGND bird interpolate fast"
	A = [VHGNDinterpok]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND bird interpolate done;
	A = [VHGNDanix]; A - [VHGNDaniprevx]; A '* [VHGNDinterpacc]; A / 60000;
	A + [VHGNDaniprevx]; [VHGNDanix] = A;
	A = [VHGNDaniz]; A - [VHGNDaniprevz]; A '* [VHGNDinterpacc]; A / 60000;
	A + [VHGNDaniprevz]; [VHGNDaniz] = A;
	A = [VHGNDbirdquote]; A - [VHGNDaniprevquote]; A '* [VHGNDinterpacc]; A / 60000;
	A + [VHGNDaniprevquote]; [VHGNDbirdquote] = A;
	A = [VHGNDanidir]; A - [VHGNDtmp]; A '* [VHGNDinterpacc]; A / 60000;
	A + [VHGNDtmp]; [VHGNDanidir] = A;
    "VHGND bird interpolate done"
	end;

"VHGND mammal half phase"
	( Source period = fabs(fsecs - 0.5). Keep it as 0..30000 over a
	  60000-unit second so both normal and interpolated presentation modes use
	  the one shared world-clock gait rather than a private per-animal wobble. )
	A = [VHGNDtick]; A '% 18; A '* 60000; C = A;
	A = [VHGNDfast]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND mammal phase ready;
	A = [VHGNDinterpok]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND mammal phase ready; C + [VHGNDinterpacc];
    "VHGND mammal phase ready"
	A = C; A '/ 18; A - 30000; ? A >= 0 -> VHGND mammal phase abs;
	C = 0; C - A; A = C;
    "VHGND mammal phase abs"
	[VHGNDmammphase] = A;
	end;

"VHGND bird flap value"
	A = [VHGNDaniscale]; ? A > 10 -> VHGND bird flap value big;
	( Small-bird source flap: abs(3 - tick%6) * 15 degrees. )
	A = [VHGNDanimtick]; A '% 6; C = 3; C - A;
	? C >= 0 -> VHGND bird flap value small ready; A = 0; A - C; C = A;
    "VHGND bird flap value small ready"
	C '* 15; [VHGNDbirdflap] = C; end;
    "VHGND bird flap value big"
	A = [VHGNDanimtick]; A '% 20; C = 10; C - A;
	? C >= 0 -> VHGND bird flap value big ready; A = 0; A - C; C = A;
    "VHGND bird flap value big ready"
	C '* 9; C / 2; [VHGNDbirdflap] = C;
	end;

"VHGND bird flap interpolate"
	[VHGNDanimtick] = [VHGNDtick]; => VHGND bird flap value;
	[VHGNDbirdflapcurr] = [VHGNDbirdflap];
	A = [VHGNDfast]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND bird flap current;
	A = [VHGNDinterpok]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND bird flap current;
	A = [VHGNDtick]; A - 1; [VHGNDanimtick] = A; => VHGND bird flap value;
	[VHGNDbirdflapprev] = [VHGNDbirdflap];
	A = [VHGNDbirdflapcurr]; A - [VHGNDbirdflapprev]; A '* [VHGNDinterpacc]; A / 60000;
	A + [VHGNDbirdflapprev]; [VHGNDbirdflap] = A; end;
"VHGND bird flap current"
	[VHGNDbirdflap] = [VHGNDbirdflapcurr];
	end;

"VHGND mammal behavior"
	( NOCTIS-1.CPP:700-777. Mammals make source-seeded stop/turn decisions,
	  keep independent target speed and pitch, ease toward both according to
	  0.5/scale reaction, then travel along a continuous heading. )
	( VHGNDtick already is the source long tick = 18*secs. Do not round it
	  down to another 18-tick boundary before the /update, /18 and /5 seeds. )
	[VHGNDanitickbase] = [VHGNDtick];
	A = [VHGNDaniindex]; => SU fast srand;
	[SUfmask] = 31; => SU frnd; C + 3; [VHGNDaniupdate] = C;
	[SUfmask] = 32767; => SU frnd; A = C; A '* 800; A '/ 32767; [VHGNDanitendency] = A;
	A = [VHGNDanitickbase]; A / [VHGNDaniupdate]; A + [VHGNDaniindex]; => SU fast srand;
	[SUfmask] = 32767; => SU frnd; A = C; A '* 1000; A '/ 32767;
	? A >= [VHGNDanitendency] -> VHGND mammal wants movement;
	[VHGNDanitargetspeed] = 0;
	A = [VHGNDaniindex]; A + [VHGNDanitickbase]; => SU fast srand;
	[SUfmask] = 32767; => SU frnd; A = C; A '* 1000; A '/ 32767;
	C = [VHGNDanitendency]; C / 10; ? A >= C -> VHGND mammal targets ready;
	[SUfmask] = 32767; => SU frnd; A = C; A '* 10000; A '/ 32767; [VHGNDtmp] = A;
	[SUfmask] = 32767; => SU frnd; A = C; A '* 10000; A '/ 32767;
	C = [VHGNDtmp]; C - A; C + [VHGNDanitargetdir]; [VHGNDanitargetdir] = C;
	-> VHGND mammal targets ready;
    "VHGND mammal wants movement"
	A = [VHGNDanitickbase]; A / 18; A + [VHGNDaniindex]; => SU fast srand;
	[SUfmask] = 32767; => SU frnd; [VHGNDtmp] = C;
	A = [VHGNDtmp]; A '* 350; A '/ 32767; A + 350;
	? [VHGNDanimtype] = 0 -> VHGND mammal target speed ready;
	A = [VHGNDtmp]; A '* 200; A '/ 32767; A + 200;
	? [VHGNDanimtype] = 1 -> VHGND mammal target speed ready;
	A = [VHGNDtmp]; A '* 100; A '/ 32767; A + 400;
    "VHGND mammal target speed ready"
	[VHGNDanitargetspeed] = A;
	A = [VHGNDanitickbase]; A / 5; A + [VHGNDaniindex]; => SU fast srand;
	[SUfmask] = 32767; => SU frnd; A = C; A '* 30000; A '/ 32767; A - 500;
	A / [VHGNDanitargetspeed]; A + [VHGNDanitargetdir]; [VHGNDanitargetdir] = A;
    "VHGND mammal targets ready"
	A = [VHGNDanitargetspeed]; A - [VHGNDanispeed]; A '* 3;
	C = [VHGNDaniscale]; C '* 2; A / C; A + [VHGNDanispeed]; [VHGNDanispeed] = A;
	A = [VHGNDanitargetdir]; A - [VHGNDanidir]; A / [VHGNDaniscale];
	A + [VHGNDanidir]; [VHGNDanidir] = A;
	[VHGNDanioldx] = [VHGNDanix]; [VHGNDanioldz] = [VHGNDaniz];
	=> VHGND mammal motion vector;
	[VHGNDanisinmove] = [VHGNDanimove];
	A = [VHGNDanix]; A - [VHGNDanimove]; [VHGNDanix] = A;
	A = [VHGNDanidir]; => VHGND mammal cosine motion;
	A = [VHGNDaniz]; A - [VHGNDanimove]; [VHGNDaniz] = A;
	A = [VHGNDanix]; ? A < 0 -> VHGND mammal boundary; ? A > 3276800 -> VHGND mammal boundary;
	A = [VHGNDaniz]; ? A < 0 -> VHGND mammal boundary; ? A <= 3276800 -> VHGND mammal behavior done;
    "VHGND mammal boundary"
	[VHGNDanix] = [VHGNDanioldx]; [VHGNDaniz] = [VHGNDanioldz];
	A = [VHGNDaniz]; A - [VHGNDanimove]; A + [VHGNDanisinmove]; [VHGNDaniz] = A;
	A = [VHGNDanidir]; A + 18000; [VHGNDanidir] = A;
    "VHGND mammal behavior done"
	end;

"VHGND mammal motion vector"
	( Exact hundredth-degree pitch to radians, retaining x87 sin rather than
	  snapping wildlife movement to the camera's integral trig cache. )
	[FI] = [VHGNDanidir]; => IntToF; [FC0] = [FA0]; [FC1] = [FA1];
	[FI] = 100; => IntToF; [FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [FC0]; [FA1] = [FC1]; => FQuo;
	[FB0] = VHVDEGLO; [FB1] = VHVDEGHI; => FMul;
	=> FSin; [FC0] = [FA0]; [FC1] = [FA1];
	[FI] = [VHGNDanispeed]; => IntToF; [FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [FC0]; [FA1] = [FC1]; => FMul; => FToIntNear;
	[VHGNDanimove] = [FI]; end;

"VHGND mammal cosine motion"
	( A carries heading*100. )
	[FI] = A; => IntToF; [FC0] = [FA0]; [FC1] = [FA1];
	[FI] = 100; => IntToF; [FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1];
	[FA0] = [FC0]; [FA1] = [FC1]; => FQuo;
	[FB0] = VHVDEGLO; [FB1] = VHVDEGHI; => FMul; => FCos;
	[FC0] = [FA0]; [FC1] = [FA1]; [FI] = [VHGNDanispeed]; => IntToF;
	[FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1]; [FA0] = [FC0]; [FA1] = [FC1]; => FMul; => FToIntNear;
	[VHGNDanimove] = [FI]; end;

"VHGND bird behavior"
	( NOCTIS-1.CPP live_animal BIRD path. Target altitude selects the four
	  source flight regimes; each uses the original animal-index/tick seed,
	  target heading jitter and altitude wander before scale-dependent easing. )
	[VHGNDanitickbase] = [VHGNDtick];
	A = [VHGNDbirdtarget]; ? A '>= 1500 -> VHGND bird behavior high;
	? A > 750 -> VHGND bird behavior middle; ? A > 250 -> VHGND bird behavior descend;
	A = [VHGNDaniindex]; C = [VHGNDanitickbase]; C / 10; A + C; => SU fast srand;
	A = [VHGNDbirdtarget]; ? A < 50 -> VHGND bird behavior grounded;
	[SUfmask] = 32767; => SU frnd; A = C; A '* 100; A '/ 32767; [VHGNDanitargetspeed] = A;
	[SUfmask] = 32767; => SU frnd; A = C; A '* 1000; A '/ 32767; A - 500;
	A + [VHGNDanitargetdir]; [VHGNDanitargetdir] = A;
	-> VHGND bird behavior low altitude;
    "VHGND bird behavior grounded"
	[VHGNDanitargetspeed] = 0;
    "VHGND bird behavior low altitude"
	[SUfmask] = 7; => SU frnd; A = C; ? A != 3 -> VHGND bird behavior low drift;
	[SUfmask] = 32767; => SU frnd; A = C; A '* 1000; A '/ 32767; A + 1500;
	[VHGNDbirdtarget] = A; -> VHGND bird behavior targets ready;
    "VHGND bird behavior low drift"
	[SUfmask] = 32767; => SU frnd; A = C; A '* 500; A '/ 32767; A - 250;
	A + [VHGNDbirdtarget]; [VHGNDbirdtarget] = A; -> VHGND bird behavior targets ready;
    "VHGND bird behavior descend"
	[VHGNDanitargetspeed] = 0; [VHGNDbirdtarget] = 0; -> VHGND bird behavior targets ready;
    "VHGND bird behavior middle"
	[VHGNDanitargetspeed] = 400; A = [VHGNDbirdtarget]; A '/ 2; [VHGNDbirdtarget] = A;
	A = [VHGNDaniindex]; C = [VHGNDanitickbase]; C / 15; A + C; => SU fast srand;
	[SUfmask] = 32767; => SU frnd; A = C; A '* 1000; A '/ 32767; A - 500;
	A + [VHGNDanitargetdir]; [VHGNDanitargetdir] = A; -> VHGND bird behavior targets ready;
    "VHGND bird behavior high"
	[VHGNDanitargetspeed] = 800;
	A = [VHGNDaniindex]; C = [VHGNDanitickbase]; C / 50; A + C; => SU fast srand;
	[SUfmask] = 32767; => SU frnd; A = C; A '* 500; A '/ 32767; A - 250;
	A + [VHGNDanitargetdir]; [VHGNDanitargetdir] = A;
	[SUfmask] = 32767; => SU frnd; A = C; A '* 1000; A '/ 32767; A - 500;
	A + [VHGNDbirdtarget]; [VHGNDbirdtarget] = A;
    "VHGND bird behavior targets ready"
	A = [VHGNDbirdtarget]; ? A >= 0 -> VHGND bird behavior quote ready; [VHGNDbirdtarget] = 0;
    "VHGND bird behavior quote ready"
	A = [VHGNDanitargetspeed]; A - [VHGNDanispeed]; A '* 3;
	C = [VHGNDaniscale]; C '* 2; A / C; A + [VHGNDanispeed]; [VHGNDanispeed] = A;
	A = [VHGNDanitargetdir]; A - [VHGNDanidir]; A / [VHGNDaniscale];
	A + [VHGNDanidir]; [VHGNDanidir] = A;
	A = [VHGNDbirdtarget]; A - [VHGNDbirdquote];
	C = [VHGNDaniscale]; C '* 2; A / C; A + [VHGNDbirdquote]; [VHGNDbirdquote] = A;
	[VHGNDanioldx] = [VHGNDanix]; [VHGNDanioldz] = [VHGNDaniz];
	=> VHGND mammal motion vector; [VHGNDanisinmove] = [VHGNDanimove];
	A = [VHGNDanix]; A - [VHGNDanimove]; [VHGNDanix] = A;
	A = [VHGNDanidir]; => VHGND mammal cosine motion;
	A = [VHGNDaniz]; A - [VHGNDanimove]; [VHGNDaniz] = A;
	A = [VHGNDanix]; ? A < 0 -> VHGND bird behavior boundary; ? A > 3276800 -> VHGND bird behavior boundary;
	A = [VHGNDaniz]; ? A < 0 -> VHGND bird behavior boundary; ? A '<= 3276800 -> VHGND bird behavior done;
    "VHGND bird behavior boundary"
	[VHGNDanix] = [VHGNDanioldx]; [VHGNDaniz] = [VHGNDanioldz];
	A = [VHGNDaniz]; A - [VHGNDanimove]; A + [VHGNDanisinmove]; [VHGNDaniz] = A;
	A = [VHGNDanidir]; A + 18000; [VHGNDanidir] = A;
    "VHGND bird behavior done"
	end;

"VHGND animal distance"
	( live_animal measures the old authoritative x/y/z pose with Euclidean
	  distance. Floating squares avoid overflowing 32-bit world coordinates. )
	[FI] = [VHGNDanidx]; => IntToF; [FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1]; => FMul;
	[FT0] = [FA0]; [FT1] = [FA1];
	[FI] = [VHGNDanidy]; => IntToF; [FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1]; => FMul;
	[FB0] = [FT0]; [FB1] = [FT1]; => FAdd; [FT0] = [FA0]; [FT1] = [FA1];
	[FI] = [VHGNDanidz]; => IntToF; [FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1]; => FMul;
	[FB0] = [FT0]; [FB1] = [FT1]; => FAdd; => FSqrt; => FToIntNear;
	[VHGNDanidist] = [FI];
	end;

"VHGND render animals"
	( live_animal() mammals: persistent motion plus the shipped model's
	  feline, rabbit and kangaroo body-part deformation. )
	A = [GRiptype]; ? A != 3 -> VHGND animals done;
	A = [VHGNDanimals]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND animals done;
	A = [VHGNDanisingle]; ? A != 0 -> VHGND animal one;
	[VHGNDanii] = 0;
    "VHGND animal one"
	A = [VHGNDanii]; A '* 10; A + VHGNDanidata; [VHGNDanip] = A;
	C = [VHGNDanip];
	[VHGNDanix] = [C plus 0]; [VHGNDaniz] = [C plus 1];
	[VHGNDanidir] = [C plus 2]; [VHGNDaniphase] = [C plus 3];
	[VHGNDanimtype] = [C plus 4]; [VHGNDanispeed] = [C plus 5];
	[VHGNDaniscale] = [C plus 6]; [VHGNDanitargetdir] = [C plus 7];
	[VHGNDanitargetspeed] = [C plus 8]; [VHGNDaniindex] = [C plus 9];
	A = [VHGNDanix]; A - [VHGNDcamx]; [VHGNDanidx] = A;
	? A >= 0 -> VHGND animal axis x abs; C = 0; C - A; A = C;
    "VHGND animal axis x abs"
	? A > 250000 -> VHGND animal axis far;
	A = [VHGNDaniz]; A - [VHGNDcamz]; [VHGNDanidz] = A;
	? A >= 0 -> VHGND animal axis z abs; C = 0; C - A; A = C;
    "VHGND animal axis z abs"
	? A > 250000 -> VHGND animal axis far;
	[VHGNDrox] = [VHGNDanix]; [VHGNDroz] = [VHGNDaniz]; => VHGND rock height;
	[VHGNDaniground] = [VHGNDroy]; [VHGNDaniy] = [VHGNDroy];
	A = [VHGNDaniy]; A - [VHGNDcamy]; [VHGNDanidy] = A;
	=> VHGND animal distance;
	-> VHGND animal distance ready;
    "VHGND animal axis far"
	( One horizontal component already proves the Euclidean source distance is
	  beyond its relocation gate.  Its exact value is otherwise unobserved. )
	[VHGNDanidist] = 250001;
    "VHGND animal distance ready"

	A = [VHGNDdosim]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND animal moved;
	=> VHGND animal snapshot;
	=> VHGND mammal behavior;
    "VHGND animal moved"
	A = [VHGNDanidist]; ? A '<= 250000 -> VHGND animal ranged;
	A = [VHGNDdosim]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND animal ranged;
	[VHGNDfaunatype] = 5; => VHGND fauna relocate nearby;
	=> VHGND animal snapshot;
	( live_animal keeps the old distance for this frame and draws the relocated
	  record on the following frame. )
	[VHGNDanidist] = 250001;
    "VHGND animal ranged"
	C = [VHGNDanip];
	[C plus 0] = [VHGNDanix]; [C plus 1] = [VHGNDaniz];
	[C plus 2] = [VHGNDanidir]; [C plus 5] = [VHGNDanispeed];
	[C plus 7] = [VHGNDanitargetdir]; [C plus 8] = [VHGNDanitargetspeed];
	=> VHGND animal interpolate;
	A = [VHGNDanidist]; ? A > 150000 -> VHGND animal next;
	=> VHGND fauna view cull; A = [VHGNDviewrz]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND animal hidden;
	[SPskipmid] = 0; A = [VHGNDanidist]; ? A '< 75000 -> VHGND animal midpoint ready;
	[SPskipmid] = 1;
    "VHGND animal midpoint ready"

	( Sample the ground ahead and bend the body around the source wrap joint. )
	[VHGNDrox] = [VHGNDanix]; [VHGNDroz] = [VHGNDaniz]; => VHGND rock height;
	[VHGNDaniground] = [VHGNDroy];
	[VHGNDrox] = [VHGNDanix]; [VHGNDroz] = [VHGNDaniz];
	A = [VHGNDanidir]; A / 100; [VHVangle] = A; => VHV sincos;
	[FS0] = [VHVsin]; => FLoadF32; [FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1];
	[FI] = 50; => IntToF; => FMul; => FToIntChop;
	A = [VHGNDrox]; A - [FI]; [VHGNDrox] = A;
	[FS0] = [VHVcos]; => FLoadF32; [FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1];
	[FI] = 50; => IntToF; => FMul; => FToIntChop;
	A = [VHGNDroz]; A - [FI]; [VHGNDroz] = A;
    "VHGND animal ahead ready"
	=> VHGND rock height;
	( live_animal clamps (ay-ay2)/an_incl_prec to [-1,+1], then converts
	  atan(ratio) to degrees. an_incl_prec is exactly 50. )
	A = [VHGNDaniground]; A - [VHGNDroy];
	? A >= 0 -> VHGND animal incl positive;
	C = 0; C - 50; ? A >= C -> VHGND animal incl clamped; A = C;
	-> VHGND animal incl clamped;
    "VHGND animal incl positive"
	? A <= 50 -> VHGND animal incl clamped; A = 50;
    "VHGND animal incl clamped"
	[FI] = A; => IntToF;
	[FB0] = 0; [FB1] = 40490000h; => FAtan2;
	[FB0] = 0; [FB1] = 40668000h; => FMul;
	[FB0] = 54442D18h; [FB1] = 400921FBh; => FQuo; => FToIntNear;
	A = [FI];
    "VHGND animal incl ready"
	[VHGNDaniincl] = A;

	A = [VHGNDaniground];
	C = [VHGNDaniscale]; C '* 16; ? [VHGNDanimtype] = 0 -> VHGND animal clearance;
	C = [VHGNDaniscale]; C '* 48; ? [VHGNDanimtype] = 1 -> VHGND animal clearance;
	C = [VHGNDaniscale]; C '* 115;
    "VHGND animal clearance"
	A - C; [VHGNDaniy] = A;

	[PVh] = 3; [PVk] = 2; => SP copypv;
	[FI] = [VHGNDaniscale]; => IntToF; => F32Narrow; => FStoreF32;
	[MOxs] = [FS0]; [MOys] = [FS0]; [MOzs] = [FS0];
	[MOxa] = 0; [MOya] = 0; [MOza] = 0; A = 0; A - 1; [MOpid] = A; [MOvid] = A; [MOlist] = 0;
	[PVh] = 3; => SP modpv;
	=> VHGND mammal half phase;
	[VHGNDaniswim] = 0; A = [VHGNDsctype]; ? A != 1 -> VHGND animal land shape;
	A = [VHGNDaniground]; C = 0; C - 10; ? A <= C -> VHGND animal land shape;
	[VHGNDaniswim] = 1; [VHGNDaniy] = [VHGNDaniground];
	( Ocean mammals: flatten the complete body, hide every leg vertex, then
	  apply the source 15-degree pitch and 0..25-degree swim stroke. )
	[MOxs] = 3F800000h; [MOys] = 3F333333h; [MOzs] = 3F800000h;
	[MOxa] = 0; [MOya] = 0; [MOza] = 0; A = 0; A - 1; [MOpid] = A; [MOvid] = A; [MOlist] = 0;
	[PVh] = 3; => SP modpv;
	[MOxs] = 3F800000h; [MOys] = 0; [MOzs] = 3F800000h;
	[MOxa] = 0; [MOya] = 0; [MOza] = 0; A = 0; A - 1; [MOpid] = A; [MOvid] = A;
	=> VHGND mamm legs list; [MOlist] = pvlst; [PVh] = 3; => SP modpv;
	A = [VHGNDmammphase]; A '/ 1200; [VHGNDswimphase] = A;
	[MOxs] = 3F800000h; [MOys] = 3F800000h; [MOzs] = 3F800000h;
	[FI] = 15; => IntToF; => F32Narrow; => FStoreF32; [MOxa] = [FS0];
	[MOya] = 0; [FI] = [VHGNDswimphase]; => IntToF; => F32Narrow; => FStoreF32; [MOza] = [FS0];
	A = 0; A - 1; [MOpid] = A; [MOvid] = A; [MOlist] = 0; [PVh] = 3; => SP modpv;
	-> VHGND animal animated;

    "VHGND animal land shape"

	A = [VHGNDanimtype]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND animal shaped;
	[MOxs] = 40000000h; [MOys] = 40000000h; [MOzs] = 3F400000h;
	[MOxa] = 0; [MOya] = 0; [MOza] = 0; A = 0; A - 1; [MOpid] = A; [MOvid] = A;
	=> VHGND mamm rear list; [MOlist] = pvlst; [PVh] = 3; => SP modpv;
	[MOxs] = 3F800000h; [MOys] = 3F800000h; [MOzs] = 3F800000h;
	[FI] = 60; => IntToF; => F32Narrow; => FStoreF32; [MOxa] = [FS0];
	[MOya] = 0; [MOza] = 0; [MOlist] = 0; A = 0; A - 1; [MOpid] = A; [MOvid] = A;
	[PVh] = 3; => SP modpv;
	A = 0; A - 100; [FI] = A; => IntToF; => F32Narrow; => FStoreF32; [MOxa] = [FS0];
	[MOya] = 0; [MOza] = 0; [MOpid] = 46; [MOvid] = 1;
	=> VHGND mamm tail list; [MOlist] = pvlst; [PVh] = 3; => SP modpv;
	A = [VHGNDanimtype]; ? A = 2 -> VHGND animal shaped;
	[MOxs] = 3EA8F5C3h; [MOys] = 3EA8F5C3h; [MOzs] = 3EA8F5C3h;
	[MOxa] = 0; [MOya] = 0; [MOza] = 0; [MOlist] = 0; A = 0; A - 1; [MOpid] = A; [MOvid] = A;
	[PVh] = 3; => SP modpv;
    "VHGND animal shaped"

	[MOxs] = 3F800000h; [MOys] = 3F800000h; [MOzs] = 3F800000h;
	[FI] = [VHGNDaniincl]; => IntToF; => F32Narrow; => FStoreF32; [MOxa] = [FS0];
	[MOya] = 0; [MOza] = 0; [MOpid] = 16; [MOvid] = 2; [MOlist] = 0;
	[PVh] = 3; => SP modpv;

	A = [VHGNDanispeed]; ? A '< 50 -> VHGND animal tail gate;
	A = 45; ? [VHGNDanimtype] = 0 -> VHGND animal gait base;
	A = 60; ? [VHGNDanimtype] = 1 -> VHGND animal gait base; A = 22;
    "VHGND animal gait base"
	( period is kept in thousandths of a degree: base * source half-second
	  phase / animal scale. )
	A '* [VHGNDmammphase]; A '* 1000;
	C = [VHGNDaniscale]; C '* 60000; [VHGNDtmp] = C; A '/ [VHGNDtmp]; [VHGNDaniperiod] = A;
	A = 35; ? [VHGNDanimtype] = 0 -> VHGND animal bounce base;
	A = 50; ? [VHGNDanimtype] = 1 -> VHGND animal bounce base; A = 300;
    "VHGND animal bounce base"
	C = [VHGNDaniperiod]; C '* [VHGNDaniscale]; C '* A; C '/ 1000;
	A = [VHGNDaniy]; A - C; [VHGNDaniy] = A;
	A = [VHGNDaniperiod]; A '* 50; C = 0; C - A; [FI] = C; => IntToF;
	[FB0] = 0; [FB1] = 408F4000h; => FQuo; => F32Narrow; => FStoreF32;
	[MOxa] = [FS0]; [MOya] = 0; [MOza] = 0; [MOpid] = 16; [MOvid] = 2; [MOlist] = 0;
	[PVh] = 3; => SP modpv;
	A = [VHGNDaniperiod]; A '* 100; [FI] = A; => IntToF;
	[FB0] = 0; [FB1] = 408F4000h; => FQuo; => F32Narrow; => FStoreF32;
	[MOxa] = [FS0]; => VHGND mamm rear list; [MOlist] = pvlst; [PVh] = 3; => SP modpv;
	-> VHGND animal animated;
    "VHGND animal tail gate"
	( The source only animates an idle tail when fast_random(1), seeded by the
	  animal's original LFS index, selects it. )
	A = [VHGNDaniindex]; A '* 4; => SU fast srand;
	[SUfmask] = 1; => SU fast raw; A = [SUfval]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND animal animated;
    "VHGND animal tail wag"
	A = [VHGNDmammphase]; A '/ 250; A - 60; [FI] = A; => IntToF; => F32Narrow; => FStoreF32;
	[MOxa] = 0; [MOya] = [FS0]; [MOza] = 0; [MOpid] = 46; [MOvid] = 1;
	=> VHGND mamm tail list; [MOlist] = pvlst; [PVh] = 3; => SP modpv;
    "VHGND animal animated"
	[VHGNDanimorphs]+;

	A = [VHGNDanidir]; A / 100; A + 180; [FI] = A; => IntToF; => F32Narrow; => FStoreF32;
	[MOya] = [FS0]; [MOxa] = 0; [MOza] = 0; [MOlist] = 0; [MOpid] = 16; [MOvid] = 2;
	[PVh] = 3; => SP modpv;

	[FI] = [VHGNDanix]; => IntToF; => F32Narrow; => FStoreF32; [VHGNDanifx] = [FS0];
	[FI] = [VHGNDaniy]; => IntToF; => F32Narrow; => FStoreF32; [VHGNDanify] = [FS0];
	[FI] = [VHGNDaniz]; => IntToF; => F32Narrow; => FStoreF32; [VHGNDanifz] = [FS0];
	[FI] = [VHGNDcamx]; => IntToF; => F32Narrow; => FStoreF32; [VHGNDanicamx] = [FS0];
	[FI] = [VHGNDcamy]; => IntToF; => F32Narrow; => FStoreF32; [VHGNDanicamy] = [FS0];
	[FI] = [VHGNDcamz]; => IntToF; => F32Narrow; => FStoreF32; [VHGNDanicamz] = [FS0];
	[VHGNDdepthsort] = 0; A = [VHGNDanidist]; ? A '>= 75000 -> VHGND animal draw sort ready;
	[VHGNDdepthsort] = 1;
    "VHGND animal draw sort ready"
	( live_animal maps every mammal through p_background with XSIZE=65536
	  and YSIZE=256*T_SCALE. )
	[FI] = 65536; => IntToF; [PGFi] = FSTX; => PGF sa;
	[FI] = [VHGNDtscale]; => IntToF; [FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1];
	[FI] = 256; => IntToF; => FMul; [PGFi] = FSTY; => PGF sa;
	[PGtexf] = 5; [SPsrc] = 1;
	[PVh] = 3; [DWmode] = 1; [DWuds] = [VHGNDdepthsort];
	[DWcx] = [VHGNDanifx]; [DWcy] = [VHGNDanify]; [DWcz] = [VHGNDanifz];
	[DWcamx] = [VHGNDanicamx]; [DWcamy] = [VHGNDanicamy]; [DWcamz] = [VHGNDanicamz];
	=> SP drawpv;
	[SPcull] = 0; [SPflar] = 0; [SPhalf] = 0; => VH join mode1; [PGtexf] = 0;
	-> VHGND animal next;
    "VHGND animal hidden"
	( The only render-stage RNG is the idle-tail choice.  Preserve that draw
	  when an off-screen model skips its otherwise side-effect-free shaping. )
	A = [VHGNDanispeed]; ? A '< 50 -> VHGND animal hidden tail; -> VHGND animal next;
    "VHGND animal hidden tail"
	A = [VHGNDaniindex]; A '* 4; => SU fast srand;
	[SUfmask] = 1; => SU fast raw;
    "VHGND animal next"
	A = [VHGNDanisingle]; ? A != 0 -> VHGND animals done;
	[VHGNDanii]+; A = [VHGNDanii]; ? A '< [VHGNDanimals] -> VHGND animal one;
    "VHGND animals done"
	[SPskipmid] = 0;
	end;


"VHGND render birds"
	( Birds retain their source-selected scale, altitude and capture state,
	  descend, cruise and take off across the full LFS population. )
	A = [GRiptype]; ? A != 3 -> VHGND birds done;
	A = [VHGNDbirds]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND birds done;
	A = [VHGNDanisingle]; ? A != 0 -> VHGND bird one;
	[VHGNDanii] = 0;
    "VHGND bird one"
	A = [VHGNDanii]; A '* 12; A + VHGNDbirddata; [VHGNDanip] = A;
	C = [VHGNDanip];
	[VHGNDanix] = [C plus 0]; [VHGNDaniz] = [C plus 1];
	[VHGNDanidir] = [C plus 2]; [VHGNDaniphase] = [C plus 3];
	[VHGNDbirdquote] = [C plus 4]; [VHGNDbirdtarget] = [C plus 5];
	[VHGNDbirdlcount] = [C plus 6];
	[VHGNDaniscale] = [C plus 7];
	[VHGNDanispeed] = [C plus 8]; [VHGNDanitargetspeed] = [C plus 9];
	[VHGNDaniindex] = [C plus 10]; [VHGNDanitargetdir] = [C plus 11];
	A = [VHGNDbirdlcount]; ? A < 0 -> VHGND bird distance full;
	A = [VHGNDanix]; A - [VHGNDcamx]; [VHGNDanidx] = A;
	? A >= 0 -> VHGND bird axis x abs; C = 0; C - A; A = C;
    "VHGND bird axis x abs"
	? A > 250000 -> VHGND bird axis far;
	A = [VHGNDaniz]; A - [VHGNDcamz]; [VHGNDanidz] = A;
	? A >= 0 -> VHGND bird axis z abs; C = 0; C - A; A = C;
    "VHGND bird axis z abs"
	? A > 250000 -> VHGND bird axis far;
    "VHGND bird distance full"
	[VHGNDrox] = [VHGNDanix]; [VHGNDroz] = [VHGNDaniz]; => VHGND rock height;
	A = [VHGNDroy]; A - [VHGNDbirdquote]; [VHGNDaniy] = A;
	A = [VHGNDanix]; A - [VHGNDcamx]; [VHGNDanidx] = A;
	A = [VHGNDaniy]; A - [VHGNDcamy]; [VHGNDanidy] = A;
	A = [VHGNDaniz]; A - [VHGNDcamz]; [VHGNDanidz] = A;
	=> VHGND animal distance;
	-> VHGND bird distance ready;
    "VHGND bird axis far"
	[VHGNDanidist] = 250001;
    "VHGND bird distance ready"
	A = [VHGNDdosim]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND bird state frozen;
	=> VHGND bird snapshot;

	A = [VHGNDbirdlcount]; ? A < 0 -> VHGND bird captured;
	=> VHGND bird behavior;
	( Preserve live_animal's cautious-approach game: ordinary walking makes a
	  nearby ground bird take off, while Ctrl-stalking stays below both source
	  step thresholds.  A sufficiently close rush can still spring the original
	  difficult ambush before the bird escapes. )
	A = [VHGNDanidist]; ? A '>= 5000 -> VHGND bird near 3000;
	A = [VHGNDplayerstep]; ? A '<= 250 -> VHGND bird near 3000;
	A = [VHGNDbirdtarget]; A + 2500; [VHGNDbirdtarget] = A;
    "VHGND bird near 3000"
	A = [VHGNDanidist]; ? A '>= 3000 -> VHGND bird catch range;
	A = [VHGNDplayerstep]; ? A '<= 100 -> VHGND bird catch range;
	A = [VHGNDbirdtarget]; A + 2000; [VHGNDbirdtarget] = A;
    "VHGND bird catch range"
	A = [VHGNDanidist]; ? A '>= 1000 -> VHGND bird state updated;
	( live_animal uses 250*reaction altitude and 500*reaction speed,
	  with reaction=0.5/scale.  Keep the integer port's size response so
	  the deliberately slow 80-unit stalk can actually close the gap. )
	[VHGNDtmp] = [VHGNDaniscale];
	A = 125; A '/ [VHGNDtmp]; [VHGNDbirdtarget] = A;
	A = 250; A '/ [VHGNDtmp]; [VHGNDanitargetspeed] = A;
	A = [VHGNDanidist]; ? A '>= 500 -> VHGND bird state updated;
	A = 0; A - 25; [VHGNDbirdlcount] = A;
	[VHGNDbirdtarget] = 0; [VHGNDcaptures]+; -> VHGND bird captured;

    "VHGND bird captured"
	A = [VHGNDbirdlcount]; ? A '>= 0 -> VHGND bird state updated;
	C = 0; C - A; [VHGNDtmp] = C;
	A = [VHGNDcamx]; A - [VHGNDanix]; A '/ [VHGNDtmp]; A + [VHGNDanix]; [VHGNDanix] = A;
	A = [VHGNDcamz]; A - [VHGNDaniz]; A '/ [VHGNDtmp]; A + [VHGNDaniz]; [VHGNDaniz] = A;
	A = [VHGNDbirdquote]; A / [VHGNDtmp]; C = [VHGNDbirdquote]; C - A; [VHGNDbirdquote] = C;
	A = [VHGNDbirdlcount]; A + 1; ? A = 0 -> VHGND bird caught still;
	A = [VHGNDbirdlcount]; A + A; C = [VHGsurfstep]; C + A; [VHGsurfstep] = C;
	[VHGNDbirdlcount]+;
    "VHGND bird caught still"
	[VHGNDanispeed] = 0; [VHGNDanitargetspeed] = 0;

    "VHGND bird state updated"

    "VHGND bird state frozen"
	A = [VHGNDbirdlcount]; ? A < 0 -> VHGND bird ranged;
	A = [VHGNDanidist]; ? A '<= 250000 -> VHGND bird ranged;
	A = [VHGNDdosim]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND bird ranged;
	[VHGNDfaunatype] = 1; => VHGND fauna relocate nearby;
	=> VHGND bird snapshot;
	[VHGNDbirdtarget] = [VHGNDbirdquote];
	[VHGNDanidist] = 250001;
    "VHGND bird ranged"
	C = [VHGNDanip]; [C plus 0] = [VHGNDanix]; [C plus 1] = [VHGNDaniz];
	[C plus 2] = [VHGNDanidir]; [C plus 4] = [VHGNDbirdquote];
	[C plus 5] = [VHGNDbirdtarget]; [C plus 6] = [VHGNDbirdlcount];
	[C plus 8] = [VHGNDanispeed]; [C plus 9] = [VHGNDanitargetspeed];
	[C plus 11] = [VHGNDanitargetdir];
	=> VHGND bird interpolate;
	A = [VHGNDanidist]; ? A > 150000 -> VHGND bird next;
	=> VHGND fauna view cull; A = [VHGNDviewrz]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND bird next;
	[SPskipmid] = 0; A = [VHGNDanidist]; ? A '< 75000 -> VHGND bird midpoint ready;
	[SPskipmid] = 1;
    "VHGND bird midpoint ready"

	[VHGNDrox] = [VHGNDanix]; [VHGNDroz] = [VHGNDaniz]; => VHGND rock height;
	A = [VHGNDaniscale]; C = A; C '* 9; A = [VHGNDroy]; A - C; A - [VHGNDbirdquote]; [VHGNDaniy] = A;

	[PVh] = 5; [PVk] = 4; => SP copypv;
	[FI] = [VHGNDaniscale]; => IntToF; => F32Narrow; => FStoreF32;
	[MOxs] = [FS0]; [MOys] = [FS0]; [MOzs] = [FS0];
	[MOxa] = 0; [MOya] = 0; [MOza] = 0; A = 0; A - 1; [MOpid] = A; [MOvid] = A; [MOlist] = 0;
	[PVh] = 5; => SP modpv;

	A = [VHGNDbirdlcount]; ? A < 0 -> VHGND bird caught pose;
	A = [VHGNDbirdquote]; ? A '>= 50 -> VHGND bird flight pose;
	A = [VHGNDsctype]; ? A != 1 -> VHGND bird ground pose;
	A = [VHGNDroy]; A - [VHGNDbirdquote]; ? A >= 0 -> VHGND bird flight pose;
    "VHGND bird ground pose"
	( Ground birds close their wings around the source wing joint. )
	[VHGNDgroundbirds]+;
	( dy = max(0, 1 - quote * (0.5 / scale)). Keep thousandths so small
	  source birds do not inherit the old blanket 50-unit closing ramp. )
	A = [VHGNDbirdquote]; A '* 500; A '/ [VHGNDaniscale];
	C = 1000; C - A; ? C >= 0 -> VHGND bird ground fold ready; C = 0;
    "VHGND bird ground fold ready"
	A = C; A '* 45; [VHGNDaniperiod] = A;
	C '* 75; [VHGNDbirdflap] = C;
	[MOxs] = 3F800000h; [MOys] = 3F800000h; [MOzs] = 3F800000h;
	[MOpid] = 1; [MOvid] = 0; [MOxa] = 0;
	[FI] = [VHGNDaniperiod]; => IntToF; [FB0] = 0; [FB1] = 408F4000h;
	=> FQuo; => F32Narrow; => FStoreF32; [MOya] = [FS0];
	[FI] = [VHGNDbirdflap]; => IntToF; [FB0] = 0; [FB1] = 408F4000h;
	=> FQuo; => F32Narrow; => FStoreF32; [MOza] = [FS0];
	=> VHGND bird wing one list; [MOlist] = pvlst; [PVh] = 5; => SP modpv;
	A = 0; A - [VHGNDaniperiod]; [FI] = A; => IntToF;
	[FB0] = 0; [FB1] = 408F4000h; => FQuo; => F32Narrow; => FStoreF32; [MOya] = [FS0];
	A = 0; A - [VHGNDbirdflap]; [FI] = A; => IntToF;
	[FB0] = 0; [FB1] = 408F4000h; => FQuo; => F32Narrow; => FStoreF32; [MOza] = [FS0];
	=> VHGND bird wing two list; [MOlist] = pvlst; [PVh] = 5; => SP modpv;
	-> VHGND bird approach pose;

    "VHGND bird flight pose"
	[MOxs] = 3F800000h; [MOys] = 3F800000h; [MOzs] = 3F800000h;
	[MOpid] = 18; [MOvid] = 1; [MOya] = 0; [MOza] = 0;
	A = 0; A - 75; [FI] = A; => IntToF; => F32Narrow; => FStoreF32; [MOxa] = [FS0];
	=> VHGND bird legs list; [MOlist] = pvlst; [PVh] = 5; => SP modpv;
	=> VHGND bird flap interpolate;
    "VHGND bird flap apply"
	[MOpid] = 1; [MOvid] = 0; [MOxa] = 0; [MOya] = 0;
	A = 0; A - [VHGNDbirdflap]; [FI] = A; => IntToF; => F32Narrow; => FStoreF32; [MOza] = [FS0];
	=> VHGND bird wing one list; [MOlist] = pvlst; [PVh] = 5; => SP modpv;
	[FI] = [VHGNDbirdflap]; => IntToF; => F32Narrow; => FStoreF32; [MOza] = [FS0];
	=> VHGND bird wing two list; [MOlist] = pvlst; [PVh] = 5; => SP modpv;
	-> VHGND bird approach pose;

    "VHGND bird caught pose"
	A = 180; A '/ [VHGNDbirdlcount]; [FI] = A; => IntToF; => F32Narrow; => FStoreF32;
	[MOxa] = [FS0]; [MOya] = 0; [MOza] = 0; [MOpid] = 1; [MOvid] = 0; [MOlist] = 0;
	[PVh] = 5; => SP modpv;
	-> VHGND bird heading pose;

    "VHGND bird approach pose"
	A = [VHGNDbirdquote]; ? A '>= 500 -> VHGND bird heading pose;
	A = 250; A - [VHGNDbirdquote]; ? A >= 0 -> VHGND bird approach abs; C = 0; C - A; A = C;
    "VHGND bird approach abs"
	A '/ 10; C = 0; C - A; [FI] = C; => IntToF; => F32Narrow; => FStoreF32;
	[MOxa] = [FS0]; [MOya] = 0; [MOza] = 0; [MOpid] = 1; [MOvid] = 0; [MOlist] = 0;
	[PVh] = 5; => SP modpv;

    "VHGND bird heading pose"
	A = [VHGNDanidir]; A / 100; [FI] = A; => IntToF; => F32Narrow; => FStoreF32;
	[MOya] = [FS0]; [MOxa] = 0; [MOza] = 0; [MOlist] = 0; [MOpid] = 1; [MOvid] = 0;
	[PVh] = 5; => SP modpv;

	[FI] = [VHGNDanix]; => IntToF; => F32Narrow; => FStoreF32; [VHGNDanifx] = [FS0];
	[FI] = [VHGNDaniy]; => IntToF; => F32Narrow; => FStoreF32; [VHGNDanify] = [FS0];
	[FI] = [VHGNDaniz]; => IntToF; => F32Narrow; => FStoreF32; [VHGNDanifz] = [FS0];
	[FI] = [VHGNDcamx]; => IntToF; => F32Narrow; => FStoreF32; [VHGNDanicamx] = [FS0];
	[FI] = [VHGNDcamy]; => IntToF; => F32Narrow; => FStoreF32; [VHGNDanicamy] = [FS0];
	[FI] = [VHGNDcamz]; => IntToF; => F32Narrow; => FStoreF32; [VHGNDanicamz] = [FS0];
	[VHGNDdepthsort] = 0; A = [VHGNDanidist]; ? A '>= 75000 -> VHGND bird draw sort ready;
	[VHGNDdepthsort] = 1;
    "VHGND bird draw sort ready"
	[VHGNDdrawmode] = 0; A = [VHGNDanidist]; ? A '>= 12500 -> VHGND bird draw mode ready;
	A = [VHGNDaniindex]; A & 1; A + 1; [VHGNDdrawmode] = A;
    "VHGND bird draw mode ready"
	[FI] = 65536; => IntToF; [PGFi] = FSTX; => PGF sa;
	[FI] = [VHGNDtscale]; => IntToF; [FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1];
	[FI] = 256; => IntToF; => FMul; [PGFi] = FSTY; => PGF sa;
	[PGtexf] = 5; [SPsrc] = 1;
	[PVh] = 5; [DWmode] = [VHGNDdrawmode]; [DWuds] = [VHGNDdepthsort];
	[DWcx] = [VHGNDanifx]; [DWcy] = [VHGNDanify]; [DWcz] = [VHGNDanifz];
	[DWcamx] = [VHGNDanicamx]; [DWcamy] = [VHGNDanicamy]; [DWcamz] = [VHGNDanicamz];
	=> SP drawpv; [SPcull] = 0; [SPflar] = 0; [SPhalf] = 0;
	A = [VHGNDdrawmode]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND bird join plain; ? A = 1 -> VHGND bird join textured;
	[VHRiters] = 3; => VH join mode2; -> VHGND bird join done;
    "VHGND bird join textured"
	=> VH join mode1; -> VHGND bird join done;
    "VHGND bird join plain"
	=> VH join mode0;
    "VHGND bird join done"
	[PGtexf] = 0;

	A = [VHGNDbirdlcount]; ? A '>= 0 -> VHGND bird next;
	C = 0; C - 10; ? A <= C -> VHGND bird next;
	[VHSx0] = [VHGNDanifx]; [VHSy0] = [VHGNDanify]; [VHSz0] = [VHGNDanifz];
	[VHSx1] = [VHGNDanicamx]; [VHSz1] = [VHGNDanicamz];
	A = [VHGNDcamy]; A - 50; [FI] = A; => IntToF; => F32Narrow; => FStoreF32; [VHSy1] = [FS0];
	[VHScolor] = 62; => VH stick3d;
	A = [VHGNDcamx]; A - 50; [FI] = A; => IntToF; => F32Narrow; => FStoreF32;
	[VHSx1] = [FS0]; => VH stick3d;
	A = [VHGNDcamx]; A + 50; [FI] = A; => IntToF; => F32Narrow; => FStoreF32;
	[VHSx1] = [FS0]; => VH stick3d;
	[VHSx1] = [VHGNDanicamx];
	A = [VHGNDcamz]; A + 50; [FI] = A; => IntToF; => F32Narrow; => FStoreF32;
	[VHSz1] = [FS0]; => VH stick3d;
	A = [VHGNDcamz]; A - 50; [FI] = A; => IntToF; => F32Narrow; => FStoreF32;
	[VHSz1] = [FS0]; => VH stick3d;
    "VHGND bird next"
	A = [VHGNDanisingle]; ? A != 0 -> VHGND birds done;
	[VHGNDanii]+; A = [VHGNDanii]; ? A '< [VHGNDbirds] -> VHGND bird one;
    "VHGND birds done"
	[SPskipmid] = 0;
	end;


"VHGND capsule"
	( NOCTIS-1.CPP:1200-1221.  The settled drop capsule keeps both signed
	  textured polycupola shells and structural grids, centred 900 units above
	  the landing point, plus the three nested beacon rays. polycupola's local
	  three-dimensional distance solve moves only panels around the walker and
	  is the original capsule door; omitting it leaves an inert closed cage. )
	A = [VHGlanded]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND capsule done;
	A = [VHGNDcamx]; A - [VHGNDdropx]; ? A >= 0 -> VHGND capsule absx; C = 0; C - A; A = C;
    "VHGND capsule absx"
	? A > 1064960 -> VHGND capsule done;
	A = [VHGNDcamz]; A - [VHGNDdropz]; ? A >= 0 -> VHGND capsule absz; C = 0; C - A; A = C;
    "VHGND capsule absz"
	? A > 1064960 -> VHGND capsule done;
	( polycupola opens only the glass panels nearest the walker.  Its exact
	  per-panel motion lives in VHC capsule view; the structural grid remains
	  fixed, as it does in the original. )
	[VHCopen] = 0;

	[VHCroof] = 0; [VHCcapsule] = 1; [SPcull] = 0; [SPflar] = 0; [SPhalf] = 0;
	A = [VHGNDcamx]; A - [VHGNDdropx]; [VHVcamxi] = A;
	A = [VHGNDcamz]; A - [VHGNDdropz]; [VHVcamzi] = A;
	( VHGNDdropy is hpoint, the terrain surface at the capsule.  VHGNDcamy
	  is the eye coordinate hpoint-600; mirror the source's temporary cam_y
	  offsets directly rather than adding the eye-height offset twice. )
	A = [VHGNDcamy]; A - [VHGNDdropy]; A + 1415; [VHVcamyi] = A;
	[VHValpha] = [VHGNDalpha]; [VHVbeta] = [VHGNDbeta]; => VH set view;
	[VHCyor] = 0FFFFFFFFh; => VH polycupola; => VH cupola grid;

	A = [VHGNDcamy]; A - [VHGNDdropy]; A + 385; [VHVcamyi] = A;
	=> VH set view; [VHCyor] = 1; => VH polycupola; => VH cupola grid;
	"VHGND capsule shell done"

	( Beacon coordinates are relative to the capsule seam, matching the
	  source's 2 km / 1 km / 500 m nested intrinsically luminous sticks.
	  Stick flare mode 1 skips alternating points; repeating the three rays
	  six times in the night zone is the source's brightness boost. )
	A = [VHGNDcamy]; A - [VHGNDdropy]; A + 900; [VHVcamyi] = A;
	=> VH set view;
	[VHSflare] = 1; [VHGNDbeaconpass] = 0;
    "VHGND capsule beacon"
	[VHSx0] = 0; [VHSy0] = 3304718336; [VHSz0] = 0;
	[VHSx1] = 1120403456; [VHSy1] = 3388220416; [VHSz1] = 0; => VH stick3d;
	A = [VHGNDdepth]; ? A '>= 48 -> VHGND capsule beacon repeat;
	[VHSx0] = 0; [VHSy0] = 3305119744; [VHSz0] = 0;
	[VHSx1] = 0; [VHSy1] = 3379831808; [VHSz1] = 0; => VH stick3d;
	A = [VHGNDdepth]; ? A '>= 24 -> VHGND capsule beacon repeat;
	[VHSx0] = 0; [VHSy0] = 3305324544; [VHSz0] = 0;
	[VHSx1] = 0; [VHSy1] = 3371443200; [VHSz1] = 1120403456; => VH stick3d;
    "VHGND capsule beacon repeat"
	[VHGNDbeaconpass]+; A = [GRSKnightzone]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND capsule beacon done;
	A = [VHGNDbeaconpass]; ? A < 6 -> VHGND capsule beacon;
    "VHGND capsule beacon done"
	[VHSflare] = 0;
	[VHCcapsule] = 0; [VHCopen] = 0;

	( Restore the landed camera for any caller-side overlays. )
	[VHVcamxi] = [VHGNDcamx]; [VHVcamyi] = [VHGNDcamy]; [VHVcamzi] = [VHGNDcamz];
	=> VH set view;
    "VHGND capsule done"
	end;

"VHGND moving capsule"
	( NOCTIS-1.CPP:4317-4337.  During descent and ascent the pod is
	  camera-relative; the terrain moves below it while both hemispheres
	  retain polycupola's local opening aperture. )
	[VHCroof] = 0; [VHCcapsule] = 1; [VHCopen] = 0; [SPcull] = 0; [SPflar] = 0; [SPhalf] = 0;
	[VHVcamxi] = 0; [VHVcamyi] = 1030; [VHVcamzi] = 0;
	[VHValpha] = [VHGNDalpha]; [VHVbeta] = [VHGNDbeta]; => VH set view;
	[VHCyor] = 0FFFFFFFFh; => VH polycupola; => VH cupola grid;
	[VHVcamyi] = 0; => VH set view;
	[VHCyor] = 1; => VH polycupola; => VH cupola grid;
	[VHCcapsule] = 0;
	[VHVcamxi] = [VHGNDcamx]; [VHVcamyi] = [VHGNDcamy]; [VHVcamzi] = [VHGNDcamz];
	=> VH set view;
	end;

"VHGND render ruins"
	( Reflection fallback retains a separate marked-tile pass.  Normal landed
	  terrain already carries ruins in its faithful unit traversal and does not
	  call this compensating path. )
	[VHGNDruinpass] = 0; A = [VHGlanded]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND render ruins done;
	A = [VHGNDruinanchor]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND render ruins done;
	[VHGNDruinpass] = 1; [VHGNDlodstep] = 1; [VHGNDlodradius] = VHGNDFAR;
	A = 0; A - 1; [VHGNDmindepth] = A; [VHGNDmaxdepth] = VHGNDFAR;
	A = [VHGNDcamtx]; A - VHGNDFAR; [VHGNDxlo] = A;
	? A >= 0 -> VHGND ruins render xlo ready; [VHGNDxlo] = 0;
    "VHGND ruins render xlo ready"
	A = [VHGNDcamtx]; A + VHGNDFAR; [VHGNDxhi] = A;
	? A '<= 198 -> VHGND ruins render xhi ready; [VHGNDxhi] = 198;
    "VHGND ruins render xhi ready"
	A = [VHGNDcamtz]; A - VHGNDFAR; [VHGNDzlo] = A;
	? A >= 0 -> VHGND ruins render zlo ready; [VHGNDzlo] = 0;
    "VHGND ruins render zlo ready"
	A = [VHGNDcamtz]; A + VHGNDFAR; [VHGNDzhi] = A;
	? A '<= 198 -> VHGND ruins render zhi ready; [VHGNDzhi] = 198;
    "VHGND ruins render zhi ready"
	[VHGNDz] = [VHGNDzhi];
    "VHGND ruins render z"
	[VHGNDx] = [VHGNDxlo];
    "VHGND ruins render x"
	A = [VHGNDz]; A '* VHGNDMAP; A + [VHGNDx]; [VHGNDh1] = A;
	A = VHGNDruins; A + [VHGNDh1]; A = [A]; ? A != 0 -> VHGND ruins render tile;
	A = VHGNDruins; A + [VHGNDh1]; A + 1; A = [A]; ? A != 0 -> VHGND ruins render tile;
	A = VHGNDruins; A + [VHGNDh1]; A + 201; A = [A]; ? A != 0 -> VHGND ruins render tile;
	A = VHGNDruins; A + [VHGNDh1]; A + 200; A = [A]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND ruins render next x;
    "VHGND ruins render tile"
	=> VHGND tile;
    "VHGND ruins render next x"
	[VHGNDx]+; A = [VHGNDx]; ? A '<= [VHGNDxhi] -> VHGND ruins render x;
	[VHGNDz]-; A = [VHGNDz]; ? A >= [VHGNDzlo] -> VHGND ruins render z;
	[VHGNDruinpass] = 0;
    "VHGND render ruins done"
	end;

"VHGND traverse"
	A = [VHGNDcamtx]; A - [VHGNDlodradius]; [VHGNDxlo] = A;
	? A >= 0 -> VHGND traverse xlo ready; [VHGNDxlo] = 0;
    "VHGND traverse xlo ready"
	A = [VHGNDcamtx]; A + [VHGNDlodradius]; [VHGNDxhi] = A;
	C = 199; C - [VHGNDlodstep]; ? A '<= C -> VHGND traverse xhi ready; [VHGNDxhi] = C;
    "VHGND traverse xhi ready"
	A = [VHGNDcamtz]; A - [VHGNDlodradius]; [VHGNDzlo] = A;
	? A >= 0 -> VHGND traverse zlo ready; [VHGNDzlo] = 0;
    "VHGND traverse zlo ready"
	A = [VHGNDcamtz]; A + [VHGNDlodradius]; [VHGNDzhi] = A;
	C = 199; C - [VHGNDlodstep]; ? A '<= C -> VHGND traverse zhi ready; [VHGNDzhi] = C;
    "VHGND traverse zhi ready"
	[VHGNDz] = [VHGNDzhi];
    "VHGND render z"
	[VHGNDx] = [VHGNDxlo];
    "VHGND render x"
	=> VHGND tile;
	A = [VHGNDx]; A + [VHGNDlodstep]; [VHGNDx] = A;
	? A '<= [VHGNDxhi] -> VHGND render x;
	A = [VHGNDz]; A - [VHGNDlodstep]; [VHGNDz] = A;
	( zlo may legitimately be zero near the map edge.  This comparison must
	  be signed so the first step below zero terminates instead of wrapping
	  into a huge unsigned tile index. )
	? A >= [VHGNDzlo] -> VHGND render z;
	end;

"VHGND traverse faithful"
	( NIV+ iperficie()/fragment(): one map tile per fragment, depth 64,
	  and quadrant-dependent far-to-near painter order.  Bounds of 65 retain
	  every tile whose centre can truncate to source depth 64 while avoiding
	  the source loops over cells that its own depth test must reject. )
	[VHGNDlodstep] = 1; [VHGNDlodradius] = 65;
	A = 0; A - 1; [VHGNDmindepth] = A; [VHGNDmaxdepth] = VHGNDFAR;
	A = [VHGNDbeta]; ? A >= 0 -> VHGND faithful beta ready; A + 360;
    "VHGND faithful beta ready"
	A % 360; [VHGNDtmp] = A;
	? A < 45 -> VHGND faithful north; ? A '>= 315 -> VHGND faithful north;
	? A < 135 -> VHGND faithful east; ? A < 225 -> VHGND faithful south;
	-> VHGND faithful west;

    "VHGND faithful x bounds"
	A = [VHGNDcamtx]; A - 65; [VHGNDxlo] = A;
	? A >= 0 -> VHGND faithful xlo ready; [VHGNDxlo] = 0;
    "VHGND faithful xlo ready"
	A = [VHGNDcamtx]; A + 65; [VHGNDxhi] = A;
	? A '<= 198 -> VHGND faithful x bounds done; [VHGNDxhi] = 198;
    "VHGND faithful x bounds done"
	end;

    "VHGND faithful z bounds"
	A = [VHGNDcamtz]; A - 65; [VHGNDzlo] = A;
	? A >= 0 -> VHGND faithful zlo ready; [VHGNDzlo] = 0;
    "VHGND faithful zlo ready"
	A = [VHGNDcamtz]; A + 65; [VHGNDzhi] = A;
	? A '<= 198 -> VHGND faithful z bounds done; [VHGNDzhi] = 198;
    "VHGND faithful z bounds done"
	end;

    "VHGND faithful north"
	=> VHGND faithful x bounds;
	A = [VHGNDcamtz]; A - 1; [VHGNDzlo] = A;
	? A >= 0 -> VHGND faithful north zlo ready; [VHGNDzlo] = 0;
    "VHGND faithful north zlo ready"
	A = [VHGNDcamtz]; A + 65; [VHGNDzhi] = A;
	? A '<= 198 -> VHGND faithful north zhi ready; [VHGNDzhi] = 198;
    "VHGND faithful north zhi ready"
	[VHGNDz] = [VHGNDzhi];
    "VHGND faithful north z"
	[VHGNDx] = [VHGNDxlo];
    "VHGND faithful north left"
	A = [VHGNDx]; C = [VHGNDcamtx]; ? A >= C -> VHGND faithful north right start;
	=> VHGND tile; [VHGNDx]+; -> VHGND faithful north left;
    "VHGND faithful north right start"
	[VHGNDx] = [VHGNDxhi];
    "VHGND faithful north right"
	A = [VHGNDx]; C = [VHGNDcamtx]; ? A < C -> VHGND faithful north next z;
	=> VHGND tile; [VHGNDx]-; -> VHGND faithful north right;
    "VHGND faithful north next z"
	[VHGNDz]-; A = [VHGNDz]; ? A >= [VHGNDzlo] -> VHGND faithful north z;
	end;

    "VHGND faithful south"
	=> VHGND faithful x bounds;
	A = [VHGNDcamtz]; A - 65; [VHGNDzlo] = A;
	? A >= 0 -> VHGND faithful south zlo ready; [VHGNDzlo] = 0;
    "VHGND faithful south zlo ready"
	A = [VHGNDcamtz]; A + 1; [VHGNDzhi] = A;
	? A '<= 198 -> VHGND faithful south zhi ready; [VHGNDzhi] = 198;
    "VHGND faithful south zhi ready"
	[VHGNDz] = [VHGNDzlo];
    "VHGND faithful south z"
	[VHGNDx] = [VHGNDxlo];
    "VHGND faithful south left"
	A = [VHGNDx]; C = [VHGNDcamtx]; ? A >= C -> VHGND faithful south right start;
	=> VHGND tile; [VHGNDx]+; -> VHGND faithful south left;
    "VHGND faithful south right start"
	[VHGNDx] = [VHGNDxhi];
    "VHGND faithful south right"
	A = [VHGNDx]; C = [VHGNDcamtx]; ? A < C -> VHGND faithful south next z;
	=> VHGND tile; [VHGNDx]-; -> VHGND faithful south right;
    "VHGND faithful south next z"
	[VHGNDz]+; A = [VHGNDz]; ? A '<= [VHGNDzhi] -> VHGND faithful south z;
	end;

    "VHGND faithful east"
	=> VHGND faithful z bounds;
	A = [VHGNDcamtx]; A - 65; [VHGNDxlo] = A;
	? A >= 0 -> VHGND faithful east xlo ready; [VHGNDxlo] = 0;
    "VHGND faithful east xlo ready"
	A = [VHGNDcamtx]; A + 1; [VHGNDxhi] = A;
	? A '<= 198 -> VHGND faithful east xhi ready; [VHGNDxhi] = 198;
    "VHGND faithful east xhi ready"
	[VHGNDx] = [VHGNDxlo];
    "VHGND faithful east x"
	[VHGNDz] = [VHGNDzhi];
    "VHGND faithful east upper"
	A = [VHGNDz]; C = [VHGNDcamtz]; ? A <= C -> VHGND faithful east lower start;
	=> VHGND tile; [VHGNDz]-; -> VHGND faithful east upper;
    "VHGND faithful east lower start"
	[VHGNDz] = [VHGNDzlo];
    "VHGND faithful east lower"
	A = [VHGNDz]; C = [VHGNDcamtz]; ? A > C -> VHGND faithful east next x;
	=> VHGND tile; [VHGNDz]+; -> VHGND faithful east lower;
    "VHGND faithful east next x"
	[VHGNDx]+; A = [VHGNDx]; ? A '<= [VHGNDxhi] -> VHGND faithful east x;
	end;

    "VHGND faithful west"
	=> VHGND faithful z bounds;
	A = [VHGNDcamtx]; A - 1; [VHGNDxlo] = A;
	? A >= 0 -> VHGND faithful west xlo ready; [VHGNDxlo] = 0;
    "VHGND faithful west xlo ready"
	A = [VHGNDcamtx]; A + 65; [VHGNDxhi] = A;
	? A '<= 198 -> VHGND faithful west xhi ready; [VHGNDxhi] = 198;
    "VHGND faithful west xhi ready"
	[VHGNDx] = [VHGNDxhi];
    "VHGND faithful west x"
	[VHGNDz] = [VHGNDzhi];
    "VHGND faithful west upper"
	A = [VHGNDz]; C = [VHGNDcamtz]; ? A <= C -> VHGND faithful west lower start;
	=> VHGND tile; [VHGNDz]-; -> VHGND faithful west upper;
    "VHGND faithful west lower start"
	[VHGNDz] = [VHGNDzlo];
    "VHGND faithful west lower"
	A = [VHGNDz]; C = [VHGNDcamtz]; ? A > C -> VHGND faithful west next x;
	=> VHGND tile; [VHGNDz]+; -> VHGND faithful west lower;
    "VHGND faithful west next x"
	[VHGNDx]-; A = [VHGNDx]; ? A >= [VHGNDxlo] -> VHGND faithful west x;
	end;

"VHGND object view setup"
	( Fixed 15-bit camera vectors for the conservative distant-tile cull. )
	[VHVangle] = [VHGNDbeta]; => VHV sincos;
	[PGFt] = [VHVsin]; => PGF ldf32; [FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1];
	[FI] = 32768; => IntToF; => FMul; => FToIntNear; [VHGNDviewsinb] = [FI];
	[PGFt] = [VHVcos]; => PGF ldf32; [FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1];
	[FI] = 32768; => IntToF; => FMul; => FToIntNear; [VHGNDviewcosb] = [FI];
	[VHVangle] = [VHGNDalpha]; => VHV sincos;
	[PGFt] = [VHVsin]; => PGF ldf32; [FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1];
	[FI] = 32768; => IntToF; => FMul; => FToIntNear; [VHGNDviewsina] = [FI];
	[PGFt] = [VHVcos]; => PGF ldf32; [FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1];
	[FI] = 32768; => IntToF; => FMul; => FToIntNear; [VHGNDviewcosa] = [FI];
	end;

"VHGND object view cull"
	( Coordinates are in 256-world-unit steps.  A four-tile envelope covers
	  the widest distant foliage and giant-tree silhouette, so only wholly
	  invisible tiles are rejected before their source RNG is consumed. )
	A = [VHGNDx]; A < 14; A + 8192; A - [VHGNDcamx]; A '/ 256; [VHGNDdx] = A;
	A = [VHGNDz]; A < 14; A + 8192; A - [VHGNDcamz]; A '/ 256; [VHGNDdz] = A;
	( The signed divisions truncate each component toward zero.  Their squared
	  length therefore never exceeds the exact distance, making the source's
	  42-tile depth cutoff safe to reject before camera rotation and sqrt. )
	A = [VHGNDdx]; A '* [VHGNDdx]; [VHGNDtmp] = A;
	A = [VHGNDdz]; A '* [VHGNDdz]; A + [VHGNDtmp];
	? A '>= 7225344 -> VHGND object view hidden;
	A = [VHGNDdz]; A '* [VHGNDviewsinb];
	C = [VHGNDdx]; C '* [VHGNDviewcosb]; A + C; A '/ 32768; [VHGNDviewrx] = A;
	A = [VHGNDdz]; A '* [VHGNDviewcosb];
	C = [VHGNDdx]; C '* [VHGNDviewsinb]; A - C; A '/ 32768;
	A '* [VHGNDviewcosa]; A '/ 32768; [VHGNDviewrz] = A;
	A = 0; A - 128; ? [VHGNDviewrz] <= A -> VHGND object view hidden;
	A = [VHGNDviewrz]; ? A <= 0 -> VHGND object view visible;
	A '* 3; A '/ 4; A + 128; [VHGNDtmp] = A;
	A = [VHGNDviewrx]; ? A >= 0 -> VHGND object view abs ready;
	C = 0; C - A; A = C;
    "VHGND object view abs ready"
	? A > [VHGNDtmp] -> VHGND object view hidden;
    "VHGND object view visible"
	[VHGNDviewrz] = 1; end;
    "VHGND object view hidden"
	[VHGNDviewrz] = 0; end;

"VHGND fauna view cull"
	( Simulation, model deformation and rendering RNG have already run.  Reject
	  only a complete model whose centre is beyond a conservative 8,192-unit
	  envelope behind or beside the 320x200 view.  Shipped mammal geometry is
	  bounded below 7,000 units even at maximum scale and deformation; birds are
	  smaller, so the shared envelope cannot clip a contributing polygon. )
	A = [VHGNDanix]; A - [VHGNDcamx]; A '/ 256; [VHGNDdx] = A;
	A = [VHGNDaniz]; A - [VHGNDcamz]; A '/ 256; [VHGNDdz] = A;
	A = [VHGNDdz]; A '* [VHGNDviewsinb];
	C = [VHGNDdx]; C '* [VHGNDviewcosb]; A + C; A '/ 32768; [VHGNDviewrx] = A;
	A = [VHGNDdz]; A '* [VHGNDviewcosb];
	C = [VHGNDdx]; C '* [VHGNDviewsinb]; A - C; A '/ 32768;
	A '* [VHGNDviewcosa]; A '/ 32768; [VHGNDviewrz] = A;
	A = 0; A - 32; ? [VHGNDviewrz] <= A -> VHGND fauna view hidden;
	A = [VHGNDviewrz]; ? A <= 0 -> VHGND fauna view visible;
	A '* 3; A '/ 4; A + 32; [VHGNDtmp] = A;
	A = [VHGNDviewrx]; ? A >= 0 -> VHGND fauna view abs ready;
	C = 0; C - A; A = C;
    "VHGND fauna view abs ready"
	? A > [VHGNDtmp] -> VHGND fauna view hidden;
    "VHGND fauna view visible"
	[VHGNDviewrz] = 1; end;
    "VHGND fauna view hidden"
	[VHGNDviewrz] = 0; end;

"VHGND tile"
	( fragment()'s source Manhattan precheck rejects only cells that cannot
	  enter the depth-64 circle.  Do it before shade/depth floating work. )
	A = [VHGNDlodstep]; ? A != 1 -> VHGND tile source range generic;
	=> VHGND faithful tile admission native;
	A = [VHGNDmanhattan]; ? A > 90 -> VHGND tile done;
	-> VHGND tile depth computed;
    "VHGND tile source range generic"
	A = [VHGNDcamtx]; A - [VHGNDx]; ? A >= 0 -> VHGND tile abs x ready;
	C = 0; C - A; A = C;
    "VHGND tile abs x ready"
	[VHGNDtmp] = A;
	A = [VHGNDcamtz]; A - [VHGNDz]; ? A >= 0 -> VHGND tile abs z ready;
	C = 0; C - A; A = C;
    "VHGND tile abs z ready"
	A + [VHGNDtmp]; ? A > 90 -> VHGND tile done;
	"VHGND tile source range ready"
	A = [VHGNDz]; A '* VHGNDMAP; A + [VHGNDx]; [VHGNDh1] = A;
	( Source rejects the raw distance bin above 64 before decrementing it.
	  Do that before the deterministic shade draw so rejected horizon cells
	  pay neither RNG nor ledger/hash work. )
	=> VHGND tile depth;
    "VHGND tile depth computed"
	A = [VHGNDrawdepth]; ? A > VHGNDFAR -> VHGND tile done;
	A = [VHGNDdepth]; ? A > [VHGNDmaxdepth] -> VHGND tile done;
	A = [VHGNDmindepth]; ? A < 0 -> VHGND tile depth accepted;
	A = [VHGNDdepth]; ? A '<= [VHGNDmindepth] -> VHGND tile done;
    "VHGND tile depth accepted"
	=> VHGND tile shade;
	( The traversal bounds guarantee all four map points are within the
	  200x200 surface.  Inline MEM get byte's exact nw+offset load and mask;
	  this removes four generic helper calls from every accepted tile. )
	E = nw; E + RPSM; E + [VHGNDh1];
	A = [E]; A & 255; [VHGNDs1] = A;
	A = E; A + [VHGNDlodstep]; A = [A]; A & 255; [VHGNDs2] = A;
	C = [VHGNDlodstep]; C '* VHGNDMAP;
	A = E; A + C; A + [VHGNDlodstep]; A = [A]; A & 255; [VHGNDs3] = A;
	A = E; A + C; A = [A]; A & 255; [VHGNDs4] = A;
	A = [GRiptype]; ? A != 3 -> VHGND tile ground present;
	A = [VHGNDsctype]; ? A != 1 -> VHGND tile ground present;
	A = [VHGNDs1]; A + [VHGNDs2]; A + [VHGNDs3]; A + [VHGNDs4];
	? A = 0 -> VHGND tile done;
    "VHGND tile ground present"
	[VHGNDruined] = 0;
	A = VHGNDruins; A + [VHGNDh1]; A = [A]; ? A != 0 -> VHGND tile ruined;
	A = VHGNDruins; A + [VHGNDh1]; A + [VHGNDlodstep]; A = [A]; ? A != 0 -> VHGND tile ruined;
	A = [VHGNDlodstep]; A '* VHGNDMAP; A + [VHGNDlodstep]; A + VHGNDruins; A + [VHGNDh1]; A = [A]; ? A != 0 -> VHGND tile ruined;
	A = [VHGNDlodstep]; A '* VHGNDMAP; A + VHGNDruins; A + [VHGNDh1]; A = [A]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND tile ruin ready;
    "VHGND tile ruined"
	[VHGNDruined] = 1;
    "VHGND tile ruin ready"
	( fragment() mirrors terrain heights about sea level.  The reflected pass
	  deliberately stops before the near-detail object path below. )
	A = [VHGNDmirror]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND tile heights ready;
	A = 0; A - [VHGNDs1]; [VHGNDs1] = A;
	A = 0; A - [VHGNDs2]; [VHGNDs2] = A;
	A = 0; A - [VHGNDs3]; [VHGNDs3] = A;
	A = 0; A - [VHGNDs4]; [VHGNDs4] = A;
	[VHGNDreflected]+;
    "VHGND tile heights ready"
	A = [VHGNDshade]; [SPtinta] = A; [DBcol] = A;
	A = [VHGNDruined]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND tile texture ready;
	A = [VHGNDshade]; A & 63; A + 64; [SPtinta] = A; [DBcol] = A;
	[FI] = 512; => IntToF; [PGFi] = FSTX; => PGF sa; [PGFi] = FSTY; => PGF sa;
	A = [VHGNDmirror]; ? A != 0 -> VHGND tile texture ready; [VHGNDruindrawn]+;
    "VHGND tile texture ready"
	[SPescr] = 0; [DBflar] = 0; [DBent] = 0;
	( Source keeps every terrain triangle textured.  Its culling flag changes
	  texture sampling density only; deserts invert the ordinary near/far
	  choice so distant sand does not grow coarser toward the horizon. )
	[SPcull] = 0; A = [VHGNDsctype]; ? A != 3 -> VHGND tile cull normal;
	A = [VHGNDdepth]; ? A '>= 4 -> VHGND tile cull ready; [SPcull] = 1;
	-> VHGND tile cull ready;
    "VHGND tile cull normal"
	A = [VHGNDdepth]; ? A < 4 -> VHGND tile cull ready; [SPcull] = 1;
    "VHGND tile cull ready"
	A = [VHGNDmirror]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND tile mirror cull ready; [SPcull] = 0;
    "VHGND tile mirror cull ready"

	[VHGNDtilepolys] = 0;
	[VHGNDvctri] = 0; A = fw; [PJfwbase] = A;
	=> VHGND terrain triangle load native; => VHGND terrain facing;
	A = [VHGNDmirror]; ? A != 0 -> VHGND tile first mirror facing;
	A = [FCret]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND tile first done; -> VHGND tile first visible;
    "VHGND tile first mirror facing"
	A = [FCret]; ? A != 0 -> VHGND tile first done;
	A = [VHGNDs1]; A + [VHGNDs2]; A + [VHGNDs4]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND tile first done;
    "VHGND tile first visible"
	[VHGNDtilepolys]+;
	A = [VHGNDlodstep]; ? A != 1 -> VHGND tile first flat;
	-> VHGND tile first textured;
    "VHGND tile first flat"
	[PGtexf] = 0; [PJnrv] = 3; => PG poly3d; -> VHGND tile first done;
    "VHGND tile first textured"
	[PGtexf] = 5; [VHGNDvctri] = 0; => VHGND terrain mapped;
    "VHGND tile first done"

	[VHGNDvctri] = 1; => VHGND terrain triangle load native; => VHGND terrain facing;
	A = [VHGNDmirror]; ? A != 0 -> VHGND tile second mirror facing;
	A = [FCret]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND tile second done; -> VHGND tile second visible;
    "VHGND tile second mirror facing"
	A = [FCret]; ? A != 0 -> VHGND tile second done;
	A = [VHGNDs2]; A + [VHGNDs3]; A + [VHGNDs4]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND tile second done;
    "VHGND tile second visible"
	[VHGNDtilepolys]+;
	A = [VHGNDlodstep]; ? A != 1 -> VHGND tile second flat;
	-> VHGND tile second textured;
    "VHGND tile second flat"
	[PGtexf] = 0; [PJnrv] = 3; => PG poly3d; -> VHGND tile second done;
    "VHGND tile second textured"
	[PGtexf] = 5; [VHGNDvctri] = 1; => VHGND terrain mapped;
    "VHGND tile second done"

	A = [VHGNDruined]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND tile texture restored;
	[FI] = [VHGNDtscale]; => IntToF;
	[FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1]; [FI] = 256; => IntToF; => FMul;
	[PGFi] = FSTX; => PGF sa; [PGFi] = FSTY; => PGF sa;
    "VHGND tile texture restored"
	A = [VHGNDruinpass]; ? A != 0 -> VHGND tile done;
	A = [VHGNDmirror]; ? A != 0 -> VHGND tile done;
	A = [VHGNDlodstep]; ? A != 1 -> VHGND tile done;
	( fragment() paints the settled capsule in its terrain cell before fauna
	  and surface objects, so nearer cells can cover it in painter order. )
	A = [VHGNDdropx]; A / VHGNDTS; ? A != [VHGNDx] -> VHGND tile capsule done;
	A = [VHGNDdropz]; A / VHGNDTS; ? A != [VHGNDz] -> VHGND tile capsule done;
	=> VHGND capsule;
    "VHGND tile capsule done"
	A = [VHGNDdepth]; ? A > VHGNDOBJECTFAR -> VHGND tile done;
	? A '<= 8 -> VHGND tile detail visible;
	A = [VHGNDtilepolys]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND tile done;
    "VHGND tile detail visible"
	=> VHGND render tile fauna;
	=> VHGND tile objects;
	"VHGND tile done"
	end;

"VHGND render tile fauna"
