	( NIV+ scans the mixed life-form array in original ID order for every
	  fragment.  Mammals and birds are compacted separately here, so merge
	  their stored source IDs while retaining the dynamic cell test. )
	A = [GRiptype]; ? A != 3 -> VHGND tile fauna done;
	[VHGNDmii] = 0; [VHGNDbii] = 0; [VHGNDanisingle] = 1;
    "VHGND tile fauna merge"
	[VHGNDfaunamid] = 2147483647; [VHGNDfaunabid] = 2147483647;
	A = [VHGNDmii]; ? A '>= [VHGNDanimals] -> VHGND tile fauna mammal id ready;
	A '* 10; A + VHGNDanidata; C = A; [VHGNDfaunamid] = [C plus 9];
    "VHGND tile fauna mammal id ready"
	A = [VHGNDbii]; ? A '>= [VHGNDbirds] -> VHGND tile fauna bird id ready;
	A '* 12; A + VHGNDbirddata; C = A; [VHGNDfaunabid] = [C plus 10];
    "VHGND tile fauna bird id ready"
	A = [VHGNDfaunamid]; ? A != 2147483647 -> VHGND tile fauna choose;
	A = [VHGNDfaunabid]; ? A = 2147483647 -> VHGND tile fauna done;
    "VHGND tile fauna choose"
	A = [VHGNDfaunamid]; ? A '< [VHGNDfaunabid] -> VHGND tile fauna mammal;

    "VHGND tile fauna bird"
	A = [VHGNDbii]; A '* 12; A + VHGNDbirddata; [VHGNDanip] = A; C = A;
	[VHGNDanix] = [C plus 0]; [VHGNDaniz] = [C plus 1]; => VHGND fauna tile match;
	A = [VHGNDviewrz]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND tile fauna bird next;
	[VHGNDanii] = [VHGNDbii]; => VHGND render birds;
    "VHGND tile fauna bird next"
	[VHGNDbii]+; -> VHGND tile fauna merge;

    "VHGND tile fauna mammal"
	A = [VHGNDmii]; A '* 10; A + VHGNDanidata; [VHGNDanip] = A; C = A;
	[VHGNDanix] = [C plus 0]; [VHGNDaniz] = [C plus 1]; => VHGND fauna tile match;
	A = [VHGNDviewrz]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND tile fauna mammal next;
	[VHGNDanii] = [VHGNDmii]; => VHGND render animals;
    "VHGND tile fauna mammal next"
	[VHGNDmii]+; -> VHGND tile fauna merge;

    "VHGND tile fauna done"
	[VHGNDanisingle] = 0; [SPskipmid] = 0;
	end;

"VHGND fauna tile match"
	[VHGNDviewrz] = 0;
	A = [VHGNDanix]; A / VHGNDTS;
	? A >= 0 -> VHGND fauna tile x high; A = 0;
    "VHGND fauna tile x high"
	? A '<= 199 -> VHGND fauna tile x ready; A = 199;
    "VHGND fauna tile x ready"
	? A != [VHGNDx] -> VHGND fauna tile match done;
	A = [VHGNDaniz]; A / VHGNDTS;
	? A >= 0 -> VHGND fauna tile z high; A = 0;
    "VHGND fauna tile z high"
	? A '<= 199 -> VHGND fauna tile z ready; A = 199;
    "VHGND fauna tile z ready"
	? A != [VHGNDz] -> VHGND fauna tile match done;
	[VHGNDviewrz] = 1;
    "VHGND fauna tile match done"
	end;

"VHGND tile objects"
	( Packed source quadrant: low two bits count, then three 2-bit classes. )
	A = [VHGNDz]; A '* VHGNDMAP; A + [VHGNDx]; [VHGNDh1] = A;
	A = nw; A + ROBJ; A + [VHGNDh1]; A = [A]; A & 255;
	[VHGNDobjbyte] = A; A & 3; [VHGNDocount] = A;
	[VHGNDobjid] = 0;
    "VHGND object loop"
	A = [VHGNDocount]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND objects done;
	A = [VHGNDobjbyte]; C = [VHGNDobjid]; C + 1; C < 1; A > C; A & 3;
	[VHGNDobjclass] = A;
	A = [VHGNDh1]; A '* 3; A + [VHGNDobjid]; [VHGNDobjcachep] = A;
	C = VHGNDobjcachex; C + A; [VHGNDoox] = [C];
	C = VHGNDobjcachey; C + A; [VHGNDooy] = [C];
	C = VHGNDobjcachez; C + A; [VHGNDooz] = [C];
	C = VHGNDobjcacheseed; C + A; [SUfseed] = [C];
	A = [VHGNDobjclass]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND object rock;
	? A = 1 -> VHGND object veget; ? A = 2 -> VHGND object tree;
	-> VHGND object next;
    "VHGND object rock"
	=> VHGND rock; -> VHGND object next;
    "VHGND object veget"
	=> VHGND veget; -> VHGND object next;
    "VHGND object tree"
	A = [VHGNDooy]; C = 0; C - 15000; ? A <= C -> VHGND object tall tree;
	=> VHGND bush; -> VHGND object next;
    "VHGND object tall tree"
	=> VHGND tree;
    "VHGND object next"
	[VHGNDobjid]+; [VHGNDocount]-; -> VHGND object loop;
    "VHGND objects done"
	end;

"VHGND veget"
	( ciuffo(): no grass at depth four. Depth three is the source's
	  exact greenmush 1..4 by 1..8 distant speckle mass. Depths two,
	  one and zero draw respectively three, four and six faces for each
	  of the original one or 1..8 terminal subdivisions. )
	A = [VHGNDdepth]; ? A >= 4 -> VHGND veget done;
	A = [VHGNDdepth]; ? A = 3 -> VHGND veget distant;
	[VHGNDgrassdistant] = 0; [VHGNDgrassgroups] = 1;
	[VHGNDgrassfaces] = 3; [VHGNDgrassrad] = 250; [VHGNDgrassrange] = 500;
	A = [VHGNDdepth]; ? A = 2 -> VHGND veget count ready;
	[SUfmask] = 7; => SU frnd; C + 1; [VHGNDgrassgroups] = C;
	[VHGNDgrassfaces] = 4; A = [VHGNDdepth]; ? A = 1 -> VHGND veget count ready;
	[VHGNDgrassfaces] = 6; -> VHGND veget count ready;
    "VHGND veget distant"
	[VHGNDmushx] = [VHGNDoox]; A = [VHGNDooy]; A + 150; [VHGNDmushy] = A;
	[VHGNDmushz] = [VHGNDooz]; [VHGNDmushmask1] = 3; [VHGNDmushmask2] = 7;
	[VHGNDmushscale] = 1023; [VHGNDmushbase] = 216; [VHGNDmushcolmask] = 31;
	=> VHGND greenmush; -> VHGND veget done;
    "VHGND veget count ready"
	A = [VHGNDgrassgroups]; A '* [VHGNDgrassfaces]; [VHGNDgrasstotal] = A;
	[SPcull] = 0; [DBflar] = 0; [DBent] = 0;
    "VHGND veget blade"
	A = [VHGNDgrasstotal]; ? A <= 0 -> VHGND veget done;
	[VHGNDgrassbx] = [VHGNDoox]; A = [VHGNDooy]; A + 150; [VHGNDgrassby] = A;
	[VHGNDgrassbz] = [VHGNDooz];
	A = [VHGNDgrassdistant]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND veget base ready;
	( greenmush correction: centre the 1023-unit random cube. )
	[SUfmask] = 1023; => SU frnd; A = [VHGNDoox]; A + 511; A - C; [VHGNDgrassbx] = A;
	[SUfmask] = 1023; => SU frnd; A = [VHGNDgrassby]; A + 511; A - C; [VHGNDgrassby] = A;
	[SUfmask] = 1023; => SU frnd; A = [VHGNDooz]; A + 511; A - C; [VHGNDgrassbz] = A;
    "VHGND veget base ready"
	( Terminal endpoint: x/z vary over the source 500-unit range; height
	  is a full 1000-unit fast_flandom draw, or a tiny distant speckle. )
	[SUfmask] = 32767; => SU frnd; A = C; A '* [VHGNDgrassrange]; A '/ 32767; A + [VHGNDgrassbx]; [VHGNDtmp] = A;
	[SUfmask] = 32767; => SU frnd; A = C; A '* [VHGNDgrassrange]; A '/ 32767; C = [VHGNDtmp]; C - A; [VHGNDgrassax] = C;
	[SUfmask] = 32767; => SU frnd; A = C; A '* [VHGNDgrassrange]; A '/ 32767; A + [VHGNDgrassbz]; [VHGNDtmp] = A;
	[SUfmask] = 32767; => SU frnd; A = C; A '* [VHGNDgrassrange]; A '/ 32767; C = [VHGNDtmp]; C - A; [VHGNDgrassaz] = C;
	[SUfmask] = 32767; => SU frnd; A = C; A '* 1000; A '/ 32767;
	C = [VHGNDgrassby]; C - A; [VHGNDgrassay] = C;
	A = [VHGNDgrassdistant]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND veget colour;
	A = [VHGNDgrassby]; A - 64; [VHGNDgrassay] = A;
    "VHGND veget colour"
	[SUfmask] = 31; => SU frnd; C + 192; [DBcol] = C;
	A = [VHGNDgrassdistant]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND veget near colour; [DBcol] = C; C + 24; [DBcol] = C;
    "VHGND veget near colour"
	[VHGNDvi] = 0;
	A = [VHGNDgrasstotal]; A & 1; ? A = 0 -> VHGND veget base z;
	A = [VHGNDgrassbx]; A - [VHGNDgrassrad]; [VHGNDvv] = A; [VHGNDvslot] = FSINX; => VHGND vload;
	[VHGNDvv] = [VHGNDgrassby]; [VHGNDvslot] = FSINY; => VHGND vload;
	[VHGNDvv] = [VHGNDgrassbz]; [VHGNDvslot] = FSINZ; => VHGND vload; [VHGNDvi]+;
	A = [VHGNDgrassbx]; A + [VHGNDgrassrad]; [VHGNDvv] = A; [VHGNDvslot] = FSINX; => VHGND vload;
	[VHGNDvv] = [VHGNDgrassby]; [VHGNDvslot] = FSINY; => VHGND vload;
	[VHGNDvv] = [VHGNDgrassbz]; [VHGNDvslot] = FSINZ; => VHGND vload; [VHGNDvi]+;
	-> VHGND veget apex;
    "VHGND veget base z"
	[VHGNDvv] = [VHGNDgrassbx]; [VHGNDvslot] = FSINX; => VHGND vload;
	[VHGNDvv] = [VHGNDgrassby]; [VHGNDvslot] = FSINY; => VHGND vload;
	A = [VHGNDgrassbz]; A - [VHGNDgrassrad]; [VHGNDvv] = A; [VHGNDvslot] = FSINZ; => VHGND vload; [VHGNDvi]+;
	[VHGNDvv] = [VHGNDgrassbx]; [VHGNDvslot] = FSINX; => VHGND vload;
	[VHGNDvv] = [VHGNDgrassby]; [VHGNDvslot] = FSINY; => VHGND vload;
	A = [VHGNDgrassbz]; A + [VHGNDgrassrad]; [VHGNDvv] = A; [VHGNDvslot] = FSINZ; => VHGND vload; [VHGNDvi]+;
    "VHGND veget apex"
	[VHGNDvv] = [VHGNDgrassax]; [VHGNDvslot] = FSINX; => VHGND vload;
	[VHGNDvv] = [VHGNDgrassay]; [VHGNDvslot] = FSINY; => VHGND vload;
	[VHGNDvv] = [VHGNDgrassaz]; [VHGNDvslot] = FSINZ; => VHGND vload;
	[PJnrv] = 3; => PG poly3d;
	[VHGNDgrasstotal]-; -> VHGND veget blade;
    "VHGND veget done"
	end;

"VHGND greenmush"
	( NOCTIS-1.CPP greenmush(): project sparse leaf centres and stamp the
	  original six-pixel silhouette directly into the adapted page. )
	A = [VHGNDmushscale]; A > 1; [VHGNDtmp] = A;
	A = [VHGNDmushx]; A + [VHGNDtmp]; [VHGNDmushx] = A;
	A = [VHGNDmushy]; A + [VHGNDtmp]; [VHGNDmushy] = A;
	A = [VHGNDmushz]; A + [VHGNDtmp]; [VHGNDmushz] = A;
	( C's signed long >>14 is an arithmetic shift.  This matters for the
	  negative world Y of every landed tree and therefore for every foliage
	  RNG stream. )
	A = [VHGNDmushx]; A >> 14; C = A;
	A = [VHGNDmushy]; A >> 14; C + A;
	A = [VHGNDmushz]; A >> 14; A + C; => SU fast srand;
	[SUfmask] = [VHGNDmushmask1]; => VHGND render random; C + 1; [VHGNDmushouter] = C;
    "VHGND greenmush outer"
	[SUfmask] = [VHGNDmushscale]; => VHGND render random; A = [VHGNDmushx]; A - C; [VHGNDmushpx] = A;
	=> VHGND render random; A = [VHGNDmushy]; A - C; [VHGNDmushpy] = A;
	=> VHGND render random; A = [VHGNDmushz]; A - C; [VHGNDmushpz] = A;
	( getcoords consumes scalar input slot zero, independent of whichever
	  polygon vertex the previous caller left selected. )
	[VHGNDvi] = 0;
	[VHGNDvv] = [VHGNDmushpx]; [VHGNDvslot] = FSINX; => VHGND vload;
	[VHGNDvv] = [VHGNDmushpy]; [VHGNDvslot] = FSINY; => VHGND vload;
	[VHGNDvv] = [VHGNDmushpz]; [VHGNDvslot] = FSINZ; => VHGND vload;
	=> PG getcoords;
	A = [GCret]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND greenmush outer next;
	[SUfmask] = [VHGNDmushmask2]; => VHGND render random; C + 1; [VHGNDmushinner] = C;
    "VHGND greenmush inner"
	[SUfmask] = 7; => VHGND render random; A = [GCy]; A + C; A '* 320; [VHGNDmushoff] = A;
	=> VHGND render random; A = [GCx]; A + C; C = [VHGNDmushoff]; C + A; [VHGNDmushoff] = C;
	[SUfmask] = [VHGNDmushcolmask]; => VHGND render random; C + [VHGNDmushbase];
	D = nw; D + RADPT; D + [VHGNDmushoff];
	[D plus 4] = C; [D plus 5] = C; [D plus 3] = C; [D plus 324] = C;
	A = D; A - 316; [A] = C; A = D; A - 636; [A] = C;
	[VHGNDmushinner]-; A = [VHGNDmushinner]; ? A > 0 -> VHGND greenmush inner;
    "VHGND greenmush outer next"
	[VHGNDmushouter]-; A = [VHGNDmushouter]; ? A > 0 -> VHGND greenmush outer;
	end;

"VHGND render random"
	( Exact SU fast raw transition using x86's unsigned 32-bit square.
	  MUL leaves the low half in A and high half in D, matching Mul64u. )
	A = [SUfseed]; { F7 E0 }
	C = A; C & 0FFh; B = D; B & 0FFh; C + B; C & 0FFh;
	A & 0FFFFFF00h; A | C; [SUfeax] = A;
	B = [SUfseed]; B + A; [SUfseed] = B;
	A & [SUfmask]; [SUfval] = A; C = A;
	end;

"VHGND tree"
	( albero()/build_fractal_tree(): retain the world-level parameters from
	  build_surface, seed each tree from x+y+z+3, and execute the source's
	  complete depth-first branch structure.  Ordinary trees use two layers;
	  the rare 333 giant retains all four and its five-sided 72-degree limbs. )
	A = [VHGNDoox]; A + [VHGNDooy]; A + [VHGNDooz]; A + 3; => SU fast srand;
	[SUfmask] = 511; => VHGND render random; [VHTkind] = C;
	[VHGNDtreescale] = [GRtreescale]; [VHTreduc] = [GRtreespread];
	[VHTwidth] = [GRbranchwidth]; [VHGNDtreelayers] = 2;
	[VHGNDtreescalef] = [GRtreescalef]; [VHTreducf] = [GRtreespreadf];
	[VHTwidthf] = [GRbranchwidthf]; [VHTpeakf] = [GRtreepeakf];
	[VHTforks] = 2; [VHTpeak] = [GRtreepeak];
	[VHTrootcol] = [GRrootshade]; [VHTleafcol] = 192;
	[VHGNDtreefaces] = 3;
	A = [VHTkind]; ? A = 333 -> VHGND tree giant;
	A = [VHGNDdepth]; ? A > 20 -> VHGND tree far;
	? A > 10 -> VHGND tree middle;
	? A > 3 -> VHGND tree distant;
	A = [VHTkind]; A > 3; ? A = 11 -> VHGND tree forced broadleaf;
	? A = 22 -> VHGND tree forced conifer; -> VHGND tree configured;

    "VHGND tree far"
	A = [GRtreescale]; A / 2; [VHGNDtmp] = A;
	[VHGNDmushmask1] = 3; [VHGNDmushmask2] = 7; -> VHGND tree mush ready;
    "VHGND tree middle"
	A = [GRtreescale]; A '* 7; A / 10; [VHGNDtmp] = A;
	[VHGNDmushmask1] = 7; [VHGNDmushmask2] = 15; -> VHGND tree mush ready;
    "VHGND tree distant"
	A = [GRtreescale]; A '* 9; A / 10; [VHGNDtmp] = A;
	[VHGNDmushmask1] = 15; [VHGNDmushmask2] = 31;
    "VHGND tree mush ready"
	A = [VHGNDooy]; A - [VHGNDtmp]; [VHGNDmushy] = A;
	[VHGNDmushx] = [VHGNDoox]; [VHGNDmushz] = [VHGNDooz];
	[VHGNDmushscale] = [GRmushscale]; [VHGNDmushbase] = 223; [VHGNDmushcolmask] = 31;
	=> VHGND greenmush; -> VHGND tree done;
"VHGND tree forced broadleaf"
	[VHTforks] = 3; A = [GRtreepeak]; A '* 2; [VHTpeak] = A;
	[GRtreeci] = 2; { D9 87 <dGRtreepeakf mtp bytesperunit> DA 8F <dGRtreeci mtp bytesperunit>
	  D9 9F <dVHTpeakf mtp bytesperunit> }
	[VHTrootcol] = 128; -> VHGND tree configured;
"VHGND tree forced conifer"
	[VHTforks] = 2; A = [GRtreepeak]; A / 2; [VHTpeak] = A;
	{ D9 87 <dGRtreepeakf mtp bytesperunit> DC 8F <dGRK050L mtp bytesperunit>
	  D9 9F <dVHTpeakf mtp bytesperunit> }
	[VHTrootcol] = 128; [VHTleafcol] = 64; -> VHGND tree configured;
"VHGND tree giant"
	A = [GRtreescale]; A '* 2; [VHGNDtreescale] = A;
	A = [GRbranchwidth]; A '* 3; A / 2; [VHTwidth] = A;
	A = [GRtreepeak]; A '* 3; A / 2; [VHTpeak] = A;
	[GRtreeci] = 2; { D9 87 <dGRtreescalef mtp bytesperunit> DA 8F <dGRtreeci mtp bytesperunit>
	  D9 9F <dVHGNDtreescalef mtp bytesperunit> }
	[GRtreeci] = 3; { D9 87 <dGRbranchwidthf mtp bytesperunit> DA 8F <dGRtreeci mtp bytesperunit>
	  DC 8F <dGRK050L mtp bytesperunit> D9 9F <dVHTwidthf mtp bytesperunit> }
	{ D9 87 <dGRtreepeakf mtp bytesperunit> DA 8F <dGRtreeci mtp bytesperunit>
	  DC 8F <dGRK050L mtp bytesperunit> D9 9F <dVHTpeakf mtp bytesperunit> }
	A = [VHGNDdepth]; ? A > 11 -> VHGND tree giant mush;
	? A > 7 -> VHGND tree giant middle; ? A > 4 -> VHGND tree giant near;
	( The closest GIANT_TREE keeps the source's full four-layer,
	  three-division stack and five-sided 72-degree silhouette. )
	[VHGNDtreelayers] = 4; [VHTforks] = 3; [VHGNDtreefaces] = 5;
	-> VHGND tree configured;
    "VHGND tree giant middle"
	[VHGNDtreelayers] = 3; [VHTforks] = 2; [VHGNDtreefaces] = 3;
	-> VHGND tree configured;
    "VHGND tree giant near"
	[VHGNDtreelayers] = 4; [VHTforks] = 3; [VHGNDtreefaces] = 3;
	-> VHGND tree configured;
    "VHGND tree giant mush"
	A = [GRtreescale]; A / 2; [VHGNDtmp] = A;
	[VHGNDmushx] = [VHGNDoox]; A = [VHGNDooy]; A - [VHGNDtmp]; [VHGNDmushy] = A;
	[VHGNDmushz] = [VHGNDooz]; [VHGNDmushmask1] = 7; [VHGNDmushmask2] = 15;
	[VHGNDmushscale] = [GRmushscale]; [VHGNDmushbase] = 223; [VHGNDmushcolmask] = 31;
	=> VHGND greenmush; -> VHGND tree done;
"VHGND tree configured"
	[SPcull] = 0; [DBent] = 0; [VHGNDtreelevel] = 0; [VHGNDtreefloat] = 1;
	=> VHGND tree trig init;
	[FI] = [VHGNDoox]; => IntToF; => F32Narrow; => FStoreF32; A = VHGNDtsx; [A] = [FS0];
	[FI] = [VHGNDooy]; => IntToF; => F32Narrow; => FStoreF32; A = VHGNDtsy; [A] = [FS0];
	[FI] = [VHGNDooz]; => IntToF; => F32Narrow; => FStoreF32; A = VHGNDtsz; [A] = [FS0];
	A = VHGNDtsscale; [A] = [VHGNDtreescalef];
	A = VHGNDtslayers; [A] = [VHGNDtreelayers]; A = VHGNDtsroot; [A] = 1;
	A = VHGNDtsocc; [A] = 0;
	-> VHGND tree node enter;

"VHGND tree node enter"
	A = VHGNDtsx; A + [VHGNDtreelevel]; [VHGNDtreebxf] = [A];
	[PGFt] = [VHGNDtreebxf]; => PGF ldf32; => FToIntChop; [VHGNDtreebx] = [FI];
	A = VHGNDtsy; A + [VHGNDtreelevel]; [VHGNDtreebyf] = [A];
	[PGFt] = [VHGNDtreebyf]; => PGF ldf32; => FToIntChop; [VHGNDtreeby] = [FI];
	A = VHGNDtsz; A + [VHGNDtreelevel]; [VHGNDtreebzf] = [A];
	[PGFt] = [VHGNDtreebzf]; => PGF ldf32; => FToIntChop; [VHGNDtreebz] = [FI];
	A = VHGNDtsscale; A + [VHGNDtreelevel]; [VHGNDtreescalef] = [A];
	[PGFt] = [VHGNDtreescalef]; => PGF ldf32; => FToIntChop; [VHGNDtreescale] = [FI];
	A = VHGNDtslayers; A + [VHGNDtreelevel]; [VHGNDtreelayers] = [A];
	A = VHGNDtsroot; A + [VHGNDtreelevel]; [VHGNDtreeisroot] = [A];
	A = VHGNDtsocc; A + [VHGNDtreelevel]; [VHGNDtreeocc] = [A];
	[VHGNDtreebranches] = 1; A = [VHGNDtreeisroot]; ? A != 0 -> VHGND tree node count ready;
	[SUfmask] = [VHTforks]; => SU frnd; C + 1; [VHGNDtreebranches] = C;
    "VHGND tree node count ready"
	A = VHGNDtscount; A + [VHGNDtreelevel]; [A] = [VHGNDtreebranches];
	A = VHGNDtsbranch; A + [VHGNDtreelevel]; [A] = 0;
	( The source seed truncates the sum of its binary32 node coordinates. )
	{ D9 87 <dVHGNDtreebxf mtp bytesperunit> D8 87 <dVHGNDtreebyf mtp bytesperunit>
	  D8 87 <dVHGNDtreebzf mtp bytesperunit> DA 87 <dVHGNDh1 mtp bytesperunit>
	  DD 9F <dFA0 mtp bytesperunit> }
	=> FToIntChop; A = [FI]; C = VHGNDtslseed; C + [VHGNDtreelevel]; [C] = A;
	C = [VHGNDtreeisroot]; ? C = 0 -> VHGND tree node nonroot range;
	{ D9 87 <dVHGNDtreescalef mtp bytesperunit> D8 8F <dVHTpeakf mtp bytesperunit>
	  DC 8F <dGRK020L mtp bytesperunit> D9 9F <dVHGNDtreerangef mtp bytesperunit> }
	-> VHGND tree node range ready;
"VHGND tree node nonroot range"
	{ D9 87 <dVHGNDtreescalef mtp bytesperunit> D8 8F <dVHTpeakf mtp bytesperunit>
	  DC 8F <dGRK050L mtp bytesperunit> D9 9F <dVHGNDtreerangef mtp bytesperunit> }
"VHGND tree node range ready"
	[PGFt] = [VHGNDtreerangef]; => PGF ldf32; => FToIntChop; [VHGNDtreerange] = [FI];
	C = VHGNDtsrange; C + [VHGNDtreelevel]; [C] = [VHGNDtreerangef];
	-> VHGND tree node branch;

"VHGND tree node branch"
	A = VHGNDtsx; A + [VHGNDtreelevel]; [VHGNDtreebxf] = [A];
	[PGFt] = [VHGNDtreebxf]; => PGF ldf32; => FToIntChop; [VHGNDtreebx] = [FI];
	A = VHGNDtsy; A + [VHGNDtreelevel]; [VHGNDtreebyf] = [A];
	[PGFt] = [VHGNDtreebyf]; => PGF ldf32; => FToIntChop; [VHGNDtreeby] = [FI];
	A = VHGNDtsz; A + [VHGNDtreelevel]; [VHGNDtreebzf] = [A];
	[PGFt] = [VHGNDtreebzf]; => PGF ldf32; => FToIntChop; [VHGNDtreebz] = [FI];
	A = VHGNDtsscale; A + [VHGNDtreelevel]; [VHGNDtreescalef] = [A];
	[PGFt] = [VHGNDtreescalef]; => PGF ldf32; => FToIntChop; [VHGNDtreescale] = [FI];
	A = VHGNDtslayers; A + [VHGNDtreelevel]; [VHGNDtreelayers] = [A];
	A = VHGNDtsroot; A + [VHGNDtreelevel]; [VHGNDtreeisroot] = [A];
	A = VHGNDtsocc; A + [VHGNDtreelevel]; [VHGNDtreeocc] = [A];
	A = VHGNDtscount; A + [VHGNDtreelevel]; [VHGNDtreebranches] = [A];
	A = VHGNDtsbranch; A + [VHGNDtreelevel]; [VHGNDtreebranch] = [A];
	A = [VHGNDtreebranch]; ? A '>= [VHGNDtreebranches] -> VHGND tree node pop;
	A = VHGNDtsrange; A + [VHGNDtreelevel]; [VHGNDtreerangef] = [A];
	[PGFt] = [VHGNDtreerangef]; => PGF ldf32; => FToIntChop; [VHGNDtreerange] = [FI];
	=> VHGND tree direction;
	A = VHGNDtslseed; A + [VHGNDtreelevel]; C = [A]; [VHTlseed] = C;
	C + 3; [A] = C; A = [VHTlseed]; => SU fast srand;
	A = [VHGNDtreelayers]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND tree terminal;
	A = [VHGNDtreebranches]; ? A = 1 -> VHGND tree endpoint pi;

	{ D9 87 <dVHGNDtreeangle mtp bytesperunit> D9 FF
	  D8 8F <dVHGNDtreerangef mtp bytesperunit>
	  D8 87 <dVHGNDtreebxf mtp bytesperunit> D9 9F <dVHGNDtreeexf mtp bytesperunit>
	  D9 87 <dVHGNDtreeangle mtp bytesperunit> D9 FE
	  D8 8F <dVHGNDtreerangef mtp bytesperunit>
	  D8 87 <dVHGNDtreebzf mtp bytesperunit> D9 9F <dVHGNDtreeezf mtp bytesperunit> }
	-> VHGND tree endpoint ready;
    "VHGND tree endpoint pi"
	{ D9 87 <dVHGNDtreebxf mtp bytesperunit> D8 A7 <dVHGNDtreerangef mtp bytesperunit>
	  D9 9F <dVHGNDtreeexf mtp bytesperunit> }
	[VHGNDtreeezf] = [VHGNDtreebzf];
    "VHGND tree endpoint ready"
	[PGFt] = [VHGNDtreeexf]; => PGF ldf32; => FToIntChop; [VHGNDtreeex] = [FI];
	[PGFt] = [VHGNDtreeezf]; => PGF ldf32; => FToIntChop; [VHGNDtreeez] = [FI];
	[SUfmask] = 32767; => SU frnd; [GRtreeci] = C; => GR tree flandom;
	C = [VHGNDtreeisroot]; ? C = 0 -> VHGND tree child nonroot height;
	{ D9 87 <dGRtreefl mtp bytesperunit> D8 8F <dGRrootheightf mtp bytesperunit>
	  DC 87 <dGRK010L mtp bytesperunit> D8 8F <dVHGNDtreescalef mtp bytesperunit>
	  D9 87 <dVHGNDtreebyf mtp bytesperunit> DE E1 D9 9F <dVHGNDtreeeyf mtp bytesperunit> }
	-> VHGND tree child height ready;
"VHGND tree child nonroot height"
	{ D9 87 <dGRtreefl mtp bytesperunit> DC 87 <dGRK025L mtp bytesperunit>
	  D8 8F <dVHGNDtreescalef mtp bytesperunit> DC 8F <dGRK025L mtp bytesperunit>
	  D9 87 <dVHGNDtreebyf mtp bytesperunit> DE E1 D9 9F <dVHGNDtreeeyf mtp bytesperunit> }
"VHGND tree child height ready"
	[PGFt] = [VHGNDtreeeyf]; => PGF ldf32; => FToIntChop; [VHGNDtreeey] = [FI];
	{ D9 87 <dVHGNDtreescalef mtp bytesperunit> D8 8F <dVHTreducf mtp bytesperunit>
	  D9 9F <dVHTnextscf mtp bytesperunit>
	  D9 87 <dVHGNDtreescalef mtp bytesperunit> D8 8F <dVHTwidthf mtp bytesperunit>
	  D9 9F <dVHGNDtreebr mtp bytesperunit>
	  D9 87 <dVHTreducf mtp bytesperunit> D8 8F <dVHGNDtreescalef mtp bytesperunit>
	  D8 8F <dVHTwidthf mtp bytesperunit> D9 9F <dVHGNDtreeer mtp bytesperunit> }
	[PGFt] = [VHTnextscf]; => PGF ldf32; => FToIntChop; [VHTnextsc] = [FI];
	A = [VHGNDtreeocc]; A '* 9; A + [VHTrootcol]; [VHTfacebase] = A;
	[VHTfacestep] = 4; [DBflar] = [GRtreeflares]; => VHGND tree limb;
	A = VHGNDtsbranch; A + [VHGNDtreelevel]; C = [A]; C + 1; [A] = C;
	[VHGNDtreelevel]+;
	A = VHGNDtsx; A + [VHGNDtreelevel]; [A] = [VHGNDtreeexf];
	A = VHGNDtsy; A + [VHGNDtreelevel]; [A] = [VHGNDtreeeyf];
	A = VHGNDtsz; A + [VHGNDtreelevel]; [A] = [VHGNDtreeezf];
	A = VHGNDtsscale; A + [VHGNDtreelevel]; [A] = [VHTnextscf];
	A = VHGNDtslayers; A + [VHGNDtreelevel]; C = [VHGNDtreelayers]; C - 1; [A] = C;
	A = VHGNDtsroot; A + [VHGNDtreelevel]; [A] = 0;
	A = VHGNDtsocc; A + [VHGNDtreelevel]; C = [VHGNDtreeocc]; C + 1; [A] = C;
	-> VHGND tree node enter;

    "VHGND tree terminal"
	( Terminal source node: each subdivision is its own randomized leaf fan. )
	( x2 = x + fast_flandom()*range - fast_flandom()*range.  Keep the
	  first partial sum wide across the second RNG call, then narrow only at
	  the source's float assignment. )
	[SUfmask] = 32767; => SU frnd; [GRtreeci] = C; => GR tree flandom;
	[PGFi] = FSW0; [PGFt] = [GRtreefl]; => PGF setf32;
	[PGFi] = FSW1; [PGFt] = [VHGNDtreerangef]; => PGF setf32;
	[PGFi] = FSW0; => PGF a; [PGFi] = FSW1; => PGF mul; [PGFi] = FSW0; => PGF sa;
	[PGFi] = FSW1; [PGFt] = [VHGNDtreebxf]; => PGF setf32;
	[PGFi] = FSW1; => PGF a; [PGFi] = FSW0; => PGF add;
	[GRtreeacc0] = [FA0]; [GRtreeacc0 plus 1] = [FA1];
	[SUfmask] = 32767; => SU frnd; [GRtreeci] = C; => GR tree flandom;
	[PGFi] = FSW0; [PGFt] = [GRtreefl]; => PGF setf32;
	[PGFi] = FSW1; [PGFt] = [VHGNDtreerangef]; => PGF setf32;
	[PGFi] = FSW0; => PGF a; [PGFi] = FSW1; => PGF mul;
	[FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1]; [FA0] = [GRtreeacc0]; [FA1] = [GRtreeacc0 plus 1];
	=> FSub; => F32Narrow; => FStoreF32; [VHGNDtreeleafx] = [FS0];

	( z2 follows the same live-x87 schedule. )
	[SUfmask] = 32767; => SU frnd; [GRtreeci] = C; => GR tree flandom;
	[PGFi] = FSW0; [PGFt] = [GRtreefl]; => PGF setf32;
	[PGFi] = FSW1; [PGFt] = [VHGNDtreerangef]; => PGF setf32;
	[PGFi] = FSW0; => PGF a; [PGFi] = FSW1; => PGF mul; [PGFi] = FSW0; => PGF sa;
	[PGFi] = FSW1; [PGFt] = [VHGNDtreebzf]; => PGF setf32;
	[PGFi] = FSW1; => PGF a; [PGFi] = FSW0; => PGF add;
	[GRtreeacc0] = [FA0]; [GRtreeacc0 plus 1] = [FA1];
	[SUfmask] = 32767; => SU frnd; [GRtreeci] = C; => GR tree flandom;
	[PGFi] = FSW0; [PGFt] = [GRtreefl]; => PGF setf32;
	[PGFi] = FSW1; [PGFt] = [VHGNDtreerangef]; => PGF setf32;
	[PGFi] = FSW0; => PGF a; [PGFi] = FSW1; => PGF mul;
	[FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1]; [FA0] = [GRtreeacc0]; [FA1] = [GRtreeacc0 plus 1];
	=> FSub; => F32Narrow; => FStoreF32; [VHGNDtreeleafz] = [FS0];

	( fy[2] = y-fast_flandom()*scaling; widthscale1=scaling*globalwidth. )
	[SUfmask] = 32767; => SU frnd; [GRtreeci] = C; => GR tree flandom;
	[PGFi] = FSW0; [PGFt] = [GRtreefl]; => PGF setf32;
	[PGFi] = FSW1; [PGFt] = [VHGNDtreescalef]; => PGF setf32;
	[PGFi] = FSW0; => PGF a; [PGFi] = FSW1; => PGF mul;
	[FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1]; [PGFi] = FSW0; [PGFt] = [VHGNDtreebyf]; => PGF setf32;
	=> FSub; => F32Narrow; => FStoreF32; [VHGNDtreeleafdrop] = [FS0];
	[PGFi] = FSW0; [PGFt] = [VHGNDtreescalef]; => PGF setf32;
	[PGFi] = FSW1; [PGFt] = [VHTwidthf]; => PGF setf32;
	[PGFi] = FSW0; => PGF a; [PGFi] = FSW1; => PGF mul;
	=> F32Narrow; => FStoreF32; [VHGNDtreebr] = [FS0];
	[VHGNDtreeex] = [VHGNDtreebx]; [VHGNDtreeey] = [VHGNDtreeby]; [VHGNDtreeez] = [VHGNDtreebz];
	[SUfmask] = 31; => SU frnd; C + [VHTleafcol]; [VHGNDtreecol] = C;
	[VHTfacebase] = C; [VHTfacestep] = 2;
	[DBflar] = [GRleafflares]; => VHGND tree leaves;
	A = VHGNDtsbranch; A + [VHGNDtreelevel]; C = [A]; C + 1; [A] = C;
	-> VHGND tree node branch;

    "VHGND tree node pop"
	A = [VHGNDtreelevel]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND tree done;
	[VHGNDtreelevel]-; -> VHGND tree node branch;
"VHGND tree done"
	[DBflar] = 0; [VHGNDtreefloat] = 0;
	end;

"VHGND bush"
	( cespuglio(): low-ground tree-class objects are bushes. At depth three
	  the source collapses them to greenmush foliage. Nearer bushes use
	  scaling 3000, reduction .75, width .15 and one/two/three/four child
	  branches selected by the original depth-dependent bit mask. )
	A = [VHGNDdepth]; ? A >= 3 -> VHGND bush distant;
	[VHGNDtreescale] = 3000; [VHGNDtreerange] = 2250;
	[VHGNDtreebr] = 450; [VHGNDtreeer] = 337; [VHGNDtreefaces] = 3;
	[SUfmask] = 3; => SU frnd; [VHGNDtreeorient] = C;
	[SUfmask] = 31; => SU frnd; C + 192; [VHGNDtreecol] = C;
	[VHGNDtreebranches] = 1; A = [VHGNDdepth]; ? A = 2 -> VHGND bush mask one;
	? A = 1 -> VHGND bush mask two; [SUfmask] = 3; -> VHGND bush count;
    "VHGND bush mask one"
	[VHGNDtreefaces] = 2; [SUfmask] = 1; -> VHGND bush count;
    "VHGND bush mask two"
	[SUfmask] = 2;
    "VHGND bush count"
	=> SU frnd; C + 1; [VHGNDtreebranches] = C;
	[SPcull] = 0; [DBflar] = 0; [DBent] = 0; [VHGNDtreebranch] = 0;
    "VHGND bush branch loop"
	A = [VHGNDtreebranch]; ? A '>= [VHGNDtreebranches] -> VHGND bush done;
	[VHGNDtreebx] = [VHGNDoox]; [VHGNDtreeby] = [VHGNDooy]; [VHGNDtreebz] = [VHGNDooz];
	=> VHGND tree direction;
	A = [VHGNDtreepx]; A + [VHGNDoox]; [VHGNDtreeex] = A;
	A = [VHGNDtreepz]; A + [VHGNDooz]; [VHGNDtreeez] = A;
	( y2 = y - (fast_flandom()+.25)*3000*.25 )
	[SUfmask] = 32767; => SU frnd; A = C; A '* 750; A '/ 32767; A + 187;
	C = [VHGNDooy]; C - A; [VHGNDtreeey] = C;
	[VHTfacebase] = 0; [VHTfacestep] = 4;
	[VHGNDtreefloat] = 0; [DBflar] = [GRtreeflares]; => VHGND tree limb;
	( Terminal layer: width 2250*.15 and range 2250*1.5*.5. )
	[VHGNDtreeleafrad] = 337;
	[SUfmask] = 32767; => SU frnd; A = C; A '* 1687; A '/ 32767; A + [VHGNDtreeex]; [VHGNDtmp] = A;
	[SUfmask] = 32767; => SU frnd; A = C; A '* 1687; A '/ 32767; C = [VHGNDtmp]; C - A; [VHGNDtreeleafx] = C;
	[SUfmask] = 32767; => SU frnd; A = C; A '* 1687; A '/ 32767; A + [VHGNDtreeez]; [VHGNDtmp] = A;
	[SUfmask] = 32767; => SU frnd; A = C; A '* 1687; A '/ 32767; C = [VHGNDtmp]; C - A; [VHGNDtreeleafz] = C;
	[SUfmask] = 32767; => SU frnd; A = C; A '* 2250; A '/ 32767; [VHGNDtreeleafdrop] = A;
	[SUfmask] = 31; => SU frnd; C + 192; [VHGNDtreecol] = C;
	[VHTfacebase] = C; [VHTfacestep] = 2;
	[DBflar] = [GRleafflares]; => VHGND tree leaves;
	[VHGNDtreebranch]+; -> VHGND bush branch loop;
    "VHGND bush distant"
	[VHGNDmushx] = [VHGNDoox]; [VHGNDmushy] = [VHGNDooy]; [VHGNDmushz] = [VHGNDooz];
	[VHGNDmushmask1] = 7; [VHGNDmushmask2] = 7; [VHGNDmushscale] = [GRmushscale];
	[VHGNDmushbase] = 209; [VHGNDmushcolmask] = 31; => VHGND greenmush;
    "VHGND bush done"
	end;

"VHGND tree direction"
	( Native b_angle is not an integral-degree lookup.  Borland narrows
	  b_angle_delta=(float)(2*M_PI/subdivs), narrows its half-angle, then
	  narrows every +=delta step.  Those low bits feed the next node seed. )
	[PGFt] = 40C90FDBh; => PGF ldf32; [PGFi] = FSW0; => PGF sa;
	[FI] = [VHGNDtreebranches]; => IntToF; => VHGND fb fa;
	[PGFi] = FSW0; => PGF a; => FQuo; => F32Narrow; => FStoreF32; [VHGNDtmp] = [FS0];
	[PGFt] = [VHGNDtmp]; => PGF ldf32;
	[FB0] = [GRK050L]; [FB1] = [GRK050H]; => FMul;
	=> F32Narrow; => FStoreF32; [VHGNDtreeangle] = [FS0];
	[VHGNDvi] = 0;
    "VHGND tree direction advance"
	A = [VHGNDvi]; ? A '>= [VHGNDtreebranch] -> VHGND tree direction trig;
	[PGFt] = [VHGNDtreeangle]; => PGF ldf32; [PGFi] = FSW0; => PGF sa;
	[PGFi] = FSW1; [PGFt] = [VHGNDtmp]; => PGF setf32;
	[PGFi] = FSW0; => PGF a; [PGFi] = FSW1; => PGF add;
	=> F32Narrow; => FStoreF32; [VHGNDtreeangle] = [FS0];
	[VHGNDvi]+; -> VHGND tree direction advance;
    "VHGND tree direction trig"
    "VHGND tree direction done"
	end;

"VHGND tree limb"
	( Distant integer-tree fallback remains a single bounded centreline. )
	A = [VHGNDtreefloat]; ? A != 0 -> VHGND tree limb native faces;
    "VHGND tree limb integer endpoints"
	[FI] = [VHGNDtreebx]; => IntToF; => F32Narrow; => FStoreF32; [VHSx0] = [FS0];
	[FI] = [VHGNDtreeby]; => IntToF; => F32Narrow; => FStoreF32; [VHSy0] = [FS0];
	[FI] = [VHGNDtreebz]; => IntToF; => F32Narrow; => FStoreF32; [VHSz0] = [FS0];
	[FI] = [VHGNDtreeex]; => IntToF; => F32Narrow; => FStoreF32; [VHSx1] = [FS0];
	[FI] = [VHGNDtreeey]; => IntToF; => F32Narrow; => FStoreF32; [VHSy1] = [FS0];
	[FI] = [VHGNDtreeez]; => IntToF; => F32Narrow; => FStoreF32; [VHSz1] = [FS0];
    "VHGND tree limb endpoints ready"
	[VHSflare] = 0; [VHScolor] = 62; => VH stick3d;
	end;

"VHGND tree limb native faces"
	( Source branch face: 3x8 repeating matrix over the live p_background
	  window.  PGtexoff models txtr += 48 after every face, including a
	  back-facing face, and is restored before descending to the child. )
	[SPterrain] = 0; [SPmapfast] = 1; [SPpixfast] = 0; [SPtrifast] = 0;
	[VHGNDtreefloat] = 2;
	A = [DBflar]; A & 15; ? A = 0 -> VHGND tree limb pixel fast;
	A & 1; ? A = 0 -> VHGND tree limb mapper ready;
    "VHGND tree limb pixel fast"
	[SPpixfast] = 1;
    "VHGND tree limb mapper ready"
	[FI] = 3; => IntToF; [PGFi] = FSTX; => PGF sa;
	[FI] = 8; => IntToF; [PGFi] = FSTY; => PGF sa;
	[PGtexf] = 5; [PGtexoff] = 0; [SPsrc] = 1; [SPflar] = [DBflar];
	[VHGNDtreeside] = 0;
    "VHGND tree limb face"
	A = [VHGNDtreeside]; ? A '>= [VHGNDtreefaces] -> VHGND tree limb done;
	A '* [VHTfacestep]; A + [VHTfacebase]; [SPtinta] = A;
	[VHGNDvi] = 0;
	[VHGNDtreecx] = [VHGNDtreebxf]; [VHGNDtreecy] = [VHGNDtreebyf]; [VHGNDtreecz] = [VHGNDtreebzf];
	[VHGNDtreerad] = [VHGNDtreebr]; A = 360; A / [VHGNDtreefaces]; [VHGNDtmp] = A;
	A '* [VHGNDtreeside]; [VHGNDtreeangle] = A; => VHGND tree polar vertex;
	A = [VHGNDtreeangle]; A + [VHGNDtmp]; [VHGNDtreeangle] = A; => VHGND tree polar vertex;
	A = [VHGNDtreeisroot]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND tree limb upper;
	[VHGNDtreepoint] = 0; => VHGND tree root height;
	[VHGNDtreepoint] = 1; => VHGND tree root height;
    "VHGND tree limb upper"
	[VHGNDtreecx] = [VHGNDtreeexf]; [VHGNDtreecy] = [VHGNDtreeeyf]; [VHGNDtreecz] = [VHGNDtreeezf]; [VHGNDtreerad] = [VHGNDtreeer];
	=> VHGND tree polar vertex;
	A = [VHGNDtreeangle]; A - [VHGNDtmp]; [VHGNDtreeangle] = A; => VHGND tree polar vertex;
	=> PG facing; A = [FCret]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND tree limb advance;
	[PJnrv] = 4; => PG polymap;
    "VHGND tree limb advance"
	A = [PGtexoff]; A + 48; [PGtexoff] = A;
	[VHGNDtreeside]+; -> VHGND tree limb face;
    "VHGND tree limb done"
	[SPmapfast] = 0; [SPpixfast] = 0; [VHGNDtreefloat] = 1;
	=> VHGND tree texture restore;
	end;

"VHGND tree leaves"
	( Terminal source fan.  Its second ring point is rotation+72 and its tip
	  is rotation+36 regardless of branchdetail.  The 1x3 repeating texture
	  and 48-byte face stride mirror the source's live txtr window. )
	[SPterrain] = 0; [SPmapfast] = 1; [SPpixfast] = 0; [SPtrifast] = 1;
	[VHGNDtreefloat] = 2;
	A = [DBflar]; A & 15; ? A = 0 -> VHGND tree leaf pixel fast;
	A & 1; ? A = 0 -> VHGND tree leaf mapper ready;
    "VHGND tree leaf pixel fast"
	[SPpixfast] = 1;
    "VHGND tree leaf mapper ready"
	[FI] = 1; => IntToF; [PGFi] = FSTX; => PGF sa;
	[FI] = 3; => IntToF; [PGFi] = FSTY; => PGF sa;
	[PGtexf] = 5; [PGtexoff] = 0; [SPsrc] = 1; [SPflar] = [DBflar];
	[VHGNDtreerad] = [VHGNDtreebr];
	[VHGNDtreeside] = 0;
    "VHGND tree leaf face"
	A = [VHGNDtreeside]; ? A '>= [VHGNDtreefaces] -> VHGND tree leaves done;
	A '* [VHTfacestep]; A + [VHTfacebase]; [SPtinta] = A;
	[VHGNDvi] = 0; [VHGNDtreecx] = [VHGNDtreebxf]; [VHGNDtreecy] = [VHGNDtreebyf]; [VHGNDtreecz] = [VHGNDtreebzf];
	A = 360; A / [VHGNDtreefaces]; A '* [VHGNDtreeside]; [VHGNDtreeangle] = A;
	=> VHGND tree polar vertex;
	A = [VHGNDtreeangle]; A + 72; [VHGNDtreeangle] = A; => VHGND tree polar vertex;
	A = [VHGNDtreeangle]; A - 36; [VHGNDtreeangle] = A; => VHGND tree leaf tip vertex;
	[PJnrv] = 3; => PG polymap;
	( greenmush remains the integer projection helper; convert the exact leaf
	  tip only at this compatibility boundary instead of interpreting bits. )
	[PGFt] = [VHGNDtreepx]; => PGF ldf32; => FToIntChop; [VHGNDmushx] = [FI];
	[PGFt] = [VHGNDtreeleafdrop]; => PGF ldf32; => FToIntChop; [VHGNDmushy] = [FI];
	[PGFt] = [VHGNDtreepz]; => PGF ldf32; => FToIntChop; [VHGNDmushz] = [FI];
	[VHGNDmushmask1] = 15; [VHGNDmushmask2] = 3; [VHGNDmushscale] = 1023;
	[VHGNDmushbase] = 223; [VHGNDmushcolmask] = 31; => VHGND greenmush;
	A = [PGtexoff]; A + 48; [PGtexoff] = A;
	[VHGNDtreeside]+; -> VHGND tree leaf face;
    "VHGND tree leaves done"
	[SPmapfast] = 0; [SPpixfast] = 0; [SPtrifast] = 0;
	[VHGNDtreefloat] = 1;
	=> VHGND tree texture restore;
	end;

"VHGND tree leaf tip vertex"
	( fx[2]/fz[2] keep x2 + trig*range + wind live until their one
	  binary32 array store, matching the native expression. )
	=> VHGND tree sincos;
	[PGFi] = FSW0; [PGFt] = [VHVcos]; => PGF setf32;
	[PGFi] = FSW1; [PGFt] = [VHGNDtreerangef]; => PGF setf32;
	[PGFi] = FSW0; => PGF a; [PGFi] = FSW1; => PGF mul; [PGFi] = FSW0; => PGF sa;
	[PGFi] = FSW1; [PGFt] = [VHGNDtreeleafx]; => PGF setf32;
	[PGFi] = FSW1; => PGF a; [PGFi] = FSW0; => PGF add; [PGFi] = FSW0; => PGF sa;
	[PGFi] = FSW1; [PGFt] = [VHGNDtreewindx]; => PGF setf32;
	[PGFi] = FSW0; => PGF a; [PGFi] = FSW1; => PGF add; => F32Narrow;
	A = FSINX; A + [VHGNDvi]; [PGFi] = A; => PGF sa; => FStoreF32; [VHGNDtreepx] = [FS0];
	[PGFi] = FSW0; [PGFt] = [VHVsin]; => PGF setf32;
	[PGFi] = FSW1; [PGFt] = [VHGNDtreerangef]; => PGF setf32;
	[PGFi] = FSW0; => PGF a; [PGFi] = FSW1; => PGF mul; [PGFi] = FSW0; => PGF sa;
	[PGFi] = FSW1; [PGFt] = [VHGNDtreeleafz]; => PGF setf32;
	[PGFi] = FSW1; => PGF a; [PGFi] = FSW0; => PGF add; [PGFi] = FSW0; => PGF sa;
	[PGFi] = FSW1; [PGFt] = [VHGNDtreewindz]; => PGF setf32;
	[PGFi] = FSW0; => PGF a; [PGFi] = FSW1; => PGF add; => F32Narrow;
	A = FSINZ; A + [VHGNDvi]; [PGFi] = A; => PGF sa; => FStoreF32; [VHGNDtreepz] = [FS0];
	A = FSINY; A + [VHGNDvi]; [PGFi] = A; [PGFt] = [VHGNDtreeleafdrop]; => PGF setf32;
	[VHGNDvi]+;
	end;

"VHGND tree wind"
	( wdircos/wdirsin = 10*cos/sin(wdir)*wp.  The capsule stores direction
	  in sixteenth-degrees and power as wp*100. )
	A = [VHGCwindangle]; A + 8; A / 16; [VHVangle] = A; => VHV sincos;
	[FI] = [VHGCwindpower]; => IntToF; [PGFi] = FSW1; => PGF sa;
	[PGFi] = FSW0; [PGFt] = [VHVcos]; => PGF setf32;
	[PGFi] = FSW0; => PGF a; [PGFi] = FSW1; => PGF mul; [PGFi] = FSW0; => PGF sa;
	[FI] = 10; => IntToF; => VHGND fb fa; [PGFi] = FSW0; => PGF a; => FQuo;
	=> F32Narrow; => FStoreF32; [VHGNDtreewindx] = [FS0];
	[PGFi] = FSW0; [PGFt] = [VHVsin]; => PGF setf32;
	[PGFi] = FSW0; => PGF a; [PGFi] = FSW1; => PGF mul; [PGFi] = FSW0; => PGF sa;
	[FI] = 10; => IntToF; => VHGND fb fa; [PGFi] = FSW0; => PGF a; => FQuo;
	=> F32Narrow; => FStoreF32; [VHGNDtreewindz] = [FS0];
	end;

"VHGND tree texture restore"
	[PGtexoff] = 0; [PGtexf] = 0; [SPflar] = 0;
	[FI] = [VHGNDtscale]; => IntToF; [FB0] = [FA0]; [FB1] = [FA1];
	[FI] = 256; => IntToF; => FMul; [PGFi] = FSTX; => PGF sa; [PGFi] = FSTY; => PGF sa;
	end;

"VHGND tree root height"
	( build_fractal_tree seats both bottom vertices of its root branch on
	  hpoint().  The polar coordinates are binary32, then implicitly chopped
	  to the long parameters.  Reuse the exact landed HPOINT chain and retain
	  the traversal/camera globals which that shared helper uses as scratch. )
	[VHTcamxsave] = [VHGNDcamx]; [VHTcamzsave] = [VHGNDcamz];
	[VHTseedtilesave] = [VHGNDh1];
	A = FSINX; A + [VHGNDtreepoint]; [PGFi] = A; => PGF a; => FToIntChop;
	[VHGNDcamx] = [FI];
	A = FSINZ; A + [VHGNDtreepoint]; [PGFi] = A; => PGF a; => FToIntChop;
	[VHGNDcamz] = [FI];
	=> VHGND eye height;
	A = FSINY; A + [VHGNDtreepoint]; [PGFi] = A;
	[PGFt] = [VHGNDpy]; => PGF setf32;
	[VHGNDcamx] = [VHTcamxsave]; [VHGNDcamz] = [VHTcamzsave];
	[VHGNDh1] = [VHTseedtilesave];
	end;

"VHGND tree polar vertex"
	A = [VHGNDtreefloat]; ? A = 2 -> VHGND tree polar float vertex;
	=> VHGND tree polar point;
	-> VHGND tree vertex load;

"VHGND tree polar float vertex"
	=> VHGND tree sincos;
	{ D9 87 <dVHVcos mtp bytesperunit> D8 8F <dVHGNDtreerad mtp bytesperunit>
	  D8 87 <dVHGNDtreecx mtp bytesperunit> D9 9F <dVHGNDtreepx mtp bytesperunit>
	  D9 87 <dVHVsin mtp bytesperunit> D8 8F <dVHGNDtreerad mtp bytesperunit>
	  D8 87 <dVHGNDtreecz mtp bytesperunit> D9 9F <dVHGNDtreepz mtp bytesperunit> }
	A = FSINX; A + [VHGNDvi]; [PGFi] = A; [PGFt] = [VHGNDtreepx]; => PGF setf32;
	A = FSINY; A + [VHGNDvi]; [PGFi] = A; [PGFt] = [VHGNDtreecy]; => PGF setf32;
	A = FSINZ; A + [VHGNDvi]; [PGFi] = A; [PGFt] = [VHGNDtreepz]; => PGF setf32;
	[VHGNDvi]+;
	end;

"VHGND tree trig init"
	( NOCTIS builds lft_sin/lft_cos by repeatedly adding the binary64
	  pi/180 step.  Direct degree multiplication differs in low bits and can
	  change greenmush seeds after the source's float-to-long casts. )
	A = [VHTtrigready]; ? A != 0 -> VHGND tree trig init done;
	[VHTtrigi] = 0; [VHTtriga0] = 0; [VHTtriga1] = 0;
    "VHGND tree trig build"
	[PGFt] = [VHTtriga0]; [PGFu] = [VHTtriga1]; => PGF ldf64;
	[PGFi] = FSW0; => PGF sa; => FCos; => F32Narrow; => FStoreF32;
	A = VHTcostab; A + [VHTtrigi]; [A] = [FS0];
	[PGFi] = FSW0; => PGF a; => FSin; => F32Narrow; => FStoreF32;
	A = VHTsintab; A + [VHTtrigi]; [A] = [FS0];
	[PGFi] = FSW0; => PGF a; [FB0] = VHVDEGLO; [FB1] = VHVDEGHI; => FAdd;
	[VHTtriga0] = [FA0]; [VHTtriga1] = [FA1];
	[VHTtrigi]+; A = [VHTtrigi]; ? A <= 360 -> VHGND tree trig build;
	[VHTtrigready] = 1;
    "VHGND tree trig init done"
	end;

"VHGND tree sincos"
	A = [VHGNDtreeangle]; A % 360; ? A >= 0 -> VHGND tree trig normalized; A + 360;
    "VHGND tree trig normalized"
	C = VHTsintab; C + A; [VHVsin] = [C];
	C = VHTcostab; C + A; [VHVcos] = [C];
	end;

"VHGND tree polar point"
	[VHVangle] = [VHGNDtreeangle]; => VHV sincos;
	[FI] = [VHGNDtreerad]; => IntToF; [PGFi] = FSW1; => PGF sa;
	[PGFi] = FSW0; [PGFt] = [VHVcos]; => PGF setf32;
	[PGFi] = FSW0; => PGF a; [PGFi] = FSW1; => PGF mul; => PGF int;
	A = [FI]; A + [VHGNDtreecx]; [VHGNDtreepx] = A;
	[PGFi] = FSW0; [PGFt] = [VHVsin]; => PGF setf32;
	[PGFi] = FSW0; => PGF a; [PGFi] = FSW1; => PGF mul; => PGF int;
	A = [FI]; A + [VHGNDtreecz]; [VHGNDtreepz] = A;
	end;

"VHGND tree vertex load"
	[VHGNDvv] = [VHGNDtreepx]; [VHGNDvslot] = FSINX; => VHGND vload;
	[VHGNDvv] = [VHGNDtreecy]; [VHGNDvslot] = FSINY; => VHGND vload;
	[VHGNDvv] = [VHGNDtreepz]; [VHGNDvslot] = FSINZ; => VHGND vload; [VHGNDvi]+;
	end;

"VHGND rock"
	( roccia(): deterministic tetrahedral stones.  Depths above two retain
	  the source's single-triangle LOD.  Close groups use fivefold scale,
	  repeat fast_random(rockdensity) times, halve scale after each stone,
	  and move each following centre by the original cdown-weighted draws. )
	[VHGNDrocktile] = [VHGNDh1];
	A = [VHGNDz]; A '* VHGNDMAP; A + [VHGNDx]; => SU fast srand;
	[SUfmask] = [VHGNDrockdensity]; => VHGND render random; [VHGNDcdown] = C;
	A = C; ? A = 0 -> VHGND rock done;
	A = [VHGNDdepth]; ? A > 2 -> VHGND rock distant;
	[VHGNDrockworkscale] = [VHGNDrockscale];
	A = [VHGNDrockworkscale]; A '* 5; [VHGNDrockworkscale] = A;
	( setfx(quartz): close stones use the planet's quartz translucency state. )
	[SPflar] = [VHGNDquartz]; [DBflar] = [VHGNDquartz];
	[SPcull] = 0; [SPhalf] = 0; [SPescr] = 0; [DBent] = 0;
	[PGtexf] = 5; [SPsrc] = 1;
    "VHGND rock repeat"

	( Source order is five base-coordinate draws, four colour draws, then
	  the apex draw.  This order is procedural state, not cosmetic. )
	( base point 0: x-rand(scale), z-rand(scale) )
	[SUfmask] = 32767; => SU frnd; A = C; A '* [VHGNDrockworkscale]; A '/ 32767;
	C = [VHGNDoox]; C - A; [VHGNDrx0] = C;
	[SUfmask] = 32767; => SU frnd; A = C; A '* [VHGNDrockworkscale]; A '/ 32767;
	C = [VHGNDooz]; C - A; [VHGNDrz0] = C;

	( base point 1: centre x, z+rand(scale) )
	[VHGNDrx1] = [VHGNDoox];
	[SUfmask] = 32767; => SU frnd; A = C; A '* [VHGNDrockworkscale]; A '/ 32767;
	C = [VHGNDooz]; C + A; [VHGNDrz1] = C;

	( base point 2: x+rand(scale), z-rand(scale) )
	[SUfmask] = 32767; => SU frnd; A = C; A '* [VHGNDrockworkscale]; A '/ 32767;
	C = [VHGNDoox]; C + A; [VHGNDrx2] = C;
	[SUfmask] = 32767; => SU frnd; A = C; A '* [VHGNDrockworkscale]; A '/ 32767;
	C = [VHGNDooz]; C - A; [VHGNDrz2] = C;

	[SUfmask] = 63; => SU frnd; [VHGNDrcolor] = C;
	[SUfmask] = 7; => SU frnd; C + [VHGNDrcolor]; [VHGNDrcol0] = C;
	[SUfmask] = 15; => SU frnd; C + [VHGNDrcolor]; [VHGNDrcol1] = C;
	[SUfmask] = 31; => SU frnd; C + [VHGNDrcolor]; [VHGNDrcol2] = C;

	( apex height = supplied fragment ground - 100 - fast_flandom()*peak )
	[SUfmask] = 32767; => SU frnd; A = C; A '* [VHGNDrockpeak]; A '/ 32767;
	C = [VHGNDooy]; C - 100; C - A; [VHGNDrtop] = C;

	( hpoint() for the three base vertices; deterministic results are reused
	  across their two adjoining faces. )
	[VHGNDrox] = [VHGNDrx0]; [VHGNDroz] = [VHGNDrz0]; => VHGND rock height; [VHGNDry0] = [VHGNDroy];
	[VHGNDrox] = [VHGNDrx1]; [VHGNDroz] = [VHGNDrz1]; => VHGND rock height; [VHGNDry1] = [VHGNDroy];
	[VHGNDrox] = [VHGNDrx2]; [VHGNDroz] = [VHGNDrz2]; => VHGND rock height; [VHGNDry2] = [VHGNDroy];

	( face 0: base0, base1, apex )
	[VHGNDvi] = 0; [VHGNDvv] = [VHGNDrx0]; [VHGNDvslot] = FSINX; => VHGND vload;
	[VHGNDvv] = [VHGNDry0]; [VHGNDvslot] = FSINY; => VHGND vload;
	[VHGNDvv] = [VHGNDrz0]; [VHGNDvslot] = FSINZ; => VHGND vload; [VHGNDvi]+;
	[VHGNDvv] = [VHGNDrx1]; [VHGNDvslot] = FSINX; => VHGND vload;
	[VHGNDvv] = [VHGNDry1]; [VHGNDvslot] = FSINY; => VHGND vload;
	[VHGNDvv] = [VHGNDrz1]; [VHGNDvslot] = FSINZ; => VHGND vload; [VHGNDvi]+;
	[VHGNDvv] = [VHGNDoox]; [VHGNDvslot] = FSINX; => VHGND vload;
	[VHGNDvv] = [VHGNDrtop]; [VHGNDvslot] = FSINY; => VHGND vload;
	[VHGNDvv] = [VHGNDooz]; [VHGNDvslot] = FSINZ; => VHGND vload;
	=> PG facing; A = [FCret]; ? A != 0 -> VHGND rock face one;
	[DBcol] = [VHGNDrcol0]; [SPtinta] = [VHGNDrcol0]; => VHGND rock draw face;

	"VHGND rock face one"
	( face 1: base1, base2, apex )
	[VHGNDvi] = 0; [VHGNDvv] = [VHGNDrx1]; [VHGNDvslot] = FSINX; => VHGND vload;
	[VHGNDvv] = [VHGNDry1]; [VHGNDvslot] = FSINY; => VHGND vload;
	[VHGNDvv] = [VHGNDrz1]; [VHGNDvslot] = FSINZ; => VHGND vload; [VHGNDvi]+;
	[VHGNDvv] = [VHGNDrx2]; [VHGNDvslot] = FSINX; => VHGND vload;
	[VHGNDvv] = [VHGNDry2]; [VHGNDvslot] = FSINY; => VHGND vload;
	[VHGNDvv] = [VHGNDrz2]; [VHGNDvslot] = FSINZ; => VHGND vload; [VHGNDvi]+;
	[VHGNDvv] = [VHGNDoox]; [VHGNDvslot] = FSINX; => VHGND vload;
	[VHGNDvv] = [VHGNDrtop]; [VHGNDvslot] = FSINY; => VHGND vload;
	[VHGNDvv] = [VHGNDooz]; [VHGNDvslot] = FSINZ; => VHGND vload;
	=> PG facing; A = [FCret]; ? A != 0 -> VHGND rock face two;
	[DBcol] = [VHGNDrcol1]; [SPtinta] = [VHGNDrcol1]; => VHGND rock draw face;

	"VHGND rock face two"
	( face 2: base2, base0, apex )
	[VHGNDvi] = 0; [VHGNDvv] = [VHGNDrx2]; [VHGNDvslot] = FSINX; => VHGND vload;
	[VHGNDvv] = [VHGNDry2]; [VHGNDvslot] = FSINY; => VHGND vload;
	[VHGNDvv] = [VHGNDrz2]; [VHGNDvslot] = FSINZ; => VHGND vload; [VHGNDvi]+;
	[VHGNDvv] = [VHGNDrx0]; [VHGNDvslot] = FSINX; => VHGND vload;
	[VHGNDvv] = [VHGNDry0]; [VHGNDvslot] = FSINY; => VHGND vload;
	[VHGNDvv] = [VHGNDrz0]; [VHGNDvslot] = FSINZ; => VHGND vload; [VHGNDvi]+;
	[VHGNDvv] = [VHGNDoox]; [VHGNDvslot] = FSINX; => VHGND vload;
	[VHGNDvv] = [VHGNDrtop]; [VHGNDvslot] = FSINY; => VHGND vload;
	[VHGNDvv] = [VHGNDooz]; [VHGNDvslot] = FSINZ; => VHGND vload;
	=> PG facing; A = [FCret]; ? A != 0 -> VHGND rock faces done;
	[DBcol] = [VHGNDrcol2]; [SPtinta] = [VHGNDrcol2]; => VHGND rock draw face;
	"VHGND rock faces done"
	( Source rockrep centre walk and scale decay. )
	[SUfmask] = 32767; => SU frnd; A = C; A '* 1000; A '* [VHGNDcdown]; A '/ 32767;
	A + [VHGNDoox]; [VHGNDtmp] = A;
	[SUfmask] = 32767; => SU frnd; A = C; A '* 1000; A '* [VHGNDcdown]; A '/ 32767;
	C = [VHGNDtmp]; C - A; [VHGNDoox] = C;
	[SUfmask] = 32767; => SU frnd; A = C; A '* 1000; A '* [VHGNDcdown]; A '/ 32767;
	A + [VHGNDooz]; [VHGNDtmp] = A;
	[SUfmask] = 32767; => SU frnd; A = C; A '* 1000; A '* [VHGNDcdown]; A '/ 32767;
	C = [VHGNDtmp]; C - A; [VHGNDooz] = C;
	[VHGNDrox] = [VHGNDoox]; [VHGNDroz] = [VHGNDooz]; => VHGND rock height;
	[VHGNDooy] = [VHGNDroy];
	A = [VHGNDrockworkscale]; A '/ 2; [VHGNDrockworkscale] = A;
	[VHGNDcdown]-; A = [VHGNDcdown]; ? A > 0 -> VHGND rock repeat;
	[SPflar] = 0; [DBflar] = 0; [SPcull] = 0; [SPhalf] = 0;
	-> VHGND rock done;

    "VHGND rock draw face"
	( At depths zero and one NIV+ duplicates the apex into vertex four and
	  maps p_background.  Depth two keeps the solid three-vertex face. )
	A = [VHGNDdepth]; ? A >= 2 -> VHGND rock draw solid;
	[VHGNDvi]+;
	[VHGNDvv] = [VHGNDoox]; [VHGNDvslot] = FSINX; => VHGND vload;
	[VHGNDvv] = [VHGNDrtop]; [VHGNDvslot] = FSINY; => VHGND vload;
	[VHGNDvv] = [VHGNDooz]; [VHGNDvslot] = FSINZ; => VHGND vload;
	[PJnrv] = 4; => PG polymap; end;
    "VHGND rock draw solid"
	[PJnrv] = 3; => PG poly3d; end;

    "VHGND rock distant"
	( roccia() depths three through seven: a single irregular triangle,
	  independently peaked at all three vertices. )
	[VHGNDrx0] = [VHGNDoox]; [VHGNDrz0] = [VHGNDooz];
	[VHGNDrx1] = [VHGNDoox]; [VHGNDrz1] = [VHGNDooz];
	[SUfmask] = 32767; => VHGND render random; A = C; A '* [VHGNDrockscale]; A '/ 32767;
	C = [VHGNDoox]; C + [VHGNDrockscale]; C - A; [VHGNDrx2] = C;
	=> VHGND render random; A = C; A '* [VHGNDrockscale]; A '/ 32767;
	C = [VHGNDooz]; C + [VHGNDrockscale]; C - A; [VHGNDrz2] = C;
	=> VHGND render random; A = C; A '* [VHGNDrockpeak]; A '/ 32767;
	C = [VHGNDooy]; C - 100; C - A; [VHGNDry0] = C;
	=> VHGND render random; A = C; A '* [VHGNDrockpeak]; A '/ 32767;
	C = [VHGNDooy]; C - 100; C - A; [VHGNDry1] = C;
	=> VHGND render random; A = C; A '* [VHGNDrockpeak]; A '/ 32767;
	C = [VHGNDooy]; C - 100; C - A; [VHGNDry2] = C;
	[SUfmask] = 71; => VHGND render random; [DBcol] = C;
	[SPcull] = 0; [DBflar] = 0; [DBent] = 0; [VHGNDvi] = 0;
	[VHGNDvv] = [VHGNDrx0]; [VHGNDvslot] = FSINX; => VHGND vload;
	[VHGNDvv] = [VHGNDry0]; [VHGNDvslot] = FSINY; => VHGND vload;
	[VHGNDvv] = [VHGNDrz0]; [VHGNDvslot] = FSINZ; => VHGND vload; [VHGNDvi]+;
	[VHGNDvv] = [VHGNDrx1]; [VHGNDvslot] = FSINX; => VHGND vload;
	[VHGNDvv] = [VHGNDry1]; [VHGNDvslot] = FSINY; => VHGND vload;
	[VHGNDvv] = [VHGNDrz1]; [VHGNDvslot] = FSINZ; => VHGND vload; [VHGNDvi]+;
	[VHGNDvv] = [VHGNDrx2]; [VHGNDvslot] = FSINX; => VHGND vload;
	[VHGNDvv] = [VHGNDry2]; [VHGNDvslot] = FSINY; => VHGND vload;
	[VHGNDvv] = [VHGNDrz2]; [VHGNDvslot] = FSINZ; => VHGND vload;
	=> PG facing; A = [FCret]; ? A = 0 -> VHGND rock done;
	[PJnrv] = 3; => PG poly3d;
    "VHGND rock done"
	end;

"VHGND rock height"
	[VHGNDcamx] = [VHGNDrox]; [VHGNDcamz] = [VHGNDroz]; => VHGND eye height;
	A = [VHGNDheight]; A + 600; [VHGNDroy] = A;
	[VHGNDcamx] = [VHGNDviewx]; [VHGNDcamz] = [VHGNDviewz]; [VHGNDh1] = [VHGNDrocktile];
	end;

"VHGND tile depth"
	( hpdep = sqrt((pos_x-tile_centre_x)^2 +
	               (pos_z-tile_centre_z)^2), then depth=(hpdep>>14)-1. )
	A = [VHGNDx]; A < 14; C = [VHGNDlodstep]; C < 13; A + C;
	C = [VHGNDcamx]; C - A;
	[VHGNDdx] = C;
	A = [VHGNDz]; A < 14; C = [VHGNDlodstep]; C < 13; A + C;
	C = [VHGNDcamz]; C - A;
	[VHGNDdz] = C;
	( Fuse the existing IntToF / square / add / sqrt / chop chain without
	  changing its qword spill points or x87 control-word transition. )
	{
	    DB 87 <dVHGNDdx mtp bytesperunit> (fild dx)
	    DD 9F <dFA0 mtp bytesperunit>      (fstp qword FA)
	    DD 87 <dFA0 mtp bytesperunit>      (fld qword FA)
	    DC 8F <dFA0 mtp bytesperunit>      (fmul qword FA)
	    DD 9F <dFT0 mtp bytesperunit>      (fstp qword dx2)
	    DB 87 <dVHGNDdz mtp bytesperunit> (fild dz)
	    DD 9F <dFA0 mtp bytesperunit>      (fstp qword FA)
	    DD 87 <dFA0 mtp bytesperunit>      (fld qword FA)
	    DC 8F <dFA0 mtp bytesperunit>      (fmul qword FA)
	    DD 9F <dFA0 mtp bytesperunit>      (fstp qword dz2)
	    DD 87 <dFA0 mtp bytesperunit>      (fld qword dz2)
	    DC 87 <dFT0 mtp bytesperunit>      (fadd qword dx2)
	    DD 9F <dFA0 mtp bytesperunit>      (fstp qword sum)
	    DD 87 <dFA0 mtp bytesperunit>      (fld qword sum)
	    D9 FA                              (fsqrt)
	    DD 9F <dFA0 mtp bytesperunit>      (fstp qword distance)
	    D9 AF <dGRcwc mtp bytesperunit>    (fldcw chop)
	    DD 87 <dFA0 mtp bytesperunit>      (fld qword distance)
	    DB 9F <dFI mtp bytesperunit>       (fistp dword depth)
	    D9 AF <dGRcwn mtp bytesperunit>    (fldcw nearest)
	}
	A = [FI]; A > 14; [VHGNDrawdepth] = A;
	A - 1; ? A >= 0 -> VHGND tile depth ready; A = 0;
    "VHGND tile depth ready"
	[VHGNDdepth] = A;
	end;

"VHGND faithful tile admission native"
	( Unit-tile source Manhattan gate and accepted-cell depth in one call.
	  The early return keeps rejected cells at NIV+'s exact no-map-index/no-x87
	  side-effect boundary without paying a second native-call transition. )
	{
	    8B 87 <dVHGNDcamtx mtp bytesperunit>
	    2B 87 <dVHGNDx mtp bytesperunit>
	    89 C1
	    C1 F9 1F
	    31 C8
	    29 C8
	    89 C3
	    8B 87 <dVHGNDcamtz mtp bytesperunit>
	    2B 87 <dVHGNDz mtp bytesperunit>
	    89 C1
	    C1 F9 1F
	    31 C8
	    29 C8
	    01 D8
	    89 87 <dVHGNDmanhattan mtp bytesperunit>
	    83 F8 5A
	    7E 01
	    C3

	    8B 87 <dVHGNDz mtp bytesperunit>
	    69 C0 C8 00 00 00
	    03 87 <dVHGNDx mtp bytesperunit>
	    89 87 <dVHGNDh1 mtp bytesperunit>

	    8B 87 <dVHGNDx mtp bytesperunit>
	    C1 E0 0E
	    05 00 20 00 00
	    8B 9F <dVHGNDcamx mtp bytesperunit>
	    29 C3
	    89 9F <dVHGNDdx mtp bytesperunit>
	    8B 87 <dVHGNDz mtp bytesperunit>
	    C1 E0 0E
	    05 00 20 00 00
	    8B 9F <dVHGNDcamz mtp bytesperunit>
	    29 C3
	    89 9F <dVHGNDdz mtp bytesperunit>

	    DB 87 <dVHGNDdx mtp bytesperunit>
	    DD 9F <dFA0 mtp bytesperunit>
	    DD 87 <dFA0 mtp bytesperunit>
	    DC 8F <dFA0 mtp bytesperunit>
	    DD 9F <dFT0 mtp bytesperunit>
	    DB 87 <dVHGNDdz mtp bytesperunit>
	    DD 9F <dFA0 mtp bytesperunit>
	    DD 87 <dFA0 mtp bytesperunit>
	    DC 8F <dFA0 mtp bytesperunit>
	    DD 9F <dFA0 mtp bytesperunit>
	    DD 87 <dFA0 mtp bytesperunit>
	    DC 87 <dFT0 mtp bytesperunit>
	    DD 9F <dFA0 mtp bytesperunit>
	    DD 87 <dFA0 mtp bytesperunit>
	    D9 FA
	    DD 9F <dFA0 mtp bytesperunit>
	    D9 AF <dGRcwc mtp bytesperunit>
	    DD 87 <dFA0 mtp bytesperunit>
	    DB 9F <dFI mtp bytesperunit>
	    D9 AF <dGRcwn mtp bytesperunit>
	    8B 87 <dFI mtp bytesperunit>
	    C1 F8 0E
	    89 87 <dVHGNDrawdepth mtp bytesperunit>
	    48
	    85 C0
	    79 02
	    31 C0
	    89 87 <dVHGNDdepth mtp bytesperunit>
	}
	end;

"VHGND tile shade"
	( NOCTIS-1.CPP:1125-1150, diffuse-light arm (sh_delta == 0).
	  Each tile gets the original deterministic 8..15 base shade from
	  fast_random(7), followed by the source distance haze.  The live
	  landing fixtures are close to a perpendicular sun, so this is the
	  active source path and selects surface_palette's ground band 0..63. )
	A = [VHGNDh1]; A + [VHGNDseed]; => SU fast srand;
	[SUfmask] = 7; => VHGND render random;
	C + 8; [VHGNDshade] = C;
	A = [VHGNDdepth]; A > 1; C = [VHGNDshade]; C + A;
	? C '<= 32 -> VHGND tile shade ready; C = 32;
    "VHGND tile shade ready"
	[VHGNDshade] = C;
	end;

"VHGND terrain triangle load native"
	( Build all nine exact binary64 input coordinates for one terrain triangle
	  in a single fragment.  Integer shifts/signs and fild conversion are the
	  same operations previously paid through eighteen Lino helper calls. )
	{
	    55
	    8B B7 <dPJfwbase mtp bytesperunit>
	    8D 34 B7
	    8B 9F <dVHGNDx mtp bytesperunit>
	    C1 E3 0E
	    8B 8F <dVHGNDlodstep mtp bytesperunit>
	    C1 E1 0E
	    01 D9
	    8B 97 <dVHGNDz mtp bytesperunit>
	    C1 E2 0E
	    8B AF <dVHGNDlodstep mtp bytesperunit>
	    C1 E5 0E
	    01 D5
	    83 BF <dVHGNDvctri mtp bytesperunit> 00
	    0F 85 87 00 00 00
	    89 9F <dFI mtp bytesperunit>
	    DB 87 <dFI mtp bytesperunit>
	    DD 9E E0 07 00 00
	    89 97 <dFI mtp bytesperunit>
	    DB 87 <dFI mtp bytesperunit>
	    DD 9E 20 08 00 00
	    8B 87 <dVHGNDs1 mtp bytesperunit>
	    C1 E0 0B
	    F7 D8
	    89 87 <dFI mtp bytesperunit>
	    DB 87 <dFI mtp bytesperunit>
	    DD 9E 00 08 00 00
	    89 8F <dFI mtp bytesperunit>
	    DB 87 <dFI mtp bytesperunit>
	    DD 9E E8 07 00 00
	    89 97 <dFI mtp bytesperunit>
	    DB 87 <dFI mtp bytesperunit>
	    DD 9E 28 08 00 00
	    8B 87 <dVHGNDs2 mtp bytesperunit>
	    C1 E0 0B
	    F7 D8
	    89 87 <dFI mtp bytesperunit>
	    DB 87 <dFI mtp bytesperunit>
	    DD 9E 08 08 00 00
	    E9 82 00 00 00
	    89 8F <dFI mtp bytesperunit>
	    DB 87 <dFI mtp bytesperunit>
	    DD 9E E0 07 00 00
	    89 97 <dFI mtp bytesperunit>
	    DB 87 <dFI mtp bytesperunit>
	    DD 9E 20 08 00 00
	    8B 87 <dVHGNDs2 mtp bytesperunit>
	    C1 E0 0B
	    F7 D8
	    89 87 <dFI mtp bytesperunit>
	    DB 87 <dFI mtp bytesperunit>
	    DD 9E 00 08 00 00
	    89 8F <dFI mtp bytesperunit>
	    DB 87 <dFI mtp bytesperunit>
	    DD 9E E8 07 00 00
	    89 AF <dFI mtp bytesperunit>
	    DB 87 <dFI mtp bytesperunit>
	    DD 9E 28 08 00 00
	    8B 87 <dVHGNDs3 mtp bytesperunit>
	    C1 E0 0B
	    F7 D8
	    89 87 <dFI mtp bytesperunit>
	    DB 87 <dFI mtp bytesperunit>
	    DD 9E 08 08 00 00
	    89 9F <dFI mtp bytesperunit>
	    DB 87 <dFI mtp bytesperunit>
	    DD 9E F0 07 00 00
	    89 AF <dFI mtp bytesperunit>
	    DB 87 <dFI mtp bytesperunit>
	    DD 9E 30 08 00 00
	    8B 87 <dVHGNDs4 mtp bytesperunit>
	    C1 E0 0B
	    F7 D8
	    89 87 <dFI mtp bytesperunit>
	    DB 87 <dFI mtp bytesperunit>
	    DD 9E 10 08 00 00
	    DD 86 10 08 00 00
	    DD 9F <dFA0 mtp bytesperunit>
	    89 87 <dVHGNDvv mtp bytesperunit>
	    C7 87 <dVHGNDvi mtp bytesperunit> 02 00 00 00
	    C7 87 <dVHGNDvslot mtp bytesperunit> 00 01 00 00
	    C7 87 <dPGFi mtp bytesperunit> 02 01 00 00
	    5D
	}
	end;

"VHGND vload"
	[FI] = [VHGNDvv]; => IntToF;
	A = [VHGNDvslot]; A + [VHGNDvi]; [PGFi] = A; => PGF sa;
	end;
